# 06 · 网页前端源码（apps/web）

> **M1.8 Prediction Workbench** —— Vue 3 + Vite + Pinia 的浏览器工作台，共 68 个文件。
> 与引擎不同，**这里允许第三方依赖**（vue / pinia / vite / pinyin-pro）；「零第三方依赖」只约束 `battle-lab/`。
> 引擎源码见 **03-源码汇编**；被本文件排除的生成物见 **04-数据摘要**。

## 精简说明（重要）

- **`.vue` 文件只保留 `<script>` 与 `<template>`**：`<style>` 段已整段剔除（共 53 KB 纯样式，约占 .vue 体积的 28%）。
  被剔除处留一行 `<!-- <style> 已省略 … KB（纯样式，无逻辑） -->` 作为标记；样式不承载业务逻辑，剔除后组件语义完整。
- 除上述外**所有代码块都是原文照抄**，可以直接让 AI 基于它改代码。
- 未收录：
  - `package-lock.json`（52 KB） —— 生成物
  - `src/engine/bundle.json`（650 KB） —— 生成物
  - `src/kb/experiments.json`（81 KB） —— 生成物
  - `src/kb/glossary.json`（25 KB） —— 生成物
  - `src/kb/shikigami-detail.json`（552 KB） —— 生成物
  - `src/kb/shikigami-index.json`（47 KB） —— 生成物
  - `src/kb/soul-index.json`（28 KB） —— 生成物
- 构建目录 `node_modules/`、`dist/` 与隐藏目录一律不收录。

## 分层约束（唯一硬规则）

```
components / stores / App.vue
        │  只允许依赖 ↓
        ├─▶ src/contracts/*   三份冻结 DTO 契约
        └─▶ src/kb/useKb.ts   KB 索引（只读 DTO）

src/adapters/*  src/workers/*   ← 全项目仅有的两处允许 import @engine（= battle-lab/）
                                vite alias：@engine → ../../battle-lab
```

这条约束有测试兜底：`tests/ui1-editor.ts` 用静态扫描断言
`stores/**.ts` 与 `components/**/*.vue` 里不出现 `battle-lab` / `@engine`。

### `apps/web/vite.config.ts`

```ts
import { defineConfig } from "vite";
import vue from "@vitejs/plugin-vue";
import path from "node:path";

const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", "..");

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [vue()],
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      "@": path.resolve(__dirname, "src"),
      // ★ 引擎只允许被 adapters / workers 引用；页面与 store 不得 import 这里
      "@engine": path.resolve(ROOT, "battle-lab"),
    },
  },
  server: {
    fs: {
      // 允许读取工程根（引擎源码在 apps/web 之外）
      allow: [ROOT],
    },
  },
  worker: { format: "es" },
  build: { target: "es2022", sourcemap: true },
});
```

### `apps/web/tsconfig.json`

```json
{
  "compilerOptions": {
    "target": "ES2022",
    "module": "ESNext",
    "moduleResolution": "Bundler",
    // 本项目所有 import 都显式带 .ts 后缀（Node 24 直接跑 .ts 的需要），
    // 不打开这个开关 vue-tsc 会为每个 import 报一条 TS5097（原先 76 条里占 54 条）。
    "allowImportingTsExtensions": true,
    "noEmit": true,
    "strict": true,
    "jsx": "preserve",
    "sourceMap": true,
    "resolveJsonModule": true,
    "esModuleInterop": true,
    "skipLibCheck": true,
    "isolatedModules": true,
    "lib": [
      "ES2022",
      "DOM",
      "DOM.Iterable",
      "WebWorker"
    ],
    "types": [
      "vite/client"
    ],
    "paths": {
      "@/*": [
        "./src/*"
      ],
      "@engine/*": [
        "../../battle-lab/*"
      ]
    }
  },
  // 构建配置 vite.config.ts 不进类型检查：它用到 node:path / __dirname，
  // 需要 @types/node，而本项目的运行时依赖刻意保持为零（引擎不依赖任何第三方包）。
  // 想连构建配置一起检查，就装 @types/node 再把它加回来。
  "include": [
    "src/**/*.ts",
    "src/**/*.vue"
  ]
}
```

### `apps/web/src/main.ts`

```ts
import { createApp } from "vue";
import { createPinia } from "pinia";
import App from "./App.vue";
import "./styles/base.css";

const app = createApp(App);
/**
 * ★★★ 第 256 轮：**错误出口必须在页面上看得见**。
 *   由来：用户截图里「观看战斗」整块空白（连兜底文案都没有），而组件级 SSR、逐帧 Presenter、
 *   Worker 三种探针全绿 —— 也就是说**出了错也没人看得见**，只能靠猜。
 *   这一族（静默失败）在本仓库已经栽过好几次（启动器不打开浏览器、报表比现实乐观…），
 *   处置一致：把它变成一句看得见的话。
 *   ① 挂全局 errorHandler（渲染/生命周期/侦听器里的异常都会走到这里）；
 *   ② 往 #app 里塞一个固定的错误条（不依赖 Vue 的渲染树 —— 树自己坏了它才最需要工作）。
 */
app.config.errorHandler = (err, _inst, info) => {
  const msg = (err as any)?.message ?? String(err);
  console.error("[engine-ui] " + info + ": " + msg, err);
  try {
    const host = document.getElementById("app");
    if (!host) return;
    let bar = document.getElementById("dsh-crash");
    if (!bar) {
      bar = document.createElement("pre");
      bar.id = "dsh-crash";
      bar.style.cssText = "position:fixed;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;max-height:35vh;overflow:auto;margin:0;padding:10px 14px;background:#3a1414;color:#ffd9d9;font:12px/1.5 ui-monospace,Consolas,monospace;border-top:1px solid #a33;z-index:9999;white-space:pre-wrap";
      host.appendChild(bar);
    }
    bar.textContent = "⚠ 界面出错（" + info + "）：" + msg + "\n这一条是自动弹出来的：出错不再静默。把这段文字截图给维护方即可。" + (bar.textContent ? "\n\n" + bar.textContent : "");
  } catch { /* 连错误条都画不出来时，至少 console 里有 */ }
};
app.use(createPinia()).mount("#app");
```

### `apps/web/src/App.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// App.vue — UI 2.0 外壳（a1-4 §29-§34）
// §31 布局：Header → 红蓝横排阵容 → Validation → Simulation Control → Result
// §34 两条运行模式：模拟胜率 / 观看一场
// §29 只重做视觉层与布局，**不动** contracts / stores / adapters / workers / components 的分层
import { computed, onErrorCaptured, ref, watch } from "vue";
import { useMatchStore } from "./stores/match";
import { useSimulationStore } from "./stores/simulation";
import { canonicalMatch } from "./mock/canonical-match";
import UnitTile from "./components/lineup/UnitTile.vue";
import ShikigamiSelector from "./components/lineup/ShikigamiSelector.vue";
import SoulSelector from "./components/lineup/SoulSelector.vue";
import HealthCheck from "./components/simulation/HealthCheck.vue";
import FidelityPanel from "./components/simulation/FidelityPanel.vue";
import DetailDrawer from "./components/knowledge/DetailDrawer.vue";
import CodexView from "./components/knowledge/CodexView.vue";
import MechanicsView from "./components/mechanics/MechanicsView.vue";
import JsonIo from "./components/common/JsonIo.vue";
import DeveloperHud from "./components/dev/DeveloperHud.vue";
import ResultDashboard from "./components/result/ResultDashboard.vue";
import ReplayViewer from "./components/result/ReplayViewer.vue";
import BattleTheater from "./components/theater/BattleTheater.vue";
import { panelPrefill } from "./kb/useKb";
import type { ShikigamiIndexEntry, SoulIndexEntry } from "./kb/useKb";
import type { StatKey, GuessingMatch, GuessingUnitInput } from "./contracts/guessing-match";
// ★★★ 第 138 轮（§33）：Per-Match Fidelity 的**逐技能**一层直接进阵容编辑器 ——
//   卡面拿它显示「●3 ◐1」，抽屉拿它显示 Raw 与 Compiled 并排。取数逻辑在 contracts/fidelity-view.ts（可单测）。
import { skillStatusOf } from "./contracts/fidelity-view.ts";

const match = useMatchStore();
const sim = useSimulationStore();

type View = "sim" | "theater" | "replay" | "codex" | "mechanics";
const view = ref<View>("sim");
const VIEWS: Array<{ id: View; label: string }> = [
  { id: "sim", label: "工作台" }, { id: "theater", label: "观看战斗" },
  { id: "replay", label: "事件回放" }, { id: "codex", label: "知识库" }, { id: "mechanics", label: "机制实验" },
];
/** §34：两条运行模式 */
const mode = ref<"winrate" | "watch">("winrate");

/**
 * ★★★ 第 256 轮：**这一层也要有错误出口**。
 *   用户截图里「观看战斗」整块空白 —— 组件级 SSR / 逐帧 Presenter / Worker 三种探针全绿，
 *   说明那个空白是**渲染树里抛了异常而没人接**（Vue 把子树卸掉，页面就只剩空的）。
 *   这里接住子树的异常并**画出来**：空白页从此变成一句能截图的话。
 */
const crash = ref<string | null>(null);
onErrorCaptured((err, _inst, info) => {
  crash.value = info + "：" + ((err as any)?.message ?? String(err));
  console.error("[engine-ui][App] " + crash.value, err);
  return false;   // 已经画出来了，不再往上冒（全局那一层留给渲染树之外的错误）
});

const target = ref<{ side: "red" | "blue"; index: number } | null>(null);
const soulTarget = ref<{ side: "red" | "blue"; index: number } | null>(null);
/**
 * ★ 第 138 轮：抽屉要能回答「这个单位的逐技能编译状态」——所以除 unit 外还得记住它是哪一边、第几个。
 *   只按名字找是不行的：同一只式神可以合法地出现在双方（对弈两边各 5 只）。
 */
const drawer = ref<{ unit: GuessingUnitInput | null; tab: "skills" | "ai" | "soul"; side: "red" | "blue"; index: number }>({ unit: null, tab: "skills", side: "red", index: 0 });
const activeTile = ref<string | null>(null);
const ioError = ref<string | null>(null);
const hudOpen = ref(false);

const filled = computed(() => match.filledCount);
const unknownCount = computed(() => sim.validation?.strategySnapshot.unknownCount ?? null);
const engineReady = computed(() => !!sim.validation);

function openSelector(side: "red" | "blue", index: number) { target.value = { side, index }; }
function openSoul(side: "red" | "blue", index: number) { soulTarget.value = { side, index }; }
function pickShikigami(x: ShikigamiIndexEntry) {
  if (!target.value) return;
  const { side, index } = target.value;
  const u = match.unit(side, index);
  const untouched = !u.shikigamiId || (u.stats.attack === 0 && u.stats.hp === 0 && u.stats.defense === 0 && u.stats.speed === 0);
  match.setShikigami(side, index, x.i, x.n);
  if (untouched) {
    const pre = panelPrefill(x.i);
    if (pre) for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(pre)) match.setStat(side, index, k as StatKey, v);
  }
  target.value = null;
  activeTile.value = side + index;
  refreshValidation();
}
function pickSoul(s: SoulIndexEntry | null) {
  if (!soulTarget.value) return;
  match.setSoul(soulTarget.value.side, soulTarget.value.index, s ? s.i : null);
  soulTarget.value = null;
  refreshValidation();
}
function openDetail(side: "red" | "blue", index: number, tab: "skills" | "ai" | "soul") {
  drawer.value = { unit: match.unit(side, index), tab, side, index };
}
/**
 * ★★★ 第 138 轮：某个单位的逐技能编译状态。
 *   判据是 **side + 名字**（不是 index）：Fidelity 载荷按「红/蓝 + 式神名」组织，
 *   而名字是对弈里唯一稳定、也是报告里点名用的标识。
 */
function skillStatusFor(side: "red" | "blue", name: string) {
  return skillStatusOf(sim.validation?.fidelity ?? null, side, name);
}

let timer: any = null;
function refreshValidation() { clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(() => sim.validate(match.match), 400); }
watch(() => JSON.stringify(match.match.red.units.map(u => [u.shikigamiId, u.soulId, u.stats])), refreshValidation);
watch(() => JSON.stringify(match.match.blue.units.map(u => [u.shikigamiId, u.soulId, u.stats])), refreshValidation);

function loadFixture() { match.replace(canonicalMatch()); refreshValidation(); }
function onImport(m: GuessingMatch) { match.replace(m); ioError.value = null; refreshValidation(); }
function swap() { match.swap(); refreshValidation(); }

const simCount = ref(10000);
const adaptive = ref(false);
function run() {
  if (adaptive.value) { adaptive.value = false; runAdaptive(); return; }
  sim.simulate(match.match, { simulations: simCount.value, seed: "AUTO", progressEvery: 500 });
}
/** 自适应：先跑 1k，Wilson 区间仍宽于 6 个百分点就再加到 10k —— 省时间又不假装精确 */
async function runAdaptive() {
  sim.simulate(match.match, { simulations: 1000, seed: "AUTO", progressEvery: 250 });
  const wait = setInterval(() => {
    if (sim.running || !sim.result) return;
    clearInterval(wait);
    const ci = sim.result.confidenceInterval;
    const width = ci ? (ci.high - ci.low) * 100 : 100;
    if (width > 6 && sim.result.simulations < 10000) {
      sim.simulate(match.match, { simulations: 10000, seed: "AUTO", progressEvery: 500 });
    }
  }, 250);
}
const theaterSeed = ref(20261001);
function watchOne(seed: number) { theaterSeed.value = seed; view.value = "theater"; sim.replay(match.match, seed); }
/**
 * ★★★ 第 256 轮：**「观看战斗」这个标签页以前只切视图** —— 没录过录像时就只剩一句
 *   「还没有可观看的战斗。」（用户实测报的就是这个：点了却看不了）。
 *   现在它走 store 的 `watchOrReplay`：有录像只切视图（不重复录），没录像**当场录一场**。
 */
function openTheater() { view.value = "theater"; sim.watchOrReplay(match.match, theaterSeed.value); }
function openReplay(seed: number) { view.value = "replay"; sim.replay(match.match, seed); }
function startMode() { if (mode.value === "watch") watchOne(theaterSeed.value); else run(); }
</script>

<template>
  <div class="shell">
    <header class="top">
      <div class="brand">
        <span class="crest">弈</span>
        <span>阴阳师 · 对弈模拟器</span>
        <span class="ver mono">Engine v0.1 · GameData 2026-10</span>
      </div>
      <nav>
        <!-- ★ 第 256 轮：「观看战斗」这个标签走 openTheater（没录像就当场录一场），其余标签只切视图 -->
        <button v-for="v in VIEWS" :key="v.id" class="tab" :class="{ active: view === v.id }"
                @click="v.id === 'theater' ? openTheater() : (view = v.id)">{{ v.label }}</button>
      </nav>
      <div class="hud">
        <span class="badge" :class="unknownCount === 0 ? 'ok' : 'warn'"
              :title="unknownCount === null ? '尚未运行引擎校验' : '存在 UNKNOWN 机制策略 —— 结果不构成机制结论'">
          ⚠ {{ unknownCount === null ? "UNKNOWN 未评估" : unknownCount + " UNKNOWN" }}
        </span>
        <span class="badge" :class="engineReady ? 'ok' : 'dim'">{{ engineReady ? "引擎已就绪" : "等待校验" }}</span>
        <button class="tab hudbtn" :class="{ active: hudOpen }" @click="hudOpen = !hudOpen">HUD</button>
      </div>
    </header>

    <!-- ★ 第 256 轮：子树抛异常时，这里必须出现一句看得见的话（而不是整块空白） -->
    <div v-if="crash" class="errbar" style="margin:10px 16px">
      ⚠ 界面出错（{{ crash }}）—— 这一条是自动弹出来的：出错不再静默。把这段截图给维护方即可。
      <button @click="crash = null" style="margin-left:8px">✕</button>
    </div>

    <template v-if="view === 'sim'">
      <!-- §34：两条运行模式 -->
      <div class="modebar">
        <button class="mode" :class="{ on: mode === 'winrate' }" @click="mode = 'winrate'">
          <b>模拟胜率</b><span class="tiny dim">跑 N 局，看胜率与不确定性</span>
        </button>
        <button class="mode" :class="{ on: mode === 'watch' }" @click="mode = 'watch'">
          <b>观看一场</b><span class="tiny dim">录一场完整战斗，逐帧看机制</span>
        </button>
        <span class="spacer" />
        <button @click="loadFixture">载入典型对局 #001</button>
        <JsonIo :match="match.match" @import="onImport" @swap="swap" @error="(e: string) => ioError = e" />
        <button @click="match.reset()">清空</button>
        <span class="dim small">已填 <b class="mono">{{ filled }}</b>/10</span>
        <span v-if="match.savedAt" class="dim tiny">草稿已保存</span>
      </div>
      <div v-if="ioError" class="errbar">✗ {{ ioError }} <button @click="ioError = null">✕</button></div>

      <!-- §31：红蓝横排（更像游戏阵容，也省纵向空间） -->
      <main class="board">
        <section class="row red">
          <div class="sidehead">🔴 红方</div>
          <div class="tiles">
            <UnitTile v-for="(u, i) in match.match.red.units" :key="u.unitId" :unit="u" side="red" :index="i"
              :active="activeTile === 'red' + i" :skill-status="skillStatusFor('red', u.name)"
              @open-selector="openSelector('red', i)" @open-soul="openSoul('red', i)"
              @clear="match.clearUnit('red', i); refreshValidation()"
              @set-stat="(k: StatKey, v: number) => { match.setStat('red', i, k, v) }"
              @open-detail="(t: 'skills' | 'ai' | 'soul') => openDetail('red', i, t)"
              @activate="activeTile = 'red' + i" />
          </div>
        </section>

        <div class="vs">VS</div>

        <section class="row blue">
          <div class="sidehead">蓝方 🔵</div>
          <div class="tiles">
            <UnitTile v-for="(u, i) in match.match.blue.units" :key="u.unitId" :unit="u" side="blue" :index="i"
              :active="activeTile === 'blue' + i" :skill-status="skillStatusFor('blue', u.name)"
              @open-selector="openSelector('blue', i)" @open-soul="openSoul('blue', i)"
              @clear="match.clearUnit('blue', i); refreshValidation()"
              @set-stat="(k: StatKey, v: number) => { match.setStat('blue', i, k, v) }"
              @open-detail="(t: 'skills' | 'ai' | 'soul') => openDetail('blue', i, t)"
              @activate="activeTile = 'blue' + i" />
          </div>
        </section>
      </main>

      <!-- Validation：健康检查 + 本局可信度（§33） -->
      <section class="validation">
        <HealthCheck :match="match.match" :report="sim.validation" />
        <FidelityPanel :fidelity="sim.validation?.fidelity ?? null" />
      </section>

      <!-- Simulation Control -->
      <section class="control">
        <div class="ctlrow">
          <span class="ctllabel">模拟次数</span>
          <button v-for="n in [100, 1000, 10000]" :key="n" class="nbtn" :class="{ on: !adaptive && simCount === n }"
                  @click="adaptive = false; simCount = n">{{ n >= 1000 ? n / 1000 + "k" : n }}</button>
          <button class="nbtn" :class="{ on: adaptive }" @click="adaptive = true" title="先跑 1k，区间过宽再加到 10k">自适应</button>
          <span class="spacer" />
          <label v-if="mode === 'watch'" class="seed">seed <input type="number" v-model.number="theaterSeed" style="width:110px" /></label>
          <button class="primary big" :disabled="sim.running || filled < 10" @click="startMode">
            {{ mode === "watch" ? "▶ 观看这一场" : "▶ 开始模拟" }}
          </button>
          <button v-if="sim.running" @click="sim.cancel()">停止</button>
        </div>
        <div v-if="sim.progress" class="prog">
          <div class="bar"><i :style="{ width: sim.percent + '%' }"></i></div>
          <div class="mono tiny dim">{{ sim.progress.completed.toLocaleString() }} / {{ sim.progress.total.toLocaleString() }}
            <template v-if="sim.progress.runningRedWinRate !== null"> · 红 {{ (sim.progress.runningRedWinRate * 100).toFixed(1) }}%</template>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div v-if="sim.error" class="errbox">✗ {{ sim.error }}</div>
      </section>

      <div v-if="sim.result || sim.running" class="resultwrap">
        <ResultDashboard v-if="sim.result" :result="sim.result" :fidelity="sim.validation?.fidelity ?? null"
                         @replay="openReplay" @watch="watchOne" @close="sim.result = null" />
      </div>
    </template>

    <template v-else-if="view === 'theater'">
      <div class="page">
        <BattleTheater v-if="sim.replayFile" :replay="sim.replayFile" :seed="theaterSeed" @close="view = 'sim'" />
        <div v-else class="empty">
          <!-- ★ 第 256 轮：三种状态分开说 —— 「正在录」不再是空白等待，「录失败」也不再冒充「还没有」 -->
          <p v-if="sim.replayPending">正在录这一场…（seed <b class="mono">{{ theaterSeed }}</b>）</p>
          <p v-else-if="sim.error" class="errbar">✗ 录制失败：{{ sim.error }}</p>
          <p v-else>还没有可观看的战斗。</p>
          <p class="dim small">在【工作台 → 观看一场】里录一场，或在结果页的样本对局点「观看战斗」。</p>
          <div class="seedbox">
            <input type="number" v-model.number="theaterSeed" placeholder="输入 seed" />
            <button :disabled="filled < 10 || sim.replayPending" @click="openTheater()">观看这一场</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </template>

    <template v-else-if="view === 'replay'">
      <div class="page">
        <ReplayViewer v-if="sim.replayData" :seed="sim.replayData.seed" :events="sim.replayData.events" :units="sim.replayData.units"
                      :turns="sim.replayData.turns" :winner="sim.replayData.winner as any" @close="view = 'sim'" />
        <div v-else class="empty">
          <p>还没有可回放的对局。</p>
          <div class="seedbox">
            <input type="number" v-model.number="theaterSeed" placeholder="输入 seed" />
            <button :disabled="filled < 10" @click="openReplay(theaterSeed)">按 seed 回放</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </template>

    <template v-else-if="view === 'codex'"><div class="page"><CodexView /></div></template>
    <template v-else><div class="page"><MechanicsView /></div></template>

    <DeveloperHud :open="hudOpen" @close="hudOpen = false" />
    <ShikigamiSelector :open="!!target" @pick="pickShikigami" @close="target = null" />
    <SoulSelector :open="!!soulTarget" @pick="pickSoul" @close="soulTarget = null" />
    <DetailDrawer :open="!!drawer.unit" :unit="drawer.unit" :tab="drawer.tab"
      :skill-status="drawer.unit ? skillStatusFor(drawer.side, drawer.unit.name) : []"
      @close="drawer.unit = null" @tab="(t: 'skills' | 'ai' | 'soul') => drawer.tab = t" />
  </div>
</template>

<!-- <style> 已省略 4 KB（纯样式，无逻辑） -->
```

### `apps/web/src/contracts/fidelity-view.ts`

```ts
// contracts/fidelity-view.ts — ★★★ 第 138 轮：Per-Match Fidelity 的**逐技能**视图契约（a1-4 §33）
//
// 背景（第 137 轮审核的结论）：§33 要求「卡上按逐技能机制实现度 ● FULL / ◐ PARTIAL 并可展开」，
// 而当时的实现是「数据在不在」的四色圆点，展开抽屉里只有原始技能文本 —— **编译产物到不了界面**：
// kernel/fidelity.ts 把 30 个技能聚合成 6 个计数，逐技能那一层在载荷里根本不存在。
//
// 这一层把它补上。两条纪律：
//   ① **结构镜像**：页面层不许 import @engine（vite.config 的 alias 注释在守这条），所以这里自己声明形状；
//      漂移由 tests/ui10-fidelity.ts 拿**真引擎产出的载荷**逐字段核对。
//   ② **词汇只有一份**：记号与短标签声明在这里，卡面 / 抽屉 / 面板都读它；与 kernel/fidelity.ts 的
//      SKILL_CLASS_MARK / SKILL_CLASS_LABEL **逐字节相等**由 ui10 断言（不许各写各的、慢慢写歪）。

export type SkillFidelityClass = "FULL" | "PARTIAL" | "PASSIVE" | "NONE";

/** 引擎 MatchFidelity.skills[] 的一条（载荷里的原样） */
export interface SkillFidelityView {
  unit: string;
  side: "red" | "blue";
  name: string;
  klass: SkillFidelityClass;
  /** 未解析的机制句数量 */
  unparsed: number;
  /** 引擎**明确跳过**的记录条数 */
  skipped: number;
  /** 依赖哪些未建模机制（动词名）—— ★ 它不参与分档，界面上必须与档位分开显示 */
  unmodeled: string[];
}

/** 记号与短标签（与 kernel/fidelity.ts 逐字节一致，ui10 断言） */
export const SKILL_MARK: Record<SkillFidelityClass, string> = { FULL: "●", PARTIAL: "◐", PASSIVE: "○", NONE: "✕" };
export const SKILL_LABEL: Record<SkillFidelityClass, string> = {
  FULL: "完整编译", PARTIAL: "部分编译", PASSIVE: "被动（触发条件未建模）", NONE: "主动但无可执行数值",
};

/** 严重度顺序（卡面小圆点取「最重」的那一档）：NONE > PARTIAL > PASSIVE > FULL */
export const SKILL_SEVERITY: SkillFidelityClass[] = ["NONE", "PARTIAL", "PASSIVE", "FULL"];
/**
 * **显示**顺序（记数串与逐条清单按它排）：与档位表同序 —— 先 ● 后 ◐。
 * ★ 与 SKILL_SEVERITY 是两个不同的东西（一个问「最重是谁」，一个问「先写谁」），
 *   所以它们是两张表；混用会让记数串读成 ◐1 ●2（第 138 轮第一版就是这样，SSR 渲染时被抓到）。
 */
export const SKILL_TIER_ORDER: SkillFidelityClass[] = ["FULL", "PARTIAL", "PASSIVE", "NONE"];

export interface FidelityCountView { label: string; value: string; note?: string }
export interface FidelityGapView { what: string; severity: string; detail: string }
export interface FidelityLayerView {
  layer: string; title: string; klass: string;
  counts: FidelityCountView[]; gaps: FidelityGapView[];
  bar: { filled: number; total: number } | null;
}
export interface FidelityView {
  klass: string;
  layers: FidelityLayerView[];
  reasons: string[];
  caveats: string[];
  /** ★ 第 138 轮：逐技能编译状态（引擎侧同名字段；缺省时按「没有这一层信息」处理） */
  skills?: SkillFidelityView[];
}

/** 取某个单位的逐技能状态（载荷缺失 / 名字对不上时返回空数组，界面显示「无编译信息」） */
export function skillStatusOf(fidelity: unknown, side: "red" | "blue", name: string): SkillFidelityView[] {
  const list = (fidelity as FidelityView | null)?.skills;
  if (!Array.isArray(list)) return [];
  return list.filter(s => s.side === side && s.unit === name);
}

/** 最重的一档（列表为空时 null）。判据用 SKILL_SEVERITY，不另写一套顺序。 */
export function worstSkillClass(list: SkillFidelityView[]): SkillFidelityClass | null {
  for (const k of SKILL_SEVERITY) if (list.some(s => s.klass === k)) return k;
  return null;
}

/** 记数串（形如 ●2 ◐1）；按 SKILL_SEVERITY 排，保证同输入同输出 */
export function skillTally(list: SkillFidelityView[]): string {
  return SKILL_TIER_ORDER.filter(k => list.some(s => s.klass === k))
    .map(k => SKILL_MARK[k] + list.filter(s => s.klass === k).length).join(" ");
}

/** 逐技能状态按单位分组（**保序**：先出现的单位在前，单位内保持引擎给的顺序） */
export function groupSkillStatus(list: SkillFidelityView[]): Array<{ key: string; unit: string; side: "red" | "blue"; skills: SkillFidelityView[]; tally: string; worst: SkillFidelityClass | null }> {
  const out: Array<{ key: string; unit: string; side: "red" | "blue"; skills: SkillFidelityView[]; tally: string; worst: SkillFidelityClass | null }> = [];
  for (const s of list) {
    const key = s.side + "/" + s.unit;
    let g = out.find(x => x.key === key);
    if (!g) { g = { key, unit: s.unit, side: s.side, skills: [], tally: "", worst: null }; out.push(g); }
    g.skills.push(s);
  }
  for (const g of out) { g.tally = skillTally(g.skills); g.worst = worstSkillClass(g.skills); }
  return out;
}
```

### `apps/web/src/contracts/guessing-match.ts`

```ts
// contracts/guessing-match.ts — ★ UI / Engine 边界契约（冻结）
// 规则：本文件**零依赖**，不得 import 任何 Engine 内部类型。
// 规则：**永远不出现 soulPieces[]** —— 那是 BuildMatch，不是 GuessingMatch。

export const GUESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1 as const;

export const MatchSourceType = {
  MANUAL: "MANUAL",
  SCREENSHOT: "SCREENSHOT",
  JSON: "JSON",
  FIXTURE: "FIXTURE",
} as const;
export type MatchSourceType = (typeof MatchSourceType)[keyof typeof MatchSourceType];

/** 八维面板。百分比类以**百分数**存储（28 表示 28%），与游戏界面一致，避免录入歧义。 */
export interface GuessingStats {
  attack: number;
  hp: number;
  defense: number;
  speed: number;
  critRate: number;
  critDamage: number;
  effectHit: number;
  effectResist: number;
}

export interface GuessingUnitInput {
  /** 稳定 id，仅用于 UI 定位（交换/删除/重排时不变） */
  unitId: string;
  shikigamiId: number;
  /** 冗余存储名称，便于离线展示与导入可读性；引擎以 shikigamiId 为准 */
  name: string;
  stats: GuessingStats;
  /** 御魂图标对应的御魂 id；未识别时为 null（只影响战斗效果，不影响面板） */
  soulId: number | null;
}

export interface GuessingTeam {
  units: GuessingUnitInput[];
}

export interface GuessingMatch {
  schemaVersion: typeof GUESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION;
  id: string;
  red: GuessingTeam;
  blue: GuessingTeam;
  ruleset: "GUESSING";
  source?: {
    type: MatchSourceType;
    capturedAt?: string;
    note?: string;
  };
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export const STAT_KEYS = ["attack", "hp", "defense", "speed", "critRate", "critDamage", "effectHit", "effectResist"] as const;
export type StatKey = (typeof STAT_KEYS)[number];
export const PERCENT_STAT_KEYS: StatKey[] = ["critRate", "critDamage", "effectHit", "effectResist"];
export const STAT_LABELS: Record<StatKey, string> = {
  attack: "攻击", hp: "生命", defense: "防御", speed: "速度",
  critRate: "暴击", critDamage: "暴击伤害", effectHit: "效果命中", effectResist: "效果抵抗",
};

export const TEAM_SIZE = 5;

let seq = 0;
export function newUnitId(): string { return "u" + (++seq).toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6); }

export function emptyStats(): GuessingStats {
  return { attack: 0, hp: 0, defense: 0, speed: 0, critRate: 0, critDamage: 150, effectHit: 0, effectResist: 0 };
}

export function emptyUnit(name = ""): GuessingUnitInput {
  return { unitId: newUnitId(), shikigamiId: 0, name, stats: emptyStats(), soulId: null };
}

export function emptyMatch(): GuessingMatch {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    schemaVersion: GUESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    id: "m" + Date.now().toString(36),
    red: { units: Array.from({ length: TEAM_SIZE }, () => emptyUnit()) },
    blue: { units: Array.from({ length: TEAM_SIZE }, () => emptyUnit()) },
    ruleset: "GUESSING",
    source: { type: MatchSourceType.MANUAL },
    createdAt: now, updatedAt: now,
  };
}

/** 红蓝互换（UX 护栏 #1 需要它是一等操作） */
export function swapSides(m: GuessingMatch): GuessingMatch {
  const red = m.blue.units.map(u => ({ ...u, unitId: newUnitId() }));
  const blue = m.red.units.map(u => ({ ...u, unitId: newUnitId() }));
  return { ...m, red: { units: red }, blue: { units: blue }, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
}
```

### `apps/web/src/contracts/simulation-result.ts`

```ts
// contracts/simulation-result.ts — ★ 结果契约（冻结）
// UI 不需要自己重算任何东西；这里没有的字段就是引擎没提供。
import type { GuessingStats } from "./guessing-match.ts";

export interface WilsonInterval {
  method: "WILSON";
  level: number;      // 0.95
  low: number;
  high: number;
}

export interface AggregateBattleStats {
  avgTurns: number;
  medianTurns: number;
  p95Turns: number;
  firstKillRedRate: number | null;
  avgSurvivorsRed: number;
  avgSurvivorsBlue: number;
  avgRemainHpRed: number;
  avgRemainHpBlue: number;
  avgOrbSpentRed: number;
  avgOrbSpentBlue: number;
  /** 每只式神的聚合贡献（按 unitId 索引） */
  perUnit: PerUnitStats[];
}

export interface PerUnitStats {
  unitId: string;
  name: string;
  side: "red" | "blue";
  avgDamageDealt: number;
  avgDamageTaken: number;
  avgHealing: number;
  kills: number;
  deaths: number;
  deathRate: number;
  avgActions: number;
  skillUsage: Record<string, number>;
  soulTriggerCount: number;
  controlApplied: number;
  controlResisted: number;
}

/** 机制策略快照：与结果一起永久保存 */
export interface StrategySnapshot {
  startup: { id: string; status: "UNKNOWN" | "HYPOTHESIS" | "VERIFIED" };
  triggerPriority: { id: string; status: "UNKNOWN" | "HYPOTHESIS" | "VERIFIED" };
  death: { id: string; status: "UNKNOWN" | "HYPOTHESIS" | "VERIFIED" };
  endgame: { id: string; status: "UNKNOWN" | "HYPOTHESIS" | "VERIFIED" };
  resource: { id: string; status: "UNKNOWN" | "HYPOTHESIS" | "VERIFIED" };
  unknownCount: number;
}

export interface CoverageSnapshot {
  unitsJoined: number; unitsTotal: number;
  skillsAvailable: number; skillsTotal: number;
  aiHigh: number; aiTotal: number;
  soulsResolved: number; soulsTotal: number;
  soulEffectsFull: number; soulEffectsPartial: number;
  unknownBuffRefs: string[];
  unsupportedCapabilities: string[];
}

export const WarningKind = {
  UNKNOWN_STRATEGY: "UNKNOWN_STRATEGY",
  LOW_AI_CONFIDENCE: "LOW_AI_CONFIDENCE",
  SOUL_EFFECT_UNSUPPORTED: "SOUL_EFFECT_UNSUPPORTED",
  UNKNOWN_BUFF_REF: "UNKNOWN_BUFF_REF",
  HIGH_STALL_RATE: "HIGH_STALL_RATE",
  SMALL_SAMPLE: "SMALL_SAMPLE",
  MODEL_SENSITIVE: "MODEL_SENSITIVE",
  DATA_MISSING: "DATA_MISSING",
} as const;
export type WarningKind = (typeof WarningKind)[keyof typeof WarningKind];

export interface SimulationWarning {
  kind: WarningKind;
  severity: "INFO" | "WARN" | "BLOCK";
  message: string;
  detail?: string;
}

/** 模型不确定性：与 Monte Carlo 抽样误差**严格分开** */
export interface ModelUncertainty {
  /** 各候选策略下的红胜率 */
  perStrategy: Array<{ label: string; group: "STARTUP" | "TRIGGER" | "DEATH" | "ENDGAME" | "TURN_LIMIT"; redWinRate: number | null; note?: string }>;
  min: number | null;
  max: number | null;
  /** max - min，即"结论会不会因为未知机制而翻转" */
  spread: number | null;
  verdict: "STABLE" | "SENSITIVE" | "INCONCLUSIVE";
}

export interface SimulationOptions {
  simulations: number;
  seed: number | "AUTO";
  /** 覆盖默认策略（用于策略敏感性） */
  strategyOverrides?: Record<string, string>;
  maxTurns?: number;
  /** 进度回调批次 */
  progressEvery?: number;
}

export interface RepresentativeSeed {
  seed: number;
  winner: "red" | "blue" | "draw" | "timeout";
  turns: number;
  label?: string;
}

export interface SimulationResult {
  schemaVersion: 1;
  resultId: string;
  matchId: string;

  simulations: number;
  redWins: number;
  blueWins: number;
  draws: number;
  timeouts: number;
  errors: number;
  /** 分母口径：只含有效局，errors 永不进入 */
  decisive: number;

  redWinRate: number | null;
  blueWinRate: number | null;
  confidenceInterval: WilsonInterval | null;
  blueConfidenceInterval: WilsonInterval | null;

  battleStats: AggregateBattleStats;
  strategySnapshot: StrategySnapshot;
  modelUncertainty: ModelUncertainty | null;
  coverage: CoverageSnapshot;
  warnings: SimulationWarning[];

  engineVersion: string;
  gameDataVersion: string;
  createdAt: string;

  seeds?: RepresentativeSeed[];
}

export function isConclusionReady(r: SimulationResult): { ready: boolean; reasons: string[] } {
  const reasons: string[] = [];
  if (r.strategySnapshot.unknownCount > 0) reasons.push(`存在 ${r.strategySnapshot.unknownCount} 项 UNKNOWN 机制策略`);
  if (r.decisive < 1000) reasons.push(`有效样本仅 ${r.decisive} 局`);
  if (r.timeouts / Math.max(1, r.simulations) > 0.2) reasons.push("超时比例 > 20%");
  if (r.errors > 0) reasons.push(`${r.errors} 局异常`);
  return { ready: reasons.length === 0, reasons };
}
```

### `apps/web/src/contracts/worker-api.ts`

```ts
// contracts/worker-api.ts — ★ Worker API（冻结，UI 永远不知道 Battle 内部实现）
import type { GuessingMatch } from "./guessing-match.ts";
import type { SimulationOptions, SimulationResult, CoverageSnapshot, StrategySnapshot, SimulationWarning } from "./simulation-result.ts";

export interface SimulationProgress {
  completed: number;
  total: number;
  redWins: number;
  blueWins: number;
  draws: number;
  timeouts: number;
  errors: number;
  /** 当前滚动估计（可能为 null，样本不足时不假装有值） */
  runningRedWinRate: number | null;
  runningCI: { low: number; high: number } | null;
  elapsedMs: number;
}

export interface SimulationStarted {
  type: "SIMULATION_STARTED";
  requestId: string;
  startedAt: string;
}

export interface SimulationProgressMsg {
  type: "SIMULATION_PROGRESS";
  requestId: string;
  progress: SimulationProgress;
}

export interface SimulationCompleted {
  type: "SIMULATION_COMPLETED";
  requestId: string;
  result: SimulationResult;
}

export interface SimulationFailed {
  type: "SIMULATION_FAILED";
  requestId: string;
  message: string;
  detail?: string;
}

export interface ValidationReport {
  ok: boolean;
  blockers: string[];
  warnings: SimulationWarning[];
  coverage: CoverageSnapshot;
  strategySnapshot: StrategySnapshot;
  /**
   * ★ Per-Match Fidelity（a1-4 §1-§10）：五层各自给**非评价性状态分类**，
   * 绝不合成"真实性百分比"。阵容编辑器在跑之前就能看到它（§33）。
   */
  fidelity?: unknown;
}

export interface ValidationReady {
  type: "VALIDATION_READY";
  requestId: string;
  report: ValidationReport;
}

export interface ReplayPrepared {
  type: "REPLAY_PREPARED";
  requestId: string;
  seed: number;
  /** 事件流（已序列化，UI 只做展示与步进） */
  events: ReplayEvent[];
  units: ReplayUnit[];
  turns: number;
  winner: "red" | "blue" | "draw" | "timeout";
  /**
   * ★ G4：完整 Replay 文件（帧流 + 事件链 + Checkpoint/Delta + 终局状态）。
   * Battle Theater 靠它做到"任意帧可重建战场"——**不需要**再跑一遍引擎。
   * 旧客户端可以忽略这个字段（加法式扩展，不改变已有语义）。
   */
  replay?: unknown;
}

export interface ReplayUnit {
  unitId: string; side: "red" | "blue"; name: string; maxHp: number;
}
export interface ReplayEvent {
  index: number;
  turn: number;
  code: string;
  actorUnitId?: string;
  targetUnitId?: string;
  text: string;
  /** 展示用的结构化 detail（Event Inspector 用） */
  detail?: Record<string, unknown>;
  /** 该事件发生后的快照（用于步进时还原血条/行动条） */
  snapshot?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

export type WorkerRequest =
  | { type: "VALIDATE"; requestId: string; match: GuessingMatch }
  | { type: "SIMULATE"; requestId: string; match: GuessingMatch; options: SimulationOptions }
  | { type: "CANCEL"; requestId: string }
  | { type: "REPLAY"; requestId: string; match: GuessingMatch; seed: number }
  | { type: "PING"; requestId: string };

export type WorkerResponse =
  | SimulationStarted | SimulationProgressMsg | SimulationCompleted | SimulationFailed
  | ValidationReady | ReplayPrepared
  | { type: "PONG"; requestId: string };
```

### `apps/web/src/engine/assemble.ts`

```ts
// engine/assemble.ts — 浏览器侧的「竞猜对局装配」
// ★ 与 battle-lab/soul/guessing-match.ts 共用 compile/character.ts 的同一份装配逻辑，
//   差异只有一处：数据不再从磁盘读，而是从预打包的 EngineBundle 注入。
// ★ 本文件保持 **零 Node API**，因此既能在 Worker 里跑，也能被 Node 测试直接 import 做同构校验。
import { Battle, DEFAULT_RULES } from "../../../../battle-lab/kernel/battle.ts";
import type { TraceLevel } from "../../../../battle-lab/kernel/battle.ts";
import { Team } from "../../../../battle-lab/kernel/team.ts";
import { ResourcePolicies } from "../../../../battle-lab/kernel/resource.ts";
import { StartupPolicies, DeathPolicies, GuessingRulesets, PassivePolicies, LifestealPolicies, ImmunePolicies } from "../../../../battle-lab/kernel/policies.ts";
import { buildCharacter, selectSkillByAI, applyIrCapabilities } from "../../../../battle-lab/compile/character.ts";
import type { LoadedChar } from "../../../../battle-lab/compile/character.ts";
import { compileBuff, toStatusDef } from "../../../../battle-lab/compile/buff-compiler.ts";
import { makeGuessingEntity, verifyGuessingPanel } from "../../../../battle-lab/soul/guessing-factory.ts";
import type { GuessingBuildResult } from "../../../../battle-lab/soul/guessing-factory.ts";
import { compileSoulBook } from "../../../../battle-lab/soul/effects.ts";
import { installSubscribers } from "../../../../battle-lab/compile/subscribers.ts";
import type { SubscriberKit } from "../../../../battle-lab/compile/subscribers.ts";
import { loadTips, setTipsTable } from "../../../../battle-lab/compile/tips.ts";
import { loadBuffNames, setBuffNames } from "../../../../battle-lab/compile/buff-names.ts";
/** ★★★ 第 174 轮：先机子句的**动词名**从编译器引入 —— 与 compile/passive-runtime.ts 同一条规矩（只有一份声明） */
import { APPEND_CAST_VERB } from "../../../../battle-lab/compile/skill-compiler.ts";
import type { SoulDef } from "../../../../battle-lab/soul/loadout.ts";
import type { GuessingMatch } from "../contracts/guessing-match.ts";
import { STAT_KEYS as UI_STAT_KEYS } from "../contracts/guessing-match.ts";

/** 预打包的知识库切片（由 tools/build-engine-bundle.mjs 生成） */
export interface EngineBundle {
  schemaVersion: 1;
  engineVersion: string;
  gameDataVersion: string;
  builtAt: string;
  shiki: any[];
  ai: any[];
  buffs: any[];
  souls: SoulDef[];
  onmyojiAI: { profiles: any[] };
  /** ★ 第 71 轮：data/kb/tips.json 的 {id,name}；[tips_N] 的解析依据 */
  tips?: { id: string; name: string }[];
}

/** UI 八维 -> 引擎 DisplayStats。契约里百分比已是百分数，与引擎展示口径一致，无需换算。 */
const UI_TO_ENGINE: Record<string, string> = {
  attack: "atk", hp: "hp", defense: "def", speed: "spd",
  critRate: "cri", critDamage: "crid", effectHit: "efh", effectResist: "efr",
};
export function toEngineDisplay(stats: any): Record<string, number> {
  const out: any = {};
  for (const k of UI_STAT_KEYS) out[UI_TO_ENGINE[k]] = Number(stats[k] ?? 0);
  return out;
}

export interface EngineKit {
  bundle: EngineBundle;
  /** 每只式神的技能/AI 只编译一次，避免 10,000 局重复编译 */
  charCache: Map<number, LoadedChar>;
  soulDefs: Map<number, SoulDef>;
  soulBook: ReturnType<typeof compileSoulBook>;
  buffs: Map<string, any>;
  shikiById: Map<number, any>;
  aiById: Map<number, any>;
  omyById: Map<number, any>;
  charMeta: Map<number, { name: string; type?: string }>;
  /** 编译期问题（技能 LOW / 缺 AI），与对局无关，整个会话复用 */
  compileIssues: string[];
  /** ★ 占位符表（编译一次，全对局复用）。缺了它分类器会把 "40% [tips_2]" 看成无条件。 */
  tipsMap: Map<string, { id: string; name: string; desc: string }>;
  /**
   * ★★★ 第 186 轮：**状态名字表**（名字 → id 列表）—— 名字形态的状态引用（正文写「雄姿英发」而不是 [buff_x]）要用它。
   *   与 tipsMap 同一条纪律：**入口显式设置**，编译器只读单例；不设置就是空表（名字形态不解析，fail-closed）。
   */
  buffNamesMap: Map<string, string[]>;
  /** ★★★ 第 190 轮：按 defId 取状态定义（跨进程续跑的兜底；见 createKit 里的注） */
  statusDefOf: (defId: string) => any | null;
}

let buffFailures = 0;
export function createKit(bundle: EngineBundle): EngineKit {
  const buffs = new Map<string, any>();
  for (const b of bundle.buffs) {
    try { buffs.set(String(b.id), compileBuff(b)); } catch { buffFailures++; }
  }
  const soulDefs = new Map<number, SoulDef>(bundle.souls.map(s => [Number(s.id), s]));
  const shikiById = new Map<number, any>(bundle.shiki.map(s => [Number(s.id), s]));
  const aiById = new Map<number, any>(bundle.ai.map(a => [Number(a.id), a]));
  const omyById = new Map<number, any>((bundle.onmyojiAI?.profiles ?? []).map(p => [Number(p.id), p]));
  const charMeta = new Map<number, { name: string; type?: string }>();
  for (const s of bundle.shiki) {
    const g = String(s.gradeRaw ?? s.grade ?? s.rarity ?? "");
    charMeta.set(Number(s.id), { name: s.name, type: (g === "阴阳师" || g === "英杰") ? "ONMYOJI" : "SHIKIGAMI" });
  }
  const kit: EngineKit = {
    bundle, charCache: new Map(), soulDefs, soulBook: compileSoulBook(bundle.souls), buffs,
    shikiById, aiById, omyById, charMeta, compileIssues: [],
    // ★ 第 101 轮：编译期解析"形态类放置单位"要读 tips 表 ⇒ 装配时**显式设置**（唯一入口，不靠兜底）
    tipsMap: (() => { const m = loadTips((bundle as any).tips ?? []); setTipsTable(m); return m; })(),
    // ★★★ 第 186 轮：**状态名字表**同样在这里显式设置（浏览器侧从预打包的 bundle 取，与 node 侧读的是同一份数据）
    buffNamesMap: (() => { const m = loadBuffNames(bundle.buffs ?? []); setBuffNames(m); return m; })(),
    /**
     * ★★★ 第 190 轮：**状态定义的兜底来源**（跨进程续跑必需）。
     *
     * 由来：kernel/state.ts 的 restoreBattle 按 defId 从 StatusBook 取状态定义，
     *   而 StatusBook 是**模块级单例**、此前只在技能执行到某个状态时**顺手注册**（compile/character.ts）。
     *   同一个进程里跑过前 20 回合再恢复 ⇒ 表是热的、恢复无损；
     *   换一个进程恢复（真正的「杀掉 Engine → 重新加载」）⇒ 表是冷的，
     *   状态被静默丢掉、只留一条 restore:missing-statusdef 的痕 —— 实测指纹当场漂移
     *   （2d7c7507e18db61f → a85410dcc35735ea，tools/state-cli.ts）。
     *   这里把「知识库在场 ⇒ 状态定义可取」这条不变量交给 kit 自己承担（唯一入口，与 tips / 名字表同一条纪律）。
     */
    statusDefOf: (defId: string) => { const cb = buffs.get(String(defId)); return cb ? toStatusDef(cb) : null; },
  };
  // 预热：把本局可能用到的技能/AI 编译结果缓存起来（首次装配时填充）
  return kit;
}

/** 取（并缓存）某只式神的已编译技能表。teamId/entId 只影响被丢弃的 Entity 外壳，不影响技能对象。 */
function skillsFor(kit: EngineKit, id: number): LoadedChar | null {
  const hit = kit.charCache.get(id);
  if (hit) return hit;
  const s = kit.shikiById.get(id);
  if (!s) return null;
  const lc = buildCharacter(s, kit.aiById.get(id), kit.omyById.get(id), kit.buffs, 0, 0);
  kit.charCache.set(id, lc);
  for (const i of lc.issues) kit.compileIssues.push(`${s.name}: ${i}`);
  return lc;
}

/**
 * ★ G0：按 shikigamiId 取（并缓存）运行时技能表，供 **状态恢复** 重建运行时。
 * 存在的意义：restoreBattle 需要"从身份重建运行时"的能力，而这份能力必须与装配路径
 * 完全同源（同一份 buildCharacter / 同一个缓存），否则恢复出来的行为会与线上悄悄漂移。
 */
export function runtimeFor(kit: EngineKit, shikigamiId: number): { skills: any[] } | null {
  const lc = skillsFor(kit, shikigamiId);
  if (!lc) return null;
  return { skills: lc.entity.skills };
}

/** 编译期画像：技能数 / AI 置信度，供覆盖度统计与 UI 展示（带缓存，不重复编译） */
export interface UnconditionalRule { label: string; cost: number; isBasic: boolean }
/** Fidelity 的 Skill 层需要"这个技能解析出了什么、还漏了什么" */
export interface SkillFidelity {
  name: string; confidence: "HIGH" | "MEDIUM" | "LOW";
  /** 鬼火消耗：<0 = 被动技能（引擎没有触发条件模型，属已知边界） */
  cost: number;
  parsed: { damage: number; heal: number; shield: number; timeline: number; status: number; orb: number };
  unparsedSentences: number;
  /** 原文明确写了「有N%概率…」并被成功归因到效果上的槽位数（掷骰施加） */
  probAttributed: number;
  /** 原文写了概率但归因有歧义、只能退回"必定施加"的处数 —— 这是**已知的过度施加**，必须显示出来 */
  probAmbiguous: number;
  /** 原文写明并被成功归因的「持续N回合」槽位数（真的按这个回合数结算） */
  durationStated: number;
  /** 原文写了「持续N回合」但归因有歧义的处数（只能退回印记描述或 1 回合） */
  durationAmbiguous: number;
  /**
   * ★ 这个技能依赖哪些**引擎没有建模**的机制（召唤 / 复活 / 反击 / 免疫…）。
   * 以前 Fidelity 只能说「N 句机制未建模」，说不清是哪个机制 ——
   * 有了它就能逐条列出"依赖什么"与"引擎缺什么"。来源见 battle-lab/compile/unmodeled.ts。
   */
  unmodeled: Array<{ verb: string; missing: string; severity?: "WARN" | "INFO" }>;
  /**
   * ★★★ 第 112 轮：**"引擎明确跳过"的记录**（放置单位的 formSkips、偷取的 stealSkips）。
   *   它们此前**到不了 Fidelity**：引擎诚实记下了"这条没做"，而每局报告看不见 ——
   *   于是含千姬/绘世花鸟卷的阵容在 SKILL 层显示「完整编译（无可执行缺口）」。
   */
  skips: Array<{ reason: string; evidence: string; kind: "PLACEMENT" | "STEAL" }>;
}
/**
 * ★★★ 第 174 轮：**「先机 → 施放『X』」这条子句的报表放行** —— 判据是引擎自己的产物，且 fail-closed。
 *
 * 由来：第 164–172 轮把这条子句接通了（被动运行时装 BATTLE_START 钩子 → kernel/sub-cast.ts 走伪回合），
 *   可编译器的**句子分类**仍写着 MECHANIC_UNPARSED —— 因为编译器只看得见自己这一个技能，
 *   看不见「『X』到底有没有场地」。于是**报表比代码悲观**：御馔津「狐狩界」在卡面上一直是
 *   ◐ 部分编译，而它唯一没解析的那句正是已经跑通的先机（tests/g96-senki-field.ts 第 [3] 节）。
 *
 * 放行判据（四条都要满足，任一条不满足 ⇒ 照旧记缺口）：
 *   ① 未建模记录里**有且只有一条** [tips_7] + 施放 的追加施放子句（两条同形就不猜哪一条）；
 *   ② 子句里的引用（`[skill_NNN_NN]` 或『名字』）能在**同一个角色的技能表**里解析到；
 *   ③ 被引用的那条技能**真的带 field**（幻境 / 结界）—— 这正是运行时装钩子的前提；
 *   ④ 该子句**确实对应一句** MECHANIC_UNPARSED（调用方核对，见 charInfo）。
 *   ★ 「狐狩界」引用的是它自己（自指也解析得到，与运行时按 e.skills 查找的口径一致）。
 *
 * ★ 它只动**报表**（未解析句数、依赖机制列表），**不动 IR**：编译器说"我没解析这句"是事实，
 *   运行时说"这句有执行点"也是事实，两个事实都留在产物里 —— 这也是为什么放行放在装配层而不是编译器：
 *   编译器拿不到兄弟技能（compileSkill(charId, charName, sk) 没有第三个入参），
 *   而为它加一个"有时传有时不传"的入参，正是本仓库反复警告的**两条路径漂移**。
 * ★ 反向钉子见 tests/g97-senki-release.ts（引用解析不到 / 没 field / 两条同形 / 非先机，一律不放行）。
 */
export function senkiReleaseOf(ir: any, byId: Map<string, any>): string | null {
  const hits = ((ir?.unmodeledMechanics ?? []) as any[]).filter((m: any) =>
    m?.verb === APPEND_CAST_VERB && /\[tips_7\]/.test(String(m.clause ?? "")) && /施放/.test(String(m.clause ?? "")));
  if (hits.length !== 1) return null;
  const clause = String(hits[0].clause);
  const idM = /\[skill_(\d+_\d+)\]/.exec(clause);
  const nameM = /施放『([^』]+)』/.exec(clause);
  const target = idM ? byId.get(idM[1])
    : nameM ? [...byId.values()].find((x: any) => String(x?.name ?? "") === nameM[1])
    : undefined;
  if (!target?.field) return null;
  return clause;
}

export function charInfo(kit: EngineKit, id: number): {
  skills: number; aiConfidence: "HIGH" | "MEDIUM" | "LOW" | "NONE"; issues: string[];
  unconditional: UnconditionalRule[]; annotations: string[];
  skillDetails: SkillFidelity[]; aiUnresolvedConds: number;
} {
  const lc = skillsFor(kit, id);
  if (!lc) return { skills: 0, aiConfidence: "NONE", issues: ["不在知识库"], unconditional: [], annotations: [], skillDetails: [], aiUnresolvedConds: 0 };
  /** ★★★ 第 174 轮：先机放行要按**同角色的技能表**解析引用（判据见 senkiReleaseOf） */
  const irById = new Map<string, any>(lc.ir.map((y: any) => [String(y.id), y]));
  const skillDetails: SkillFidelity[] = lc.ir.map((x: any) => {
    /**
     * ★★★ 第 174 轮：先机子句的放行。**两侧对得上才真放行**：
     *   左边 = 判据说这条子句有执行点（senkiReleaseOf）；右边 = 它**确实**少掉正好一句未解析。
     *   任一侧对不上就按原样报 —— 这样"放行"永远不会多放一句、也不会把无关的缺口带走。
     */
    const candidate = senkiReleaseOf(x, irById);
    const unparsedAll = (x.sentences ?? []).filter((s: any) => s.kind === "MECHANIC_UNPARSED");
    const unparsedKept = candidate ? unparsedAll.filter((s: any) => !String(s.text ?? "").includes(candidate)) : unparsedAll;
    const released: string | null = candidate && unparsedKept.length === unparsedAll.length - 1 ? candidate : null;
    const unmodeledAll = ((x.unmodeledMechanics ?? []) as any[]);
    const unmodeledKept = released ? unmodeledAll.filter((m: any) => String(m.clause ?? "") !== released) : unmodeledAll;
    return {
      name: x.name, confidence: x.confidence, cost: x.cost,
      parsed: {
        damage: x.damage.length, heal: x.heal.length, shield: x.shield.length,
        timeline: x.timeline.length, status: x.statusRefs.length, orb: x.orb.gain > 0 ? 1 : 0,
      },
      unparsedSentences: unparsedKept.length,
      probAttributed: (x.effectProbs ?? []).length,
      probAmbiguous: (x.effectProbSkips ?? []).length,
      durationStated: (x.statusDurations ?? []).length,
      durationAmbiguous: (x.durationSkips ?? []).length,
      unmodeled: unmodeledKept.map((m: any) => ({ verb: m.verb, missing: m.missing, ...(m.severity ? { severity: m.severity } : {}) })),
      skips: [
        ...((x.formSkips ?? []) as any[]).map((s: any) => ({ reason: String(s.reason), evidence: String(s.evidence ?? ""), kind: "PLACEMENT" as const })),
        ...((x.stealSkips ?? []) as any[]).map((s: any) => ({ reason: String(s.reason), evidence: String(s.evidence ?? ""), kind: "STEAL" as const })),
      ],
    };
  });
  // 「没有写明触发条件」的规则：引擎按每回合都满足处理（仍受鬼火约束），但这多半不是官方行为
  const unconditional: UnconditionalRule[] = (lc.ai.unconditionalRules ?? []).map((label: string) => {
    const idx = lc.ai.skillRules.find(r => label.startsWith(r.skill.raw || "\u0000"))?.skill.index;
    const sk = idx == null ? null : lc.entity.skills[idx];
    return { label, cost: sk?.cost ?? -1, isBasic: !!sk && sk.cost === 0 && (sk.ir?.damage?.length ?? 0) > 0 };
  });
  return {
    skills: lc.ir.length, aiConfidence: (lc.ai.confidence ?? "LOW") as any, issues: lc.issues,
    /** ★ 第 114 轮：AI 规则里**条件解析不出**的条数（此前这些规则被当成「条件成立」放行） */
    aiUnresolvedConds: (lc.ai.unresolvedConds ?? []).length,
    unconditional, annotations: lc.ai.skillAnnotations ?? [], skillDetails,
  };
}

export interface AssembledMatch {
  battle: Battle;
  red: GuessingBuildResult[];
  blue: GuessingBuildResult[];
  issues: string[];
  /** 显示层同速：截图精度可能翻转出手顺序，必须显式暴露 */
  displayTies: Array<{ spd: number; members: string[] }>;
  /**
   * 御魂 4 件套效果的安装报告（装了几件 / 几件 UNSUPPORTED / 各触发了几次）。
   * 它此前被**直接丢弃**：报告里只能写"已加载"，写不出"其中 2 件永远不会触发"。
   * 本局没有御魂战斗效果时为 null —— 注意 null 不等于"御魂没问题"，它只说明没装进任何东西。
   */
  soulReport: import("../../../../battle-lab/soul/effect-system.ts").InstallReport | null;
}

/**
 * 竞猜口径装配。**绝不应用御魂静态属性**（面板即权威输入）。
 * 御魂图标 = 该 4 件套生效，因此把单个 soulId 复制 4 份 —— 这是猜测口径下唯一可自洽的读法，
 * 且已在 UI 中明确告知用户（图标无法区分 2 件套与 4 件套时，按 4 件套处理）。
 */
/**
 * ★ G0：把**运行时回调**接到战斗上。这类东西（技能选择器、AI 谓词）是函数，
 * 永远不该进 BattleState —— 它们必须能从知识库重新生成。
 * 装配与"从存档恢复"必须调用**同一个** attachRuntime，否则恢复出来的战斗会静默降级
 * （实测过一次：漏接 skillSelector，全场退化成普攻，事件数从 125 掉到 63）。
 */
/**
 * ★ G3：装配次数计数器。存在的唯一目的是**证明**"回放不是第二次执行引擎"（a1-4 §36）：
 * 从 Replay 文件播放时这个数字必须一动不动。
 */
export let assembleCount = 0;

export function attachRuntime(battle: any, kit?: SubscriberKit | null): void {
  battle.skillSelector = (b: any, a: any, e: any) => selectSkillByAI(b, a, e);
  /**
   * ★★★ 第 131/132/133 轮：**订阅（御魂 / 被动 / 替友方拦死）由 compile/subscribers.ts 统一安装**。
   *
   * 三次同类事故推着它走到这里：
   *   · 第 131 轮：替友方拦死只装在装配路径上 ⇒ 恢复后条款静默失效；
   *   · 第 132 轮：御魂与被动同样只在装配路径上 ⇒ "存档前在跑、续跑后消失"；
   *   · 第 133 轮：Node 侧参考装配（CLI / 基线）**既没有御魂也没有替友方拦死** —— 又是"两条路径装的不一样"。
   * ⇒ 现在只有**一处实现**（battle-lab/compile/subscribers.ts），装配与恢复、网页与 Node 都调它；
   *   顺序（御魂 → 被动 → 替友方拦死）也在那里定 —— 默认 priority 是 REGISTRATION_ORDER，
   *   同优先级事件的先后由注册顺序决定，所以顺序是行为的一部分。
   */
  const rep = installSubscribers(battle, kit);
  (battle as any).soulReport = rep.soul;
  (battle as any).passiveReport = rep.passive;
  (battle as any).allyGuardReport = rep.allyGuard;
}

export interface AssembleOverrides {
  /** 红区 B：开局结算顺序（HYPOTHESIS 之间可切换，用于策略敏感性） */
  startup?: string;
  /** 红区 C：击杀成立时点 */
  death?: string;
  /** 红区 A：同时触发的优先级 */
  priority?: string;
  /** 同速时的出手决定方式 */
  tieBreak?: string;
}

/**
 * ★ 第 34 轮修：`traceLevel` / `recorder` 以前只有 runOne 在**装配之后**补设，
 * 直接调用 assembleMatch 的调用方（多个测试）传了也没人接 —— 而 Node 只剥类型不做检查，
 * 所以"我以为在跑 NONE 档"实际跑的是 SUMMARY：观测没关掉，性能与语义都不对。
 * 现在它和其它装配选项一样，由构造参数一路传到底。
 */
export function assembleMatch(kit: EngineKit, match: GuessingMatch, opts: { seed: number; maxTurns?: number; passives?: "NOT_EXECUTED" | "PARSED_CANDIDATES_ONLY"; traceLevel?: TraceLevel; recorder?: { onFrame(battle: any, frame: number): void } } & AssembleOverrides = { seed: 1 }): AssembledMatch {
  assembleCount++;
  const issues: string[] = [];
  const results: Record<string, GuessingBuildResult[]> = { red: [], blue: [] };
  const teams: Record<string, Team> = {};

  for (const side of ["red", "blue"] as const) {
    const teamId = side === "red" ? 0 : 1;
    const team = new Team(teamId, side.toUpperCase(), ResourcePolicies.COMMUNITY_4_8_5_345, 1);
    let entId = (teamId + 1) * 100;
    for (const u of match[side].units) {
      const id = ++entId;
      const slot = match[side].units.indexOf(u) + 1;
      if (!u.shikigamiId) { issues.push(`${side === "red" ? "红" : "蓝"}方第 ${slot} 位未选择式神`); continue; }
      const lc = skillsFor(kit, u.shikigamiId);
      const built = makeGuessingEntity(id, teamId, {
        shikigamiId: u.shikigamiId,
        observed: toEngineDisplay(u.stats),
        soulIds: u.soulId == null ? [] : [u.soulId, u.soulId, u.soulId, u.soulId],
      }, { soulDefs: kit.soulDefs, charMeta: kit.charMeta });

      // ★ G0：把知识库身份写进实体，状态序列化后才能重建运行时
      built.entity.shikigamiId = u.shikigamiId;
      // 图标 = 该 4 件套生效：实体侧也记 4 份，和 makeGuessingEntity 的口径保持一致
      // （只记 1 份会让任何按"满 4 件"判断的路径静默失效）
      built.entity.soulIds = u.soulId == null ? [] : [u.soulId, u.soulId, u.soulId, u.soulId];
      if (lc) {
        built.entity.skills = lc.entity.skills.map((sk: any) => ({ ...sk }));   // 每局独占技能对象，避免跨局串状态
        /**
         * ★★★ 第 130 轮：**能力位也要跟着技能一起搬过来**。
         *   竞猜口径的单位是 makeGuessingEntity 造的（不是 buildCharacter 的实体），
         *   只拷 skills 的话，「无法被治疗」这类**只写在技能原文里**的能力位会静默丢掉
         *   （探针 tools/_probe-noheal130.ts 实测：装配后又变回 receivesHeal=true）。
         *   共用助手在 battle-lab/compile/character.ts —— 一处实现，两条装配路径都调它。
         */
        applyIrCapabilities(built.entity, lc.ir);
        built.entity.notes.push(`kb-skills=${lc.ir.length} ai=${lc.ai.confidence}`);
        if (!lc.ir.length) issues.push(`${u.name} 无技能数据`);
        if (lc.ai.confidence === "LOW") issues.push(`${u.name} AI 置信度 LOW`);
      } else {
        issues.push(`${u.name}(id=${u.shikigamiId}) 不在知识库中，将只有普攻（${side} 第 ${slot} 位）`);
      }
      const v = verifyGuessingPanel(built);
      if (!v.ok) issues.push(`${u.name} 面板回显不一致: ${v.detail.join("; ")}`);

      team.add(built.entity);
      results[side].push(built);
    }
    teams[side] = team;
  }

  const battle = new Battle([teams.red, teams.blue], {
    seed: opts.seed,
    rules: { ...DEFAULT_RULES, maxTurns: opts.maxTurns ?? 300 },
    policies: {
      resource: ResourcePolicies.COMMUNITY_4_8_5_345,
      startup: StartupPolicies[opts.startup ?? "NAIVE_SOUL_THEN_PASSIVE_THEN_SENKI"],
      death: DeathPolicies[opts.death ?? "DEATH_AFTER_PREVENTION"],
      priority: opts.priority ?? "REGISTRATION_ORDER",
      tieBreak: opts.tieBreak ?? "SPEED_THEN_RANDOM",
      guessing: GuessingRulesets.COMMUNITY_2020_UNVERIFIED,
      // ★ 第 70 轮：被动技能执行与否（红区 E）。默认 NOT_EXECUTED ⇒ 与历史结果逐位一致。
      passives: PassivePolicies[opts.passives ?? "NOT_EXECUTED"],
      // ★★ 第 93/94 轮：两条新红区也要进产物快照 ——
      //   否则「用哪种口径算出来的」在网页侧看不出来（引擎默认值在 kernel/policies.ts）。
      lifesteal: LifestealPolicies.APPLIED_AS_HEAL,
      immune: ImmunePolicies.DEBUFF_COVERS_CONTROL,
    },
    traceLevel: opts.traceLevel,
  });
  if (opts.recorder) (battle as any).recorder = opts.recorder;
  /**
   * ★★★ 第 132 轮：**御魂 / 被动 / 替友方拦死三种订阅都只在这里装**（attachRuntime 内部）。
   *
   * 这里是装配路径；恢复路径由 restoreBattle 调**同一个** attachRuntime（并把 kit 传进去）。
   * 订阅是函数、不进 BattleState，两边必须装同一套 —— 否则续跑会静默降级。
   * ★ 原先这三段（御魂 + 被动）写在这里，于是**恢复路径根本没有装**；搬进去之后只装一份，
   *   也不会出现"同 id 装两遍"（bus.register 不去重）。
   * ★ 安装报告仍然挂在 battle 上（soulReport / passiveReport / allyGuardReport），
   *   供 Fidelity / Theater / 测试读取 —— 搬家的只是**调用点**，不是账。
   */
  attachRuntime(battle, kit);

  const bySpd = new Map<number, string[]>();
  for (const side of ["red", "blue"] as const) for (const u of match[side].units) {
    if (!u.shikigamiId) continue;
    const spd = u.stats.speed;
    if (!bySpd.has(spd)) bySpd.set(spd, []);
    bySpd.get(spd)!.push(`${side === "red" ? "红" : "蓝"}·${u.name}`);
  }
  const displayTies = [...bySpd.entries()].filter(([, v]) => v.length > 1).map(([spd, v]) => ({ spd, members: v }));

  /** ★ 第 132 轮：安装报告一律从 battle 上读（装它们的地方已经搬到 attachRuntime 里） */
  return { battle, red: results.red, blue: results.blue, issues, displayTies, soulReport: (battle as any).soulReport ?? null };
}

/** 单局：装配 + 跑完，并把 trace 打上回合戳（回放器需要） */
export async function runOne(kit: EngineKit, match: GuessingMatch, seed: number, opts: { maxTurns?: number; annotate?: boolean; traceLevel?: "NONE" | "SUMMARY" | "FULL"; recorder?: any } & AssembleOverrides = {}) {
  // ★ G4：观测粒度由调用方决定（10k 局用 NONE/SUMMARY，Theater 用 FULL + 记录器）
  const a = assembleMatch(kit, match, { seed, maxTurns: opts.maxTurns, startup: opts.startup, death: opts.death, priority: opts.priority, tieBreak: opts.tieBreak, traceLevel: opts.traceLevel, recorder: opts.recorder });
  if (opts.annotate !== false) {
    const tr: any[] = a.battle.trace;
    const orig = tr.push.bind(tr);
    (tr as any).push = (...items: any[]) => { for (const it of items) { if (it && typeof it === "object" && it.turn === undefined) it.turn = a.battle.turn; } return orig(...items); };
  }
  const r = await a.battle.run();
  return { ...a, result: r };
}

export function buffCompileFailures() { return buffFailures; }

/**
 * 每一局的 seed 由 (基准 seed, 局序号) **雪崩散列**得到，而不是简单的 seedBase + i。
 *
 * 起因是一个真实的担忧：mulberry32 对相邻种子的雪崩较弱，若相邻几局共享同一段
 * 开局随机序列，Wilson 区间就会**偏窄**（假装比实际更确定）。
 *
 * 实测结论（典型对局 #001，40 区块 × 250 局 = 10,000 局，区块胜率卡方检验）：
 *   顺序 seed：区块 SD 3.34% vs 二项理论 2.96%（比值 1.13），chi² = 49.7，df = 39
 *   散列 seed：区块 SD 3.26% vs 二项理论 2.96%（比值 1.10），chi² = 47.3，df = 39
 * 两者都落在独立同分布的 95% 区间内（≈ 39 ± 17.4）——
 * **即：没有检出顺序 seed 的相关性**。散列只是留有余量的防御性选择，不是修复某个已证实的缺陷。
 * 早期用 10 个区块估 SD 时看到的"波动异常"是 SD 估计本身的噪声（10 个区块时 SD 的
 * 相对不确定度约 24%），不足以支撑任何结论。
 */
export function mixSeed(seedBase: number, i: number): number {
  let h = (seedBase ^ 0x9E3779B9) >>> 0;
  h = Math.imul(h ^ ((i + 0x85EBCA6B) >>> 0), 0xCC9E2D51) >>> 0;
  h = (h ^ (h >>> 15)) >>> 0;
  h = Math.imul(h, 0x2545F491) >>> 0;
  h = (h ^ (h >>> 13)) >>> 0;
  h = (h ^ (h >>> 16)) >>> 0;
  return (h % 2147483647) || 1;
}
```

### `apps/web/src/adapters/engine-adapter.ts`

```ts
// adapters/engine-adapter.ts — ★ UI-2：真引擎适配器
// 对外接口与 mock-adapter **完全一致**，Worker 协议不变。
// 引擎只在 Worker 线程里跑；主线程拿到的永远是 DTO。
import type { SimulateAdapter } from "./simulate.ts";
import { ENGINE_VERSION, GAME_DATA_VERSION } from "./simulate.ts";
import type { GuessingMatch } from "../contracts/guessing-match.ts";
import type {
  SimulationOptions, SimulationResult, SimulationWarning, StrategySnapshot, CoverageSnapshot,
  PerUnitStats, RepresentativeSeed, WarningKind, ModelUncertainty,
} from "../contracts/simulation-result.ts";
import type { ValidationReport, ReplayEvent, ReplayUnit } from "../contracts/worker-api.ts";
import { createKit, assembleMatch, runOne, charInfo, mixSeed } from "../engine/assemble.ts";
import type { EngineBundle, EngineKit } from "../engine/assemble.ts";
import { wilson } from "../../../../battle-lab/runner/monte-carlo.ts";
import { SnapshotRecorder } from "../../../../battle-lab/kernel/snapshot.ts";
import { buildReplay } from "../../../../battle-lab/kernel/replay.ts";
import { captureState } from "../../../../battle-lab/kernel/state.ts";
import { assessFidelity } from "../../../../battle-lab/kernel/fidelity.ts";
import type { FidelityInput, FidelityUnitInput } from "../../../../battle-lab/kernel/fidelity.ts";

import { interpretEffect } from "../../../../battle-lab/soul/effect-system.ts";

/** 单局异常上限：超过就中止整轮（避免每局都抛同一个错，白白等十分钟） */
const MAX_ERRORS = 20;
/** 逐单位贡献统计的抽样上限：全量跑胜率，贡献度用有代表性的子样本（结果里会标注） */
const PER_UNIT_SAMPLE = 2000;

/**
 * ★★★ 第 137 轮：**观测档位 → 运行模式**的映射（§35 / §34）。
 *
 * 由来：审核结论里有一条「engine-adapter 的 NONE/FULL 映射无断言」——
 * 这两处原来各自写着一个字符串字面量（批量模拟写 "NONE"、观看一场写 "FULL"），
 * 谁都没法断言"哪个模式该用哪一档"，而且改错一个字也不会有任何测试变红。
 * 现在把它抽成一个**可调用的纯函数**：断言能直接问它（tests/ui13-trace-vocab.ts §[6]），
 * 两个调用点也都走它，代码里不再有第二处字面量。
 *
 *   · batch（模拟胜率，1k–10k 局）：观测关到 NONE —— 逐单位贡献由引擎 stats 提供，
 *     关掉 trace 不丢任何统计（g2/g20 已断言三档的**全部统计**逐字节一致）；
 *   · watch（观看一场）：FULL —— Theater 要拖到任意一帧，必须有事件流 + 逐帧快照。
 */
export function traceLevelFor(mode: "batch" | "watch"): "NONE" | "FULL" {
  return mode === "watch" ? "FULL" : "NONE";
}

/** 引擎未暴露的指标：**报 0 并显式声明**，绝不编造 */
const UNSUPPORTED_METRICS = ["avgOrbSpent*（引擎未记录鬼火消耗计数器）"];

function supportOf(kit: EngineKit, id: number | null): "FULL" | "PARTIAL" | "UNSUPPORTED" {
  if (id == null) return "UNSUPPORTED";
  const ir = kit.soulBook.get(Number(id));
  if (!ir) return "UNSUPPORTED";
  const interp = interpretEffect(ir);
  const ok = interp.kind !== "UNSUPPORTED" && !!ir.trigger;
  return ok ? "FULL" : (ir.trigger ? "PARTIAL" : "UNSUPPORTED");
}

function strategyFrom(battle: any): StrategySnapshot {
  const p = battle?.policies ?? {};
  const mk = (x: any) => ({
    id: typeof x === "string" ? x : (x?.id ?? "?"),
    status: (x && typeof x === "object" && x.status) ? x.status : "HYPOTHESIS",
  });
  const snap: StrategySnapshot = {
    startup: mk(p.startup), triggerPriority: mk(p.priority), death: mk(p.death),
    endgame: mk(p.guessing), resource: mk(p.resource), unknownCount: 0,
  };
  snap.unknownCount = [snap.startup, snap.triggerPriority, snap.death, snap.endgame, snap.resource]
    .filter(x => x.status === "UNKNOWN").length;
  return snap;
}

const UNKNOWN_STRAT: StrategySnapshot = {
  startup: { id: "?", status: "HYPOTHESIS" }, triggerPriority: { id: "?", status: "HYPOTHESIS" },
  death: { id: "?", status: "HYPOTHESIS" }, endgame: { id: "?", status: "HYPOTHESIS" },
  resource: { id: "?", status: "HYPOTHESIS" }, unknownCount: 0,
};

export function createEngineAdapter(bundle: EngineBundle): SimulateAdapter {
  ensureCapSupport(bundle as any);
  let kit: EngineKit | null = null;
  const getKit = (): EngineKit => (kit ??= createKit(bundle));

  /** 装配一次探针对局：校验 / 覆盖度 / 策略快照（不跑战斗） */
  function probe(match: GuessingMatch) {
    return assembleMatch(getKit(), match, { seed: 1 });
  }

  function coverageOf(match: GuessingMatch, issues: string[]): CoverageSnapshot {
    const k = getKit();
    const units = [...match.red.units, ...match.blue.units].filter(u => u.shikigamiId > 0);
    const joined = units.filter(u => k.shikiById.has(u.shikigamiId));
    let skillsAvailable = 0, aiHigh = 0;
    for (const u of joined) {
      // ★ 用**编译后**的 AI 置信度，而不是数据文件里的字段（后者在 ai.json 里根本不存在）
      const info = charInfo(k, u.shikigamiId);
      if (info.skills > 0) skillsAvailable++;
      if (info.aiConfidence === "HIGH" || info.aiConfidence === "MEDIUM") aiHigh++;
    }
    const souls = units.map(u => u.soulId).filter((x): x is number => x != null);
    /**
     * ★★ 第 37 轮：这里原先是 `souls.length` —— 也就是"填了御魂 id"就算"已解析"。
     *   于是 id 根本不在知识库里的御魂也会被算成已解析，界面（DeveloperHud「御魂已解析」、
     *   HealthCheck「御魂定义」）就会显示满分。这正是 §29 那类**把"有输入"说成"解析成功"**的过度声称。
     *   现在它等于标签真正承诺的东西：该 id 在知识库里查得到定义。
     *   注意区分第二个轴：**战斗效果支持度**由 soulEffectsFull/Partial 单独报（FULL/PARTIAL/UNSUPPORTED），
     *   两者不是一回事 —— "定义解析成功"绝不等于"战斗效果已实现"。
     */
    const soulsUnknown = souls.filter(id => !k.soulDefs.has(id)).length;
    const supports = souls.map(id => supportOf(k, id));
    const unsupported = supports.filter(s => s === "UNSUPPORTED").length;
    const partial = supports.filter(s => s === "PARTIAL").length;
    const nUncond = units.reduce((acc, u) => acc + charInfo(k, u.shikigamiId).unconditional.length, 0);
    const unconditionalNote: string[] = nUncond ? [`${nUncond} 条 AI 施放规则没有触发条件（按每回合都放处理）`] : [];
    return {
      unitsJoined: joined.length, unitsTotal: 10,
      skillsAvailable, skillsTotal: joined.length,
      aiHigh, aiTotal: joined.length,
      soulsResolved: souls.length - soulsUnknown, soulsTotal: joined.length,
      soulEffectsFull: supports.filter(s => s === "FULL").length,
      soulEffectsPartial: partial,
      unknownBuffRefs: [],
      unsupportedCapabilities: [
        ...unconditionalNote,
        ...(unsupported ? [`${unsupported} 个御魂的战斗效果未实现`] : []),
        ...(soulsUnknown ? [`${soulsUnknown} 个御魂 id 不在知识库里（定义无法解析）`] : []),
        ...(issues.some(i => i.includes("LOW")) ? ["部分式神技能/AI 编译置信度为 LOW"] : []),
        ...UNSUPPORTED_METRICS,
      ],
    };
  }

  return {
    async validate(match): Promise<ValidationReport> {
      const blockers: string[] = [];
      const warnings: SimulationWarning[] = [];
      for (const side of ["red", "blue"] as const) {
        match[side].units.forEach((u, i) => {
          const tag = `${side === "red" ? "红" : "蓝"}方第 ${i + 1} 位`;
          if (!u.shikigamiId) { blockers.push(`${tag}未选择式神`); return; }
          if (!u.stats.attack || !u.stats.hp) blockers.push(`${tag}「${u.name}」面板未填（攻击/生命为 0）`);
          if (u.shikigamiId >= 10 && u.shikigamiId <= 16) blockers.push(`${tag}「${u.name}」是阴阳师/英杰，对弈竞猜不含（规则 GUESSING_NO_ONMYOJI）`);
        });
      }
      const a = blockers.length ? null : probe(match);
      const issues = a ? a.issues : [];
      // ★ 把**本局这 10 只**的技能编译问题一并纳入报告（按 unitId 作用域取，避免跨对局串味）
      const k = getKit();
      for (const side of ["red", "blue"] as const) {
        for (const u of match[side].units) {
          if (!u.shikigamiId) continue;
          for (const i of charInfo(k, u.shikigamiId).issues) issues.push(`${u.name}：${i}`);
        }
      }
      for (const i of issues.filter(x => x.includes("面板回显不一致"))) blockers.push(i);
      const cov = coverageOf(match, issues);
      const strat = a ? strategyFrom(a.battle) : UNKNOWN_STRAT;
      /**
       * ★★ 第 41 轮：御魂的**可执行效果**必须逐局报出来。
       *
       * coverage 里的 soulEffectsFull / Partial 说的是「知识库对这件御魂的支持度」，
       * 与「本局装上它会不会真的发生什么」是两件事。tests/g31-soul-liveness.ts 逐件实测：
       * 70 件里只有 3 件改变对局；32 件标 PARTIAL 的**全部**零可观测效果。
       *
       * 原因在 soul/effect-system.ts 的设计里：未匹配到可执行句式的御魂仍然注册 handler，
       * 事件会被投递、会被计数，但**不改变任何状态**。于是「已加载 N 件御魂」
       * 很容易被读成「N 件御魂在起作用」—— 这个数字以前只存在于装配返回值里，
       * 没有任何界面读它。现在按局报出来。
       */
      const sr: any = a?.soulReport;
      if (sr && sr.installed.length) {
        const real = (sr.installed as any[]).filter(x => x.kind !== "UNSUPPORTED").length;
        const empty = sr.installed.length - real;
        const detail = "未匹配到可执行句式的御魂只注册空 handler：事件会投递给它们，但不改变任何状态。"
          + "实测见 battle-lab/tests/g31-soul-liveness.ts（70 件里只有 3 件改变对局）。";
        if (real === 0) {
          warnings.push({
            kind: "SOUL_EFFECT_UNSUPPORTED", severity: "WARN",
            message: `本局 ${sr.installed.length} 种御魂效果**没有一种可执行**（其中 ${empty} 种只登记不生效）`,
            detail,
          });
        } else if (empty > 0) {
          warnings.push({
            kind: "SOUL_EFFECT_UNSUPPORTED", severity: "INFO",
            message: `本局 ${sr.installed.length} 种御魂效果：${real} 种可执行、${empty} 种只登记不生效`,
            detail,
          });
        }
      }

      if (strat.unknownCount > 0) warnings.push({
        kind: "UNKNOWN_STRATEGY", severity: "WARN",
        message: `存在 ${strat.unknownCount} 项 UNKNOWN 机制策略`,
        detail: "竞猜残局规则未实测；开局结算顺序 / 同时触发优先级 / 死亡结算均为社区假设。数值可用于比较，不构成机制结论。",
      });
      const uniq = [...new Set(issues)];
      const passive = uniq.filter(i => i.includes("被动技能，引擎未建模"));
      const partial = uniq.filter(i => i.includes("已解析出可执行数值"));
      const gap = uniq.filter(i => i.includes("未解析出任何可执行数值"));
      if (passive.length) warnings.push({
        kind: "LOW_AI_CONFIDENCE", severity: "INFO",
        message: `${passive.length} 个被动技能未建模（引擎无触发条件模型）`,
        detail: passive.join("；"),
      });
      if (partial.length) warnings.push({
        kind: "LOW_AI_CONFIDENCE", severity: "INFO",
        message: `${partial.length} 个技能的主动效果已解析，但描述中的附加效果未建模`,
        detail: partial.join("；"),
      });
      for (const i of gap) warnings.push({ kind: "LOW_AI_CONFIDENCE", severity: "WARN", message: i, detail: "该技能完全没有可执行数值，实战表现会明显偏弱" });
      for (const i of uniq) {
        if (i.includes("AI 置信度 LOW")) warnings.push({ kind: "LOW_AI_CONFIDENCE", severity: "WARN", message: i });
        else if (i.includes("无技能数据") || i.includes("不在知识库")) warnings.push({ kind: "DATA_MISSING", severity: "WARN", message: i });
      }
      // ★ AI 施放规则的**触发条件缺口**：数据里根本没写条件 → 引擎只能每回合都放
      const uncond: Array<{ who: string; label: string; cost: number; spamRisk: boolean }> = [];
      for (const side of ["red", "blue"] as const) {
        for (const u of match[side].units) {
          if (!u.shikigamiId) continue;
          for (const r of charInfo(getKit(), u.shikigamiId).unconditional) {
            // 0 鬼火的技能不受鬼火约束 → 会真的被无限重复施放（思金神 601「智火长明」即此例）
            uncond.push({ who: u.name, label: r.label, cost: r.cost, spamRisk: r.cost === 0 && !r.isBasic });
          }
        }
      }
      if (uncond.length) {
        const risky = uncond.filter(x => x.spamRisk);
        warnings.push({
          kind: "LOW_AI_CONFIDENCE",
          severity: risky.length ? "WARN" : "INFO",
          message: `${uncond.length} 条 AI 施放规则在数据里没有触发条件，引擎按「每回合都放」处理${risky.length ? `，其中 ${risky.length} 条为 0 鬼火技能（不受鬼火约束，会被反复施放）` : "（仍受鬼火约束）"}`,
          detail: uncond.map(x => `${x.who}：${x.label}（${x.cost < 0 ? "被动" : x.cost + " 鬼火"}）`).join("；"),
        });
      }
      if (cov.soulEffectsPartial > 0) warnings.push({
        kind: "SOUL_EFFECT_UNSUPPORTED", severity: "WARN",
        message: `${cov.soulEffectsPartial} 个御魂的战斗效果只实现了部分触发条件`,
        detail: "御魂静态面板不参与竞猜计算（面板已是截图终值），但战斗特效会被低估。",
      });
      if (a?.displayTies.length) warnings.push({
        kind: "MODEL_SENSITIVE", severity: "INFO",
        message: `显示层同速：${a.displayTies.map(t => `${t.spd} 速 ${t.members.join("/")}`).join("；")}`,
        detail: "截图只给整数速度；同速单位的出手先后由可行区间决定，可能翻转结果。",
      });

      // ★ Per-Match Fidelity（§1-§10、§33）：阵容编辑器在**跑之前**就能看到五层状态
      const fidelity = blockers.length && !a ? null : buildFidelity(getKit(), match, strat);
      return { ok: blockers.length === 0, blockers, warnings, coverage: cov, strategySnapshot: strat, fidelity };
    },

    async simulate(match, options, onProgress, shouldCancel): Promise<SimulationResult> {
      const k = getKit();
      const total = Math.max(1, options.simulations);
      const seedBase = options.seed === "AUTO" ? (Date.now() % 1_000_000_000) : Number(options.seed);
      const maxTurns = options.maxTurns ?? 300;
      const t0 = Date.now();
      const every = Math.max(10, Math.min(options.progressEvery ?? 500, Math.ceil(total / 20)));

      // 警告与模型不确定性都在主循环之后追加，但 warn() 需要提前可用
      const warnings: SimulationWarning[] = [];
      const warn = (kind: WarningKind, severity: SimulationWarning["severity"], message: string, detail?: string) =>
        warnings.push({ kind, severity, message, ...(detail ? { detail } : {}) });

      let redWins = 0, blueWins = 0, draws = 0, timeouts = 0, errors = 0;
      let firstKillRedBattles = 0, firstKillKnown = 0;
      let survRed = 0, survBlue = 0, hpRed = 0, hpBlue = 0;
      const turnsAll: number[] = [];
      const seeds: RepresentativeSeed[] = [];
      const issues = new Set<string>();
      const acc = new Map<string, PerUnitStats>();
      let strat: StrategySnapshot | null = null;
      let lastError = "";
      let sampled = 0;

      // 引擎实体 id -> UI unitId（红 101..105 / 蓝 201..205）
      const idMap = new Map<number, { unitId: string; side: "red" | "blue"; name: string }>();
      for (const side of ["red", "blue"] as const) {
        match[side].units.forEach((u, i) => {
          if (u.shikigamiId) idMap.set((side === "red" ? 100 : 200) + i + 1, { unitId: u.unitId, side, name: u.name });
        });
      }
      for (const [, m] of idMap) acc.set(m.unitId, {
        unitId: m.unitId, name: m.name, side: m.side, avgDamageDealt: 0, avgDamageTaken: 0, avgHealing: 0,
        kills: 0, deaths: 0, deathRate: 0, avgActions: 0, skillUsage: {}, soulTriggerCount: 0,
        controlApplied: 0, controlResisted: 0,
      });

      for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) {
        const seed = mixSeed(seedBase, i);   // ★ 雪崩散列，避免相邻 seed 相关导致 CI 偏窄
        try {
          // ★ G5/§35：批量模拟把观测关到 NONE —— 10k 局不需要事件流，
        //   逐单位贡献改由引擎自己的 stats 提供（§45），因此关掉 trace 不丢任何统计。
        const a = await runOne(k, match, seed, { maxTurns, annotate: false, traceLevel: traceLevelFor("batch") });
          strat ??= strategyFrom(a.battle);
          for (const x of a.issues) issues.add(x);
          const r = a.result;
          turnsAll.push(r.turns);
          let kind: RepresentativeSeed["winner"];
          if (r.reason === "TURN_LIMIT") { timeouts++; kind = "timeout"; }
          else if (r.winner === 0) { redWins++; kind = "red"; }
          else if (r.winner === 1) { blueWins++; kind = "blue"; }
          else { draws++; kind = "draw"; }
          if (!seeds.some(s => s.winner === kind)) seeds.push({ seed, winner: kind, turns: r.turns });

          survRed += a.battle.teams[0].aliveMembers().length;
          survBlue += a.battle.teams[1].aliveMembers().length;
          hpRed += a.battle.teams[0].aliveMembers().reduce((s: number, m: any) => s + m.hp, 0);
          hpBlue += a.battle.teams[1].aliveMembers().reduce((s: number, m: any) => s + m.hp, 0);
          const fd = (a.battle as any).firstDeathId;
          if (fd != null) { firstKillKnown++; if (fd >= 200) firstKillRedBattles++; }

          if (sampled < PER_UNIT_SAMPLE) { accumulateStats(a, acc, idMap); sampled++; }
        } catch (err: any) {
          errors++; lastError = String(err?.message ?? err);
          if (errors >= MAX_ERRORS) break;
        }
        if ((i + 1) % every === 0 || i === total - 1) {
          onProgress({ completed: i + 1, total, redWins, blueWins, draws, timeouts, errors, elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0 });
          await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, 0));   // 让出事件循环，才能收到 CANCEL
          if (shouldCancel()) throw new Error("CANCELLED");
        }
      }

      // ── 模型不确定性：把 UNKNOWN/HYPOTHESIS 策略换一种假设，看结论会不会翻转 ──
      // 与抽样误差**严格分开**：抽样误差靠加大 N 缩小，模型不确定性加大 N 也不会变小。
      let uncertainty: ModelUncertainty | null = null;
      if (total >= 800 && redWins + blueWins > 0) {
        const nSens = Math.max(150, Math.min(500, Math.round(total / 10)));
        const variants: Array<{ label: string; group: ModelUncertainty["perStrategy"][number]["group"]; opts: any; note: string }> = [
          { label: `基准（${(strat ?? UNKNOWN_STRAT).startup.id.slice(0, 22)}）`, group: "STARTUP", opts: {}, note: "当前使用的假设" },
          { label: "开局：先机先于御魂/被动", group: "STARTUP", opts: { startup: "SENKI_FIRST" }, note: "官方只说明先机‘战斗开始时动作’，与御魂先后未知" },
          { label: "死亡：HP≤0 即视为被击败", group: "DEATH", opts: { death: "HP_ZERO_IS_KILLED" }, note: "免死/转化是否拦在死亡之前未实测" },
          { label: "回合上限 100（残局加速强）", group: "TURN_LIMIT", opts: { maxTurns: 100 }, note: "竞猜残局增伤曲线未实测" },
          { label: "回合上限 600（残局加速弱）", group: "TURN_LIMIT", opts: { maxTurns: 600 }, note: "同上，取另一端" },
        ];
        const per: ModelUncertainty["perStrategy"] = [];
        for (const v of variants) {
          let r0 = 0, b0 = 0;
          for (let i = 0; i < nSens; i++) {
            try {
              const a = await runOne(k, match, mixSeed(seedBase + 7919, i), { maxTurns: v.opts.maxTurns ?? maxTurns, ...v.opts, annotate: false });
              if (a.result.reason === "TURN_LIMIT") continue;
              if (a.result.winner === 0) r0++; else if (a.result.winner === 1) b0++;
            } catch { /* 敏感性扫描的个别异常不计入主结果 */ }
          }
          const d = r0 + b0;
          per.push({ label: v.label, group: v.group, redWinRate: d ? r0 / d : null, note: `${v.note}（N=${nSens}）` });
          onProgress({ completed: total, total, redWins, blueWins, draws, timeouts, errors, elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0 });
          if (shouldCancel()) throw new Error("CANCELLED");
        }
        const rates = per.map(p => p.redWinRate).filter((x): x is number => x !== null);
        const min = rates.length ? Math.min(...rates) : null;
        const max = rates.length ? Math.max(...rates) : null;
        const spread = min !== null && max !== null ? max - min : null;
        uncertainty = {
          perStrategy: per, min, max, spread,
          verdict: spread === null ? "INCONCLUSIVE" : spread > 0.1 ? "SENSITIVE" : "STABLE",
        };
        if (uncertainty.verdict === "SENSITIVE") warn("MODEL_SENSITIVE", "BLOCK",
          `结论对未实测机制敏感：换一种假设，红方胜率在 ${(min! * 100).toFixed(1)}%~${(max! * 100).toFixed(1)}% 之间漂移`,
          "漂移 " + (spread! * 100).toFixed(1) + " 个百分点。这不是抽样误差，加大模拟次数不会消除它。");
        else if (uncertainty.verdict === "STABLE") warn("MODEL_SENSITIVE", "INFO",
          `结论对已知的未实测机制不敏感（漂移 ${(spread! * 100).toFixed(1)} 个百分点）`,
          "仍然不代表机制已被验证，只说明这些候选假设给出相近的结论。");
      }

      const ran = redWins + blueWins + draws + timeouts;
      const decisive = redWins + blueWins;
      const sorted = turnsAll.slice().sort((a, b) => a - b);
      const pick = (p: number) => sorted.length ? sorted[Math.min(sorted.length - 1, Math.floor(sorted.length * p))] : 0;
      const n = Math.max(1, ran);

      if (strat && strat.unknownCount > 0) warn("UNKNOWN_STRATEGY", "WARN", `存在 ${strat.unknownCount} 项 UNKNOWN 机制策略`, "结果可用于阵容比较，不构成机制结论");
      if (timeouts / n > 0.2) warn("HIGH_STALL_RATE", "WARN", `${((timeouts / n) * 100).toFixed(1)}% 的对局在 ${maxTurns} 回合内未终结`, "未终结局不计入任何一方胜率，因此红蓝胜率之和不含它们");
      if (decisive < 100) warn("SMALL_SAMPLE", "WARN", `有效样本仅 ${decisive} 局`, "Wilson 区间会很宽，不足以判断强弱");
      if (sampled < ran) warn("SMALL_SAMPLE", "INFO", `逐单位贡献统计只用前 ${sampled} 局`, "胜率用全量样本；贡献度是有代表性的子样本");
      if (errors) warn("DATA_MISSING", errors >= MAX_ERRORS ? "BLOCK" : "WARN", `${errors} 局异常`, lastError + (errors >= MAX_ERRORS ? "（已达上限，提前中止）" : ""));
      if (ran < total) warn("DATA_MISSING", "WARN", `只完成了 ${ran}/${total} 局`, "结果基于不完整样本");

      const perUnit = [...acc.values()].map(p => ({
        ...p,
        avgDamageDealt: Math.round(p.avgDamageDealt / Math.max(1, sampled)),
        avgDamageTaken: Math.round(p.avgDamageTaken / Math.max(1, sampled)),
        avgActions: +(p.avgActions / Math.max(1, sampled)).toFixed(1),
        deathRate: +(p.deaths / Math.max(1, sampled)).toFixed(3),
      }));

      return {
        schemaVersion: 1,
        resultId: "r" + seedBase.toString(36) + "-" + total,
        matchId: match.id,
        simulations: ran, redWins, blueWins, draws, timeouts, errors, decisive,
        redWinRate: decisive ? redWins / decisive : null,
        blueWinRate: decisive ? blueWins / decisive : null,
        confidenceInterval: decisive ? { method: "WILSON", level: 0.95, ...ci(wilson(redWins, decisive)) } : null,
        blueConfidenceInterval: decisive ? { method: "WILSON", level: 0.95, ...ci(wilson(blueWins, decisive)) } : null,
        battleStats: {
          avgTurns: sorted.length ? +(sorted.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / sorted.length).toFixed(1) : 0,
          medianTurns: pick(0.5), p95Turns: pick(0.95),
          firstKillRedRate: firstKillKnown ? firstKillRedBattles / firstKillKnown : null,
          avgSurvivorsRed: +(survRed / n).toFixed(2), avgSurvivorsBlue: +(survBlue / n).toFixed(2),
          avgRemainHpRed: Math.round(hpRed / n), avgRemainHpBlue: Math.round(hpBlue / n),
          avgOrbSpentRed: 0, avgOrbSpentBlue: 0,   // 引擎无此计数器 —— 已在 unsupportedCapabilities 声明
          perUnit,
        },
        strategySnapshot: strat ?? UNKNOWN_STRAT,
        modelUncertainty: uncertainty,
        coverage: coverageOf(match, [...issues]),
        warnings,
        engineVersion: ENGINE_VERSION,
        gameDataVersion: GAME_DATA_VERSION,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        seeds: seeds.slice(0, 4),
      };
    },

    async replay(match, seed) {
      const k = getKit();
      // ★ G4：回放走 FULL 档（事件流 + 逐帧 Checkpoint/Delta），
      //   这样 Theater 拖到任意一帧都能重建战场，而不是只能顺着事件列表读。
      const recorder = new SnapshotRecorder(64);
      const a = await runOne(k, match, seed, { maxTurns: 300, annotate: true, traceLevel: traceLevelFor("watch"), recorder });
      const idMap = new Map<number, { unitId: string; side: "red" | "blue"; name: string }>();
      for (const side of ["red", "blue"] as const) {
        match[side].units.forEach((u, i) => {
          if (u.shikigamiId) idMap.set((side === "red" ? 100 : 200) + i + 1, { unitId: u.unitId, side, name: u.name });
        });
      }
      const units: ReplayUnit[] = [];
      for (const side of ["red", "blue"] as const) {
        for (const b of a[side]) {
          const u = idMap.get(b.entity.id);
          if (u) units.push({ unitId: u.unitId, side, name: b.entity.name, maxHp: b.entity.maxHp });
        }
      }
      const nm = (id: any) => idMap.get(id)?.name ?? (id != null ? "#" + id : "?");
      const events: ReplayEvent[] = (a.battle.trace as any[]).map((e, i) => ({
        index: i, turn: e.turn ?? 0, code: e.t,
        actorUnitId: idMap.get(e.src)?.unitId,
        targetUnitId: idMap.get(e.dst ?? e.id)?.unitId,
        text: renderEvent(e, nm),
        detail: safeJson(e),
      }));
      // 完整 Replay 文件：帧流 + 事件链 + Checkpoint/Delta + 终局状态指纹
      const replay = buildReplay(a.battle as any, recorder, {
        engineVersion: ENGINE_VERSION, gameDataVersion: GAME_DATA_VERSION,
        matchId: match.id, seed, maxTurns: 300,
        policyIds: {
          startup: (a.battle as any).policies.startup.id, priority: (a.battle as any).policies.priority,
          tieBreak: (a.battle as any).policies.tieBreak, death: (a.battle as any).policies.death.id,
          resource: (a.battle as any).policies.resource.id, guessing: (a.battle as any).policies.guessing.id,
        },
        red: match.red.units.map(u => u.name), blue: match.blue.units.map(u => u.name),
      }, captureState(a.battle), { winner: a.result.winner, turns: a.result.turns, reason: a.result.reason });

      return {
        events, units, turns: a.result.turns,
        winner: a.result.reason === "TURN_LIMIT" ? "timeout" : (a.result.winner === 0 ? "red" : a.result.winner === 1 ? "blue" : "draw"),
        replay,
      };
    },
  };
}

/** 能力矩阵：来自引擎自己的覆盖度数据（随 bundle 一起下发），**不手写支持度** */
let CAP_SUPPORT: Record<string, "FULL" | "PARTIAL" | "NONE"> = {};
function ensureCapSupport(bundle: any) {
  if (Object.keys(CAP_SUPPORT).length) return;
  CAP_SUPPORT = Object.fromEntries(((bundle?.capabilities?.rows ?? []) as any[]).map(r => [String(r.key), r.support as any]));
}

/**
 * 组装 FidelityInput：全部数据都来自引擎与知识库，没有任何一处是"估"出来的。
 * 特别地：GAME-VERIFIED 恒为 false —— 我们从没做过游戏内大规模验证，不许假装做过。
 */
function buildFidelity(k: EngineKit, match: GuessingMatch, strat: any): any {
  const units: FidelityUnitInput[] = [];
  const capUse = new Map<string, number>();

  for (const side of ["red", "blue"] as const) {
    for (const u of match[side].units) {
      if (!u.shikigamiId) continue;
      const info = charInfo(k, u.shikigamiId);
      const soulSupport = u.soulId == null ? null : supportOf(k, u.soulId);
      units.push({
        name: u.name, side,
        // ★ 「填了没」只能看四个**绝对值**维度：暴击/爆伤/命中/抵抗在游戏里本来就可能是 0，
        //   拿"非 0"当判据会把合法面板误判成没填（第一版就是这么错的：8/80）。
        panelFilled: ["attack", "hp", "defense", "speed"].filter(x => Number((u.stats as any)[x]) > 0).length,
        soul: u.soulId == null ? null : { id: u.soulId, resolved: k.soulDefs.has(u.soulId), support: soulSupport as any },
        skills: info.skillDetails.map(s => ({ ...s, confidence: s.confidence as any })),
```
