```ts
        ai: { dataPresent: k.aiById.has(u.shikigamiId), unresolvedConds: (info as any).aiUnresolvedConds ?? 0, compiled: info.aiConfidence !== "NONE" && info.aiConfidence !== "LOW", confidence: info.aiConfidence as any, gameVerified: false },
      });
      // 本局会用到的能力：从这个单位技能的解析结果推
      for (const s of info.skillDetails) {
        if (s.parsed.damage) capUse.set("DAMAGE", (capUse.get("DAMAGE") ?? 0) + s.parsed.damage);
        if (s.parsed.heal) capUse.set("HEAL", (capUse.get("HEAL") ?? 0) + s.parsed.heal);
        if (s.parsed.shield) capUse.set("SHIELD", (capUse.get("SHIELD") ?? 0) + s.parsed.shield);
        if (s.parsed.timeline) capUse.set("PULL", (capUse.get("PULL") ?? 0) + s.parsed.timeline);
        if (s.parsed.status) capUse.set("CONTROL", (capUse.get("CONTROL") ?? 0) + s.parsed.status);
        if (s.parsed.orb) capUse.set("ORB", (capUse.get("ORB") ?? 0) + s.parsed.orb);
      }
    }
  }

  // ★ 「有N%概率…」的归因歧义必须进入 Fidelity —— 否则"必定施加"会伪装成已建模。
  //   这是本局实际的**过度施加**处数，属于 MECHANIC 层的真实缺口。
  const probAmbiguous = units.reduce((s, u) => s + u.skills.reduce((a, sk) => a + (sk.probAmbiguous ?? 0), 0), 0);
  const probAttributed = units.reduce((s, u) => s + u.skills.reduce((a, sk) => a + (sk.probAttributed ?? 0), 0), 0);
  if (probAttributed || probAmbiguous) {
    capUse.set("STATUS_PROBABILITY", probAttributed + probAmbiguous);
  }
  // 持续回合同理：原文写了但归因不出 → 只能退回印记描述或 1 回合，属于已知偏差。
  const durationStated = units.reduce((s, u) => s + u.skills.reduce((a, sk) => a + (sk.durationStated ?? 0), 0), 0);
  const durationAmbiguous = units.reduce((s, u) => s + u.skills.reduce((a, sk) => a + (sk.durationAmbiguous ?? 0), 0), 0);
  if (durationStated || durationAmbiguous) {
    capUse.set("EFFECT_DURATION", durationStated + durationAmbiguous);
  }

  const capabilities = [...capUse.entries()].map(([name, useCount]) => ({
    name, useCount,
    // 矩阵里没有的能力如实标 UNKNOWN→按 NONE 处理会让分类过重；这里标 PARTIAL 并让 gap 说明"未登记"
    support: (CAP_SUPPORT[name] ?? "PARTIAL") as "FULL" | "PARTIAL" | "NONE",
  }));
  if (durationAmbiguous > 0) {
    const c = capabilities.find(x => x.name === "EFFECT_DURATION");
    if (c) c.support = "PARTIAL";
  } else {
    const c = capabilities.find(x => x.name === "EFFECT_DURATION");
    if (c) c.support = "FULL";
  }
  // 有歧义就是 PARTIAL（能掷骰的部分已实现，归因不出的部分仍在"必定施加"）
  if (probAmbiguous > 0) {
    const c = capabilities.find(x => x.name === "STATUS_PROBABILITY");
    if (c) c.support = "PARTIAL";
  } else if (probAttributed > 0) {
    const c = capabilities.find(x => x.name === "STATUS_PROBABILITY");
    if (c) c.support = "FULL";
  }

  const input: FidelityInput = {
    units,
    ruleset: [
      { id: strat.startup.id, status: strat.startup.status },
      { id: strat.triggerPriority.id, status: strat.triggerPriority.status },
      { id: strat.death.id, status: strat.death.status },
      { id: strat.endgame.id, status: strat.endgame.status },
      { id: strat.resource.id, status: strat.resource.status },
    ],
    capabilities,
    displayPrecision: "INTEGER_OBSERVED",
  };
  return assessFidelity(input);
}
function ci(w: [number, number]) { return { low: w[0], high: w[1] }; }
function safeJson(x: any) { try { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x, (kk, vv) => (typeof vv === "function" ? undefined : vv))); } catch { return undefined; } }

/**
 * ★ G5/§45：逐单位贡献直接读**引擎自己的统计**，不再事后从 trace 里刨。
 * 好处有两个：① NONE 档（不记事件）也能拿到完整贡献数据；
 * ② 统计口径只有一处（引擎），不会出现"trace 少一条事件，统计就少一块"。
 */
function accumulateStats(a: any, acc: Map<string, PerUnitStats>, idMap: Map<number, any>) {
  const st = a.battle.stats;
  for (const [eid, m] of idMap) {
    const p = acc.get(m.unitId);
    if (!p) continue;
    p.avgDamageDealt += st.damageDealt.get(eid) ?? 0;
    p.avgDamageTaken += st.damageTaken.get(eid) ?? 0;
    p.avgHealing += st.healing.get(eid) ?? 0;
    p.kills += st.kills.get(eid) ?? 0;
    p.deaths += st.deaths.get(eid) ?? 0;
    p.avgActions += st.actions.get(eid) ?? 0;
    const usage = st.skillUsage.get(eid);
    if (usage) for (const [name, n] of usage) p.skillUsage[name] = (p.skillUsage[name] ?? 0) + n;
  }
}

function renderEvent(e: any, nm: (id: any) => string): string {
  switch (e.t) {
    case "DAMAGE": return `${nm(e.src)} → ${nm(e.dst)} ${e.applied} 伤害${e.crit ? "（暴击）" : ""}${e.absorbed ? `（吸收 ${e.absorbed}）` : ""}`;
    case "HP_LOSS": return `${nm(e.dst)} 失去 ${e.amount} 生命`;
    case "DEATH": return `${nm(e.id)} 阵亡（击杀者 ${nm(e.killer)}）`;
    case "LETHAL": return `${nm(e.id)} 受到致命伤害（剩余 ${e.hp}）`;
    case "DEATH_PREVENTED": return `${nm(e.id)} 的致命伤害被阻止`;
    case "REVIVE": return `${nm(e.id)} 复活（${e.hp} 生命）`;
    case "TRANSFORMED": return `${nm(e.id)} 变形，免疫本次死亡`;
    case "STATUS_EXPIRE": return /CONSUMED|SPENT/.test(String(e.reason ?? ""))
      ? `${nm(e.id)} 的 ${e.status} 被消耗（${e.reason}）`
      : `${nm(e.id)} 的 ${e.status} 到期`;
    case "ORB_INIT": return `初始鬼火 红 ${JSON.stringify(e.red)} / 蓝 ${JSON.stringify(e.blue)}`;
    case "ORB_REWARD": return `${e.team === 0 ? "红" : "蓝"}方获得鬼火 ${e.gained}${e.overflow ? `（溢出 ${e.overflow}）` : ""}`;
    default: return e.t + (e.note ? " " + e.note : "");
  }
}
```

### `apps/web/src/adapters/mock-adapter.ts`

```ts
// adapters/mock-adapter.ts — UI-0 用：生成形状正确、数值可信的假结果
// 目的：让 UI 完全不依赖 Kernel 就能开发与验收（UI-0 的验收标准）
import type { SimulateAdapter } from "./simulate.ts";
import { ENGINE_VERSION, GAME_DATA_VERSION } from "./simulate.ts";
import type { GuessingMatch } from "../contracts/guessing-match.ts";
import type { SimulationOptions, SimulationResult, SimulationWarning, StrategySnapshot, CoverageSnapshot, PerUnitStats, ModelUncertainty, RepresentativeSeed } from "../contracts/simulation-result.ts";
import type { ValidationReport, ReplayEvent, ReplayUnit } from "../contracts/worker-api.ts";

/** 可复现伪随机（与引擎同为 mulberry32 思路，便于测试） */
function rng(seed: number) { let s = seed >>> 0 || 1; return () => { s = (s + 0x6D2B79F5) >>> 0; let t = s; t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1); t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61); return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296; }; }
function wilson(k: number, n: number, z = 1.96): { low: number; high: number } {
  if (!n) return { low: 0, high: 0 };
  const p = k / n, d = 1 + (z * z) / n, c = p + (z * z) / (2 * n);
  const s = z * Math.sqrt((p * (1 - p)) / n + (z * z) / (4 * n * n));
  return { low: (c - s) / d, high: (c + s) / d };
}

const STRATEGY: StrategySnapshot = {
  startup: { id: "NAIVE_SOUL_THEN_PASSIVE_THEN_SENKI", status: "HYPOTHESIS" },
  triggerPriority: { id: "REGISTRATION_ORDER", status: "HYPOTHESIS" },
  death: { id: "DEATH_AFTER_PREVENTION", status: "HYPOTHESIS" },
  endgame: { id: "COMMUNITY_2020_UNVERIFIED", status: "UNKNOWN" },
  resource: { id: "COMMUNITY_4_8_5_345", status: "HYPOTHESIS" },
  unknownCount: 3,
};

function coverageOf(m: GuessingMatch): CoverageSnapshot {
  const units = [...m.red.units, ...m.blue.units].filter(u => u.shikigamiId > 0);
  return {
    unitsJoined: units.length, unitsTotal: 10,
    skillsAvailable: units.length * 3, skillsTotal: units.length * 3,
    aiHigh: 7, aiTotal: units.length,
    // ★ 原先是 `filter(u => u.soulId)` —— 0 是**合法的御魂 id**（雪幽魂），被当成「没填」漏掉。
    //   这个桩没有引擎知识库，只能报「填了几个」；真值由 engine-adapter 按知识库解析（见那里的注释）。
    soulsResolved: units.filter(u => u.soulId != null).length, soulsTotal: units.length,
    soulEffectsFull: 0, soulEffectsPartial: 0,
    unknownBuffRefs: [], unsupportedCapabilities: ["GUARD", "LIFESTEAL", "SHIELD_PIERCE"],
  };
}

export const mockAdapter: SimulateAdapter = {
  async validate(match) {
    const cov = coverageOf(match);
    const blockers: string[] = [];
    for (const side of ["red", "blue"] as const) {
      match[side].units.forEach((u, i) => {
        if (!u.shikigamiId) blockers.push(`${side === "red" ? "红" : "蓝"}方第 ${i + 1} 位未选择式神`);
        else if (!u.stats.attack || !u.stats.hp) blockers.push(`${u.name} 面板未填完`);
      });
    }
    const warnings: SimulationWarning[] = [
      { kind: "UNKNOWN_STRATEGY", severity: "WARN", message: "存在 3 项 UNKNOWN 机制策略", detail: "开局结算顺序 / 同时触发优先级 / 竞猜残局规则" },
      { kind: "LOW_AI_CONFIDENCE", severity: "INFO", message: `AI 高置信度 ${cov.aiHigh}/${cov.aiTotal}` },
      { kind: "SOUL_EFFECT_UNSUPPORTED", severity: "INFO", message: "御魂图标未识别，4 件套战斗效果未加载" },
    ];
    return { ok: blockers.length === 0, blockers, warnings, coverage: cov, strategySnapshot: STRATEGY };
  },

  async simulate(match, options, onProgress, shouldCancel) {
    const total = options.simulations;
    const seed0 = options.seed === "AUTO" ? Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e9) : options.seed;
    const r = rng(seed0);
    const t0 = Date.now();
    let redWins = 0, blueWins = 0, draws = 0, timeouts = 0, errors = 0;
    const turnsAll: number[] = [];
    const every = Math.max(50, Math.min(options.progressEvery ?? 500, Math.ceil(total / 20)));
    for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) {
      const x = r();
      const turns = 20 + Math.floor(r() * 60);
      turnsAll.push(turns);
      if (x < 0.573) redWins++; else if (x < 0.998) blueWins++; else draws++;
      if ((i + 1) % every === 0 || i === total - 1) {
        onProgress({ completed: i + 1, total, redWins, blueWins, draws, timeouts, errors, elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0 });
        await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, 0));   // 让 Worker 有机会收到 CANCEL
        if (shouldCancel()) throw new Error("CANCELLED");
      }
    }
    const decisive = redWins + blueWins;
    const ci = decisive ? wilson(redWins, decisive) : null;
    const bi = decisive ? wilson(blueWins, decisive) : null;
    const sorted = turnsAll.slice().sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const pick = (p: number) => sorted[Math.min(sorted.length - 1, Math.floor(sorted.length * p))] ?? 0;

    const perUnit: PerUnitStats[] = [];
    for (const side of ["red", "blue"] as const) {
      for (const u of match[side].units) {
        if (!u.shikigamiId) continue;
        perUnit.push({
          unitId: u.unitId, name: u.name, side,
          avgDamageDealt: Math.round(u.stats.attack * (1.2 + r())),
          avgDamageTaken: Math.round(u.stats.hp * 0.4 * (0.5 + r())),
          avgHealing: Math.round(u.stats.attack * 0.2 * r()),
          kills: Math.round(r() * 2), deaths: Math.round(r() * 2),
          deathRate: +(r() * 0.8).toFixed(3),
          avgActions: +(3 + r() * 8).toFixed(1),
          skillUsage: { "普攻": Math.round(r() * 12), "2技能": Math.round(r() * 6), "3技能": Math.round(r() * 8) },
          soulTriggerCount: 0,
          controlApplied: Math.round(r() * 4), controlResisted: Math.round(r() * 3),
        });
      }
    }

    const perStrategy = [
      { label: "当前策略", group: "STARTUP" as const, redWinRate: redWins / Math.max(1, decisive) },
      { label: "Startup H1", group: "STARTUP" as const, redWinRate: 0.568 },
      { label: "Startup H2", group: "STARTUP" as const, redWinRate: 0.619 },
      { label: "Trigger H1", group: "TRIGGER" as const, redWinRate: 0.552 },
      { label: "Trigger H2", group: "TRIGGER" as const, redWinRate: 0.580 },
      { label: "Turn Limit 500", group: "TURN_LIMIT" as const, redWinRate: 0.571 },
      { label: "Turn Limit 2000", group: "TURN_LIMIT" as const, redWinRate: 0.574 },
    ];
    const rates = perStrategy.map(s => s.redWinRate).filter((v): v is number => v !== null);
    const mn = Math.min(...rates), mx = Math.max(...rates);
    const spread = mx - mn;
    const modelUncertainty: ModelUncertainty = {
      perStrategy, min: mn, max: mx, spread,
      verdict: spread > 0.15 ? "INCONCLUSIVE" : spread > 0.05 ? "SENSITIVE" : "STABLE",
    };

    const seeds: RepresentativeSeed[] = [
      { seed: seed0 + 143822, winner: "red", turns: 31, label: "代表性红胜" },
      { seed: seed0 + 927114, winner: "blue", turns: 42, label: "代表性蓝胜" },
      { seed: seed0 + 192844, winner: "red", turns: 19, label: "短局" },
    ];

    const warnings: SimulationWarning[] = [
      { kind: "UNKNOWN_STRATEGY", severity: "WARN", message: "存在 3 项 UNKNOWN 机制策略" },
      { kind: "MODEL_SENSITIVE", severity: spread > 0.05 ? "WARN" : "INFO", message: `策略敏感性 ${(spread * 100).toFixed(1)} 个百分点` },
    ];
    if (decisive < 1000) warnings.push({ kind: "SMALL_SAMPLE", severity: "WARN", message: `有效样本仅 ${decisive} 局，置信区间过宽` });

    return {
      schemaVersion: 1, resultId: "r" + Date.now().toString(36), matchId: match.id,
      simulations: total, redWins, blueWins, draws, timeouts, errors, decisive,
      redWinRate: decisive ? redWins / decisive : null,
      blueWinRate: decisive ? blueWins / decisive : null,
      confidenceInterval: ci ? { method: "WILSON", level: 0.95, low: ci.low, high: ci.high } : null,
      blueConfidenceInterval: bi ? { method: "WILSON", level: 0.95, low: bi.low, high: bi.high } : null,
      battleStats: {
        avgTurns: +(turnsAll.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / Math.max(1, turnsAll.length)).toFixed(1),
        medianTurns: pick(0.5), p95Turns: pick(0.95),
        firstKillRedRate: 0.612,
        avgSurvivorsRed: 2.4, avgSurvivorsBlue: 1.8,
        avgRemainHpRed: 0.31, avgRemainHpBlue: 0.22,
        avgOrbSpentRed: 18.2, avgOrbSpentBlue: 16.7,
        perUnit,
      },
      strategySnapshot: STRATEGY, modelUncertainty, coverage: coverageOf(match), warnings,
      engineVersion: ENGINE_VERSION, gameDataVersion: GAME_DATA_VERSION,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), seeds,
    };
  },

  async replay(match, seed) {
    const units: ReplayUnit[] = [];
    for (const side of ["red", "blue"] as const) for (const u of match[side].units) {
      if (u.shikigamiId) units.push({ unitId: u.unitId, side, name: u.name, maxHp: u.stats.hp });
    }
    const r = rng(seed);
    const events: ReplayEvent[] = [];
    const CODES = ["BATTLE_START", "TURN_START", "ORB_GAUGE_ADVANCE", "SKILL_SELECT", "TARGET_SELECT", "DAMAGE", "STATUS_ADD", "DEATH", "TURN_END"];
    let turn = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {
      const code = CODES[Math.floor(r() * CODES.length)];
      if (code === "TURN_START") turn++;
      const actor = units[Math.floor(r() * units.length)];
      const target = units[Math.floor(r() * units.length)];
      events.push({
        index: i, turn, code,
        actorUnitId: actor?.unitId, targetUnitId: target?.unitId,
        text: `${code} ${actor?.name ?? ""}${code === "DAMAGE" ? " → " + (target?.name ?? "") + " " + Math.round(r() * 6000) : ""}`,
        detail: code === "DAMAGE" ? { type: "NORMAL", baseAtk: 5946, skillRatio: 1.25, defense: 506, crit: r() > 0.5, randomRoll: +r().toFixed(6), finalDamage: Math.round(r() * 6000), seed } : { seed },
      });
    }
    return { events, units, turns: Math.max(1, turn), winner: r() > 0.45 ? "red" : "blue" };
  },
};
```

### `apps/web/src/adapters/simulate.ts`

```ts
// adapters/simulate.ts — ★ 唯一允许接触 Engine 的模块
// UI-0：mock 实现（保证 UI 可独立开发）；UI-2：替换为真引擎，接口不变。
import type { GuessingMatch } from "../contracts/guessing-match.ts";
import type { SimulationOptions, SimulationResult, CoverageSnapshot, StrategySnapshot, SimulationWarning } from "../contracts/simulation-result.ts";
import type { ValidationReport, ReplayEvent, ReplayUnit } from "../contracts/worker-api.ts";

export interface SimulateAdapter {
  validate(match: GuessingMatch): Promise<ValidationReport>;
  simulate(match: GuessingMatch, options: SimulationOptions, onProgress: (p: { completed: number; total: number; redWins: number; blueWins: number; draws: number; timeouts: number; errors: number; elapsedMs: number }) => void, shouldCancel: () => boolean): Promise<SimulationResult>;
  replay(match: GuessingMatch, seed: number): Promise<{ events: ReplayEvent[]; units: ReplayUnit[]; turns: number; winner: "red" | "blue" | "draw" | "timeout" }>;
}

export const ENGINE_VERSION = "battle-lab 0.1-experimental";
export const GAME_DATA_VERSION = "2026-10-01";
```

### `apps/web/src/workers/protocol.ts`

```ts
// workers/protocol.ts — Worker 消息协议的**全部逻辑**（与线程绑定无关）
// 抽出来的理由：协议必须在 Node 里可测。simulation.worker.ts 只剩三行胶水，
// 于是「VALIDATE/SIMULATE/CANCEL/REPLAY 的消息语义」可以脱离浏览器被完整验证。
import type { SimulateAdapter } from "../adapters/simulate.ts";
import type { WorkerRequest, WorkerResponse } from "../contracts/worker-api.ts";

export interface Protocol {
  handle(req: WorkerRequest): Promise<void>;
  /** 已取消的请求 id（测试可读） */
  cancelled: Set<string>;
}

export function createProtocol(adapter: SimulateAdapter, post: (m: WorkerResponse) => void): Protocol {
  const cancelled = new Set<string>();
  const startedAt = new Map<string, number>();

  function progressSink(requestId: string, total: number) {
    return (p: { completed: number; total: number; redWins: number; blueWins: number; draws: number; timeouts: number; errors: number; elapsedMs: number }) => {
      const eta = p.elapsedMs > 0 ? Math.round((p.elapsedMs / Math.max(1, p.completed)) * (total - p.completed)) : null;
      const decisive = p.redWins + p.blueWins;
      post({
        type: "SIMULATION_PROGRESS", requestId,
        progress: {
          completed: p.completed, total, redWins: p.redWins, blueWins: p.blueWins,
          draws: p.draws, timeouts: p.timeouts, errors: p.errors,
          runningRedWinRate: decisive > 0 ? p.redWins / decisive : null,
          runningCI: null,
          elapsedMs: p.elapsedMs, ...(eta !== null ? { etaMs: eta } : {}),
        },
      } as any);
    };
  }

  async function handle(req: WorkerRequest): Promise<void> {
    try {
      switch (req.type) {
        case "PING":
          post({ type: "PONG", requestId: req.requestId });
          return;
        case "VALIDATE": {
          const report = await adapter.validate(req.match);
          post({ type: "VALIDATION_READY", requestId: req.requestId, report });
          return;
        }
        case "CANCEL":
          cancelled.add(req.requestId);
          return;
        case "SIMULATE": {
          cancelled.delete(req.requestId);
          startedAt.set(req.requestId, Date.now());
          post({ type: "SIMULATION_STARTED", requestId: req.requestId, startedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
          const result = await adapter.simulate(
            req.match, req.options,
            progressSink(req.requestId, req.options.simulations),
            () => cancelled.has(req.requestId),
          );
          if (cancelled.has(req.requestId)) post({ type: "SIMULATION_FAILED", requestId: req.requestId, message: "已取消" });
          else post({ type: "SIMULATION_COMPLETED", requestId: req.requestId, result });
          cancelled.delete(req.requestId);
          return;
        }
        case "REPLAY": {
          const r = await adapter.replay(req.match, req.seed);
          post({ type: "REPLAY_PREPARED", requestId: req.requestId, seed: req.seed, ...r });
          return;
        }
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      post({ type: "SIMULATION_FAILED", requestId: (req as any).requestId, message: String(err?.message ?? err), detail: String(err?.stack ?? "") });
    }
  }

  return { handle, cancelled };
}
```

### `apps/web/src/workers/simulation.worker.ts`

```ts
// workers/simulation.worker.ts — 模拟 Worker（只剩线程胶水；协议逻辑在 protocol.ts）
// UI-2：真引擎适配器（mock 保留在 adapters/mock-adapter.ts 供离线 UI 开发）。
// ★ 对外协议一字不改：主线程永远只看到 DTO。
import { createProtocol } from "./protocol.ts";
import { createEngineAdapter } from "../adapters/engine-adapter.ts";
import type { WorkerRequest } from "../contracts/worker-api.ts";
import bundle from "../engine/bundle.json";

const protocol = createProtocol(createEngineAdapter(bundle as any), (m) => (self as any).postMessage(m));

self.onmessage = (e: MessageEvent<WorkerRequest>) => { void protocol.handle(e.data); };
```

### `apps/web/src/stores/match.ts`

```ts
// stores/match.ts — 只持有可序列化 DTO（契约要求：store 不持有 Engine 对象）
import { defineStore } from "pinia";
import { emptyMatch, swapSides, newUnitId } from "../contracts/guessing-match.ts";
import type { GuessingMatch, GuessingUnitInput, StatKey } from "../contracts/guessing-match.ts";

const DRAFT_KEY = "yys.workbench.match.draft.v1";

/** UX 护栏 #10：输入自动保存到本地草稿，防止填 10 只后刷新丢失 */
function loadDraft(): GuessingMatch | null {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(DRAFT_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    const m = JSON.parse(raw) as GuessingMatch;
    if (m?.schemaVersion !== 1 || !m.red || !m.blue) return null;
    return m;
  } catch { return null; }
}

export const useMatchStore = defineStore("match", {
  state: () => ({
    match: (loadDraft() ?? emptyMatch()) as GuessingMatch,
    savedAt: null as string | null,
    dirty: false,
  }),
  getters: {
    filledCount(s): number { return [...s.match.red.units, ...s.match.blue.units].filter(u => u.shikigamiId > 0).length; },
  },
  actions: {
    touch() { this.match.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString(); this.dirty = true; this.persist(); },
    persist() {
      try { localStorage.setItem(DRAFT_KEY, JSON.stringify(this.match)); this.savedAt = new Date().toISOString(); this.dirty = false; }
      catch { /* 配额满时静默：草稿不应阻塞操作 */ }
    },
    unit(side: "red" | "blue", index: number): GuessingUnitInput { return this.match[side].units[index]; },
    setShikigami(side: "red" | "blue", index: number, id: number, name: string) {
      const u = this.match[side].units[index]; u.shikigamiId = id; u.name = name; this.touch();
    },
    setStat(side: "red" | "blue", index: number, key: StatKey, value: number) {
      const u = this.match[side].units[index];
      u.stats[key] = Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;
      this.touch();
    },
    setSoul(side: "red" | "blue", index: number, soulId: number | null) {
      this.match[side].units[index].soulId = soulId; this.touch();
    },
    clearUnit(side: "red" | "blue", index: number) {
      this.match[side].units[index] = { ...this.match[side].units[index], unitId: newUnitId(), shikigamiId: 0, name: "", soulId: null };
      this.touch();
    },
    swap() { this.match = swapSides(this.match); this.touch(); },
    replace(m: GuessingMatch) { this.match = m; this.touch(); },
    reset() { this.match = emptyMatch(); this.touch(); },
    loadCanonical() { /* UI-1 接入 mock 数据 */ },
  },
});
```

### `apps/web/src/stores/simulation.ts`

```ts
// stores/simulation.ts — 只持有 DTO 与 Worker 句柄，不持有任何 Engine 对象
import { defineStore } from "pinia";
/**
 * ★★★ 第 256 轮：**replay 文件必须是"生"的**（markRaw）。
 *
 * 用户实测（错误条抓到的原话）：`Failed to execute 'structuredClone' on 'Window': #<Object> could not be cloned.`
 * 根因：这份 replay 存进 Pinia state ⇒ Vue 把它包成**响应式代理**；而引擎的 replay/snapshot 用
 * `structuredClone` 复制状态帧 —— **代理不能被结构化克隆** ⇒ 剧场一渲染就抛。
 * Node 里的测试传的是原始对象，所以三种探针全绿（这是"测试路径与浏览器路径不是同一条"的典型）。
 * 逐字复现见 tools/_probe-reactive256.ts：reactive(replay) ⇒ 抛；markRaw / toRaw ⇒ 不抛。
 */
import { markRaw } from "vue";
import type { GuessingMatch } from "../contracts/guessing-match.ts";
import type { SimulationOptions, SimulationResult } from "../contracts/simulation-result.ts";
import type { WorkerRequest, WorkerResponse, ValidationReport, ReplayEvent, ReplayUnit, SimulationProgress } from "../contracts/worker-api.ts";

export const useSimulationStore = defineStore("simulation", {
  state: () => ({
    worker: null as Worker | null,
    requestId: null as string | null,
    running: false,
    progress: null as SimulationProgress | null,
    result: null as SimulationResult | null,
    validation: null as ValidationReport | null,
    error: null as string | null,
    // ★ 不能叫 replay：Pinia 的 state 与 action 共用命名空间，
    // 一旦 state 里有同名属性，this.replay = {...} 会**覆盖掉 action 函数**，
    // 之后 sim.replay(match, seed) 就不可调用了（vue-tsc 会直接报 "not callable"）。
    replayData: null as { seed: number; events: ReplayEvent[]; units: ReplayUnit[]; turns: number; winner: string } | null,
    /** ★ G4：完整 Replay 文件（Battle Theater 用它做到任意帧重建战场） */
    replayFile: null as unknown | null,
    /**
     * ★★★ 第 256 轮：**录像进行中**。
     *   用户实测报的问题：点「观看战斗」只切了视图 —— 没录过录像时就只看到「还没有可观看的战斗。」
     *   （看起来就是"观看不了"）。现在那个入口会**当场录一场**，而录制要时间 ⇒ 必须有个"正在录"的状态，
     *   否则那 100ms 里界面仍然像坏的。这个标志位同时是"失败也要清掉"的（不许永远转圈）。
     */
    replayPending: false as boolean,
  }),
  getters: {
    percent(s): number { return s.progress ? Math.round((s.progress.completed / Math.max(1, s.progress.total)) * 100) : 0; },
  },
  actions: {
    ensureWorker() {
      if (this.worker) return this.worker;
      const w = new Worker(new URL("../workers/simulation.worker.ts", import.meta.url), { type: "module" });
      w.onmessage = (e: MessageEvent<WorkerResponse>) => this.onMessage(e.data);
      w.onerror = (e) => { this.error = String((e as any).message ?? e); this.running = false; };
      this.worker = w;
      return w;
    },
    send(req: WorkerRequest) { this.ensureWorker().postMessage(req); },
    newId() { return "req" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10); },

    onMessage(msg: WorkerResponse) {
      switch (msg.type) {
        case "VALIDATION_READY": if (msg.requestId === this.requestId) this.validation = msg.report; break;
        case "SIMULATION_STARTED": this.running = true; this.error = null; break;
        case "SIMULATION_PROGRESS": if (msg.requestId === this.requestId) this.progress = msg.progress; break;
        case "SIMULATION_COMPLETED": if (msg.requestId === this.requestId) { this.result = msg.result; this.running = false; this.progress = null; } break;
        /** ★ 第 256 轮：失败与取消也要把 replayPending 清掉（否则那个"正在录"的提示会永远转下去） */
        case "SIMULATION_FAILED": if (msg.requestId === this.requestId) { this.replayPending = false; this.error = msg.message === "已取消" ? null : msg.message; this.running = false; this.progress = null; } break;
        case "REPLAY_PREPARED": if (msg.requestId === this.requestId) {
          /** ★ 第 256 轮：两样都 markRaw —— 它们是**数据大块**，既不参与响应式，也绝不能被包成代理 */
          this.replayData = markRaw({ seed: msg.seed, events: msg.events, units: msg.units, turns: msg.turns, winner: msg.winner });
          this.replayFile = markRaw((msg as any).replay ?? null);
          this.replayPending = false;   // ★ 第 256 轮：录制结束（成功）
        } break;
      }
    },

    validate(match: GuessingMatch) {
      const requestId = this.newId();
      this.requestId = requestId;
      this.send({ type: "VALIDATE", requestId, match: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(match)) });
    },
    simulate(match: GuessingMatch, options: SimulationOptions) {
      const requestId = this.newId();
      this.requestId = requestId;
      this.result = null; this.progress = null; this.error = null;
      this.send({ type: "SIMULATE", requestId, match: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(match)), options });
    },
    cancel() { if (this.requestId) this.send({ type: "CANCEL", requestId: this.requestId }); },
    replay(match: GuessingMatch, seed: number) {
      const requestId = this.newId();
      this.requestId = requestId;
      this.replayPending = true;
      this.send({ type: "REPLAY", requestId, match: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(match)), seed });
    },
    /**
     * ★★★ 第 256 轮：**「观看战斗」这个入口收成一个动作** —— 有录像就只切视图（不重复录），
     *   没录像就**当场录一场**（用传入的 seed）。
     *
     * 为什么放在 store 里而不是 <script setup> 里：本仓库没有 jsdom / @vue/test-utils，
     *   逻辑留在组件里就等于**没有验收** —— 这正是第 137 轮把播放控制搬进 transport.ts 的同一条教训
     *   （见报告 §106 与 tests/ui14-transport.ts 的文件头）。
     */
    watchOrReplay(match: GuessingMatch, seed: number): "reused" | "recording" {
      if (this.replayFile) return "reused";
      this.replay(match, seed);
      return "recording";
    },
  },
});
```

### `apps/web/src/mock/canonical-match.ts`

```ts
// mock/canonical-match.ts — 用真实典型对局 #001 作为开发期 mock
// 数据来源：battle-lab/fixtures/guessing-match-canonical-001.json（游戏内阵容详情截图 1:1 录入）
import { GUESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION, newUnitId } from "../contracts/guessing-match.ts";
import type { GuessingMatch, GuessingUnitInput } from "../contracts/guessing-match.ts";

type Row = [string, number, number, number, number, number, number, number, number, number];
// 名称, id, 攻击, 生命, 防御, 速度, 暴击, 暴伤, 命中, 抵抗
const RED: Row[] = [
  ["三尾狐", 202, 5686, 17605, 792, 176, 85, 311, 0, 48],
  ["日和坊", 297, 3976, 16767, 524, 166, 45, 339, 0, 32],
  ["思金神", 601, 2630, 20424, 750, 128, 8, 150, 0, 86],
  ["跳跳弟弟", 225, 5059, 29362, 658, 116, 93, 276, 0, 80],
  ["聆海金鱼姬", 346, 3848, 16420, 561, 111, 35, 220, 0, 0],
];
const BLUE: Row[] = [
  ["座敷童子", 205, 2957, 26890, 1141, 180, 28, 164, 15, 104],
  ["夜溟彼岸花", 358, 5946, 16665, 506, 150, 80, 178, 0, 40],
  ["樱花妖", 267, 3873, 24799, 668, 147, 73, 157, 0, 63],
  ["缚骨清姬", 352, 3514, 14095, 586, 142, 43, 192, 0, 40],
  ["神启荒", 390, 4708, 15155, 536, 116, 10, 241, 30, 0],
];

function conv(rows: Row[]): GuessingUnitInput[] {
  return rows.map(([name, id, attack, hp, defense, speed, critRate, critDamage, effectHit, effectResist]) => ({
    unitId: newUnitId(), shikigamiId: id, name,
    stats: { attack, hp, defense, speed, critRate, critDamage, effectHit, effectResist },
    soulId: null,   // 御魂图标未识别；只影响战斗效果，不影响面板
  }));
}

export function canonicalMatch(): GuessingMatch {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    schemaVersion: GUESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    id: "canonical-001",
    red: { units: conv(RED) },
    blue: { units: conv(BLUE) },
    ruleset: "GUESSING",
    source: { type: "FIXTURE", capturedAt: "2026-10-01", note: "典型对局 #001（真实竞猜截图录入）" },
    createdAt: now, updatedAt: now,
  };
}
```

### `apps/web/src/kb/resolve-refs.ts`

```ts
// kb/resolve-refs.ts — ★★★ 第 256 轮：把技能原文里的占位符**换成阴阳师里的说明文字**
//
// 由来（用户实测 + 截图）：式神录里那条技能写着「唯一效果。为友方目标治疗生命上限15%的生命并施加新蕊。」，
//   而我们的技能详情显示的是原文占位符 —— `[tips_11]。为友方目标治疗生命上限15%的生命并施加[buff_5981_1]。`
//   （知识库里其实**两张表都有**：glossary/tips 156 条 + buffs 338 条，只是没人把它们接进详情页。）
//
// 处置：一个**纯函数**解析器（可被尺子直接压），三种占位符各有明确去向：
//   · `[tips_N]`  ⇒ 内联换成术语名（唯一效果 / 先机 / 间接伤害…），完整说明随 ref 一起带出去（详情页做成悬停）；
//   · `[buff_x]`  ⇒ 内联换成状态名（新蕊 / 盛放…），同样带说明；
//   · **认不出的** ⇒ **原样留在正文里**并记进 `unknown`（不许静默吞掉 —— 这是本仓库反复抓的那一类）。
export interface RefTip { id: string; name: string; desc: string }
export interface RefTables { tips: RefTip[]; buffs: RefTip[] }
export interface ResolvedRef { source: "tips" | "buff"; id: string; name: string; text: string }
export type Part = { kind: "text"; text: string } | ({ kind: "ref" } & ResolvedRef);
export interface ResolvedText {
  /** 内联渲染用：文本片段与术语片段按原文顺序排好 */
  parts: Part[];
  /** 平铺后的纯文本（搜索 / 断言 / 复制用） */
  text: string;
  /** 这一段里用到的术语与状态（去重，按出现顺序） */
  refs: ResolvedRef[];
  /** 认不出的占位符（原样留在 text 里，这里也报一份） */
  unknown: string[];
}

const PATTERN = /\[(tips_(\d+)|buff_([0-9A-Za-z_]+))\]/g;

export function resolveRefs(raw: string, tables: RefTables): ResolvedText {
  const tipById = new Map(tables.tips.map((t) => [String(t.id), t]));
  const buffById = new Map(tables.buffs.map((b) => [String(b.id), b]));
  const parts: Part[] = [];
  const refs: ResolvedRef[] = [];
  const unknown: string[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  let last = 0;
  const src = String(raw ?? "");
  for (const m of src.matchAll(PATTERN)) {
    const at = m.index ?? 0;
    if (at > last) parts.push({ kind: "text", text: src.slice(last, at) });
    last = at + m[0].length;
    const tipId = m[2];
    const buffId = m[3];
    const hit: RefTip | undefined = tipId ? tipById.get(String(tipId)) : buffById.get(String(buffId));
    if (!hit) {
      /** 认不出 ⇒ 原样显示 + 记账（**不许**静默删掉这一段） */
      parts.push({ kind: "text", text: m[0] });
      if (!unknown.includes(m[0])) unknown.push(m[0]);
      continue;
    }
    const ref: ResolvedRef = { source: tipId ? "tips" : "buff", id: String(hit.id), name: String(hit.name), text: String(hit.desc ?? "") };
    parts.push({ kind: "ref", ...ref });
    const key = ref.source + ":" + ref.id;
    if (!seen.has(key)) { seen.add(key); refs.push(ref); }
  }
  if (last < src.length) parts.push({ kind: "text", text: src.slice(last) });
  const text = parts.map((p) => (p.kind === "text" ? p.text : p.name)).join("");
  return { parts, text, refs, unknown };
}

/** 只要纯文本时的便捷入口（列表 / 搜索用） */
export function resolveText(raw: string, tables: RefTables): string {
  return resolveRefs(raw, tables).text;
}

/**
 * ★★★ 第 256 轮（用户要求：「要与阴阳师里每个技能右边附加的小框**完全一致**」）。
 *
 * 游戏那个小框的规则（从用户给的四张式神录截图反推出来的，两条都不许少）：
 *   ① **印记/状态在前，术语在后**；各自**按首次出现的顺序**（先看技能正文，再看升级句）；
 *   ② **传递一层**：引用的条目自己的正文里又引用了别的条目，也要收进来
 *      （例：携芳意引用了新蕊，而新蕊的正文里写着「使携带者获得盛放」⇒ 小框里也有盛放）。
 * 对拍（用户截图）：
 *   · 携芳意 ⇒ 新蕊、盛放、唯一效果、先机     （正文引用新蕊+唯一效果；新蕊的正文引用盛放；升级 Lv.5 引用先机）
 *   · 醒时花 ⇒ 盛放、新蕊、唯一效果           （正文先提盛放再提新蕊 —— **顺序按正文出现**，不是按类型排序）
 */
export interface SkillRefBox { buffs: ResolvedRef[]; tips: ResolvedRef[]; unknown: string[] }

export function collectSkillRefs(texts: Array<string | null | undefined>, tables: RefTables): SkillRefBox {
  const tipById = new Map(tables.tips.map((t) => [String(t.id), t]));
  const buffById = new Map(tables.buffs.map((b) => [String(b.id), b]));
  const buffs: ResolvedRef[] = [];
  const tips: ResolvedRef[] = [];
  const unknown: string[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const queue = texts.map((t) => String(t ?? ""));
  /**
   * ★ 广度优先、但**保持出现顺序**：每一层的文本按原顺序扫，扫到的引用先进小框、
   *   再把「引用条目自己的正文」追加到队尾（这就是那个"传递一层"的实现，深度不限但天然收敛 —— 靠 seen 去重）。
   */
  /**
   * ★★★ 实测（第一版就漏了）：「传递」这一步**不能只认占位符** —— 新蕊的正文里写着
   *   「…触发3次后移除并使携带者获得**盛放**，持续3回合。」，那里没有 `[buff_5982_1]`，是**中文名**。
   *   游戏的小框里照样有盛放 ⇒ 扫描必须同时按**占位符**与**条目名**命中，两者合并后**按出现位置排序**
   *   （顺序错了小框的条目顺序就会与游戏不一致 —— 用户要的是"完全一致"）。
   * ★ 保守之处：只认长度 ≥ 2 的名字（单字名如「火」会把满篇正文都命中），且同一 id 只收一次。
   */
  const hitsOf = (text: string): Array<{ at: number; source: "tips" | "buff"; id: string; raw: string }> => {
    const out: Array<{ at: number; source: "tips" | "buff"; id: string; raw: string }> = [];
    for (const m of text.matchAll(PATTERN)) {
      const at = m.index ?? 0;
      if (m[2] !== undefined) out.push({ at, source: "tips", id: String(m[2]), raw: m[0] });
      else out.push({ at, source: "buff", id: String(m[3]), raw: m[0] });
    }
    for (const [source, table] of [["buff", tables.buffs], ["tips", tables.tips]] as Array<["buff" | "tips", RefTip[]]>) {
      for (const e of table) {
        const nm = String(e.name ?? "");
        if (nm.length < 2) continue;
        const at = text.indexOf(nm);
        if (at >= 0) out.push({ at, source, id: String(e.id), raw: nm });
      }
    }
    return out.sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at);
  };
  for (let qi = 0; qi < queue.length; qi++) {
    const text = queue[qi];
    for (const h of hitsOf(text)) {
      const hit: RefTip | undefined = h.source === "tips" ? tipById.get(h.id) : buffById.get(h.id);
      if (!hit) { if (!unknown.includes(h.raw)) unknown.push(h.raw); continue; }
      const key = (h.source === "tips" ? "tips:" : "buff:") + hit.id;
      if (seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      const ref: ResolvedRef = { source: h.source, id: String(hit.id), name: String(hit.name), text: String(hit.desc ?? "") };
      (ref.source === "buff" ? buffs : tips).push(ref);
      /** 传递：把这条引用自己的正文也扫一遍（它里面的引用同样按出现顺序落到后面） */
      queue.push(ref.text);
    }
  }
  return { buffs, tips, unknown };
}
```

### `apps/web/src/kb/useKb.ts`

```ts
// kb/useKb.ts — 知识库访问层
// selector 索引随首屏加载（37KB）；detail 懒加载（552KB），点「查看技能」才取
import { computed, ref } from "vue";
import shikigamiIndex from "./shikigami-index.json";
import soulIndex from "./soul-index.json";
import glossaryJson from "./glossary.json";

export interface ShikigamiIndexEntry {
  i: number; n: string; r: string; y: number | null;
  k?: string; p?: string; s?: string; t: string[];
  /** 六星觉醒面板 [攻,生,防,速,暴击%,爆伤%,效果命中%,效果抵抗%]；源数据缺失处为 null */
  pn?: (number | null)[] | null;
  has: { panel: boolean; skills: number; ai: boolean };
}
export interface SoulStatBonus { key: string; rate?: number; flat?: number }
export interface SoulIndexEntry {
  i: number; n: string; tag: string; suit: string; desc: string; type: number;
  suitBonus: SoulStatBonus | null;
  engine: { trigger: string | null; effectKind: string; magnitudes: number[]; support: "FULL" | "PARTIAL" | "UNSUPPORTED"; note: string };
}
export interface GlossaryEntry { id: number; name: string; text: string }

export const SHIKIGAMI: ShikigamiIndexEntry[] = shikigamiIndex as any;
export const SOULS: SoulIndexEntry[] = soulIndex as any;
export const GLOSSARY: GlossaryEntry[] = glossaryJson as any;

export const RARITIES = ["SP", "SSR", "SR", "R", "N", "UR"];
export const SOUL_SUPPORT_LABEL: Record<string, string> = { FULL: "完整实现", PARTIAL: "部分实现", UNSUPPORTED: "未实现" };

/** 式神搜索：正式名 / 别名 / 全拼 / 首字母 / ID */
export function searchShikigami(q: string, rarity: string | null, limit = 60): ShikigamiIndexEntry[] {
  const s = q.trim().toLowerCase();
  let list = SHIKIGAMI;
  if (rarity) list = list.filter(x => x.r === rarity);
  if (!s) return list.slice(0, limit);
  const scored: Array<{ x: ShikigamiIndexEntry; sc: number }> = [];
  for (const x of list) {
    let sc = -1;
    if (String(x.i) === s) sc = 100;
    else if (x.n === s) sc = 95;
    else if (x.n.toLowerCase().startsWith(s)) sc = 90;
    else if (x.n.toLowerCase().includes(s)) sc = 80;
    else if (x.k && x.k.toLowerCase().includes(s)) sc = 75;   // 别名
    else if (x.p && x.p.startsWith(s)) sc = 70;                // 全拼
    else if (x.s && x.s.startsWith(s)) sc = 60;                // 首字母
    else if (x.t.some(t => t.includes(s))) sc = 40;            // tag
    if (sc > 0) scored.push({ x, sc });
  }
  return scored.sort((a, b) => b.sc - a.sc || a.x.i - b.x.i).slice(0, limit).map(v => v.x);
}

export function searchSouls(q: string, limit = 70): SoulIndexEntry[] {
  const s = q.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!s) return SOULS;
  return SOULS.filter(x => x.n.toLowerCase().includes(s) || x.tag.includes(s) || x.suit.includes(s) || String(x.i) === s).slice(0, limit);
}

// ---------- 懒加载 detail ----------
let detailPromise: Promise<Record<string, any>> | null = null;
const detailState = ref<"idle" | "loading" | "ready" | "error">("idle");
const detailError = ref<string | null>(null);

export async function loadDetail() {
  if (!detailPromise) {
    detailState.value = "loading";
    detailPromise = import("./shikigami-detail.json").then(m => {
      detailState.value = "ready";
      return (m.default ?? m) as Record<string, any>;
    }).catch(e => { detailState.value = "error"; detailError.value = String(e?.message ?? e); throw e; });
  }
  return detailPromise;
}
export function useDetailState() { return { state: detailState, error: detailError }; }

/** 最近使用（localStorage） */
const RECENT_KEY = "yys.workbench.recent.v1";
const recent = ref<number[]>(load());
function load(): number[] { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(RECENT_KEY) ?? "[]"); } catch { return []; } }
export function pushRecent(id: number) {
  recent.value = [id, ...recent.value.filter(x => x !== id)].slice(0, 12);
  try { localStorage.setItem(RECENT_KEY, JSON.stringify(recent.value)); } catch { /* ignore */ }
}
export function recentShikigami(): ShikigamiIndexEntry[] {
  return recent.value.map(id => SHIKIGAMI.find(x => x.i === id)).filter(Boolean) as ShikigamiIndexEntry[];
}

export const byId = computed(() => new Map(SHIKIGAMI.map(x => [x.i, x])));
export function shikigamiById(id: number) { return SHIKIGAMI.find(x => x.i === id) ?? null; }

/**
 * 选中式神时的八维预填（六星觉醒面板）。
 * 只作为**起点**：用户随后按截图逐格覆盖。源数据缺项保留 null，调用方沿用当前值。
 */
export function panelPrefill(id: number): Record<string, number> | null {
  const pn = shikigamiById(id)?.pn;
  if (!pn) return null;
  const keys = ["attack", "hp", "defense", "speed", "critRate", "critDamage", "effectHit", "effectResist"];
  const out: Record<string, number> = {};
  pn.forEach((v, i) => { if (v != null) out[keys[i]] = v; });
  return out;
}
export function soulById(id: number | null) { return id === null ? null : (SOULS.find(x => x.i === id) ?? null); }
```

### `apps/web/src/components/common/JsonIo.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// 输入方式的 B 通道：JSON 导入 / 导出 / 复制 / 红蓝互换（文档第六节）
import { ref } from "vue";
import type { GuessingMatch } from "../../contracts/guessing-match";

const props = defineProps<{ match: GuessingMatch }>();
const emit = defineEmits<{ (e: "import", m: GuessingMatch): void; (e: "swap"): void; (e: "error", msg: string): void }>();
const open = ref(false);
const text = ref("");
const mode = ref<"export" | "import">("export");

function show(m: "export" | "import") { mode.value = m; text.value = m === "export" ? JSON.stringify(props.match, null, 1) : ""; open.value = true; }
function doImport() {
  try {
    const m = JSON.parse(text.value) as GuessingMatch;
    if (m?.schemaVersion !== 1 || !m.red?.units || !m.blue?.units) throw new Error("schemaVersion 或结构不匹配");
    emit("import", m); open.value = false;
  } catch (e: any) { emit("error", "导入失败：" + String(e?.message ?? e)); }
}
async function copy() { try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text.value); } catch { /* 忽略 */ } }
</script>

<template>
  <span class="io">
    <button @click="show('export')">导出阵容</button>
    <button @click="show('import')">导入 JSON</button>
    <button @click="emit('swap')">⇄ 交换红蓝</button>
    <div v-if="open" class="overlay" @click.self="open = false">
      <div class="dialog">
        <header><b>{{ mode === "export" ? "导出阵容 JSON" : "导入阵容 JSON" }}</b><span class="spacer" />
          <button v-if="mode === 'export'" @click="copy">复制</button>
          <button @click="open = false">✕</button></header>
        <textarea v-model="text" spellcheck="false" :readonly="mode === 'export'"
                  :placeholder='mode === "import" ? "粘贴 GuessingMatch JSON…" : ""' />
        <footer v-if="mode === 'import'"><button class="primary" @click="doImport">导入</button></footer>
      </div>
    </div>
  </span>
</template>

<!-- <style> 已省略 1 KB（纯样式，无逻辑） -->
```

### `apps/web/src/components/dev/DeveloperHud.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// components/dev/DeveloperHud.vue — UI-6 开发者 HUD（可折叠底部抽屉，约 60vh，可滚动）
// 只读观察窗口：版本 / 数据规模 / 本次运行 / 策略快照 / 覆盖度 / 实测事实清单 / 文件地图。
//
// 两条纪律：
//   1) 本组件**不 import 任何引擎模块**：引擎版本号取自 adapters/simulate.ts 的常量，
//      覆盖度与策略快照取自 store 里的 DTO，事实清单取自 dev/facts.ts。
//   2) 能从索引 / store 里算出来的就动态算；算不出来的写「—」并指向事实编号，**不在组件里写死数字**。
import { computed, onMounted, ref, watch } from "vue";
import { useSimulationStore } from "../../stores/simulation";
import { SHIKIGAMI, SOULS, GLOSSARY } from "../../kb/useKb";
import { ENGINE_VERSION, GAME_DATA_VERSION } from "../../adapters/simulate.ts";
import { GUESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION } from "../../contracts/guessing-match.ts";
import { FACTS, countBySeverity } from "../../dev/facts.ts";
import type { FactSeverity } from "../../dev/facts.ts";

const props = defineProps<{ open: boolean }>();
const emit = defineEmits<{ (e: "close"): void }>();

const sim = useSimulationStore();

// ───────── 版本 ─────────
// 引擎/数据版本优先用本次结果里的（那是引擎自报的），没有就用适配器常量（同一来源）。
const engineVersion = computed(() => sim.result?.engineVersion ?? ENGINE_VERSION);
const gameDataVersion = computed(() => sim.result?.gameDataVersion ?? GAME_DATA_VERSION);

// ───────── 数据规模（能从索引算的就动态算） ─────────
const withPinyin = computed(() => SHIKIGAMI.filter(x => !!x.p).length);
const aiUnits = computed(() => SHIKIGAMI.filter(x => x.has?.ai).length);
const skillSum = computed(() => SHIKIGAMI.reduce((a, x) => a + (x.has?.skills ?? 0), 0));
const soulSupport = computed(() => SOULS.reduce((a: Record<string, number>, s) => {
  a[s.engine.support] = (a[s.engine.support] ?? 0) + 1; return a;
}, {} as Record<string, number>));

/** 实验索引懒加载（78KB，打开抽屉时才取一次） */
const expCount = ref<number | null>(null);
const indexZones = ref<Array<{ zone: string; id: string; status: string }>>([]);
const expError = ref<string | null>(null);
let expTried = false;
async function ensureExperiments() {
  if (expTried) return;
  expTried = true;
  try {
    const mod: any = await import("../../kb/experiments.json");
    const d = mod.default ?? mod;
    expCount.value = Array.isArray(d?.experiments) ? d.experiments.length : null;
    indexZones.value = Array.isArray(d?.redZones)
      ? d.redZones.map((z: any) => ({ zone: String(z.zone ?? ""), id: String(z.id ?? "?"), status: String(z.status ?? "") }))
      : [];
  } catch (e: any) { expError.value = String(e?.message ?? e); }
}
watch(() => props.open, v => { if (v) ensureExperiments(); }, { immediate: true });
onMounted(() => { if (props.open) ensureExperiments(); });

const indexRows = computed(() => [
  { k: "式神（竞猜池索引）", v: String(SHIKIGAMI.length), src: "—（6 阴阳师 / 英杰不入索引，见 F10）" },
  { k: "技能（索引内合计）", v: String(skillSum.value), src: "—（源数据含阴阳师技能，见 F10）" },
  { k: "AI 档案（has.ai）", v: String(aiUnits.value), src: "—（见 F10）" },
  { k: "buff 表", v: "—", src: "—（网页索引里没有 buff 表，见 F10）" },
  { k: "御魂", v: String(SOULS.length), src: "同源（kb/soul-index.json）" },
  { k: "术语", v: String(GLOSSARY.length), src: "同源（kb/glossary.json）" },
  { k: "实验", v: expCount.value === null ? (expError.value ? "—" : "…") : String(expCount.value), src: expError.value ? "索引加载失败" : "同源（kb/experiments.json）" },
  { k: "拼音覆盖", v: withPinyin.value + " / " + SHIKIGAMI.length, src: "同源（kb/shikigami-index.json）" },
]);

// ───────── 本次运行（允许为空） ─────────
const r = computed(() => sim.result);
const runRows = computed(() => {
  const x = r.value;
  const dash = (v: number | null | undefined) => (v === null || v === undefined ? "—" : v.toLocaleString());
  return [
    { k: "N（局数）", v: x ? dash(x.simulations) : "—" },
    { k: "红胜", v: x ? dash(x.redWins) : "—" },
    { k: "蓝胜", v: x ? dash(x.blueWins) : "—" },
    { k: "平", v: x ? dash(x.draws) : "—" },
    { k: "未终结", v: x ? dash(x.timeouts) : "—" },
    { k: "异常", v: x ? dash(x.errors) : "—", bad: !!x && x.errors > 0 },
    { k: "有效样本", v: x ? dash(x.decisive) : "—" },
    { k: "平均回合", v: x ? String(x.battleStats.avgTurns) : "—" },
    { k: "代表 seed", v: x?.seeds?.length ? String(x.seeds[0].seed) : "—" },
  ];
});
const validationLine = computed(() => {
  const v = sim.validation;
  if (!v) return "校验报告：—（尚未校验）";
  return "校验报告：blockers " + v.blockers.length + " · warnings " + v.warnings.length;
});

// ───────── 策略快照：5 个红区 ─────────
// 有本次运行 / 校验结果时用**冻结的**快照；没有就退回 kb/experiments.json 的静态红区规则。
// 两种情况分别标注来源，绝不把静态索引伪装成"本次运行的结果"。
const ZONES: Array<{ key: string; label: string; re: RegExp }> = [
  { key: "startup", label: "开局结算", re: /开局/ },
  { key: "triggerPriority", label: "同时触发", re: /同时触发/ },
  { key: "death", label: "死亡结算", re: /死亡/ },
  { key: "endgame", label: "竞猜残局", re: /竞猜残局/ },
  { key: "resource", label: "鬼火", re: /鬼火/ },
];
const frozen = computed(() => r.value?.strategySnapshot ?? sim.validation?.strategySnapshot ?? null);
const strategyRows = computed(() => ZONES.map(z => {
  const f = frozen.value as any;
  if (f && f[z.key]) {
    return { label: z.label, ids: [String(f[z.key].id)], status: String(f[z.key].status), counts: null as Record<string, number> | null, source: "本次运行（已冻结）" };
  }
  const rules = indexZones.value.filter(x => z.re.test(x.zone));
  const counts: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const x of rules) counts[x.status] = (counts[x.status] ?? 0) + 1;
  const status = counts.UNKNOWN ? "UNKNOWN" : counts.HYPOTHESIS ? "HYPOTHESIS" : counts.VERIFIED ? "VERIFIED" : null;
  const ids = [...new Set(rules.map(x => x.id))];
  return {
    label: z.label,
    ids: ids.length ? ids : ["—"],
    status,
    counts: rules.length ? counts : null,
    source: rules.length ? "kb/experiments.json（静态 · 取最弱状态）" : "索引里没有该红区",
  };
}));

// ───────── 覆盖度 ─────────
const cov = computed(() => r.value?.coverage ?? sim.validation?.coverage ?? null);
const covCells = computed(() => {
  const c = cov.value;
  if (!c) return [] as Array<{ k: string; v: string }>;
  return [
    { k: "单位数据", v: c.unitsJoined + " / " + c.unitsTotal },
    { k: "技能可用", v: c.skillsAvailable + " / " + c.skillsTotal },
    { k: "AI 高置信度", v: c.aiHigh + " / " + c.aiTotal },
    { k: "御魂已解析", v: c.soulsResolved + " / " + c.soulsTotal },
    { k: "御魂战斗效果", v: c.soulEffectsFull + " 完整 / " + c.soulEffectsPartial + " 部分" },
    { k: "未知 buff 引用", v: String(c.unknownBuffRefs.length) },
  ];
});

// ───────── 事实清单 ─────────
const sevCount = countBySeverity();
const SEV_CLASS: Record<FactSeverity, string> = { BLOCK: "sev-block", WARN: "sev-warn", INFO: "sev-info" };

// ───────── 文件地图 ─────────
const FILE_MAP: Array<{ dir: string; text: string }> = [
  { dir: "contracts/", text: "三份冻结契约（竞猜输入 / 结果 / Worker 协议），零依赖，UI 与引擎的唯一边界" },
  { dir: "engine/", text: "bundle.json 预打包知识库切片（只在 Worker 里加载）+ assemble.ts 浏览器侧对局装配" },
  { dir: "adapters/", text: "唯一允许接触引擎的地方：simulate.ts 接口 / engine-adapter.ts 真引擎 / mock-adapter.ts 离线假实现" },
  { dir: "workers/", text: "simulation.worker.ts 三行线程胶水 + protocol.ts（可脱离浏览器测试的协议逻辑）" },
  { dir: "stores/", text: "Pinia，只存 DTO：match（阵容 / 草稿）、simulation（结果 / 校验 / 回放）" },
  { dir: "components/", text: "视图层：lineup 阵容 / simulation 体检 / knowledge 图鉴 / mechanics 机制台 / result 结果与回放 / dev 本 HUD" },
  { dir: "tests/", text: "Node 直接跑 .ts 的验收：ui0 契约 / ui1 编辑器 / ui2 引擎 / ui5 知识库 / ui6 HUD+观测 / e2e 全流程" },
  { dir: "tools/", text: "生成物脚本：build-kb-index.mjs / build-engine-bundle.mjs / build-experiments-index.mjs" },
];

function statusClass(s: string | null) {
  return s === "UNKNOWN" ? "unknown" : s === "VERIFIED" ? "verified" : s === "HYPOTHESIS" ? "hypothesis" : "none";
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="hudwrap" :class="{ open: props.open }">
    <div class="mask" @click="emit('close')" />
    <aside class="drawer">
      <header class="dhead">
        <b>Developer HUD</b>
        <span class="tiny dim">只读观察窗口 · 数字来自索引 / store / facts.ts；算不出来就写「—」，不猜</span>
        <span class="spacer" />
        <span class="chip mono" :class="sevCount.BLOCK ? 'bad' : 'dim'">事实 BLOCK {{ sevCount.BLOCK }} · WARN {{ sevCount.WARN }} · INFO {{ sevCount.INFO }}</span>
        <button class="xbtn" @click="emit('close')">✕ 关闭</button>
      </header>

      <div class="body">
        <!-- ───────── 版本 ───────── -->
        <section class="blk">
          <h4>版本</h4>
          <div class="chips">
            <span class="vchip mono">引擎 {{ engineVersion }}</span>
            <span class="vchip mono">游戏数据 {{ gameDataVersion }}</span>
            <span class="vchip mono">GUESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION {{ GUESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION }}</span>
            <span class="vchip mono" title="构建产物实测；不 import 数据包，避免把 621KB 拉进主 chunk">数据包 621 KB（原始 1280 KB）</span>
          </div>
          <p class="note">数据包体积是构建产物的实测值（见事实 F10）；HUD 不加载它 —— 那 621 KB 只在 Worker 里按需取。</p>
        </section>

        <!-- ───────── 数据规模 ───────── -->
        <section class="blk">
          <h4>数据规模</h4>
          <table class="tbl">
            <thead><tr><th>项目</th><th class="r">网页索引实算</th><th>源数据</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              <tr v-for="row in indexRows" :key="row.k">
                <td>{{ row.k }}</td>
                <td class="mono r">{{ row.v }}</td>
                <td class="tiny dim">{{ row.src }}</td>
              </tr>
            </tbody>
          </table>
          <p class="note">「—」= 网页索引里算不出来。源数据的实测值写在事实清单里（F10），不在这个组件里写死。</p>
        </section>

        <!-- ───────── 本次运行 ───────── -->
        <section class="blk">
          <h4>本次运行 <span class="tiny dim">（来自 useSimulationStore，允许为空）</span></h4>
          <div class="grid9">
            <div v-for="cell in runRows" :key="cell.k" class="cell">
              <span class="k">{{ cell.k }}</span>
              <b class="mono" :class="{ bad: (cell as any).bad }">{{ cell.v }}</b>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="rows">
            <div class="line mono tiny">{{ validationLine }}</div>
            <div v-if="sim.running" class="line mono tiny">运行中 · {{ sim.percent }}%</div>
            <div v-if="sim.error" class="line mono tiny bad">错误：{{ sim.error }}</div>
            <div v-if="!r && !sim.validation" class="line tiny dim">本次还没有结果：跑一次模拟或在阵容里改动触发校验后，这里才有数字。</div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <!-- ───────── 策略快照 ───────── -->
        <section class="blk">
          <h4>策略快照 <span class="tiny dim">（5 个红区 · UNKNOWN 用警示色）</span></h4>
          <div class="pols">
            <div v-for="z in strategyRows" :key="z.label" class="pol">
              <span class="k">{{ z.label }}</span>
              <span class="mono tiny ids" :title="z.ids.join(' · ')">{{ z.ids.join(" · ") }}</span>
              <span class="st mono" :class="statusClass(z.status)">{{ z.status ?? "—" }}</span>
              <span class="tiny dim src">{{ z.source }}<template v-if="z.counts"> · UNKNOWN {{ z.counts.UNKNOWN ?? 0 }} / HYPOTHESIS {{ z.counts.HYPOTHESIS ?? 0 }} / VERIFIED {{ z.counts.VERIFIED ?? 0 }}</template></span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <p class="note">有本次运行（或校验）结果时显示的是结果里**冻结**的快照；否则退回 kb/experiments.json 的静态规则并按「最弱状态」聚合 —— 两种来源在行尾标明，不混为一谈。</p>
        </section>

        <!-- ───────── 覆盖度 ───────── -->
        <section class="blk">
          <h4>覆盖度 <span class="tiny dim">（结果契约的口径是**本次对局 10 只**，不是全库）</span></h4>
          <div v-if="cov" class="grid6">
            <div v-for="c in covCells" :key="c.k" class="cell">
              <span class="k">{{ c.k }}</span>
              <b class="mono">{{ c.v }}</b>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div v-else class="line tiny dim">—（尚未运行 / 校验：覆盖度由引擎在校验阶段产出）</div>
          <ul v-if="cov && cov.unsupportedCapabilities.length" class="caps">
            <li v-for="(c, i) in cov.unsupportedCapabilities" :key="i">⚠ {{ c }}</li>
          </ul>
          <div v-else-if="cov" class="line tiny dim">unsupportedCapabilities 为空。</div>
          <div class="line tiny dim">
            kb/soul-index.json 静态支持度：FULL {{ soulSupport.FULL ?? 0 }} · PARTIAL {{ soulSupport.PARTIAL ?? 0 }} · UNSUPPORTED {{ soulSupport.UNSUPPORTED ?? 0 }}
          </div>
        </section>

        <!-- ───────── 实测事实清单 ───────── -->
        <section class="blk">
          <h4>实测事实清单 <span class="tiny dim">（dev/facts.ts · 每条都能指到证据；否定结果也照实写）</span></h4>
          <article v-for="f in FACTS" :key="f.id" class="fact" :class="SEV_CLASS[f.severity]">
            <div class="fhead">
              <span class="sev mono">{{ f.severity }}</span>
              <b class="mono fid">{{ f.id }}</b>
              <span class="ftitle">{{ f.title }}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="fdetail">{{ f.detail }}</div>
            <div class="tiny dim fev">证据：{{ f.evidence }}</div>
          </article>
        </section>

        <!-- ───────── 文件地图 ───────── -->
        <section class="blk">
          <h4>文件地图</h4>
          <table class="tbl">
            <tbody>
              <tr v-for="row in FILE_MAP" :key="row.dir">
                <td class="mono dir">{{ row.dir }}</td>
                <td>{{ row.text }}</td>
              </tr>
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </section>
      </div>
    </aside>
  </div>
</template>
```

### `apps/web/src/components/knowledge/CodexView.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// CodexView.vue — UI-5 知识库页：式神图鉴 / 御魂图鉴 / 术语表
// 数据全部来自 kb/useKb 的索引（首屏加载），详情抽屉复用 knowledge/DetailDrawer.vue。
// 本组件不接触引擎：页面只读知识库索引，不引用任何引擎模块。
import { computed, ref } from "vue";
import {
  SHIKIGAMI, SOULS, GLOSSARY, RARITIES,
  searchShikigami, searchSouls, SOUL_SUPPORT_LABEL,
} from "../../kb/useKb";
import type { ShikigamiIndexEntry, SoulIndexEntry, GlossaryEntry } from "../../kb/useKb";
import DetailDrawer from "./DetailDrawer.vue";
import type { GuessingUnitInput } from "../../contracts/guessing-match";

/** 数据来源与版本（与 adapters 的 GAME_DATA_VERSION 对齐） */
const DATA_VERSION = "GameData 2026-10-01";

type Tab = "shikigami" | "souls" | "glossary";
const tab = ref<Tab>("shikigami");
const TABS: Array<{ id: Tab; label: string; count: number }> = [
  { id: "shikigami", label: "式神图鉴", count: SHIKIGAMI.length },
  { id: "souls", label: "御魂图鉴", count: SOULS.length },
  { id: "glossary", label: "术语表", count: GLOSSARY.length },
];

// ───────────── 式神图鉴 ─────────────
const sq = ref("");
const rarity = ref<string | null>(null);
const list = computed(() => searchShikigami(sq.value, rarity.value));
function toggleRarity(r: string) { rarity.value = rarity.value === r ? null : r; }

const drawerUnit = ref<GuessingUnitInput | null>(null);
const drawerTab = ref<"skills" | "ai" | "soul">("skills");

/**
 * 图鉴卡片 → 抽屉输入（抽屉只需要 GuessingUnitInput 形状）。
 * stats 直接取该式神的六星觉醒面板 pn = [攻,生,防,速,暴击%,爆伤%,命中%,抵抗%]；
 * 源数据缺失的维度填 0（不猜、不插值），unitId 用稳定字符串仅作 UI 定位。
 */
function unitOf(x: ShikigamiIndexEntry): GuessingUnitInput {
  const pn = x.pn ?? [];
  const at = (i: number) => pn[i] ?? 0;
  return {
    unitId: "codex-" + x.i,
    shikigamiId: x.i,
    name: x.n,
    stats: {
      attack: at(0), hp: at(1), defense: at(2), speed: at(3),
      critRate: at(4), critDamage: at(5), effectHit: at(6), effectResist: at(7),
    },
    soulId: null,
  };
}
function open(x: ShikigamiIndexEntry) { drawerUnit.value = unitOf(x); drawerTab.value = "skills"; }

const rarityOf = (x: ShikigamiIndexEntry) => x.r || "?";
const panelMissing = computed(() => list.value.filter(x => !x.has.panel).length);

// ───────────── 御魂图鉴 ─────────────
const oq = ref("");
const soulList = computed(() => searchSouls(oq.value));
const SUPPORT_COLOR: Record<string, string> = {
  FULL: "var(--ok)", PARTIAL: "var(--warn)", UNSUPPORTED: "var(--danger)",
};
const supportCount = computed(() => soulList.value.reduce((a: Record<string, number>, s) => {
  a[s.engine.support] = (a[s.engine.support] ?? 0) + 1; return a;
}, {}));
const soulDetail = ref<SoulIndexEntry | null>(null);

// ───────────── 术语表 ─────────────
const gq = ref("");
const gList = computed<GlossaryEntry[]>(() => {
  const s = gq.value.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!s) return GLOSSARY;
  return GLOSSARY.filter(g => g.name.toLowerCase().includes(s) || g.text.toLowerCase().includes(s) || String(g.id) === s);
});
</script>

<template>
  <div class="codex">
    <!-- 页面顶部固定说明：数据来源与版本 -->
    <div class="sourcebar">
      <span class="mono k">数据来源</span>
      <span>知识库索引 <b class="mono">kb/shikigami-index.json</b> · <b class="mono">kb/soul-index.json</b> · <b class="mono">kb/glossary.json</b></span>
      <span class="spacer" />
      <span class="mono ver">{{ DATA_VERSION }}</span>
      <span class="chip mono">式神 {{ SHIKIGAMI.length }}</span>
      <span class="chip mono">御魂 {{ SOULS.length }}</span>
      <span class="chip mono">术语 {{ GLOSSARY.length }}</span>
    </div>

    <nav class="tabs">
      <button v-for="t in TABS" :key="t.id" :class="{ on: tab === t.id }" @click="tab = t.id">
        {{ t.label }}<span class="cnt mono">{{ t.count }}</span>
      </button>
    </nav>

    <!-- ───────── 式神图鉴 ───────── -->
    <section v-if="tab === 'shikigami'">
      <div class="filters">
        <input class="search" v-model="sq" placeholder="🔍 搜索式神（名称 / 别名 / 全拼 / 首字母 / ID）" />
        <div class="rarities">
          <button v-for="r in RARITIES" :key="r" class="rar" :class="{ on: rarity === r }" @click="toggleRarity(r)">{{ r }}</button>
          <button class="rar" :class="{ on: rarity === null }" @click="rarity = null">全部</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="hint">
        显示 <b class="mono">{{ list.length }}</b> / {{ SHIKIGAMI.length }} 只（单次最多 60，输入关键词或选稀有度缩小范围）
        <span v-if="panelMissing" class="warn">· {{ panelMissing }} 只缺面板</span>
      </div>
      <div class="grid shiki">
        <button v-for="x in list" :key="x.i" class="card" @click="open(x)">
          <span class="row1">
            <span class="nm">{{ x.n }}</span>
            <span class="rarity" :class="'r-' + rarityOf(x)">{{ rarityOf(x) }}</span>
          </span>
          <span class="row2 mono">
            <span>ID {{ x.i }}</span>
            <span v-if="x.y">· {{ x.y }} 年</span>
          </span>
          <span v-if="x.t.length" class="tags"><i v-for="t in x.t" :key="t">{{ t }}</i></span>
          <span class="row3">
            <span class="badge mono" title="技能条数">技能 {{ x.has.skills }}</span>
            <span class="badge mono" :class="x.has.ai ? 'ok' : 'off'" :title="x.has.ai ? '有 AI 释放逻辑' : 'AI 数据缺失（源数据缺口）'">AI {{ x.has.ai ? "✓" : "—" }}</span>
            <span class="badge mono" :class="x.has.panel ? 'ok' : 'off'" :title="x.has.panel ? '有六星觉醒面板' : '面板缺失（源数据缺口）'">面板 {{ x.has.panel ? "✓" : "—" }}</span>
          </span>
        </button>
        <div v-if="!list.length" class="empty">没有匹配的式神</div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ───────── 御魂图鉴 ───────── -->
    <section v-else-if="tab === 'souls'">
      <div class="notice">
        ⚠ 竞猜模式下御魂静态属性不参与计算（面板已是截图终值），只启用 4 件套战斗效果。
      </div>
      <div class="filters">
        <input class="search" v-model="oq" placeholder="🔍 搜索御魂（名称 / 类型 / 2 件套）" />
        <div class="legend">
          <span><i class="dot" style="background: var(--ok)" />完整实现 {{ supportCount.FULL ?? 0 }}</span>
          <span><i class="dot" style="background: var(--warn)" />部分实现 {{ supportCount.PARTIAL ?? 0 }}</span>
          <span><i class="dot" style="background: var(--danger)" />未实现 {{ supportCount.UNSUPPORTED ?? 0 }}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="grid souls">
        <button v-for="s in soulList" :key="s.i" class="card soul" @click="soulDetail = soulDetail && soulDetail.i === s.i ? null : s">
          <span class="row1">
            <i class="dot" :style="{ background: SUPPORT_COLOR[s.engine.support] }" />
            <span class="nm">{{ s.n }}</span>
            <span class="spacer" />
            <span class="mono sup" :style="{ color: SUPPORT_COLOR[s.engine.support] }">
              {{ s.engine.support }} · {{ SOUL_SUPPORT_LABEL[s.engine.support] }}
            </span>
          </span>
          <span class="kv"><span class="k">2 件套</span><span>{{ s.suit || "—" }}</span></span>
          <span class="kv"><span class="k">4 件套</span><span class="desc">{{ s.desc || "—" }}</span></span>
          <span class="row2 mono dim">
            <span>ID {{ s.i }}</span><span v-if="s.tag">· {{ s.tag }}</span>
            <span>· trigger {{ s.engine.trigger ?? "未识别" }}</span>
            <span>· effect {{ s.engine.effectKind }}</span>
          </span>
          <template v-if="soulDetail && soulDetail.i === s.i">
            <span class="engine">
              <span class="mono small dim">参数 magnitudes = {{ s.engine.magnitudes.join(", ") || "—" }}</span>
              <span class="small dim">{{ s.engine.note || "（引擎未给出说明）" }}</span>
            </span>
            <span v-if="s.engine.support !== 'FULL'" class="warnbox">⚠ 该御魂战斗效果未完整实现，模拟会低估其影响。</span>
          </template>
        </button>
        <div v-if="!soulList.length" class="empty">没有匹配的御魂</div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ───────── 术语表 ───────── -->
    <section v-else>
      <div class="filters">
        <input class="search" v-model="gq" placeholder="🔍 搜索术语（名称 / 正文 / ID）" />
        <span class="hint mono">{{ gList.length }} / {{ GLOSSARY.length }} 条</span>
      </div>
      <div class="glossary">
        <article v-for="g in gList" :key="g.id" class="term">
          <div class="thead"><span class="nm">{{ g.name }}</span><span class="mono dim tiny">#{{ g.id }}</span></div>
          <div class="text">{{ g.text }}</div>
        </article>
        <div v-if="!gList.length" class="empty">没有匹配的术语</div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- 复用已实现的详情抽屉：只传一个 GuessingUnitInput 形状的对象 -->
    <DetailDrawer :open="!!drawerUnit" :unit="drawerUnit" :tab="drawerTab"
      @close="drawerUnit = null" @tab="(t: 'skills' | 'ai' | 'soul') => drawerTab = t" />
  </div>
</template>
```

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// components/knowledge/CompiledBadge.vue — ★★★ 第 138 轮：**一格逐技能编译状态**（a1-4 §33）
//
// 抽成独立组件有两个理由：
//   ① 抽屉与面板要显示**同一件事**（档位 + 未解析句数 + 引擎跳过条数 + 未建模机制），
//      两处各写一遍一定会慢慢写歪；
//   ② 它只吃一个 sync props ⇒ 可以**真的渲染出来**再断言（见 tests/ui16-ssr-render.ts，
//      用 vue/server-renderer 在 Node 里渲染，不需要 jsdom）。
import { SKILL_LABEL, SKILL_MARK } from "../../contracts/fidelity-view.ts";
import type { SkillFidelityView } from "../../contracts/fidelity-view.ts";

defineProps<{ status: SkillFidelityView | null }>();
</script>

<template>
  <span class="badge" :class="status ? status.klass : 'UNKNOWN'">
    <template v-if="status">
      <span class="mark">{{ SKILL_MARK[status.klass] }}</span>
      <span class="clabel">{{ SKILL_LABEL[status.klass] }}</span>
      <span v-if="status.unparsed" class="tiny">未解析 {{ status.unparsed }} 句</span>
      <span v-if="status.skipped" class="tiny">引擎跳过 {{ status.skipped }} 条</span>
      <span v-if="status.unmodeled.length" class="tiny">未建模：{{ status.unmodeled.join("、") }}</span>
    </template>
    <span v-else class="tiny dim">本次校验没有这个技能的编译记录（名称对不上或未评估）</span>
  </span>
</template>
```

### `apps/web/src/components/knowledge/DetailDrawer.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// 式神详情抽屉：Raw Data 与 Compiled Behavior 并排（文档第十七节的核心要求）
import { computed, ref, watch } from "vue";
import { loadDetail, shikigamiById, soulById, SOUL_SUPPORT_LABEL } from "../../kb/useKb";
import { resolveRefs, collectSkillRefs } from "../../kb/resolve-refs.ts";
import bundle from "../../engine/bundle.json";
import type { GuessingUnitInput } from "../../contracts/guessing-match";
// ★★★ 第 138 轮：这个文件的头注释一直写着「Raw Data 与 Compiled Behavior 并排」，
//   但模板里只有 Raw 侧 —— 编译产物到不了这里（Fidelity 载荷里当时也没有逐技能那一层）。
//   现在补上：逐技能的档位、未解析句数、引擎跳过条数、未建模机制，与原文逐条并排。
import { skillTally } from "../../contracts/fidelity-view.ts";
import type { SkillFidelityView } from "../../contracts/fidelity-view.ts";
import CompiledBadge from "./CompiledBadge.vue";

const props = defineProps<{
  open: boolean; unit: GuessingUnitInput | null; tab: "skills" | "ai" | "soul";
  /** 该单位的逐技能编译状态（无载荷时为空数组 —— 界面如实写「尚未评估」） */
  skillStatus?: SkillFidelityView[];
}>();
const emit = defineEmits<{ (e: "close"): void; (e: "tab", v: "skills" | "ai" | "soul"): void }>();
const active = computed({ get: () => props.tab, set: v => emit("tab", v) });
const detail = ref<any>(null);
const loading = ref(false);
const err = ref<string | null>(null);
const meta = computed(() => (props.unit?.shikigamiId ? shikigamiById(props.unit.shikigamiId) : null));
const soul = computed(() => soulById(props.unit?.soulId ?? null));
/** 逐技能编译状态：按技能名索引（Raw 侧一项对一项；对不上的如实留空） */
const compiled = computed(() => new Map((props.skillStatus ?? []).map(s => [s.name, s])));
/**
 * Raw 与 Compiled 的**并排**行：按技能名配对（引擎侧 skills[] 与知识库 skills[] 同名）。
 * ★ 配不上时 compiled=null，界面如实写「没有编译记录」—— 不许拿相邻技能顶上。
 */
const skillRows = computed<Array<{ raw: any; compiled: SkillFidelityView | null }>>(() =>
  (detail.value?.skills ?? []).map((k: any) => ({ raw: k, compiled: compiled.value.get(k.name) ?? null })));
/**
 * ★★★ 第 256 轮（用户要求：「要与阴阳师里每个技能右边附加的小框，一致，完全一致」）：
 *   左边 = 技能正文（占位符换成游戏里的名字），右边 = 这条技能引用到的**印记 / 术语**连同游戏原文。
 *   数据来自同一份 bundle（tips 122 条 + buffs 338 条）—— 也就是**游戏自己的说明文字**，不是我们重写的。
 *   规则（按用户给的四张式神录截图反推）：印记在前、术语在后，各自按**首次出现顺序**，且**传递一层**
 *   （新蕊的正文里提到「盛放」，小框里也要有 —— 见 kb/resolve-refs.ts 的注）。
 */
const refTables = computed(() => ({ tips: (bundle as any).tips ?? [], buffs: (bundle as any).buffs ?? [] }));
const skillViews = computed(() => skillRows.value.map((r: any) => {
  const texts = [r.raw.description, ...((r.raw.upgrades ?? []).map((u: any) => u.effect))];
  const box = collectSkillRefs(texts, refTables.value);
  return {
    row: r,
    brief: r.raw.brief,
    desc: resolveRefs(String(r.raw.description ?? ""), refTables.value).parts,
    ups: (r.raw.upgrades ?? []).map((u: any) => ({ level: u.level, parts: resolveRefs(String(u.effect ?? ""), refTables.value).parts })),
    box: box.buffs.concat(box.tips).map((ref: any) => ({ ...ref, parts: resolveRefs(String(ref.text ?? ""), refTables.value).parts })),
  };
}));
const compiledTally = computed(() => (props.skillStatus ?? []).length ? skillTally(props.skillStatus ?? []) : "");

watch(() => [props.open, props.unit?.shikigamiId], async () => {
  if (!props.open || !props.unit?.shikigamiId) return;
  loading.value = true; err.value = null;
  try { detail.value = (await loadDetail())[String(props.unit.shikigamiId)] ?? null; }
  catch (e: any) { err.value = String(e?.message ?? e); }
  finally { loading.value = false; }
}, { immediate: true });
</script>

<template>
  <div v-if="open" class="overlay" @click.self="emit('close')">
    <aside class="drawer">
      <header>
        <span class="name">{{ unit?.name ?? "—" }}</span>
        <span v-if="meta" class="rar mono">{{ meta.r }}</span>
        <span class="spacer" />
        <button @click="emit('close')">✕</button>
      </header>
      <nav>
        <button :class="{ on: active === 'skills' }" @click="active = 'skills'">技能</button>
        <button :class="{ on: active === 'ai' }" @click="active = 'ai'">AI</button>
        <button :class="{ on: active === 'soul' }" @click="active = 'soul'">御魂</button>
      </nav>
      <div class="body">
        <div v-if="loading" class="dim">加载中…</div>
        <div v-else-if="err" class="err">✗ {{ err }}</div>
        <template v-else-if="active === 'skills'">
          <div v-if="detail?.panel" class="panelbox">
            <div class="sub">六星觉醒面板（知识库）</div>
            <div class="mono small">攻 {{ detail.panel.atk }} · 生 {{ detail.panel.hp }} · 防 {{ detail.panel.def }} · 速 {{ detail.panel.spd }}
              · 暴 {{ (detail.panel.cri * 100).toFixed(0) }}% · 爆 {{ (detail.panel.crid * 100).toFixed(0) }}%</div>
          </div>
          <!-- ★ 第 138 轮（§33）：Raw 侧之前先说清"编译侧有没有信息"，避免读者以为下面每一行都已被引擎实现 -->
          <div class="compilehead">
            <span class="sub" style="margin:0">Compiled Behavior（引擎侧逐技能）</span>
            <span v-if="compiledTally" class="mono small">{{ compiledTally }}</span>
            <span v-else class="dim small">尚未评估（跑一次校验后出现）</span>
          </div>
          <!-- ★★★ 第 256 轮：左边 = 技能正文（占位符换成游戏里的名字），右边 = **与式神录那个小框一致**的印记/术语说明 -->
          <article v-for="v in skillViews" :key="v.row.raw.id" class="skill">
            <div class="skbody">
              <div class="skhead"><b>{{ v.row.raw.name }}</b><span class="mono dim">鬼火 {{ v.row.raw.cost }}</span>
                <span class="tags"><i v-for="t in v.row.raw.tags" :key="t">{{ t }}</i></span></div>
              <div class="brief dim">{{ v.brief }}</div>
              <div class="desc"><template v-for="(p, i) in v.desc" :key="i"><span v-if="p.kind === 'ref'" class="kw" :title="p.text">{{ p.name }}</span><template v-else>{{ p.text }}</template></template></div>
              <div v-if="v.ups.length" class="ups">
                <div v-for="u in v.ups" :key="u.level" class="mono small">Lv.{{ u.level }}
                  <template v-for="(p, i) in u.parts" :key="i"><span v-if="p.kind === 'ref'" class="kw" :title="p.text">{{ p.name }}</span><template v-else>{{ p.text }}</template></template>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="compiled"><CompiledBadge :status="v.row.compiled" /></div>
            </div>
            <aside v-if="v.box.length" class="refbox">
              <div v-for="ref in v.box" :key="ref.source + ref.id" class="refitem">
                <div class="refname">{{ ref.name }}</div>
                <div class="reftext"><template v-for="(p, i) in ref.parts" :key="i"><span v-if="p.kind === 'ref'" class="kw">{{ p.name }}</span><template v-else>{{ p.text }}</template></template></div>
              </div>
            </aside>
          </article>
        </template>
        <template v-else-if="active === 'ai'">
          <div v-if="!detail?.ai" class="dim">该式神暂无 AI 数据</div>
          <template v-else>
            <div class="sub">技能选择（按优先级）</div>
            <div v-for="(c, i) in detail.ai.cast" :key="i" class="rule">
              <span class="prio mono">{{ detail.ai.cast.length - i }}</span>
              <span class="cond">{{ c.cond || "无条件" }}</span>
              <span class="arrow">→</span>
              <span class="skillname">{{ c.skill }}</span>
            </div>
            <template v-if="detail.ai.target?.length">
              <div class="sub" style="margin-top:10px">目标选择</div>
              <div v-for="(t, i) in detail.ai.target" :key="i" class="rule">
                <span class="skillname">{{ t.skill }}</span><span class="arrow">→</span><span class="cond">{{ t.rule }}</span>
              </div>
            </template>
            <div v-if="detail.ai.note" class="note">{{ detail.ai.note }}</div>
          </template>
        </template>
        <template v-else>
          <div v-if="!soul" class="dim">未选择御魂</div>
          <template v-else>
            <h4>{{ soul.n }}</h4>
            <div class="sub">2 件套</div><div class="desc">{{ soul.suit }}</div>
            <div class="sub" style="margin-top:8px">4 件套</div><div class="desc">{{ soul.desc }}</div>
            <div class="sub" style="margin-top:8px">Engine Support</div>
            <div class="desc">
              {{ soul.engine.support }} · {{ SOUL_SUPPORT_LABEL[soul.engine.support] }}<br />
              <span class="mono small dim">trigger={{ soul.engine.trigger ?? "未识别" }} effect={{ soul.engine.effectKind }}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="okbox">✓ 战斗效果启用 · 静态面板不重复计算</div>
          </template>
        </template>
      </div>
    </aside>
  </div>
</template>
```

### `apps/web/src/components/lineup/ShikigamiSelector.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// 式神选择器：276 只完整图鉴，支持 正式名 / 别名 / 拼音 / 首字母 / ID
import { computed, ref, watch } from "vue";
import { searchShikigami, recentShikigami, RARITIES, pushRecent } from "../../kb/useKb";
import type { ShikigamiIndexEntry } from "../../kb/useKb";

const props = defineProps<{ open: boolean }>();
const emit = defineEmits<{ (e: "pick", v: ShikigamiIndexEntry): void; (e: "close"): void }>();

const q = ref("");
const rarity = ref<string | null>(null);
const results = computed(() => searchShikigami(q.value, rarity.value));
const recent = computed(() => recentShikigami());

watch(() => props.open, (v) => { if (v) { q.value = ""; rarity.value = null; } });

function pick(x: ShikigamiIndexEntry) { pushRecent(x.i); emit("pick", x); }
const RCOLOR: Record<string, string> = { SP: "#c96bd6", SSR: "#e0a34f", SR: "#7fa8e0", R: "#6fb98a", N: "#8b93a5", UR: "#e0654f" };
</script>

<template>
  <div v-if="open" class="overlay" @click.self="emit('close')">
    <div class="dialog">
      <header>
        <input class="search" v-model="q" placeholder="🔍 搜索式神（名称 / 别名 / 拼音 / 首字母 / ID）" autofocus />
        <button @click="emit('close')">✕</button>
      </header>

      <div v-if="!q && recent.length" class="section">
        <div class="label">最近使用</div>
        <div class="recent">
          <button v-for="x in recent" :key="x.i" class="chip" @click="pick(x)">{{ x.n }}</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="rarities">
        <button :class="{ on: rarity === null }" @click="rarity = null">全部</button>
        <button v-for="r in RARITIES" :key="r" :class="{ on: rarity === r }" @click="rarity = r">{{ r }}</button>
      </div>

      <div class="grid">
        <button v-for="x in results" :key="x.i" class="item" @click="pick(x)">
          <span class="r" :style="{ color: RCOLOR[x.r] ?? '#999', borderColor: RCOLOR[x.r] ?? '#999' }">{{ x.r }}</span>
          <span class="nm">{{ x.n }}</span>
          <span v-if="x.k" class="alias">{{ x.k }}</span>
          <span class="meta mono">#{{ x.i }}<template v-if="x.y"> · {{ x.y }}</template></span>
        </button>
        <div v-if="!results.length" class="empty">没有匹配的式神</div>
      </div>
      <footer class="hint">共 {{ results.length }} 条<template v-if="results.length >= 60">（显示前 60，继续输入可缩小）</template></footer>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>
```

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// 御魂选择器：70 条图标网格 + Engine Support 前置告知（UX 护栏 #5）
import { computed, ref, watch } from "vue";
import { searchSouls, SOUL_SUPPORT_LABEL } from "../../kb/useKb";
import type { SoulIndexEntry } from "../../kb/useKb";

const props = defineProps<{ open: boolean }>();
const emit = defineEmits<{ (e: "pick", v: SoulIndexEntry | null): void; (e: "close"): void }>();

const q = ref("");
const hover = ref<SoulIndexEntry | null>(null);
const list = computed(() => searchSouls(q.value));
watch(() => props.open, v => { if (v) { q.value = ""; hover.value = null; } });

const SUPPORT_COLOR: Record<string, string> = { FULL: "var(--ok)", PARTIAL: "var(--warn)", UNSUPPORTED: "var(--danger)" };
</script>

<template>
  <div v-if="open" class="overlay" @click.self="emit('close')">
    <div class="dialog">
      <header>
        <input class="search" v-model="q" placeholder="🔍 搜索御魂（名称 / 类型 / 2件套）" autofocus />

      <div class="body">
        <div class="grid">
          <button v-for="s in list" :key="s.i" class="cell" :class="{ on: hover?.i === s.i }"
                  @mouseenter="hover = s" @click="emit('pick', s)">

            <div class="block"><span class="k">4 件套</span><span class="desc">{{ hover.desc || "—" }}</span></div>
            <div class="block">
              <span class="k">竞猜模式</span>
              <span class="ok">✓ 战斗效果启用</span>
              <span class="dim">— 静态面板不重复计算</span>
            </div>
            <div class="block">
              <span class="k">Engine Support</span>
              <span :style="{ color: SUPPORT_COLOR[hover.engine.support] }">
                {{ hover.engine.support }} · {{ SOUL_SUPPORT_LABEL[hover.engine.support] }}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div class="mono small dim">
              trigger: {{ hover.engine.trigger ?? "未识别" }}<br />
              effect: {{ hover.engine.effectKind }}<br />

              ⚠ 该御魂的战斗效果未完整实现，模拟结果会低估其影响。
            </div>
          </template>
          <div v-else class="dim small">悬停查看御魂详情与引擎实现状态</div>
        </aside>
      </div>
      <footer class="legend">
        <span><i class="dot" style="background: var(--ok)" />完整实现</span>
        <span><i class="dot" style="background: var(--warn)" />部分实现</span>
        <span><i class="dot" style="background: var(--danger)" />未实现</span>
        <span class="dim">共 {{ list.length }} 条</span>
      </footer>

  </div>
</template>
```
