### `apps/web/src/components/lineup/UnitTile.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// components/lineup/UnitTile.vue — UI 2.0 的式神格子（a1-4 §31/§32/§33）
// §31：式神**横排**，比左右长列更像游戏实际阵容，也更省纵向空间。
// §32：默认只显示八维数字，点「编辑」才展开输入框 —— 否则页面像 Excel。
// §33：格子上直接带数据可用性圆点（技能 / AI / 面板 / 御魂实现度）。
import { computed, ref } from "vue";
import { PERCENT_STAT_KEYS, STAT_KEYS, STAT_LABELS } from "../../contracts/guessing-match";
import type { GuessingUnitInput, StatKey } from "../../contracts/guessing-match";
import { shikigamiById, soulById } from "../../kb/useKb";
// ★★★ 第 138 轮（§33）：卡面上的**逐技能机制实现度**。数据来自 Per-Match Fidelity 的 skills[]；
//   记号与标签读 contracts/fidelity-view.ts（唯一声明处，与内核逐字节一致由 ui10 断言）。
import { SKILL_LABEL, SKILL_MARK, skillTally, worstSkillClass } from "../../contracts/fidelity-view.ts";
import type { SkillFidelityView } from "../../contracts/fidelity-view.ts";

const props = defineProps<{
  unit: GuessingUnitInput; side: "red" | "blue"; index: number; active?: boolean;
  /** 该单位的逐技能编译状态（Fidelity 载荷里按 side + 名字取；缺省 = 还没跑校验） */
  skillStatus?: SkillFidelityView[];
}>();
const emit = defineEmits<{
  (e: "open-selector"): void; (e: "open-soul"): void; (e: "clear"): void;
  (e: "set-stat", key: StatKey, value: number): void;
  (e: "open-detail", tab: "skills" | "ai" | "soul"): void;
  (e: "activate"): void;
}>();
const editing = ref(false);
const meta = computed(() => (props.unit.shikigamiId ? shikigamiById(props.unit.shikigamiId) : null));
const soul = computed(() => soulById(props.unit.soulId));
const empty = computed(() => !props.unit.shikigamiId);
const s = computed(() => props.unit.stats);

/** §33：数据可用性圆点 —— 说的是"数据在不在"，不是"真实性多少" */
const dots = computed(() => {
  const m = meta.value;
  const out: Array<{ key: string; tone: "ok" | "warn" | "bad" | "idle"; title: string }> = [];
  if (!m) return out;
  out.push({ key: "面板", tone: m.has.panel ? "ok" : "bad", title: m.has.panel ? "六星面板数据存在" : "缺面板数据" });
  /**
   * ★★★ 第 138 轮：这一格以前只说「技能数据 N 条」= **数据在不在**；
   *   现在它说**编译到哪一档**（● 完整 / ◐ 部分 / ○ 被动 / ✕ 无数值）—— §33 要的正是后者。
   *   两向：没有 Fidelity 载荷时退回原来的"数据在不在"判据（不能因为拿不到载荷就不显示）。
   */
  const st = props.skillStatus ?? [];
  const worst = worstSkillClass(st);
  if (st.length) {
    out.push({
      key: "技能编译", tone: worst === "FULL" ? "ok" : worst === "NONE" ? "bad" : "warn",
      title: st.map(s => SKILL_MARK[s.klass] + " " + s.name + "：" + SKILL_LABEL[s.klass]).join("\n"),
    });
  } else {
    out.push({ key: "技能", tone: (m.has.skills ?? 0) >= 3 ? "ok" : (m.has.skills ? "warn" : "bad"), title: `技能数据 ${m.has.skills ?? 0} 条（尚未评估编译状态）` });
  }
  out.push({ key: "AI", tone: m.has.ai ? "ok" : "warn", title: m.has.ai ? "AI 规则存在" : "无 AI 规则（2026 新式神待补）" });
  if (soul.value) {
    const sup = soul.value.engine.support;
    out.push({ key: "御魂", tone: sup === "FULL" ? "ok" : sup === "PARTIAL" ? "warn" : "bad", title: `御魂战斗效果实现度：${sup}` });
  }
  return out;
});
/** 逐技能记数串（形如 ●3 ◐1）+ 悬停逐条清单；没有载荷时是空串（模板里 v-if 掉） */
const compileTally = computed(() => (props.skillStatus ?? []).length ? skillTally(props.skillStatus ?? []) : "");
const worstTone = computed(() => {
  const w = worstSkillClass(props.skillStatus ?? []);
  return w === "FULL" ? "ok" : w === "NONE" ? "bad" : w ? "warn" : "";
});
const compileTitle = computed(() => (props.skillStatus ?? [])
  .map(s => SKILL_MARK[s.klass] + " " + s.name + "：" + SKILL_LABEL[s.klass]).join("\n"));
function set(k: StatKey, v: string) { emit("set-stat", k, Number(v)); }
const fmt = (k: StatKey) => (PERCENT_STAT_KEYS.includes(k) ? s.value[k] + "%" : String(s.value[k]));
</script>

<template>
  <div class="tile" :class="[side, { empty, active, editing }]">
    <!-- 收起态：身份 + 八维数字 + 数据圆点 -->
    <button class="head" @click="empty ? emit('open-selector') : (emit('activate'), editing = !editing)">
      <span class="idx mono">{{ index + 1 }}</span>
      <span v-if="empty" class="ph">选择式神</span>
      <template v-else>
        <span class="nm">{{ unit.name }}</span>
        <span v-if="meta" class="rar mono">{{ meta.r }}</span>
      </template>
    </button>

    <div v-if="!empty" class="nums mono">
      <span v-for="k in STAT_KEYS" :key="k" :title="STAT_LABELS[k]"><i>{{ STAT_LABELS[k][0] }}</i>{{ fmt(k) }}</span>
    </div>

    <div v-if="!empty" class="foot">
      <button class="soulchip" :class="{ none: !soul }" @click.stop="emit('open-soul')">
        {{ soul ? soul.n : "无御魂" }}
      </button>
      <span class="dots">
        <i v-for="d in dots" :key="d.key" class="dot" :class="d.tone" :title="d.key + '：' + d.title" />
      </span>
      <!-- ★ 第 138 轮（§33）：逐技能机制实现度的**记数串**，鼠标悬停看逐条 -->
      <span v-if="compileTally" class="tally mono" :class="worstTone" :title="compileTitle">{{ compileTally }}</span>
      <span class="spacer" />
      <button class="mini" title="技能/AI/御魂详情" @click.stop="emit('open-detail', 'skills')">详情</button>
      <button class="mini" title="清空" @click.stop="emit('clear')">×</button>
    </div>

    <!-- 展开态（§32）：只有点了「编辑」才出现输入框 -->
    <div v-if="editing && !empty" class="editor">
      <label v-for="k in STAT_KEYS" :key="k" class="field">
        <span class="lab">{{ STAT_LABELS[k] }}<i v-if="PERCENT_STAT_KEYS.includes(k)">%</i></span>
        <input type="number" :value="s[k]" @input="set(k, ($event.target as HTMLInputElement).value)" />
      </label>
      <div class="editorfoot mono tiny dim">改动即时保存到本地草稿</div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<!-- <style> 已省略 2 KB（纯样式，无逻辑） -->
```

### `apps/web/src/components/mechanics/MechanicsView.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// MechanicsView.vue — UI-5 机制实验台：红区状态 / 机制覆盖度 / 44 个实验 / 假设对比
// UI-6 追加：观测登记（实验表之后的独立区块，只往 localStorage 里记观测，绝不改实验状态）。
// 数据来自 tools/build-experiments-index.mjs 生成的 kb/experiments.json（懒加载，78KB）。
// 本组件不接触引擎：只读索引 JSON + lab/observations.ts，不引用任何引擎模块。
import { computed, onMounted, reactive, ref } from "vue";
import {
  loadAll, listFor, add, remove, exportJson, stats, isPersistent,
  OBSERVATION_SOURCES, OBSERVATION_SOURCE_LABEL, OBSERVATION_CONFIDENCES, OBSERVATION_CONFIDENCE_LABEL,
  OBSERVATION_POLICY_NOTE, OBSERVATIONS_KEY,
} from "../../lab/observations.ts";
import type { ObservationRecord, ObservationStats, ObservationSource, ObservationConfidence } from "../../lab/observations.ts";
import { groupRedZones, TONE_VAR } from "./redZoneGroups.ts";

interface Hypothesis { id: string; note: string; observable: unknown }
interface Experiment {
  id: string; group: string; title: string; hypothesis: string; method: string; result: string;
  verdict: string; verdictLevel: string; discriminating: boolean; redZone: string;
  kind: string | null; status: string | null; confidence: string | null; chosenHypothesis: string | null;
  distinctObservables: number | null; hypotheses: Hypothesis[]; recordedAt: string | null;
}
interface RedZone { zone: string; id: string; status: string; question: string; evidence: string; source: string; rawStatus?: string }
interface Coverage { capability: string; status: string; note: string; tag: string; evidence: string; freq: number | null }
interface ExpIndex {
  meta: { dataVersion: string; gameVersion: string | null; dataRecordedAt: string | null; sources: string[]; policySources: string[] };
  groups: Array<{ group: string; zone: string; total: number; discriminating: number }>;
  experiments: Experiment[];
  redZones: RedZone[];
  redZoneStatusCounts: Record<string, number>;
  verifiedRules: { count: number; ids: string[]; note: string };
  coverage: Coverage[];
  coverageSummary: { total: number; full: number; partial: number; none: number; kernelCoverage: number | null; hardCoverage: number | null; unsupported: string[] };
  anomalies: Array<{ kind: string; msg: string }>;
}

const data = ref<ExpIndex | null>(null);
const loadError = ref<string | null>(null);
onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    const mod: any = await import("../../kb/experiments.json");
    data.value = (mod.default ?? mod) as ExpIndex;
  } catch (e: any) { loadError.value = String(e?.message ?? e); }
  refreshObs();
});

const exps = computed(() => data.value?.experiments ?? []);
const redZones = computed(() => data.value?.redZones ?? []);
const coverage = computed(() => data.value?.coverage ?? []);
const GROUP_ORDER = ["A", "B", "C", "D"];
const GROUP_LABEL: Record<string, string> = { A: "开局结算顺序", B: "同时触发优先级", C: "死亡 / 转化 / 复活", D: "对弈竞猜 Ruleset" };

// ───────── 红区状态 ─────────
// ★★ 第 62 轮：分组逻辑抽到 redZoneGroups.ts（纯函数，可被测试直接断言）。
//   原来它遍历一张写死的 STATUS_ORDER，数据里多一个状态就**静默丢条目**；
//   而且圆点颜色的 else 兜底是绿色 —— 不认识的状态会被画成"已验证"。
//   现在：顺序由清单给，**覆盖由数据决定**，不认识的状态照样成组、用中性色。
const zonesByStatus = computed(() => groupRedZones(redZones.value));
const zoneNames = computed(() => [...new Set(redZones.value.map(z => z.zone))]);
/** 组内实验条数（来自索引的 groups 汇总，不在这里重算） */
const groupTotal = (g: string) => data.value?.groups.find(x => x.group === g)?.total ?? 0;
/** 组内红区名（索引里已按实验定义文件的段注释取好） */
const groupZone = (g: string) => data.value?.groups.find(x => x.group === g)?.zone ?? g;

// ───────── 机制覆盖度 ─────────
const covFilter = ref<string>("ALL");
const COV_ORDER = ["FULL", "PARTIAL", "NONE"];
const COV_COLOR: Record<string, string> = { FULL: "var(--ok)", PARTIAL: "var(--warn)", NONE: "var(--danger)" };
const COV_LABEL: Record<string, string> = { FULL: "完整实现", PARTIAL: "部分实现", NONE: "未实现" };
const covCounts = computed(() => coverage.value.reduce((a: Record<string, number>, c) => {
  a[c.status] = (a[c.status] ?? 0) + 1; return a;
}, {} as Record<string, number>));
const covList = computed(() => (covFilter.value === "ALL" ? coverage.value : coverage.value.filter(c => c.status === covFilter.value)));

// ───────── 实验表 ─────────
const groupFilter = ref<string>("ALL");
const discFilter = ref<string>("ALL");
const expanded = ref<string | null>(null);
const filtered = computed(() => exps.value.filter(e =>
  (groupFilter.value === "ALL" || e.group === groupFilter.value) &&
  (discFilter.value === "ALL" || (discFilter.value === "YES") === e.discriminating)
));
function toggle(id: string) { expanded.value = expanded.value === id ? null : id; }
/** 观测值压成一行展示；数值原样来自索引，不做四舍五入或改写 */
function compact(v: unknown) { return JSON.stringify(v ?? null); }
const discCount = computed(() => exps.value.filter(e => e.discriminating).length);

// ───────── 假设对比：同一红区内结论（verdictLevel）不同 ─────────
const contrastPairs = computed(() => {
  const byZone = new Map<string, Experiment[]>();
  for (const e of exps.value) {
    if (!byZone.has(e.redZone)) byZone.set(e.redZone, []);
    byZone.get(e.redZone)!.push(e);
  }
  const out: Array<{ zone: string; a: Experiment; b: Experiment }> = [];
  for (const [zone, list] of byZone) {
    for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < list.length; j++) {
      if (list[i].verdictLevel !== list[j].verdictLevel) out.push({ zone, a: list[i], b: list[j] });
    }
  }
  return out;
});
const VERDICT_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  VERIFIED: "已验证", HYPOTHESIS: "已选定假设（未实测）", UNKNOWN: "未验证",
};

// ───────── 观测登记（UI-6） ─────────
// ★ 这一块**只记录**：保存一条观测不会改任何实验的 status / confidence / chosenHypothesis，
//   也不会让红区变成 VERIFIED。状态变更必须人工复核后手改实验目录里的 verified-rules.json。
//   页面顶部那句醒目说明的原话（与 lab/observations.ts 的 OBSERVATION_POLICY_NOTE 是同一句，单点维护）：
//   「登记观测只做记录，不会自动把任何规则改成 VERIFIED —— 状态变更必须人工复核后手改 verified-rules.json，
//     避免把「有人填了张表」当成「机制已被验证」。」
const obsList = ref<ObservationRecord[]>([]);
const obsStat = ref<ObservationStats>({ total: 0, bySource: {}, byExperiment: {} });
const obsPersistent = ref(true);
const obsFilter = ref<string>("ALL");
const obsError = ref<string | null>(null);
const obsSaved = ref(false);
const obsCopied = ref(false);
const obsExportText = ref<string | null>(null);
let obsSavedTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

const obsForm = reactive({
  experimentId: "",
  source: "IN_GAME" as ObservationSource,
  claim: "",
  evidenceText: "",
  confidence: "LOW" as ObservationConfidence,
  author: "",
});

function refreshObs() {
  obsList.value = loadAll();
  obsStat.value = stats();
  obsPersistent.value = isPersistent();
}
/** 筛选：全部走内存列表，按实验筛选走仓库 API（listFor 返回时间倒序） */
const obsShown = computed(() => (obsFilter.value === "ALL" ? obsList.value : listFor(obsFilter.value)));

function saveObservation() {
  obsError.value = null;
  if (!obsForm.experimentId) { obsError.value = "请先选择一个实验"; return; }
  if (!obsForm.claim.trim()) { obsError.value = "结论文本不能为空 —— 没有结论的观测无法复核"; return; }
  try {
    add({
      experimentId: obsForm.experimentId,
      source: obsForm.source,
      claim: obsForm.claim,
      evidenceText: obsForm.evidenceText,
      confidence: obsForm.confidence,
      author: obsForm.author,
    });
    refreshObs();
    obsForm.claim = "";
    obsForm.evidenceText = "";
    obsSaved.value = true;
    if (obsSavedTimer) clearTimeout(obsSavedTimer);
    obsSavedTimer = setTimeout(() => (obsSaved.value = false), 4000);
  } catch (e: any) {
    obsError.value = String(e?.message ?? e);
  }
}

function deleteObservation(id: string) {
  remove(id);
  refreshObs();
}

/** 导出：优先写剪贴板；失败（无权限 / 非安全上下文）退化为展开只读 textarea 手动复制 */
async function exportObservations() {
  const json = exportJson();
  obsExportText.value = null;
  try {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(json);
    obsCopied.value = true;
    setTimeout(() => (obsCopied.value = false), 1500);
  } catch {
    obsCopied.value = false;
    obsExportText.value = json;
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="mech">
    <div v-if="loadError" class="err">✗ 实验索引加载失败：{{ loadError }}</div>
    <div v-else-if="!data" class="loading">加载 kb/experiments.json …</div>

    <template v-else>
      <!-- 顶部固定说明 -->
      <div class="sourcebar">
        <span class="mono k">数据来源</span>
        <span>实验汇总 <b class="mono">_all.json</b> · 红区规则状态 <b class="mono">verified-rules.json</b> · 能力覆盖度 <b class="mono">coverage.json</b> · 引擎 Policy 常量</span>
        <span class="spacer" />
        <span class="mono ver">{{ data.meta.dataVersion }}</span>
        <span class="chip mono" :title="data.meta.sources.concat(data.meta.policySources).join(' · ')">
          源文件 {{ data.meta.sources.length + data.meta.policySources.length }} 个
        </span>
        <span class="chip mono">实验 {{ exps.length }}</span>
        <span class="chip mono">红区规则 {{ redZones.length }}</span>
        <span class="chip mono">覆盖度 {{ coverage.length }}</span>
      </div>

      <!-- ───────── 红区状态 ───────── -->
      <section class="panel">
        <header class="phead">
          <h3>红区状态</h3>
          <span class="badge" :class="(data.redZoneStatusCounts.VERIFIED ?? 0) === 0 ? 'warn' : 'ok'">
            VERIFIED {{ data.redZoneStatusCounts.VERIFIED ?? 0 }} / HYPOTHESIS {{ data.redZoneStatusCounts.HYPOTHESIS ?? 0 }} / UNKNOWN {{ data.redZoneStatusCounts.UNKNOWN ?? 0 }}
          </span>
        </header>
        <div class="rule warnbig">
          ⚠ UNKNOWN 机制不会被猜测，只会被显式标注：引擎在 UNKNOWN 策略下产出的任何胜率都不构成机制结论。
        </div>
        <div class="muted">
          红区共 {{ zoneNames.length }} 类：{{ zoneNames.join(" · ") }}。
          「已验证」只统计 <b class="mono">verified-rules.json</b> 的 rules（当前 {{ data.verifiedRules.count }} 条）；
          HYPOTHESIS 即使有官方/规格书来源，也仍是<b>假设</b>，不等于已验证。
        </div>

        <div v-for="g in zonesByStatus" :key="g.status" class="statusgroup">
          <div class="ghead">
            <span class="dot" :style="{ background: TONE_VAR[g.tone] }" />
            <b>{{ g.label }}</b>
            <span class="mono count">{{ g.rules.length }} 条</span>
            <span class="spacer" />
            <span class="dim small">{{ g.help }}</span>
          </div>
          <div v-if="!g.rules.length" class="empty small">（无）</div>
          <div class="rules">
            <article v-for="z in g.rules" :key="z.source + ':' + z.id" class="rule" :class="{ unknown: z.status === 'UNKNOWN' }">
              <div class="rhead">
                <span class="zchip">{{ z.zone }}</span>
                <b class="mono rid">{{ z.id }}</b>
                <span v-if="z.rawStatus" class="mono raw" title="源文件里的原始状态值">源值 {{ z.rawStatus }}</span>
              </div>
              <div class="q">{{ z.question || "（源文件未提供 question 字段）" }}</div>
              <div v-if="z.evidence" class="ev dim small">依据：{{ z.evidence }}</div>
              <div class="mono src dim tiny">{{ z.source }}</div>
            </article>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ───────── 机制覆盖度 ───────── -->
      <section class="panel">
        <header class="phead">
          <h3>机制覆盖度</h3>
          <span class="mono dim small">
            kernel {{ data.coverageSummary.kernelCoverage }}% · hard {{ data.coverageSummary.hardCoverage }}%
            · 共 {{ data.coverageSummary.total }} 项能力
          </span>
        </header>
        <div class="covbar">
          <button v-for="s in ['ALL'].concat(COV_ORDER)" :key="s" class="covbtn" :class="{ on: covFilter === s }" @click="covFilter = s">
            <i v-if="s !== 'ALL'" class="dot" :style="{ background: COV_COLOR[s] }" />
            {{ s === "ALL" ? "全部" : s + " · " + COV_LABEL[s] }}
            <b class="mono">{{ s === "ALL" ? coverage.length : (covCounts[s] ?? 0) }}</b>
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="covlist">
          <article v-for="c in covList" :key="c.capability" class="covitem">
            <div class="chead">
              <i class="dot" :style="{ background: COV_COLOR[c.status] }" />
              <b>{{ c.tag }}</b>
              <span class="mono dim tiny">{{ c.capability }}</span>
              <span class="spacer" />
              <span class="mono tiny" :style="{ color: COV_COLOR[c.status] }">{{ c.status }}</span>
              <span class="mono tiny dim" title="数据里出现的技能/效果引用次数">×{{ c.freq }}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="cnote dim small">{{ c.note || "（源文件未写说明）" }}</div>
            <div class="mono tiny dim">evidence {{ c.evidence }}</div>
          </article>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ───────── 实验表 ───────── -->
      <section class="panel">
        <header class="phead">
          <h3>实验表</h3>
          <span class="mono dim small">共 {{ exps.length }} 条 · 可判别 {{ discCount }} / 不可判别 {{ exps.length - discCount }}</span>
        </header>
        <div class="rule warnbig small">
          这 {{ exps.length }} 条实验全部是 <b>未验证 · 待游戏内观测</b>（confidence = NOT_RUN，chosenHypothesis = null）：
          表里的「结果」是<b>实验台在每种假设下应当观测到的现象</b>，不是游戏内实测结论，不能当机制结论使用。
        </div>
        <div class="filters">
          <button class="fbtn" :class="{ on: groupFilter === 'ALL' }" @click="groupFilter = 'ALL'">全部组</button>
          <button v-for="g in GROUP_ORDER" :key="g" class="fbtn" :class="{ on: groupFilter === g }" @click="groupFilter = g">
            {{ g }} · {{ GROUP_LABEL[g] }}<span class="mono cnt" :title="groupZone(g)">{{ groupTotal(g) }}</span>
          </button>
          <span class="spacer" />
          <button class="fbtn" :class="{ on: discFilter === 'ALL' }" @click="discFilter = 'ALL'">判别力 全部</button>
          <button class="fbtn" :class="{ on: discFilter === 'YES' }" @click="discFilter = 'YES'">可判别</button>
          <button class="fbtn" :class="{ on: discFilter === 'NO' }" @click="discFilter = 'NO'">不可判别</button>
        </div>
        <div class="tablewrap">
          <table class="exptable">
            <thead>
              <tr><th>ID</th><th>组</th><th>实验</th><th>判别力</th><th>结论</th><th /></tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              <template v-for="e in filtered" :key="e.id">
                <tr class="row" :class="{ open: expanded === e.id }" @click="toggle(e.id)">

                  <td><span class="gtag" :class="'g-' + e.group">{{ e.group }}</span></td>
                  <td class="title">{{ e.title }}</td>
                  <td><span class="dchip" :class="e.discriminating ? 'yes' : 'no'">{{ e.discriminating ? "可判别" : "不可判别" }}</span></td>
                  <td><span class="vchip" :class="'v-' + e.verdictLevel">{{ e.verdict }}</span></td>
                  <td class="mono dim">{{ expanded === e.id ? "▾" : "▸" }}</td>
                </tr>
                <tr v-if="expanded === e.id" class="detail">
                  <td colspan="6">
                    <div class="dgrid">
                      <div class="dblock">
                        <div class="dk">假设 <span class="dim tiny">（{{ e.hypotheses.length }} 个候选）</span></div>
                        <ul class="hyp">

                          </li>
                        </ul>

                      </div>
                      <div class="dblock">
                        <div class="dk">结果 <span class="dim tiny">（实验台观测：各假设下应有的判别性现象，尚未游戏内实测）</span></div>
                        <div class="dv">{{ e.result }}</div>

                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ───────── 观测登记（UI-6） ───────── -->
      <section class="panel">
        <header class="phead">
          <h3>观测登记</h3>
          <span class="mono dim small">
            已登记 {{ obsStat.total }} 条 · {{ obsPersistent ? "写入 localStorage（" + OBSERVATIONS_KEY + "）" : "本浏览器不可用持久化：只在本会话有效，刷新后会丢" }}
          </span>
          <span class="spacer" />
          <button class="fbtn" @click="exportObservations">{{ obsCopied ? "已复制 ✓" : "导出全部观测 JSON" }}</button>
        </header>

        <div class="rule warnbig small">{{ OBSERVATION_POLICY_NOTE }}</div>
        <div class="muted small">
          观测是**给人看的原始材料**，不是机制结论：confidence 是登记人对这条观测的把握，
          与实验表里的 <b class="mono">confidence</b>（引擎产出的 NOT_RUN 等状态）不是同一个字段，二者不会互相改写。
        </div>

        <div class="obsstats">
          <span class="chip mono">总数 {{ obsStat.total }}</span>
          <span v-for="s in OBSERVATION_SOURCES" :key="s" class="chip mono">{{ OBSERVATION_SOURCE_LABEL[s] }} {{ obsStat.bySource[s] ?? 0 }}</span>
        </div>

        <div class="obsform">
          <label class="f">
            <span class="k">实验（来自 kb/experiments.json 的 {{ exps.length }} 条）</span>
            <select v-model="obsForm.experimentId">
              <option value="">（选择实验）</option>
              <option v-for="e in exps" :key="e.id" :value="e.id">{{ e.id }} · {{ e.title }}</option>
            </select>
          </label>
          <label class="f">
            <span class="k">来源</span>
            <select v-model="obsForm.source">
              <option v-for="s in OBSERVATION_SOURCES" :key="s" :value="s">{{ OBSERVATION_SOURCE_LABEL[s] }}（{{ s }}）</option>
            </select>
          </label>
          <label class="f">
            <span class="k">置信度（登记人的把握，低 / 中 / 高）</span>
            <select v-model="obsForm.confidence">
              <option v-for="c in OBSERVATION_CONFIDENCES" :key="c" :value="c">{{ c }} · {{ OBSERVATION_CONFIDENCE_LABEL[c] }}</option>
            </select>
          </label>
          <label class="f">
            <span class="k">登记人（可留空）</span>
            <input v-model="obsForm.author" placeholder="署名或匿名" />
          </label>
          <label class="f wide">
            <span class="k">结论 —— 观测到了什么（必填）</span>
            <textarea v-model="obsForm.claim" rows="2" placeholder="例如：开局先机先于御魂结算，三次观测一致"></textarea>
          </label>
          <label class="f wide">
            <span class="k">证据 —— 在哪看到的（录屏时间点 / 帖子链接 / 数据字段；建议填）</span>
            <textarea v-model="obsForm.evidenceText" rows="2" placeholder="复核的人只能靠这一段判断这条观测能不能采信"></textarea>
          </label>
          <div class="f actions">
            <button class="fbtn on" @click="saveObservation">保存观测</button>
            <span v-if="obsError" class="mono tiny bad">{{ obsError }}</span>
            <span v-else-if="obsSaved" class="mono tiny oktext">已登记。实验状态没有被改动，仍以 verified-rules.json 为准。</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="filters">
          <span class="k">按实验筛选</span>
          <select class="obsfilter" v-model="obsFilter">
            <option value="ALL">全部（{{ obsStat.total }}）</option>
            <option v-for="e in exps" :key="e.id" :value="e.id">{{ e.id }} · {{ e.title }}（{{ obsStat.byExperiment[e.id] ?? 0 }}）</option>
          </select>
          <span class="spacer" />
          <span class="mono tiny dim">按观测时间倒序</span>
        </div>

        <div v-if="obsShown.length" class="obslist">
          <article v-for="o in obsShown" :key="o.id" class="obsitem">
            <div class="ohead">
              <b class="mono">{{ o.experimentId }}</b>
              <span class="ochip">{{ OBSERVATION_SOURCE_LABEL[o.source] ?? ("未知来源：" + o.source) }}</span>
              <span class="ochip" :class="'c-' + o.confidence">
                置信度 {{ o.confidence }}{{ OBSERVATION_CONFIDENCE_LABEL[o.confidence] ? " · " + OBSERVATION_CONFIDENCE_LABEL[o.confidence] : "" }}
              </span>
              <span class="mono tiny dim">{{ o.observedAt }}</span>
              <span v-if="o.author" class="tiny dim">@{{ o.author }}</span>
              <span class="spacer" />
              <button class="delbtn" @click="deleteObservation(o.id)">删除</button>
            </div>
            <div class="oclaim">{{ o.claim }}</div>
            <div v-if="o.evidenceText" class="oev dim small">证据：{{ o.evidenceText }}</div>
            <div v-else class="oev dim small">（未填证据 —— 复核时无法采信）</div>
          </article>
        </div>
        <div v-else class="empty small">
          还没有登记观测{{ obsFilter === "ALL" ? "" : "（当前筛选下为空）" }}。
        </div>

        <div v-if="obsExportText !== null" class="obsexport">
          <div class="tiny dim">剪贴板不可用，下面是完整 JSON，请手动复制：</div>
          <textarea class="mono" readonly :value="obsExportText" rows="6"></textarea>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ───────── 假设对比 ───────── -->
      <section class="panel">
        <header class="phead">
          <h3>假设对比</h3>
          <span class="mono dim small">同一红区内「结论相反」的实验对 · 判定口径：verdictLevel 不同</span>
        </header>
        <div v-if="contrastPairs.length" class="pairs">
          <div v-for="(p, i) in contrastPairs" :key="i" class="pair">
            <div class="pzone">{{ p.zone }}</div>
            <div class="cols">
              <article v-for="e in [p.a, p.b]" :key="e.id" class="side">
                <div class="rhead"><b class="mono">{{ e.id }}</b><span class="vchip" :class="'v-' + e.verdictLevel">{{ VERDICT_LABEL[e.verdictLevel] ?? e.verdictLevel }}</span></div>
                <div class="title">{{ e.title }}</div>
                <div class="dim small">{{ e.hypothesis }}</div>
                <div class="mono tiny dim">结论：{{ e.verdict }}</div>
              </article>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div v-else class="empty">
          无相反结论的实验对
          <div class="dim small">
            当前 {{ exps.length }} 条实验的结论状态全部相同（{{ VERDICT_LABEL.UNKNOWN }} ·
            待游戏内观测，confidence = NOT_RUN），没有任何两条给出相反结论 —— 因此这里没有可并排对比的实验。
            等某一红区先被实测确认（verified-rules.json 出现条目）后，同一红区内的已确认/未确认实验会自动成对显示。
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ───────── 数据异常与缺口 ───────── -->
      <section class="panel">
        <header class="phead">
          <h3>数据异常与缺口</h3>
          <span class="mono dim small">由 tools/build-experiments-index.mjs 生成时自检产出 · {{ data.anomalies.length }} 条</span>
        </header>
        <div class="muted small">这些是源数据里的缺口或自相矛盾之处，不是渲染问题；索引里没有 VERIFIED 结论，也没有编造任何置信度数值。</div>
        <ul class="anoms">
          <li v-for="(a, i) in data.anomalies" :key="i"><span class="mono kind">{{ a.kind }}</span> {{ a.msg }}</li>
        </ul>
      </section>
    </template>
  </div>
</template>

<!-- <style> 已省略 8 KB（纯样式，无逻辑） -->
```

### `apps/web/src/components/mechanics/redZoneGroups.ts`

```ts
// components/mechanics/redZoneGroups.ts — 红区状态分组（★ 第 62 轮从 .vue 里抽出来的纯函数）
//
// 抽出来的原因不是"整洁"，是**原来那段代码没法被测试**，而它藏着两个静默失效：
//
//   ① 分组遍历的是一张**写死的清单** STATUS_ORDER，而红区数据来自 kb/experiments.json：
//      `STATUS_ORDER.map(s => ({ rules: redZones.filter(z => z.status === s) }))`
//      —— 数据里一旦出现清单里没有的状态，**那些规则不会被渲染，也不会报错**。
//      今天不漏，是靠巧合：数据里恰好只有 UNKNOWN / HYPOTHESIS 两种，而清单里正好有三种。
//      （G7 开始产出 VERIFIED 之后还会多一种；再往后只要多任何一种，这一页就开始静默少条目。）
//
//   ② 圆点颜色的写法是嵌套三元 + else 兜底：
//      `g.status === 'UNKNOWN' ? danger : g.status === 'HYPOTHESIS' ? warn : ok`
//      —— 兜底是 **ok（绿）**。也就是说，一个界面不认识的状态会被画成"已验证"的样子。
//      静默丢条目已经够糟；把不认识的状态涂成绿色是**反向误导**。
//
// 所以规则重写成两句话：
//   · **哪些状态存在，由数据决定**；清单只管显示顺序，管不了"存在与否"；
//   · 清单里没有的状态必须**照样成组**，用中性语气（idle）与一句明确的说明。
// 这两条由 tests/ui5-knowledge.ts 直接对这个函数断言（可以喂一个假数据进去）。

export interface RedZoneLike {
  zone: string; id: string; status: string;
  question?: string; evidence?: string; source?: string; rawStatus?: string | null;
}

export type RedZoneTone = "danger" | "warn" | "ok" | "idle";

export interface RedZoneGroup {
  status: string;
  label: string;
  help: string;
  tone: RedZoneTone;
  rules: RedZoneLike[];
}

/** 显示顺序偏好。★ 它**不是**"允许出现的状态"白名单 —— 见文件头。 */
export const STATUS_ORDER = ["UNKNOWN", "HYPOTHESIS", "VERIFIED"];

export const STATUS_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  UNKNOWN: "UNKNOWN · 未知（禁止假设）",
  HYPOTHESIS: "HYPOTHESIS · 有依据的假设（未实测）",
  VERIFIED: "VERIFIED · 已实测确认",
};

export const STATUS_HELP: Record<string, string> = {
  UNKNOWN: "没有任何依据，引擎只能占位。这类机制不会被猜测，只会被显式标注。",
  HYPOTHESIS: "有来源（官方 tooltip / 规格书 / 社区实现），但没有游戏内实测确认 —— 仍是假设。",
  VERIFIED: "已由游戏内观测确认，可以当作机制结论使用。",
};

/** 语气 → 颜色变量。idle 是中性色：不认识的状态**不许**被涂成 ok。 */
export const TONE_VAR: Record<RedZoneTone, string> = {
  danger: "var(--danger)", warn: "var(--warn)", ok: "var(--ok)", idle: "var(--fg-dim)",
};

const TONE: Record<string, RedZoneTone> = { UNKNOWN: "danger", HYPOTHESIS: "warn", VERIFIED: "ok" };

/**
 * 按状态分组。**先按清单排已知的状态，再把数据里其余的接在后面** ——
 * 顺序是偏好，覆盖是义务：任何一条红区都必须落在某个组里。
 */
export function groupRedZones(zones: RedZoneLike[]): RedZoneGroup[] {
  const present = [...new Set(zones.map(z => z.status))];
  const known = STATUS_ORDER.filter(s => present.includes(s));
  const rest = present.filter(s => !STATUS_ORDER.includes(s)).sort();
  return [...known, ...rest].map(status => ({
    status,
    label: STATUS_LABEL[status] ?? status + "（界面未适配这个状态）",
    help: STATUS_HELP[status] ?? "引擎里出现了界面清单里没有的状态。它**没有被丢掉**，但界面还没有它的说明 —— 请补 STATUS_LABEL / STATUS_HELP。",
    tone: TONE[status] ?? "idle",
    rules: zones.filter(z => z.status === status),
  }));
}
```

### `apps/web/src/components/result/ReplayViewer.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// components/result/ReplayViewer.vue — UI-4 逐步回放 + 事件检查器
// 事件流由 Worker 用**相同的 seed** 重跑得到，因此这就是刚才那一场战斗本身。
// 生命值快照由 DAMAGE / HP_LOSS / DEATH / REVIVE 事件累加还原（不依赖引擎额外记录）。
import { computed, ref, watch } from "vue";
import type { ReplayEvent, ReplayUnit } from "../../contracts/worker-api.ts";

const props = defineProps<{
  seed: number;
  events: ReplayEvent[];
  units: ReplayUnit[];
  turns: number;
  winner: "red" | "blue" | "draw" | "timeout";
}>();
const emit = defineEmits<{ (e: "close"): void }>();

const idx = ref(0);
const playing = ref(false);
const speed = ref(1);
const showRaw = ref(false);
let timer: any = null;

const copied = ref(false);
async function copySeed() {
  try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(String(props.seed)); copied.value = true; setTimeout(() => (copied.value = false), 1500); }
  catch { copied.value = false; }
}

const ev = computed<ReplayEvent | null>(() => props.events[idx.value] ?? null);
const total = computed(() => props.events.length);
const byId = computed(() => new Map(props.units.map(u => [u.unitId, u])));

/** 逐事件累加出的生命值/存活状态（用于步进时还原战场） */
const states = computed(() => {
  const hp = new Map<string, number>();
  const alive = new Map<string, boolean>();
  for (const u of props.units) { hp.set(u.unitId, u.maxHp); alive.set(u.unitId, true); }
  const out: Array<{ hp: Map<string, number>; alive: Map<string, boolean> }> = [];
  for (const e of props.events) {
    const d = (e.detail ?? {}) as any;
    const uid = (id: any) => props.units.find(u => u.unitId === String(id))?.unitId;
    switch (e.code) {
      case "DAMAGE": case "HP_LOSS": {
        const t = e.targetUnitId ?? uid(d.dst);
        if (t && hp.has(t)) hp.set(t, Math.max(0, (hp.get(t) ?? 0) - Number(d.applied ?? d.amount ?? 0)));
        break;
      }
      case "DEATH": {
        const v = e.targetUnitId ?? uid(d.id);
        if (v) { alive.set(v, false); hp.set(v, 0); }
        break;
      }
      case "REVIVE": {
        const v = e.targetUnitId ?? uid(d.id);
        if (v) { alive.set(v, true); hp.set(v, Number(d.hp ?? 0)); }
        break;
      }
    }
    out.push({ hp: new Map(hp), alive: new Map(alive) });
  }
  return out;
});
const cur = computed(() => states.value[idx.value] ?? null);

const grouped = computed(() => {
  const g: Array<{ turn: number; from: number; to: number }> = [];
  props.events.forEach((e, i) => {
    const last = g[g.length - 1];
    if (last && last.turn === e.turn) last.to = i;
    else g.push({ turn: e.turn, from: i, to: i });
  });
  return g;
});

function stop() { playing.value = false; clearInterval(timer); timer = null; }
function play() {
  if (playing.value) return stop();
  playing.value = true;
  timer = setInterval(() => {
    if (idx.value >= total.value - 1) return stop();
    idx.value++;
  }, Math.max(30, 320 / speed.value));
}
function goto(i: number) { idx.value = Math.max(0, Math.min(total.value - 1, i)); }
function jumpTurn(dir: number) {
  const t = ev.value?.turn ?? 0;
  const target = grouped.value.find(g => dir > 0 ? g.turn > t : g.turn < t);
  if (target) idx.value = dir > 0 ? target.from : target.from;
}
watch(() => props.seed, () => { stop(); idx.value = 0; });
watch(total, () => { idx.value = 0; });
const pctHp = (u: ReplayUnit) => { const h = cur.value?.hp.get(u.unitId) ?? u.maxHp; return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (h / u.maxHp) * 100)); };
const WIN: Record<string, string> = { red: "红方胜利", blue: "蓝方胜利", draw: "平局（同归于尽）", timeout: "未终结（超出回合上限）" };
</script>

<template>
  <section class="viewer">
    <header class="head">
      <b>逐步回放</b>
      <span class="mono tiny dim">seed {{ seed }} · {{ total }} 个事件 · {{ turns }} 回合</span>
      <span class="badge" :class="winner">{{ WIN[winner] }}</span>
      <span class="spacer" />
      <button class="tiny-btn" @click="copySeed" :title="'复制可复现这一局的 seed'">{{ copied ? "已复制 ✓" : "复制 seed" }}</button>
      <button class="x" @click="emit('close')">✕</button>
    </header>

    <!-- 战场快照 -->
    <div class="board">
      <div v-for="side in (['red','blue'] as const)" :key="side" class="team" :class="side">
        <div class="tname">{{ side === "red" ? "🔴 红方" : "🔵 蓝方" }}</div>
        <div v-for="u in units.filter(x => x.side === side)" :key="u.unitId" class="unit"
             :class="{ dead: !(cur?.alive.get(u.unitId) ?? true), active: ev?.actorUnitId === u.unitId || ev?.targetUnitId === u.unitId }">
          <div class="nm">{{ u.name }}</div>
          <div class="hpbar"><i :style="{ width: pctHp(u) + '%' }" /></div>
          <div class="mono tiny dim">{{ Math.round(cur?.hp.get(u.unitId) ?? u.maxHp) }} / {{ u.maxHp }}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- 控制条 -->
    <div class="controls">
      <button @click="goto(0)">⏮</button>
      <button @click="jumpTurn(-1)">⏪ 上一回合</button>
      <button @click="goto(idx - 1)">◀</button>
      <button class="play" @click="play">{{ playing ? "⏸ 暂停" : "▶ 播放" }}</button>
      <button @click="goto(idx + 1)">▶</button>
      <button @click="jumpTurn(1)">⏩ 下一回合</button>
      <button @click="goto(total - 1)">⏭</button>
      <label class="sp">速度 <input type="range" min="1" max="8" v-model.number="speed" /></label>
      <span class="mono small dim">{{ idx + 1 }} / {{ total }}</span>
    </div>
    <input class="scrub" type="range" min="0" :max="Math.max(0, total - 1)" :value="idx" @input="goto(+($event.target as HTMLInputElement).value)" />

    <div class="panes">
      <!-- 事件流 -->
      <div class="stream">
        <template v-for="g in grouped" :key="g.turn">
          <div class="turnsep" :class="{ cur: ev && ev.turn === g.turn }">第 {{ g.turn }} 回合</div>
          <button v-for="i in (g.to - g.from + 1)" :key="g.from + i - 1"
                  class="evrow" :class="{ on: idx === g.from + i - 1 }"
                  @click="goto(g.from + i - 1)">
            <span class="code mono">{{ events[g.from + i - 1].code }}</span>
            <span class="txt">{{ events[g.from + i - 1].text }}</span>
          </button>
        </template>
        <div v-if="!total" class="dim small">没有事件。</div>
      </div>

      <!-- 事件检查器 -->
      <aside class="insp">
        <div class="ititle">事件检查器</div>
        <template v-if="ev">
          <div class="kv"><span class="k">序号</span><span class="mono">#{{ ev.index }}</span></div>
          <div class="kv"><span class="k">回合</span><span class="mono">{{ ev.turn }}</span></div>
          <div class="kv"><span class="k">类型</span><span class="mono">{{ ev.code }}</span></div>
          <div class="kv"><span class="k">行动者</span><span>{{ ev.actorUnitId ? (byId.get(ev.actorUnitId)?.name ?? ev.actorUnitId) : "—" }}</span></div>
          <div class="kv"><span class="k">目标</span><span>{{ ev.targetUnitId ? (byId.get(ev.targetUnitId)?.name ?? ev.targetUnitId) : "—" }}</span></div>
          <p class="desc">{{ ev.text }}</p>
          <button class="tiny-btn" @click="showRaw = !showRaw">{{ showRaw ? "隐藏" : "查看" }}原始字段</button>
          <pre v-if="showRaw" class="raw">{{ JSON.stringify(ev.detail, null, 1) }}</pre>
          <p class="note">引擎的事件流是**唯一**的战斗事实来源：仪表盘上的每一个数字都能在这里找到对应的事件。</p>
        </template>
        <div v-else class="dim small">选中一个事件查看详情。</div>
      </aside>
    </div>
  </section>
</template>

<!-- <style> 已省略 3 KB（纯样式，无逻辑） -->
```

### `apps/web/src/components/result/ResultDashboard.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// components/result/ResultDashboard.vue — UI-3 结果仪表盘
// 四层结构（文档 §9）：结果摘要 → 不确定性 → 逐单位贡献 → 样本对局
// 原则：抽样误差与模型不确定性**分开展示**，绝不混成一个「置信度」数字。
import { computed, onMounted, ref } from "vue";
import type { SimulationResult } from "../../contracts/simulation-result.ts";
import { isConclusionReady } from "../../contracts/simulation-result.ts";
import FidelityPanel from "../simulation/FidelityPanel.vue";

/**
 * 红区说明表（懒加载，来自机制实验台生成的红区登记）。
 * 目的：界面上红色的 UNKNOWN **必须能点开看到"为什么"** ——
 * 「竞猜残局 UNKNOWN」如果不给原因，用户只会看到一片红而不知道是谁的锅。
 */
interface ZoneNote { zone: string; question: string; evidence: string; status: string }
const zoneNotes = ref<Record<string, ZoneNote>>({});
const expanded = ref<string | null>(null);
onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    const mod: any = await import("../../kb/experiments.json");
    const zones: any[] = (mod.default ?? mod).redZones ?? [];
    const map: Record<string, ZoneNote> = {};
    for (const z of zones) map[z.id] = { zone: z.zone, question: z.question ?? "", evidence: z.evidence ?? "", status: z.status };
    zoneNotes.value = map;
  } catch { /* 拿不到说明表时只显示 id 与状态，绝不瞎编原因 */ }
});

const props = defineProps<{ result: SimulationResult; fidelity?: unknown | null }>();
const emit = defineEmits<{ (e: "replay", seed: number): void; (e: "watch", seed: number): void; (e: "close"): void }>();

type Tab = "outcome" | "uncertainty" | "units" | "samples";
const tab = ref<Tab>("outcome");
const pct = (x: number | null | undefined, d = 2) => (x === null || x === undefined ? "—" : (x * 100).toFixed(d) + "%");
const num = (x: number) => x.toLocaleString();

const copied = ref(false);
async function copyJson() {
  try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(props.result, null, 1)); copied.value = true; setTimeout(() => (copied.value = false), 1500); }
  catch { copied.value = false; }
}

const r = computed(() => props.result);
const ready = computed(() => isConclusionReady(props.result));
const ci = computed(() => r.value.confidenceInterval);
const mu = computed(() => r.value.modelUncertainty);

/** Wilson 区间可视化：点估计 + 区间带，坐标 0..1 */
const ciBand = computed(() => {
  const c = ci.value;
  if (!c) return null;
  return { left: (c.low * 100).toFixed(2), width: ((c.high - c.low) * 100).toFixed(2), point: ((r.value.redWinRate ?? 0) * 100).toFixed(2) };
});
/** 与 50% 的关系：区间是否跨过五五开 —— 这是"能不能说红方更强"的唯一依据 */
const crosses50 = computed(() => !!ci.value && ci.value.low <= 0.5 && ci.value.high >= 0.5);
const verdictWord = computed(() => {
  if (!ci.value || r.value.redWinRate === null) return "样本不足，无法判断";
  if (crosses50.value) return "红蓝两方无显著差异（区间跨过 50%）";
  return r.value.redWinRate > 0.5 ? "红方显著更强" : "蓝方显著更强";
});

/** 5 个红区的可解释行：状态 + 为什么是这个状态 */
const policyRows = computed(() => {
  const s = r.value.strategySnapshot;
  return [
    { label: "开局结算顺序", id: s.startup.id, status: s.startup.status },
    { label: "同时触发优先级", id: s.triggerPriority.id, status: s.triggerPriority.status },
    { label: "死亡 / 转化", id: s.death.id, status: s.death.status },
    { label: "竞猜残局", id: s.endgame.id, status: s.endgame.status },
    { label: "鬼火初始化与回火", id: s.resource.id, status: s.resource.status },
  ];
});
const unknownRows = computed(() => policyRows.value.filter(x => x.status === "UNKNOWN"));

const units = computed(() => [...r.value.battleStats.perUnit].sort((a, b) => b.avgDamageDealt - a.avgDamageDealt));
const maxDmg = computed(() => Math.max(1, ...units.value.map(u => u.avgDamageDealt)));
const topSkills = (u: any) => Object.entries(u.skillUsage ?? {}).sort((a: any, b: any) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 3).map(([k, v]) => `${k}×${v}`).join(" · ") || "—";
const SEV: Record<string, string> = { BLOCK: "danger", WARN: "warn", INFO: "dim" };
</script>

<template>
  <section class="dash">
    <header class="head">
      <b>模拟结果</b>
      <span class="mono tiny dim">{{ r.engineVersion }} · 数据 {{ r.gameDataVersion }} · N={{ num(r.simulations) }}</span>
      <span class="badge" :class="ready.ready ? 'ok' : 'warn'">
        {{ ready.ready ? "可下结论" : "不可下结论" }}
      </span>
      <span class="spacer" />
      <button class="tiny-btn" @click="copyJson">{{ copied ? "已复制 ✓" : "复制结果 JSON" }}</button>
      <button class="x" @click="emit('close')">✕</button>
    </header>

    <nav class="tabs">
      <button :class="{ on: tab === 'outcome' }" @click="tab = 'outcome'">① 结果摘要</button>
      <button :class="{ on: tab === 'uncertainty' }" @click="tab = 'uncertainty'">② 不确定性 <i v-if="r.warnings.length" class="dot" /></button>
      <button :class="{ on: tab === 'units' }" @click="tab = 'units'">③ 逐单位贡献</button>
      <button :class="{ on: tab === 'samples' }" @click="tab = 'samples'">④ 样本对局</button>
    </nav>

    <!-- ① 结果摘要 -->
    <div v-if="tab === 'outcome'" class="body">
      <div class="two">
        <div class="side red">
          <div class="lab">红方胜率</div>
          <div class="big">{{ pct(r.redWinRate) }}</div>
          <div class="mono small dim">{{ num(r.redWins) }} 胜</div>
        </div>
        <div class="side blue">
          <div class="lab">蓝方胜率</div>
          <div class="big">{{ pct(r.blueWinRate) }}</div>
          <div class="mono small dim">{{ num(r.blueWins) }} 胜</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="verdict" :class="crosses50 ? 'warn' : 'ok'">{{ verdictWord }}</div>

      <div class="grid4">
        <div class="cell"><span class="k">有效样本</span><b class="mono">{{ num(r.decisive) }}</b><span class="tiny dim">未终结局不计入分母</span></div>
        <div class="cell"><span class="k">未终结</span><b class="mono">{{ num(r.timeouts) }}</b><span class="tiny dim">超出回合上限</span></div>
        <div class="cell"><span class="k">平局</span><b class="mono">{{ num(r.draws) }}</b><span class="tiny dim">同归于尽</span></div>
        <div class="cell"><span class="k">异常</span><b class="mono" :class="{ bad: r.errors > 0 }">{{ num(r.errors) }}</b><span class="tiny dim">永不进入分母</span></div>
      </div>

      <div class="grid4">
        <div class="cell"><span class="k">平均回合</span><b class="mono">{{ r.battleStats.avgTurns }}</b></div>
        <div class="cell"><span class="k">中位回合</span><b class="mono">{{ r.battleStats.medianTurns }}</b></div>
        <div class="cell"><span class="k">p95 回合</span><b class="mono">{{ r.battleStats.p95Turns }}</b></div>
        <div class="cell"><span class="k">红方先手击杀率</span><b class="mono">{{ pct(r.battleStats.firstKillRedRate, 1) }}</b></div>
      </div>

      <div class="grid4">
        <div class="cell"><span class="k">红方平均存活</span><b class="mono">{{ r.battleStats.avgSurvivorsRed }}</b></div>
        <div class="cell"><span class="k">蓝方平均存活</span><b class="mono">{{ r.battleStats.avgSurvivorsBlue }}</b></div>
        <div class="cell"><span class="k">红方残余总生命</span><b class="mono">{{ num(r.battleStats.avgRemainHpRed) }}</b></div>
        <div class="cell"><span class="k">蓝方残余总生命</span><b class="mono">{{ num(r.battleStats.avgRemainHpBlue) }}</b></div>
      </div>

      <p class="note">分母口径：红蓝胜率的分母是「有效局」= 红胜 + 蓝胜，未终结与异常局**永远不进分母**，所以两者之和不会假凑成 100%。</p>
    </div>

    <!-- ② 不确定性 -->
    <div v-else-if="tab === 'uncertainty'" class="body">
      <!-- ★ 结果与"本局可信度"必须一起看：不给百分比，只给五层状态分类 -->
      <FidelityPanel :fidelity="fidelity ?? null" />
      <div class="block">
        <h4>抽样误差（加大模拟次数可以缩小）</h4>
        <div v-if="ciBand" class="cibar">
          <div class="track">
            <div class="band" :style="{ left: ciBand.left + '%', width: ciBand.width + '%' }" />
            <div class="mid" />
            <div class="pt" :style="{ left: ciBand.point + '%' }" />
          </div>
          <div class="mono small">红方 {{ pct(r.redWinRate) }}，Wilson 95% 置信区间 <b>[{{ pct(ci!.low) }}, {{ pct(ci!.high) }}]</b>（宽度 {{ (ci!.high - ci!.low > 0 ? ((ci!.high - ci!.low) * 100).toFixed(2) : "0") }} 个百分点）</div>
          <div class="tiny dim">虚线 = 50%；区间跨过它就意味着「双方没有显著差异」。区间宽度只反映样本量，不反映机制是否正确。</div>
        </div>
        <div v-else class="dim small">样本不足，没有区间可言。</div>
      </div>

      <div class="block">
        <h4>模型不确定性（加大模拟次数也不会缩小）</h4>
        <template v-if="mu">
          <div class="verdictbadge" :class="mu.verdict === 'SENSITIVE' ? 'bad' : mu.verdict === 'STABLE' ? 'ok' : 'warn'">
            {{ mu.verdict === "SENSITIVE" ? "结论对未实测机制敏感" : mu.verdict === "STABLE" ? "结论对这些假设不敏感" : "无法评估" }}
            <span v-if="mu.spread !== null" class="mono">漂移 {{ (mu.spread * 100).toFixed(1) }} 个百分点</span>
          </div>
          <table class="tbl">
            <thead><tr><th>假设</th><th>分组</th><th class="r">红方胜率</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              <tr v-for="(p, i) in mu.perStrategy" :key="i">
                <td>{{ p.label }}<div class="tiny dim">{{ p.note }}</div></td>
                <td class="mono tiny">{{ p.group }}</td>
                <td class="mono r">
                  <span class="minibar" :style="{ width: ((p.redWinRate ?? 0) * 100) + '%' }" />{{ pct(p.redWinRate, 1) }}
                </td>
              </tr>
            </tbody>
          </table>
          <p class="note">这些是红区（开局顺序 / 死亡结算 / 回合上限）里**尚未实测**的机制。换一种同样合理的假设，胜率就会漂移——这是模型的边界，不是抽样噪声。典型对局 #001 中回合上限从 600 降到 100，红方胜率从 67.0% 掉到 47.6%，因为竞猜残局的增伤曲线至今没有可信来源。</p>
        </template>
        <div v-else class="dim small">本次未做敏感性扫描（模拟次数少于 800 时自动跳过，避免用极小样本假装评估）。</div>
      </div>

      <div class="block">
        <h4>机制策略快照（随结果永久保存）</h4>
        <div class="legend">
          <span><i class="sw unknown" />UNKNOWN —— 游戏内没有被可靠验证过，引擎**拒绝猜**</span>
          <span><i class="sw hypothesis" />HYPOTHESIS —— 有社区/文档线索，但同样没实测</span>
          <span><i class="sw verified" />VERIFIED —— 已被游戏内观测确认</span>
        </div>
        <p v-if="unknownRows.length" class="whyred">
          红色不代表程序出错，而是「<b>结论会受它影响</b>」：
          <template v-for="(u, i) in unknownRows" :key="u.id">{{ i ? "；" : "" }}{{ u.label }} = <span class="mono">{{ u.id }}</span></template>。
          点任意一行可以看到它到底缺什么。
        </p>
        <div class="policies">
          <template v-for="p in policyRows" :key="p.label">
            <button class="pol" :class="{ open: expanded === p.label }" @click="expanded = expanded === p.label ? null : p.label">
              <span class="k">{{ p.label }}</span>
              <span class="mono tiny">{{ p.id }}</span>
              <span class="st" :class="p.status.toLowerCase()">{{ p.status }}</span>
            </button>
            <div v-if="expanded === p.label" class="poldetail">
              <template v-if="zoneNotes[p.id]">
                <div class="q">{{ zoneNotes[p.id].question || "（源文件未提供 question 字段）" }}</div>
                <div class="ev"><b>依据 / 为什么这个状态：</b>{{ zoneNotes[p.id].evidence || "（源文件未提供 evidence 字段）" }}</div>
                <div class="tiny dim">红区：{{ zoneNotes[p.id].zone }}</div>
              </template>
              <div v-else class="tiny dim">未找到该策略的说明条目 —— 不编造原因，请到「机制实验」页查看红区登记。</div>
            </div>
          </template>
        </div>
        <p class="note">
          UNKNOWN 机制不会被猜测，只会被标注。含 UNKNOWN 时结果可用于阵容比较，不能当作机制结论。
          目前最大的一个就是<b>竞猜残局</b>：初始鬼火、残局增伤/减疗曲线、超时判胜方式在 2026 年都没有可信来源，
          所以引擎把回合上限的选择权交给人（默认 300 回合），并在敏感性扫描里把"换一种假设会怎样"直接算给用户看。
        </p>
      </div>

      <div class="block">
        <h4>数据与模型覆盖度</h4>
        <div class="grid4">
          <div class="cell"><span class="k">单位数据</span><b class="mono">{{ r.coverage.unitsJoined }}/{{ r.coverage.unitsTotal }}</b></div>
          <div class="cell"><span class="k">技能可用</span><b class="mono">{{ r.coverage.skillsAvailable }}/{{ r.coverage.skillsTotal }}</b></div>
          <div class="cell"><span class="k">AI 高置信度</span><b class="mono">{{ r.coverage.aiHigh }}/{{ r.coverage.aiTotal }}</b></div>
          <div class="cell"><span class="k">御魂战斗效果</span><b class="mono">{{ r.coverage.soulEffectsFull }} 完整 / {{ r.coverage.soulEffectsPartial }} 部分</b></div>
        </div>
        <ul v-if="r.coverage.unsupportedCapabilities.length" class="caps">
          <li v-for="(c, i) in r.coverage.unsupportedCapabilities" :key="i">⚠ {{ c }}</li>
        </ul>
      </div>

      <div class="block">
        <h4>警告（{{ r.warnings.length }}）</h4>
        <div v-for="(w, i) in r.warnings" :key="i" class="warnrow" :class="SEV[w.severity]">
          <span class="sev mono">{{ w.severity }}</span>
          <div>
            <div>{{ w.message }}</div>
            <div v-if="w.detail" class="tiny dim">{{ w.detail }}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div v-if="!r.warnings.length" class="dim small">无警告。</div>
      </div>

      <div v-if="!ready.ready" class="block">
        <h4>为什么说「不可下结论」</h4>
        <ul class="caps"><li v-for="(x, i) in ready.reasons" :key="i">· {{ x }}</li></ul>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- ③ 逐单位贡献 -->
    <div v-else-if="tab === 'units'" class="body">
      <table class="tbl">
        <thead>
          <tr><th>单位</th><th class="r">平均伤害</th><th></th><th class="r">承受</th><th class="r">击杀</th><th class="r">阵亡率</th><th class="r">行动</th><th>技能使用</th></tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="u in units" :key="u.unitId" :class="u.side">
            <td><span class="side-dot" :class="u.side" />{{ u.name }}</td>
            <td class="mono r">{{ num(u.avgDamageDealt) }}</td>
            <td class="barcell"><span class="minibar" :style="{ width: (u.avgDamageDealt / maxDmg * 100) + '%' }" /></td>
            <td class="mono r">{{ num(u.avgDamageTaken) }}</td>
            <td class="mono r">{{ u.kills }}</td>
            <td class="mono r">{{ (u.deathRate * 100).toFixed(0) }}%</td>
            <td class="mono r">{{ u.avgActions }}</td>
            <td class="tiny dim">{{ topSkills(u) }}</td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <p class="note">逐单位数字取自引擎真实的 DAMAGE / DEATH 事件与行动日志；引擎没有记录的指标（如鬼火消耗）保持为 0 并在覆盖度里显式声明，绝不估算。</p>
    </div>

    <!-- ④ 样本对局 -->
    <div v-else class="body">
      <p class="small dim">下面是本次模拟中**真实出现过**的代表性对局。选一个 seed 进入逐步回放，可以看到每一回合发生了什么。</p>
      <div class="samples">
        <button v-for="s in r.seeds ?? []" :key="s.seed" class="sample" :class="s.winner" @click="emit('watch', s.seed)">
          <span class="w">{{ s.winner === "red" ? "红胜" : s.winner === "blue" ? "蓝胜" : s.winner === "draw" ? "平局" : "未终结" }}</span>
          <span class="mono tiny">seed {{ s.seed }}</span>
          <span class="mono tiny dim">{{ s.turns }} 回合</span>
          <span class="go">观看战斗 ▶</span>
        </button>
        <div v-if="!(r.seeds ?? []).length" class="dim small">本次结果未记录代表 seed。</div>
      </div>
      <p class="note">回放使用与该局**完全相同**的 seed 重新装配并运行，因此是同一场战斗，不是另一次抽样。</p>
    </div>
  </section>
</template>
```

### `apps/web/src/components/simulation/FidelityPanel.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// components/simulation/FidelityPanel.vue — ★ Per-Match Fidelity（a1-4 §4-§10、§33）
// 铁律：**不给"真实性百分比"**。五层各自给非评价性状态分类，进度条只表示"这一层有多少项是完整的"。
import { computed, ref } from "vue";
// ★★★ 第 138 轮：结构类型与逐技能词汇都移到 contracts/fidelity-view.ts（唯一声明处）；
//   这里不再自己声明一份 Fidelity 形状 —— 面板 / 卡面 / 抽屉读的是同一份契约。
import { groupSkillStatus } from "../../contracts/fidelity-view.ts";
import type { FidelityView } from "../../contracts/fidelity-view.ts";
import CompiledBadge from "../knowledge/CompiledBadge.vue";

const props = defineProps<{ fidelity: unknown | null }>();
const f = computed(() => (props.fidelity ?? null) as FidelityView | null);
const open = ref<string | null>(null);
/** ★ 第 138 轮：逐技能一节默认收起（30 行会把面板撑得很长），点标题展开 */
const openSkills = ref(false);
const skillGroups = computed(() => groupSkillStatus(f.value?.skills ?? []));

/**
 * 界面文案（短版徽标）。★★ 第 61 轮加了一条机械约束（tests/ui10-fidelity.ts）：
 *   · 键必须与 kernel/fidelity.ts 的 FIDELITY_CLASS_LABEL **完全一致**（内核加一档，这里必须跟上）；
 *   · **每一段词都必须能在内核文案里原样找到** —— 界面不许自造措辞。
 * 为什么不是直接引用内核那张表：徽标要短。但"短"不能变成"另一套说法"，
 * 所以约束写成"用词子集"，而不是两处各写一份、然后慢慢写歪。
 *
 * ★★★ PARTIAL 必须保留「可以运行」：§10 的定义是「存在 PARTIAL，但可以运行」——
 *   这一档说的是"能不能用"，不是"好不好"。而它正是用户最常看到的一档
 *   （典型对局 #001 在第 60 轮修正分类口径之后就是 PARTIAL）。
 *   原先这里只写「部分实现」，把限定语丢了 —— 一个非评价性分类读起来就成了故障灯。
 */
const KLASS_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  VERIFIED: "已验证", COMPLETE_UNVERIFIED: "代码路径完整 · 未游戏实测",
  PARTIAL: "部分实现 · 可以运行", INSUFFICIENT: "未实现 · 结果不可用",
};
const KLASS_TONE: Record<string, string> = { VERIFIED: "ok", COMPLETE_UNVERIFIED: "warn", PARTIAL: "warn", INSUFFICIENT: "bad" };
const LAYER_TONE: Record<string, string> = { COMPLETE: "ok", PARTIAL: "warn", GAPS: "bad", UNKNOWN: "idle" };
</script>

<template>
  <div class="fid">
    <div class="title">
      本局模拟可信度
      <span v-if="f" class="kbadge" :class="KLASS_TONE[f.klass] ?? 'idle'">{{ KLASS_LABEL[f.klass] ?? f.klass }}</span>
    </div>

    <template v-if="f">
      <div v-for="L in f.layers" :key="L.layer" class="layer" :class="{ open: open === L.layer }">
        <button class="lhead" @click="open = open === L.layer ? null : L.layer">
          <span class="lname">{{ L.title }}</span>
          <span class="lbar"><i :style="{ width: L.bar ? (L.bar.filled / L.bar.total * 100) + '%' : '0%' }" /></span>
          <span class="lklass" :class="LAYER_TONE[L.klass]">{{ L.klass }}</span>
        </button>
        <div v-if="open === L.layer" class="lbody">
          <div v-for="c in L.counts" :key="c.label" class="kv">
            <span class="k">{{ c.label }}</span><span class="mono">{{ c.value }}</span>
            <span v-if="c.note" class="tiny dim">{{ c.note }}</span>
          </div>
          <div v-for="(g, i) in L.gaps" :key="i" class="gap" :class="g.severity.toLowerCase()">
            <b>{{ g.what }}</b> {{ g.detail }}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!--
        ★★★ 第 138 轮（§33）：「逐技能机制实现度 ● FULL / ◐ PARTIAL」。
        在这之前面板上只有 6 个**聚合计数**，问不出"这一个技能到底是哪一档"；
        而审核（第 137 轮）指出卡面上原本只有一个「数据在不在」的圆点。
      -->
      <div v-if="skillGroups.length" class="skills">
        <button class="shead" @click="openSkills = !openSkills">
          <span>逐技能编译状态</span>
          <span class="mono tiny dim">{{ (f.skills ?? []).length }} 个技能 · {{ skillGroups.length }} 个单位</span>
          <span class="tiny dim">{{ openSkills ? "收起" : "展开" }}</span>
        </button>
        <div v-if="openSkills" class="sbody">
          <div v-for="g in skillGroups" :key="g.key" class="sgroup">
            <div class="suname">
              <b>{{ g.unit }}</b>
              <span class="mono tiny">{{ g.tally }}</span>
            </div>
            <div v-for="s in g.skills" :key="s.name" class="srow">
              <span class="sname">{{ s.name }}</span>
              <!-- ★ 与抽屉里是**同一个组件**：档位一旦改口径，两处一起改 -->
              <CompiledBadge :status="s" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div v-if="f.reasons.length" class="reasons">
        <div class="tiny dim">为什么是这个分类：</div>
        <div v-for="(r, i) in f.reasons" :key="i" class="tiny">· {{ r }}</div>
      </div>
      <div v-for="(c, i) in f.caveats" :key="i" class="caveat">{{ c }}</div>
    </template>
    <div v-else class="dim small">还没有可评估的对局（先录入双方阵容）。</div>
  </div>
</template>
```
