### `apps/web/src/components/simulation/HealthCheck.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// 输入健康检查 —— 按文档第七节：Validation → Compilation → Coverage Analysis
// 刻意**不给百分比评分**（无依据把 Coverage 映射成准确率）
import { computed } from "vue";
import type { GuessingMatch } from "../../contracts/guessing-match";
import type { ValidationReport } from "../../contracts/worker-api";
import { shikigamiById, soulById, SOUL_SUPPORT_LABEL } from "../../kb/useKb";

// report 来自真引擎（Worker 的 VALIDATE）。没有它时退回到本地知识库的估算，
// 并明确标注「未接引擎」—— 绝不把估算伪装成引擎结论。
const props = defineProps<{ match: GuessingMatch; report?: ValidationReport | null }>();
const engineData = computed(() => !!props.report);

const units = computed(() => [...props.match.red.units, ...props.match.blue.units].filter(u => u.shikigamiId > 0));
const rows = computed(() => {
  const rep = props.report;
  if (rep) {
    const c = rep.coverage;
    return [
      { label: "单位数据 Join", v: `${c.unitsJoined}/${c.unitsTotal}`, ok: c.unitsJoined === c.unitsTotal, warn: false },
      { label: "技能可用", v: `${c.skillsAvailable}/${c.skillsTotal}`, ok: c.skillsAvailable === c.skillsTotal && c.skillsTotal > 0, warn: c.skillsAvailable < c.skillsTotal },
      { label: "AI 高置信度", v: `${c.aiHigh}/${c.aiTotal}`, ok: c.aiHigh === c.aiTotal && c.aiTotal > 0, warn: c.aiHigh < c.aiTotal },
      // ★ ok 原先恒为 true → 这一行**永远**显示绿色 ✓，算出来的 warn 根本没人看（死代码）。
      //   现在：全部解析成功才 ✓；否则 ⚠（含"一个都没录"——那是输入缺口，不是错误，所以不给红）。
      { label: "御魂定义", v: `${c.soulsResolved}/${c.soulsTotal}`, ok: c.soulsTotal > 0 && c.soulsResolved === c.soulsTotal, warn: !(c.soulsTotal > 0 && c.soulsResolved === c.soulsTotal) },
      { label: "御魂战斗效果", v: `${c.soulEffectsFull} 完整 / ${c.soulEffectsPartial} 部分`, ok: c.soulEffectsPartial === 0, warn: c.soulEffectsPartial > 0 },
    ];
  }
  const n = units.value.length;
  const metas = units.value.map(u => shikigamiById(u.shikigamiId));
  const skillsOk = metas.filter(m => (m?.has.skills ?? 0) > 0).length;
  const aiOk = metas.filter(m => m?.has.ai).length;
  const souls = units.value.map(u => soulById(u.soulId)).filter(Boolean) as any[];
  const soulFull = souls.filter(s => s.engine.support === "FULL").length;
  const soulBad = souls.filter(s => s.engine.support !== "FULL").length;
  return [
    { label: "阵容完整", v: `${n}/10`, ok: n === 10 },
    { label: "式神数据 Join", v: `${n}/10`, ok: n === 10 },
    { label: "技能可用", v: `${skillsOk}/${n || 10}`, ok: n > 0 && skillsOk === n },
    { label: "AI 高置信度", v: `${aiOk}/${n || 10}`, ok: n > 0 && aiOk === n, warn: aiOk < n },
    // ★ 同上：ok 原先恒 true，warn 是死代码。全解析成功才 ✓，否则 ⚠。
    { label: "御魂定义", v: `${souls.length}/${n || 10}`, ok: n > 0 && souls.length === n, warn: !(n > 0 && souls.length === n) },
    { label: "御魂效果完整实现", v: `${soulFull}/${n || 10}`, ok: n > 0 && soulFull === n, warn: soulBad > 0 },
  ];
});
const unknownCount = computed(() => props.report?.strategySnapshot.unknownCount ?? null);
const blockers = computed(() => props.report?.blockers ?? []);
const warnCount = computed(() => (props.report?.warnings ?? []).filter(w => w.severity !== "INFO").length);
</script>

<template>
  <div class="health">
    <div class="title">模拟准备</div>
    <div class="rows">
      <div v-for="r in rows" :key="r.label" class="row">
        <span class="mark" :class="r.ok ? 'ok' : (r.warn ? 'warn' : 'bad')">{{ r.ok ? "✓" : (r.warn ? "⚠" : "✗") }}</span>
        <span class="label">{{ r.label }}</span>
        <span class="val mono">{{ r.v }}</span>
      </div>
      <!-- 三态必须分清：未评估(灰) / 有 UNKNOWN(橙) / 无 UNKNOWN(绿)。
           早先这里把"未评估"也画成红色 ⚠，看起来像出了错，其实只是还没校验 —— 红只留给真问题。 -->
      <div class="row">
        <span class="mark" :class="unknownCount === null ? 'idle' : (unknownCount ? 'warn' : 'ok')">
          {{ unknownCount === null ? "–" : (unknownCount ? "⚠" : "✓") }}
        </span>
        <span class="label">机制策略</span>
        <span class="val mono">{{ unknownCount === null ? "尚未校验" : unknownCount ? `UNKNOWN ×${unknownCount}` : "无 UNKNOWN" }}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div v-if="blockers.length" class="blockers">
      <div v-for="(b, i) in blockers" :key="i">✗ {{ b }}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="verdict" :class="blockers.length ? 'bad' : (units.length === 10 ? 'warn' : 'bad')">
      {{ blockers.length ? `${blockers.length} 项阻断，无法运行` : units.length === 10 ? (warnCount ? `可运行 · ${warnCount} 项警告（见结果页）` : "可运行") : `还需录入 ${10 - units.length} 只式神` }}
    </div>
    <p class="note">
      {{ engineData ? "覆盖度与策略快照来自真引擎的校验结果。" : "尚未运行引擎校验，以下为本地知识库估算。" }}
      不给「准确率」评分 —— 没有依据把覆盖率线性映射成预测准确率。
    </p>
  </div>
</template>

<!-- <style> 已省略 1 KB（纯样式，无逻辑） -->
```

### `apps/web/src/components/theater/BattleTheater.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// components/theater/BattleTheater.vue — ★ G4 Battle Theater 主界面（a1-4 §12）
// 布局照文档：顶部状态条 → 红蓝十张 Entity Card → 当前行动者 Banner → 双方鬼火 →
//            Action Bar（行动条） → 播放控制 + 进度条 →（右栏）事件流 + 事件链检查器
// 明确不做：立绘动画 / 技能特效 / 粒子 / 3D（§59）。这里是**机制可视化器**，不是游戏客户端。
import { computed, onBeforeUnmount, ref, toRaw, watch } from "vue";
import EntityCard from "./EntityCard.vue";
import { Presenter } from "../../theater/presenter.ts";
// ★★★ 第 137 轮：播放逻辑（帧号推进 / 关键时刻 / 倍速）搬进 theatre/transport.ts —— 纯模块才断言得了。
//   组件这边只剩"把 ref 暴露成 host"与"挂 setInterval"两件事。
import { createTransport, frameIntervalMs, SPEEDS } from "../../theater/transport.ts";
// ★ 这里原本写的是 ../../../../battle-lab/... —— 从 components/theater 往上数四层是 apps/，
//   路径短了一层。它是 **type-only** 导入，编译后被完全擦除，所以 vite 构建一直没报错，
//   只有 vue-tsc 看得见。改用 tsconfig 里已有的 @engine/* 别名，路径就不再靠人工数层级。
import type { ReplayFile } from "@engine/kernel/replay.ts";

const props = defineProps<{ replay: unknown; seed: number }>();
const emit = defineEmits<{ (e: "close"): void }>();

/**
 * ★★★ 第 256 轮：**引擎边界只许收到"生"数据**（toRaw）。
 *   起因：replay 一旦以响应式代理的身份进到引擎，`structuredClone` 会抛
 *   「#<Object> could not be cloned」——剧场当场白屏（用户实测，见 stores/simulation.ts 的注）。
 *   store 那侧已经 markRaw；这一层是**边界上的第二道**：不管谁把 replay 传进来（props / ref / reactive），
 *   交到引擎手里之前一律脱掉代理。
 */
const presenter = computed(() => Presenter.fromSerialized(toRaw(props.replay) as ReplayFile));
const idx = ref(0);
const playing = ref(false);
const speed = ref(1);
const showInspector = ref(false);
let timer: any = null;

const frameCount = computed(() => presenter.value.frameCount);
const view = computed(() => presenter.value.view(idx.value));
const result = computed(() => presenter.value.result);
const events = computed(() => presenter.value.player.frames);

const redUnits = computed(() => view.value?.units.filter(u => u.side === "red") ?? []);
const blueUnits = computed(() => view.value?.units.filter(u => u.side === "blue") ?? []);

/**
 * ★★★ 第 137 轮：**逻辑在 transport.ts 里，状态在这里**。
 *   组件把三个 ref 暴露成 TransportHost；transport 是唯一决定"下一帧是第几帧"的地方。
 *   这样"播放/单步/跳关键时刻"就能在没有 DOM 的环境里被断言（tests/ui14-transport.ts）。
 */
const transport = createTransport({
  host: {
    getIdx: () => idx.value,
    setIdx: (i: number) => { idx.value = i; },
    getPlaying: () => playing.value,
    setPlaying: (p: boolean) => { playing.value = p; },
  },
  frameCount: () => frameCount.value,
  events: () => events.value,
  // 回合归属由 Presenter 给（与 Banner 上显示的回合是同一处真相）
  turnAt: (i: number) => presenter.value.view(i)?.turn ?? 0,
});

function stop() { transport.stop(); }
function play() {
  if (playing.value) return stop();
  transport.play();
  timer = setInterval(() => {
    // ★ tick() 在末尾会自己停；它返回 false 就是"该把计时器清掉"的信号
    if (!transport.tick() && timer) { clearInterval(timer); timer = null; }
  }, frameIntervalMs(speed.value));
}
function goto(i: number) { transport.goto(i); }
function stepBy(n: number) { transport.stepBy(n); }
function nextKeyFrame(dir: number) { transport.nextKeyFrame(dir); }
function nextTurn(dir: number) { transport.nextTurn(dir); }
/**
 * ★ 计时器跟着 playing 走：transport 自己停（单步/跳帧/到末尾）时，
 *   组件这边必须把 setInterval 清掉 —— 否则"暂停了但帧号还在动"。
 */
watch(playing, (p) => { if (!p && timer) { clearInterval(timer); timer = null; } });
watch(() => props.seed, () => { stop(); idx.value = 0; });
onBeforeUnmount(stop);

const ancestry = computed(() => presenter.value.ancestry(idx.value));
// ── ★ G5 Inspectors ──
const inspTab = ref<"chain" | "damage" | "ai" | "status" | "rng">("damage");
const damage = computed(() => presenter.value.damageBreakdown(idx.value));
const ai = computed(() => presenter.value.aiDecision(idx.value));
const statuses = computed(() => presenter.value.statusTimeline());
const rng = computed(() => presenter.value.rngAt(idx.value));
const rngSummary = computed(() => presenter.value.rngSummary());
const OPEN_STATUS = computed(() => statuses.value.filter(s => s.to === null).length);
const KIND_TONE: Record<string, string> = { SKILL: "skill", DEATH: "death", REVIVE: "revive", STATUS: "status", ORB: "orb", TIMELINE: "timeline", OTHER: "" };
</script>

<template>
  <section class="theater">
    <header class="top">
      <b>Battle Theater</b>
      <span class="mono tiny dim">seed {{ seed }} · {{ frameCount }} 帧 · {{ result.turns }} 回合 · {{ result.reason }}</span>
      <span class="badge" :class="result.winner === 0 ? 'red' : result.winner === 1 ? 'blue' : 'warn'">
        {{ result.winner === 0 ? "红方胜利" : result.winner === 1 ? "蓝方胜利" : result.reason }}
      </span>
      <span class="spacer" />
      <label class="sp">倍速
        <button v-for="s in SPEEDS" :key="s" :class="{ on: speed === s }" @click="speed = s">{{ s }}×</button>
      </label>
      <button class="tiny-btn" @click="showInspector = !showInspector">{{ showInspector ? "隐藏" : "显示" }}检查器</button>
      <button class="x" @click="emit('close')">✕</button>
    </header>

    <!-- 战场 -->
    <div class="stage">
      <div class="side red">
        <div class="sidehead">
          🔴 红方
          <span class="mono tiny">存活 {{ view?.teams[0].aliveCount ?? 0 }}/5</span>
          <span class="orb mono" :title="`鬼火 ${view?.teams[0].orb} · 回火计数 ${view?.teams[0].cycle}`">
            🔥 {{ view?.teams[0].orb }}/8
            <i v-for="i in 5" :key="i" :class="{ on: (view?.teams[0].gauge ?? 0) >= i }" />
          </span>
        </div>
        <div class="cards"><EntityCard v-for="u in redUnits" :key="u.unitId" :unit="u" /></div>
      </div>

      <div class="center">
        <div v-if="view?.banner" class="banner" :class="KIND_TONE[view.banner.kind]">
          <div class="bhead">
            <span class="bkind">{{ view.banner.kind }}</span>
            <b>{{ view.banner.actor ?? view.banner.target ?? "—" }}</b>
            <span v-if="view.banner.skill" class="skill">「{{ view.banner.skill }}」</span>
            <span v-if="view.banner.target && view.banner.actor" class="arrow">→ {{ view.banner.target }}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="blines">
            <span v-for="(l, i) in view.banner.lines" :key="i" :class="l.tone ?? 'normal'">{{ l.label }} <b>{{ l.value }}</b></span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div v-else class="banner empty">—</div>
        <div class="turn mono">第 {{ view?.turn ?? 0 }} 回合 · frame {{ view?.frame ?? 0 }}/{{ frameCount - 1 }}</div>
        <div v-if="view?.warning" class="warnbox">⚠ {{ view.warning }}</div>
      </div>

      <div class="side blue">
        <div class="sidehead">
          <span class="orb mono" :title="`鬼火 ${view?.teams[1].orb} · 回火计数 ${view?.teams[1].cycle}`">
            🔥 {{ view?.teams[1].orb }}/8
            <i v-for="i in 5" :key="i" :class="{ on: (view?.teams[1].gauge ?? 0) >= i }" />
          </span>
          <span class="mono tiny">存活 {{ view?.teams[1].aliveCount ?? 0 }}/5</span>
          蓝方 🔵
        </div>
        <div class="cards"><EntityCard v-for="u in blueUnits" :key="u.unitId" :unit="u" /></div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Action Bar：行动条的真实位置（拉条/推条会直接改变它） -->
    <div class="actionbar">
      <div class="abtitle mono tiny">ACTION BAR（行动条：位置越靠右越先出手）</div>
      <div v-for="u in view?.units ?? []" :key="u.unitId" class="abrow" :class="u.side">
        <span class="abname mono">{{ u.name }}</span>
        <div class="abtrack">
          <div class="abfill" :style="{ width: (u.timeline * 100) + '%' }" />
          <span class="abdot" :style="{ left: (u.timeline * 100) + '%' }" />
        </div>
        <span class="abspd mono tiny">{{ u.stats.spd.toFixed(1) }}</span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- 控制条 -->
    <div class="controls">
      <button @click="goto(0)" title="回到开头">⏮</button>
      <button @click="nextKeyFrame(-1)" title="上一个关键时刻">⏪</button>
      <button @click="nextTurn(-1)" title="上一回合">⟲</button>
      <button @click="stepBy(-1)" title="上一帧">◀</button>
      <button class="play" @click="play">{{ playing ? "⏸ 暂停" : "▶ 播放" }}</button>
      <button @click="stepBy(1)" title="下一帧">▶</button>
      <button @click="nextTurn(1)" title="下一回合">⟳</button>
      <button @click="nextKeyFrame(1)" title="下一个关键时刻">⏩</button>
      <button @click="goto(frameCount - 1)" title="跳到结束">⏭</button>
      <span class="mono tiny dim">{{ idx + 1 }} / {{ frameCount }}</span>
    </div>
    <input class="scrub" type="range" min="0" :max="Math.max(0, frameCount - 1)" :value="idx"
           @input="stop(); goto(+($event.target as HTMLInputElement).value)" />

    <!-- 事件流 + 事件链检查器 -->
    <div v-if="showInspector" class="inspector">
      <div class="stream">
        <button v-for="(e, i) in events" :key="i" class="evrow" :class="{ on: i === idx }" @click="stop(); goto(i)">
          <span class="mono tiny">#{{ e.frame }}</span>
          <span class="code mono tiny">{{ e.t }}</span>
          <span class="txt">{{ (view?.units.find(u => u.id === (e as any).src || u.id === (e as any).actor)?.name) ?? "" }}
            <template v-if="(e as any).dst || (e as any).target"> → {{ view?.units.find(u => u.id === ((e as any).dst ?? (e as any).target))?.name }}</template>
            <template v-if="(e as any).skill"> 「{{ (e as any).skill }}」</template>
            <template v-if="(e as any).applied !== undefined"> -{{ Math.round((e as any).applied) }}</template>
            <template v-if="(e as any).amount !== undefined"> +{{ Math.round((e as any).amount) }}</template>
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>
      <aside class="chain">
        <div class="tabs">
          <button :class="{ on: inspTab === 'damage' }" @click="inspTab = 'damage'">伤害拆解</button>
          <button :class="{ on: inspTab === 'ai' }" @click="inspTab = 'ai'">AI 决策</button>
          <button :class="{ on: inspTab === 'status' }" @click="inspTab = 'status'">状态生命周期</button>
          <button :class="{ on: inspTab === 'rng' }" @click="inspTab = 'rng'">随机数</button>
          <button :class="{ on: inspTab === 'chain' }" @click="inspTab = 'chain'">事件链</button>
        </div>

        <!-- 伤害检查器（§26）：这个数字是怎么来的 -->
        <template v-if="inspTab === 'damage'">
          <template v-if="damage">
            <div class="tiny dim">最近一次伤害在 frame {{ damage.frame }}（技能：{{ damage.data.skill ?? "—" }}，类型 {{ damage.data.type }}）</div>
            <ol class="steps">
              <li v-for="(s, i) in damage.data.steps" :key="i">{{ s }}</li>
            </ol>
            <div class="kv"><span class="k">原始</span><span class="mono">{{ damage.data.raw.toFixed(1) }}</span></div>
            <div class="kv"><span class="k">最终</span><span class="mono">{{ damage.data.final.toFixed(1) }}</span></div>
            <div class="kv"><span class="k">实际扣除</span><span class="mono">{{ damage.data.applied.toFixed(1) }}</span></div>
            <div v-if="damage.data.absorbed" class="kv"><span class="k">护盾吸收</span><span class="mono">{{ damage.data.absorbed.toFixed(1) }}</span></div>
          </template>
          <div v-else class="dim small">这一帧之前还没有发生过伤害。</div>
        </template>

        <!-- AI 检查器（§25）：为什么放这个技能、为什么打这个目标 -->
        <template v-else-if="inspTab === 'ai'">
          <template v-if="ai?.skill">
            <div class="kv"><span class="k">选择</span><b>{{ ai.skill.chosen ?? "—" }}</b></div>
            <div class="kv"><span class="k">依据</span><span class="mono tiny">{{ ai.skill.reason }}</span></div>
            <div v-if="ai.skill.note" class="warnbox">{{ ai.skill.note }}</div>
            <div class="tiny dim">鬼火 {{ ai.skill.orbs }}<template v-if="ai.skill.maxOrbs">/{{ ai.skill.maxOrbs }}</template></div>
            <table class="ruletbl">
              <thead><tr><th>规则</th><th>技能</th><th>鬼火</th><th>条件</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                <tr v-for="r in ai.skill.rules" :key="r.index">
                  <td class="mono tiny">#{{ r.index }}</td>
                  <td>{{ r.skill ?? "—" }}</td>
                  <td class="mono tiny">{{ r.cost ?? "—" }}</td>
                  <td class="tiny">
                    <span :class="r.affordable ? 'ok' : 'bad'">{{ r.affordable ? "够" : "不够" }}</span>
                    <span :class="r.cond ? 'ok' : 'bad'">{{ r.cond ? "条件成立" : "条件不成立" }}</span>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </template>
          <div v-else class="dim small">这一帧之前还没有 AI 决策。</div>
          <template v-if="ai?.target">
            <div class="kv" style="margin-top:6px"><span class="k">目标</span><b>{{ view?.units.find(u => u.id === ai!.target!.chosen)?.name ?? "—" }}</b></div>
            <div class="tiny dim">规则：{{ ai.target.rule ?? "（无目标规则 → 默认取第一个敌人）" }}</div>
          </template>
        </template>

        <!-- 状态检查器（§27）：生命周期而不是快照 -->
        <template v-else-if="inspTab === 'status'">
          <div class="tiny dim">共 {{ statuses.length }} 条状态记录，当前仍挂着 {{ OPEN_STATUS }} 条</div>
          <div class="stlist">
            <div v-for="(s, i) in statuses.slice(-40)" :key="i" class="strow" :class="{ open: s.to === null, unknown: !s.known }">
              <span class="mono tiny">{{ s.unitId }}</span>
              <span class="stname">{{ s.name ?? s.status }}</span>
              <span class="mono tiny">frame {{ s.from }}<template v-if="s.to !== null"> → {{ s.to }}</template><template v-else> → 仍在</template></span>
              <span class="tiny dim">{{ s.duration ?? "?" }} 回合 · 来源 {{ s.source ?? "—" }}</span>
              <span v-if="!s.known" class="tiny bad">源数据缺失</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </template>

        <!-- 随机数检查器（§20） -->
        <template v-else-if="inspTab === 'rng'">
          <div class="tiny dim">全场抽数 {{ rngSummary.total }} 次，其中带用途标签 {{ rngSummary.labeled }} 次，未标注 {{ rngSummary.unlabeled }} 次</div>
          <div class="taglist">
            <span v-for="(n, t) in rngSummary.byTag" :key="t" class="tag" :class="{ unlabeled: t === 'unlabeled' }">{{ t }} ×{{ n }}</span>
          </div>
          <template v-if="rng">
            <div class="tiny dim" style="margin-top:6px">本帧用到：</div>
            <div v-for="d in rng.draws" :key="d.n" class="kv">
              <span class="k">#{{ d.n }}</span><span class="mono">{{ d.v.toFixed(6) }}</span><span class="tiny dim">{{ d.tag }}</span>
            </div>
          </template>
          <div v-else class="dim small">这一帧没有消耗随机数。</div>
          <p class="note">未标注的抽数如实显示为 unlabeled —— 不假装每一个随机都知道用途。</p>
        </template>

        <!-- 事件链（§28） -->
        <template v-else>
          <div v-if="ancestry.length" class="clist">
            <div v-for="(n, i) in ancestry" :key="n.seq" class="cnode" :style="{ marginLeft: (i * 10) + 'px' }">
              <span class="mono tiny">#{{ n.seq }}</span> {{ n.code }}
              <span class="tiny dim">depth {{ n.depth }} · handlers {{ n.handlerCount }}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div v-else class="dim small">该帧不属于任何事件（开局前的初始化帧）。</div>
          <p class="note">事件链是「这次伤害是被哪次技能触发的、再往上是谁」的唯一依据；开局阶段与每个回合都是节点。</p>
        </template>
      </aside>
    </div>
  </section>
</template>

<!-- <style> 已省略 6 KB（纯样式，无逻辑） -->
```

### `apps/web/src/components/theater/EntityCard.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// components/theater/EntityCard.vue — 实时 Entity Card（a1-4 §13）
// 显示：HP / Shield / 行动条位置 / Buff·Debuff / 当前八维 / 死亡·变形状态
import type { UnitView } from "../../theater/presenter.ts";

defineProps<{ unit: UnitView; compact?: boolean }>();
const fmt = (n: number) => (n >= 10000 ? (n / 10000).toFixed(1) + "w" : Math.round(n).toLocaleString());
// ★★ 第 63 轮：从 Record<string, string> 收紧成**闭合** Record<UnitView["state"], string>。
//   原来那样写，presenter 的 state 联合一旦多一个状态，这里就静默少一个键 ——
//   卡片上会显示空白（undefined），而没有任何检查会红。这正是第 61 轮 KLASS_LABEL 的同一形状。
//   收紧之后，"加状态忘了加标签"会在 vue-tsc 阶段报错，而 tests/ui12-typecheck.ts 把 vue-tsc 放进了套件。
const STATE_LABEL: Record<UnitView["state"], string> = {
  ALIVE: "存活", HP_ZERO: "HP≤0", DEAD: "已阵亡", KILLED: "被击败", TRANSFORMED: "变形中",
};
</script>

<template>
  <div class="card" :class="[unit.side, { dead: !unit.alive, actor: unit.isActor, target: unit.isTarget, transformed: unit.state === 'TRANSFORMED' }]">
    <div class="top">
      <span class="nm">{{ unit.name }}</span>
      <!--
        ★★★ 第 139 轮：判据从「死没死」改成「**状态是不是 ALIVE**」。
          原来是 v-if="!unit.alive" ⇒ 变形中的单位（alive=true）**永远不显示徽标**，
          于是样式表里那条 .state.transformed（琥珀色）是**渲染不到的规则**，
          而 §18 要求「死亡状态机可视化」——变形是这条状态机的一态，画面上却一个字都没有。
          （SSR 渲染断言当场把这件事照出来了：类名在、文字不在。）
        ★ 锁定标记从 v-else-if 改成独立判断：变形与锁定是两件事，不该互相顶掉。
      -->
      <span v-if="unit.state !== 'ALIVE'" class="state" :class="unit.state.toLowerCase()">{{ STATE_LABEL[unit.state] }}</span>
      <span v-if="unit.locked" class="lock" title="行动条被锁定">🔒</span>
    </div>

    <div class="hpbar" :title="`${Math.round(unit.hp)} / ${unit.maxHp}`">
      <i :style="{ width: unit.hpPct + '%' }" />
      <span class="hptxt mono">{{ Math.round(unit.hpPct) }}%</span>
    </div>
    <div class="hpline mono tiny">
      {{ fmt(unit.hp) }} / {{ fmt(unit.maxHp) }}
      <span v-if="unit.shield > 0" class="shield">🛡 {{ fmt(unit.shield) }}</span>
    </div>

    <!-- 行动条位置：这一格是"跑条"的可视化 -->
    <div class="track" :title="`行动条 ${(unit.timeline * 100).toFixed(1)}%`">
      <i :style="{ left: (unit.timeline * 100) + '%' }" />
    </div>

    <div v-if="unit.statuses.length" class="buffs">
      <span v-for="s in unit.statuses.slice(0, 6)" :key="s.tag ?? s.id" class="buff" :class="{ debuff: s.isDebuff }"
            :title="`${s.id}\n剩余 ${s.remaining} 回合 · ${s.stacks} 层 · tag ${s.tag ?? '—'}${s.effects ? ' · 生效效果 ' + s.effects + ' 项' : ''}`">
        {{ s.id.length > 8 ? s.id.slice(0, 8) : s.id }}<i v-if="s.stacks > 1">×{{ s.stacks }}</i>
      </span>
      <span v-if="unit.statuses.length > 6" class="tiny dim">+{{ unit.statuses.length - 6 }}</span>
    </div>

    <div v-if="!compact" class="stats mono tiny">
      <span title="攻击">攻 {{ fmt(unit.stats.atk) }}</span>
      <span title="防御">防 {{ Math.round(unit.stats.def) }}</span>
      <span title="速度">速 {{ unit.stats.spd.toFixed(2) }}</span>
      <span title="暴击 / 爆伤">暴 {{ (unit.stats.cri * 100).toFixed(0) }}/{{ (unit.stats.crid * 100).toFixed(0) }}</span>
      <!-- ★ 这两维在引擎里**没有任何消费者**（没有「先判定命中、再结算」这一级）。
           不加区分地显示，看的人会以为它在参与结算 —— 所以加「未结算」标记，
           与 Fidelity 报告里的同一条 caveat 对应。 -->
      <span class="unused" title="效果命中 / 效果抵抗：引擎未参与结算（没有「先判定命中、再结算」这一级），这里只是面板记录">命 {{ (unit.stats.efh * 100).toFixed(0) }}/{{ (unit.stats.efr * 100).toFixed(0) }}<i>未结算</i></span>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<!-- <style> 已省略 2 KB（纯样式，无逻辑） -->
```

### `apps/web/src/styles/base.css`

```css
/* styles/base.css — UI 2.0 设计令牌（a1-4 §30「和风战术分析终端」）
   原则：**底层是现代分析工具，上层才是阴阳师的视觉语言**。
   不要完全仿游戏：背景深色偏暖（墨黑/深褐），红蓝用低饱和朱红与靛青，
   卡片是半透明深色 + 细金边，强调色是金色；
   状态**不靠五颜六色**，主要靠图标 / 形状 / 文字表达。 */
:root {
  /* 底层：墨黑偏暖 */
  --bg: #12100e;            /* 墨黑带褐 */
  --bg2: #171411;           /* 和纸背衬 */
  --panel: #1b1815e6;       /* 半透明深色卡片 */
  --panel2: #221e19;
  --line: #3a3129;          /* 深褐细线 */
  --gold: #c9a961;          /* 强调：金 */
  --gold-dim: #8a7442;
  --fg: #ebe4d8;            /* 暖白 */
  --fg-dim: #a89e8d;
  --fg-faint: #756c5e;

  /* 阵营：低饱和朱红 / 靛青 */
  --red: #b4544a;
  --red-deep: #6d2f28;
  --blue: #4a6f96;
  --blue-deep: #2b445c;

  /* 语义色：克制使用，只在必须区分时出现 */
  --ok: #6a9c78;
  --warn: #c39a4e;
  --danger: #b8524a;
  --rng: #7d6ba8;

  --radius: 10px;
  --radius-sm: 6px;
  --mono: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  color-scheme: dark;
}
* { box-sizing: border-box; }
html, body, #app { height: 100%; margin: 0; }
body {
  background:
    /* 极轻的和纸纹理：两层极低透明度的斜向细纹，不抢内容 */
    repeating-linear-gradient(45deg, #ffffff05 0 1px, transparent 1px 4px),
    repeating-linear-gradient(-45deg, #ffffff03 0 1px, transparent 1px 6px),
    radial-gradient(1200px 600px at 50% -10%, #2a211a 0%, transparent 70%),
    var(--bg);
  color: var(--fg);
  font: 13px/1.55 "PingFang SC", "Microsoft YaHei", system-ui, sans-serif;
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
}
h1, h2, h3, h4 { letter-spacing: .02em; }
button {
  font: inherit; color: var(--fg); background: var(--panel2);
  border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: var(--radius-sm); padding: 5px 11px; cursor: pointer;
  transition: border-color .15s, background .15s, color .15s;
}
button:hover:not(:disabled) { border-color: var(--gold-dim); background: #2a241d; }
button:disabled { opacity: .4; cursor: not-allowed; }
button.primary { background: linear-gradient(180deg, #4a6f96, #35526f); border-color: #5b83aa; color: #f3f6fa; }
button.gold { background: linear-gradient(180deg, #6b5a2e, #4d4021); border-color: var(--gold-dim); color: #f0e4c6; }
input, select, textarea {
  font: inherit; color: var(--fg); background: #0e0c0a; border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm); padding: 5px 8px; width: 100%;
}
input:focus, select:focus, textarea:focus { outline: none; border-color: var(--gold-dim); box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px #c9a96122; }
input[type=range] { padding: 0; background: none; border: none; }
code, .mono { font-family: var(--mono); }
/* 细金边卡片：全站统一的"卡片"观感 */
.card, .panel {
  background: var(--panel);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius);
  box-shadow: inset 0 1px 0 #ffffff08, 0 1px 2px #00000060;
  backdrop-filter: blur(2px);
}
::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 10px; height: 10px; }
::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: #3a3129; border-radius: 5px; }
::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background: transparent; }
::selection { background: #c9a96144; }
```

### `apps/web/tests/e2e-workflow.ts`

```ts
// tests/e2e-workflow.ts — M1.8 §30 端到端验收（无需终端操作、无需手改 JSON）
// 走的是 UI 完全相同的链路：matchStore（Pinia）→ Worker 协议 → 引擎适配器 → 结果契约 → 回放。
// 与浏览器里点击的唯一差别是线程边界（Worker 换成了直接函数调用），协议逻辑是同一份代码。
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { dirname, join } from "node:path";
import { createPinia, setActivePinia } from "pinia";
import { createProtocol } from "../src/workers/protocol.ts";
import { createEngineAdapter } from "../src/adapters/engine-adapter.ts";
import { canonicalMatch } from "../src/mock/canonical-match.ts";
import { isConclusionReady } from "../src/contracts/simulation-result.ts";
import { useMatchStore } from "../src/stores/match.ts";
import type { WorkerRequest, WorkerResponse } from "../src/contracts/worker-api.ts";

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
let pass = 0, fail = 0;
const ok = (n: string, c: boolean, extra = "") => { if (c) { pass++; console.log("  ✓ " + n); } else { fail++; console.log("  ✗ " + n + (extra ? "  — " + extra : "")); } };
const sec = (s: string) => console.log("\n" + s);

const bundle = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(here, "..", "src", "engine", "bundle.json"), "utf8"));
const outbox: WorkerResponse[] = [];
const protocol = createProtocol(createEngineAdapter(bundle), (m) => outbox.push(m));
const send = (req: WorkerRequest) => protocol.handle(req);
const last = <T,>(type: string) => [...outbox].reverse().find(m => m.type === type) as T | undefined;
const N = Number(process.env.E2E_N ?? 10000);

setActivePinia(createPinia());
const match = useMatchStore();

console.log(`══ M1.8 §30 端到端验收（N=${N}） ══`);

sec("步骤 1/8 · 输入：把对局录进工作台（这里等价于『照着截图逐格填』的结果）");
match.replace(canonicalMatch());
ok("红蓝各 5 只，共 10 只", match.filledCount === 10, String(match.filledCount));
ok("★ 输入结构里没有御魂位置（护栏 #3）", JSON.stringify(match.match).includes("soulPieces") === false);
ok("草稿自动落盘（护栏 #10，无 localStorage 时静默）", match.savedAt === null || typeof match.savedAt === "string");

sec("步骤 2/8 · 校验（VALIDATE）");
const t0 = Date.now();
await send({ type: "VALIDATE", requestId: "v1", match: match.match });
const val = last<any>("VALIDATION_READY")!.report;
ok("校验返回且无阻断", val.ok, val.blockers.join("; "));
ok("10 只式神全部 Join 到知识库", val.coverage.unitsJoined === 10);

sec("步骤 3/8 · 覆盖度与 UNKNOWN（跑之前必须看到）");
ok("技能 10/10 可用", val.coverage.skillsAvailable === 10);
ok("AI 高置信度 10/10", val.coverage.aiHigh === 10, `${val.coverage.aiHigh}/${val.coverage.aiTotal}`);
ok("★ UNKNOWN 机制数量被显式给出（不是 0 也不是隐藏）", val.strategySnapshot.unknownCount >= 1, String(val.strategySnapshot.unknownCount));
ok("★ 结论可用性判定给出理由", (() => { const r = isConclusionReady({ strategySnapshot: val.strategySnapshot, decisive: 10000, timeouts: 0, errors: 0, simulations: 10000 } as any); return !r.ready && r.reasons.length > 0; })());
ok("数据/模型缺口逐条列出", val.warnings.length >= 2, String(val.warnings.length));

sec(`步骤 4/8 · 模拟 ${N.toLocaleString()} 局（进度不阻塞、可取消）`);
let progressCount = 0;
const tSim = Date.now();
await send({ type: "SIMULATE", requestId: "s1", match: match.match, options: { simulations: N, seed: 20261001, progressEvery: Math.max(100, N / 20) } });
const simMs = Date.now() - tSim;
progressCount = outbox.filter(m => m.type === "SIMULATION_PROGRESS").length;
const res = last<any>("SIMULATION_COMPLETED")!.result;
ok("模拟完成且无异常", res.errors === 0, `errors=${res.errors}`);
ok(`进度回调 ≥ 5 次（${progressCount} 次）`, progressCount >= 5);
ok(`${N} 局在 ${(simMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s 内完成`, simMs < 600000);

sec("步骤 5/8 · 胜率 + Wilson 置信区间");
ok("红蓝胜率都在 [0,1]", res.redWinRate > 0 && res.redWinRate < 1 && Math.abs(res.redWinRate + res.blueWinRate - 1) < 1e-9);
ok("★ Wilson 区间覆盖点估计", res.confidenceInterval.low < res.redWinRate && res.redWinRate < res.confidenceInterval.high);
ok("★ 分母口径：红+蓝 = decisive，超时/异常永不进入", res.redWins + res.blueWins === res.decisive && res.simulations === res.decisive + res.timeouts + res.draws);
ok("结果自带引擎版本与数据版本（可追溯）", !!res.engineVersion && !!res.gameDataVersion);
console.log(`      红方 ${(res.redWinRate * 100).toFixed(2)}%  Wilson95% [${(res.confidenceInterval.low * 100).toFixed(2)}%, ${(res.confidenceInterval.high * 100).toFixed(2)}%]  |  平均 ${res.battleStats.avgTurns} 回合`);

sec("步骤 6/8 · 策略敏感性（模型不确定性 ≠ 抽样误差）");
ok("敏感性扫描已执行", !!res.modelUncertainty, "N 不足 800 时会跳过");
ok("覆盖开局 / 死亡 / 回合上限三类假设", new Set(res.modelUncertainty.perStrategy.map((p: any) => p.group)).size >= 3);
ok("★ 漂移被量化，且与置信区间分开呈现", typeof res.modelUncertainty.spread === "number" && res.modelUncertainty.spread >= 0);
ok("★ 敏感时必须给出 BLOCK 级警告（不许淡化）", res.modelUncertainty.verdict !== "SENSITIVE" || res.warnings.some((w: any) => w.severity === "BLOCK"));

sec("步骤 7/8 · 选一个 seed 回放");
const seed = res.seeds?.[0]?.seed;
ok("结果给出了代表性 seed", typeof seed === "number", JSON.stringify(res.seeds));
await send({ type: "REPLAY", requestId: "r1", match: match.match, seed });
const rep = last<any>("REPLAY_PREPARED")!;
ok("回放事件流非空", rep.events.length > 10, `events=${rep.events.length}`);
ok("★ 回放的胜负与模拟里该 seed 的记录一致", rep.winner === res.seeds[0].winner, `replay=${rep.winner} seed-record=${res.seeds[0].winner}`);
ok("★ 回合数一致", rep.turns === res.seeds[0].turns, `replay=${rep.turns} seed-record=${res.seeds[0].turns}`);

sec("步骤 8/8 · 逐步回放所需的数据齐备（UI-4 的输入）");
ok("每个事件都有回合号（可分组步进）", rep.events.every((e: any) => typeof e.turn === "number"));
ok("事件带中文文案与结构化 detail", rep.events.every((e: any) => e.text && e.detail !== undefined));
ok("单位表含 maxHp（血条可还原）", rep.units.length === 10 && rep.units.every((u: any) => u.maxHp > 0));
ok("存在 DEATH/LETHAL 事件（战斗真的打完了）", rep.events.some((e: any) => ["DEATH", "LETHAL"].includes(e.code)));

sec("附加 · 取消与错误路径");
// 取消必须发生在**运行中**：先不 await 地发起，让出事件循环后再取消
const beforeCancel = outbox.filter(m => m.type === "SIMULATION_FAILED").length;
const pending = send({ type: "SIMULATE", requestId: "s2", match: match.match, options: { simulations: 200000, seed: 5, progressEvery: 500 } });
await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 250));
await send({ type: "CANCEL", requestId: "s2" });
await pending;
const afterCancel = outbox.filter(m => m.type === "SIMULATION_FAILED").length;
ok("★ 运行中取消 → 收到 SIMULATION_FAILED（而不是假装成功）", afterCancel > beforeCancel, `${beforeCancel} → ${afterCancel}`);
ok("取消的请求没有发出 SIMULATION_COMPLETED", !outbox.some(m => m.type === "SIMULATION_COMPLETED" && m.requestId === "s2"));

sec("附加 · 非法输入必须被挡住");
const broken = canonicalMatch();
broken.blue.units[4].stats.hp = 0;
await send({ type: "VALIDATE", requestId: "v2", match: broken });
const v2 = last<any>("VALIDATION_READY")!.report;
ok("面板未填 → 阻断并指明是哪一只", !v2.ok && v2.blockers.some((b: string) => /蓝方第 5 位/.test(b)), v2.blockers.join("; "));

console.log(`\n══════════════════════════════════`);
console.log(`§30 端到端：${pass} 通过 / ${fail} 失败   总耗时 ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s`);
console.log(`══════════════════════════════════`);
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
```

### `apps/web/tests/ui0-contracts.ts`

```ts
// tests/ui0-contracts.ts — UI-0 验收：契约 + Worker 协议 + mock 链路（Node 直接运行，无需浏览器）
import { canonicalMatch } from "../src/mock/canonical-match.ts";
import { mockAdapter } from "../src/adapters/mock-adapter.ts";
import { swapSides, emptyMatch, TEAM_SIZE } from "../src/contracts/guessing-match.ts";
import { isConclusionReady } from "../src/contracts/simulation-result.ts";
import type { SimulationResult } from "../src/contracts/simulation-result.ts";

let pass = 0, fail = 0;
const ok = (n: string, c: boolean, x = "") => { if (c) { pass++; console.log("  PASS " + n + (x ? "  " + x : "")); } else { fail++; console.log("  FAIL " + n + "  " + x); } };

console.log("=== UI-0 契约验收 ===");

// ── ① 契约形状：GuessingMatch 永不出现 soulPieces
const m = canonicalMatch();
const json = JSON.stringify(m);
ok("★ GuessingMatch 不含 soulPieces（竞猜 ≠ 养成）", !json.includes("soulPieces") && !json.includes("soulLoadout"));
ok("schemaVersion / ruleset 已冻结", m.schemaVersion === 1 && m.ruleset === "GUESSING");
ok("每队 5 只", m.red.units.length === TEAM_SIZE && m.blue.units.length === TEAM_SIZE);
ok("八维字段完整", m.red.units.every(u =>
  ["attack","hp","defense","speed","critRate","critDamage","effectHit","effectResist"].every(k => typeof (u.stats as any)[k] === "number")));
ok("unitId 唯一（UI 定位用）", new Set([...m.red.units, ...m.blue.units].map(u => u.unitId)).size === 10);

// ── ② 红蓝互换（UX 护栏 #1）
const swapped = swapSides(m);
ok("交换红蓝后红方 = 原蓝方", swapped.red.units.map(u => u.name).join() === m.blue.units.map(u => u.name).join());
ok("交换两次回到原阵容（名称层面）", swapSides(swapped).red.units.map(u => u.name).join() === m.red.units.map(u => u.name).join());
ok("交换后 unitId 全部刷新（不串位）",
   [...swapped.red.units, ...swapped.blue.units].every(u => ![...m.red.units, ...m.blue.units].some(o => o.unitId === u.unitId)));

// ── ③ 校验器
const empty = emptyMatch();
const vr = await mockAdapter.validate(empty);
ok("空阵容全部被拦下（blockers=10，每队 5 只）", !vr.ok && vr.blockers.length === 10, `blockers=${vr.blockers.length}`);
const vr2 = await mockAdapter.validate(m);
ok("完整阵容通过校验", vr2.ok, JSON.stringify(vr2.blockers));
ok("★ UNKNOWN 计数随校验结果返回（永不隐藏）", vr2.strategySnapshot.unknownCount > 0, `unknown=${vr2.strategySnapshot.unknownCount}`);
ok("Coverage 快照完整", vr2.coverage.unitsTotal === 10 && Array.isArray(vr2.coverage.unsupportedCapabilities));

// ── ④ 模拟：进度回调 + 契约不变式
let progressCalls = 0, lastCompleted = 0, monotonic = true;
const result: SimulationResult = await mockAdapter.simulate(
  m, { simulations: 2000, seed: 12345, progressEvery: 200 },
  (p) => { progressCalls++; if (p.completed < lastCompleted) monotonic = false; lastCompleted = p.completed; },
  () => false,
);
ok("进度回调被多次触发且单调递增", progressCalls >= 5 && monotonic, `calls=${progressCalls} last=${lastCompleted}`);

const sum = result.redWins + result.blueWins + result.draws + result.timeouts + result.errors;
ok("★ 五类结局之和 = 总模拟数（无遗漏）", sum === result.simulations, `${sum} vs ${result.simulations}`);
ok("★ errors 永不进入分母", result.decisive === result.redWins + result.blueWins,
   `decisive=${result.decisive} red+blue=${result.redWins + result.blueWins}`);
ok("Wilson 区间存在且包含点估计", !!result.confidenceInterval
   && result.confidenceInterval.method === "WILSON"
   && result.redWinRate! >= result.confidenceInterval.low
   && result.redWinRate! <= result.confidenceInterval.high,
   result.confidenceInterval ? `${(result.redWinRate!*100).toFixed(2)}% ∈ [${(result.confidenceInterval.low*100).toFixed(2)}%, ${(result.confidenceInterval.high*100).toFixed(2)}%]` : "无");
ok("★ 模型不确定性 与 抽样误差 分开返回", !!result.modelUncertainty && result.modelUncertainty.perStrategy.length > 1,
   result.modelUncertainty ? `spread=${(result.modelUncertainty.spread!*100).toFixed(1)}pp verdict=${result.modelUncertainty.verdict}` : "无");
ok("策略快照随结果保存", result.strategySnapshot.unknownCount > 0);
ok("版本随结果保存", !!result.engineVersion && !!result.gameDataVersion, `${result.engineVersion} / ${result.gameDataVersion}`);
ok("警告包含 UNKNOWN_STRATEGY", result.warnings.some(w => w.kind === "UNKNOWN_STRATEGY"));
ok("代表局 seed 返回", !!result.seeds && result.seeds.length > 0);
ok("每只式神都有贡献统计", result.battleStats.perUnit.length === 10, `perUnit=${result.battleStats.perUnit.length}`);

// ── ⑤ 结论判定：UNKNOWN 存在时必须判"不可下结论"
const v = isConclusionReady(result);
ok("★ 存在 UNKNOWN 时判定为不可下结论", !v.ready, v.reasons.join("；"));

// ── ⑥ 同 seed 可复现
const r2 = await mockAdapter.simulate(m, { simulations: 2000, seed: 12345 }, () => {}, () => false);
ok("同 seed 结果一致（可复现）", r2.redWins === result.redWins && r2.blueWins === result.blueWins,
   `${r2.redWins}/${r2.blueWins} vs ${result.redWins}/${result.blueWins}`);

// ── ⑦ 取消
let cancelled = false;
try {
  await mockAdapter.simulate(m, { simulations: 20000, seed: 1, progressEvery: 100 }, () => { cancelled = true; }, () => true);
} catch { /* 预期抛错 */ }
ok("支持取消（shouldCancel 被响应）", cancelled);

// ── ⑧ Replay 契约
const rep = await mockAdapter.replay(m, 143822);
ok("Replay 返回事件流与单位表", rep.events.length > 0 && rep.units.length === 10, `events=${rep.events.length} units=${rep.units.length}`);
ok("事件含 Event Inspector 所需 detail", rep.events.some(e => e.code === "DAMAGE" && e.detail && "randomRoll" in e.detail));
ok("事件 index 连续", rep.events.every((e, i) => e.index === i));

console.log(`\n=== UI-0: ${pass} passed, ${fail} failed ===`);
if (fail > 0) process.exitCode = 1;
```

### `apps/web/tests/ui1-editor.ts`

```ts
// tests/ui1-editor.ts — UI-1（阵容编辑器）验收测试
// 运行：node tests/ui1-editor.ts
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { dirname, join } from "node:path";

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const src = join(here, "..", "src");
let pass = 0, fail = 0;
const ok = (name: string, cond: boolean, extra = "") => {
  if (cond) { pass++; console.log("  ✓ " + name); }
  else { fail++; console.log("  ✗ " + name + (extra ? "  — " + extra : "")); }
};
const section = (s: string) => console.log("\n" + s);

const J = (p: string) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(src, p), "utf8"));
const S = (p: string) => readFileSync(join(src, p), "utf8");

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
section("[1] 契约层：竞猜输入的形状（护栏 #3：永不出现御魂位置）");
const gm = await import("../src/contracts/guessing-match.ts");
const STAT_KEYS: string[] = gm.STAT_KEYS;
const STAT_LABELS: Record<string, string> = gm.STAT_LABELS;
ok("STAT_KEYS 恰好 8 维（攻/生/防/速/暴击/爆伤/效果命中/效果抵抗）", STAT_KEYS.length === 8, STAT_KEYS.join(","));
ok("每个维度都有中文标签", STAT_KEYS.every(k => !!STAT_LABELS[k]));
const m0 = gm.emptyMatch();
ok("emptyMatch 合法（schemaVersion=1, 5v5）", m0.schemaVersion === 1 && m0.red.units.length === 5 && m0.blue.units.length === 5);
ok("空单位爆伤默认 150%（游戏基线），其余维度为 0 待填", m0.red.units[0].stats.critDamage === 150 && m0.red.units[0].stats.attack === 0);
ok("GuessingUnitInput 无 soulPieces 字段（护栏 #3）", !("soulPieces" in m0.red.units[0]));
const swapped = gm.swapSides(m0);
ok("swapSides 保留 unitId 与顺序长度", swapped.red.units.length === 5 && swapped.blue.units.length === 5);
ok("swapSides 不共享引用（改红不影响蓝）", (() => { const s = gm.swapSides(m0); s.red.units[0].name = "X"; return s.blue.units[0].name !== "X"; })());
ok("newUnitId 唯一", gm.newUnitId() !== gm.newUnitId());

section("[2] 知识库索引：276 只式神全量可检索");
const idx = J("kb/shikigami-index.json") as any[];
ok("式神索引 276 条（图鉴 100%）", idx.length === 276, "got " + idx.length);
ok("每条含 id/名称/稀有度/关键词/拼音/首字母", idx.every(x => x.i > 0 && x.n && x.r && Array.isArray(x.t)));
ok("拼音覆盖 276/276", idx.filter(x => x.p).length === 276, idx.filter(x => !x.p).map(x => x.n).join(","));
  /**
   * ★★★ 第 162 轮按事实改写：**「有面板」的判据收紧了** —— 原来判 `!!s.panel`，于是 7 只「只有速度/暴击/抵抗」
   *   的式神也被标成「有面板」（生剥鬼 / 慧明灯 / 盗人神 / 湍津姬 / 木之本樱 / 李小狼 / 妖刀姬·绯夜猎刃）。
   *   现在判据是「攻/生/防至少一项非 null」，所以覆盖数是 **269/276**，少的那 7 只如实标着。
   *   ★ 两向：这 7 只的 pn 前三位必须真的是 null（判据不是拍脑袋），且它们仍然带着速度/暴击/抵抗（数据没有丢）。
   */
  const noPanel = idx.filter(x => !x.has.panel);
  ok("has.panel 覆盖 269/276（7 只只有速度/暴击/抵抗）", idx.filter(x => x.has.panel).length === 269, "got " + idx.filter(x => x.has.panel).length);
  ok("★★ 这 7 只就是判据收紧后如实标出的那些：生剥鬼/慧明灯/盗人神/湍津姬/木之本樱/李小狼/妖刀姬·绯夜猎刃",
    noPanel.length === 7 && ["生剥鬼", "慧明灯", "盗人神", "湍津姬", "木之本樱", "李小狼", "妖刀姬·绯夜猎刃"].every(n => noPanel.some(x => x.n === n)),
    JSON.stringify(noPanel.map(x => x.n)));
  ok("★★ 而它们的 pn 前三位确实是 null（攻/生/防没有数据），后五位仍带着速度/暴击/爆伤/命中/抵抗",
    noPanel.every(x => Array.isArray(x.pn) && x.pn[0] === null && x.pn[1] === null && x.pn[2] === null && x.pn[3] != null),
    JSON.stringify(noPanel.map(x => x.n + ":" + JSON.stringify(x.pn))));
ok("has.skills>0 覆盖 276/276", idx.filter(x => x.has.skills > 0).length === 276);
const aiN = idx.filter(x => x.has.ai).length;
ok("has.ai 覆盖 272/276（4 只 2026 新式神待补）", aiN === 272, "got " + aiN);
const noAi = idx.filter(x => !x.has.ai).map(x => x.n);
ok("缺失 AI 的正是 2026 新式神", noAi.length === 4, noAi.join(","));

section("[3] 搜索：ID / 全名 / 别名 / 全拼 / 首字母");
const kb = await import("../src/kb/useKb.ts").catch(() => null as any);
// useKb 依赖 vue 的 ref/computed —— 单独复刻搜索打分函数验证索引字段可用性
const search = (q: string) => {
  const s = q.trim().toLowerCase();
  const scored = idx.map(x => {
    let sc = -1;
    if (String(x.i) === s) sc = 100;
    else if (x.n === s) sc = 95;
    else if (x.n.toLowerCase().startsWith(s)) sc = 90;
    else if (x.n.toLowerCase().includes(s)) sc = 80;
    else if (x.k && x.k.toLowerCase().includes(s)) sc = 75;
    else if (x.p && x.p.startsWith(s)) sc = 70;
    else if (x.s && x.s.startsWith(s)) sc = 60;
    else if (x.t.some((t: string) => t.includes(s))) sc = 40;
    return { x, sc };
  }).filter(v => v.sc > 0);
  return scored.sort((a, b) => b.sc - a.sc || a.x.i - b.x.i).map(v => v.x);
};
ok("按 ID 精确命中（202 三尾狐）", search("202")[0]?.n === "三尾狐" || search("202")[0]?.i === 202, JSON.stringify(search("202")[0]));
ok("按全名命中（座敷童子）", search("座敷童子")[0]?.n.includes("座敷"), JSON.stringify(search("座敷童子")[0]?.n));
ok("按拼音命中（sanweihu）", search("sanweihu").length > 0 && search("sanweihu")[0].i === 202, JSON.stringify(search("sanweihu").map(x => x.n).slice(0, 3)));
ok("按首字母命中（swh）", search("swh").some(x => x.i === 202), JSON.stringify(search("swh").map(x => x.n).slice(0, 3)));
ok("模糊词命中不报错（姬）", search("姬").length > 0, String(search("姬").length));
ok("乱码查询返回空而非异常", search("zzzzqqqq").length === 0);

section("[4] 御魂索引：70 条 + Engine Support 前置告知（护栏 #5）");
const souls = J("kb/soul-index.json") as any[];
ok("御魂 70 条（审计 0 缺失）", souls.length === 70, "got " + souls.length);
ok("每条含 2件套/4件套/引擎支持度", souls.every(s => Number.isInteger(s.i) && s.n && "suit" in s && ["FULL", "PARTIAL", "UNSUPPORTED"].includes(s.engine.support)));
ok("御魂 id 唯一（0..69，雪幽魂 id=0 是数据事实不是缺失）", new Set(souls.map(s => s.i)).size === 70);
const dist = souls.reduce((a: any, s: any) => (a[s.engine.support] = (a[s.engine.support] ?? 0) + 1, a), {});
// ★ 分布从 5/33/32 变成 6/32/32：第 25 轮把**火灵**（战斗开始时获得 3 点鬼火）从 UNSUPPORTED
//   升级为真实现（ORB_START，会写 ORB_GAIN 事件）。索引是在那之后重新生成的，
//   而这条断言原先钉在旧世界上 —— 正是"重新生成产物会暴露陈旧断言"。
ok("支持度分布 = FULL 6 / PARTIAL 32 / UNSUPPORTED 32（火灵已升为 FULL）",
   dist.FULL === 6 && dist.PARTIAL === 32 && dist.UNSUPPORTED === 32, JSON.stringify(dist));
ok("非 FULL 的御魂都带 note（供 UI 显示原因）", souls.filter(s => s.engine.support !== "FULL").every(s => typeof s.engine.note === "string"));
ok("御魂选择器不提供静态属性输入（护栏 #4：绝不重复计算）", !/静态属性/.test(S("components/lineup/SoulSelector.vue")) && !/>\s*攻击\s*</.test(S("components/lineup/SoulSelector.vue")));

section("[5] 详情懒加载数据：276 只 panel+skills");
const det = J("kb/shikigami-detail.json") as Record<string, any>;
ok("detail 键数 276", Object.keys(det).length === 276, "got " + Object.keys(det).length);
ok("每只都有 panel 且速度有效", Object.values(det).every((d: any) => d.panel && d.panel.spd > 0));
const NULL_ATK = ["433", "552", "558", "571", "587", "588", "593"];   // 源数据 Attribute.json 缺基础攻/生/防
const nullAtk = Object.entries(det).filter(([, d]: any) => d.panel.atk == null).map(([k]) => k);
ok("基础攻/生/防缺失的恰好是已知 7 只（源数据缺口，非解析错误）", nullAtk.length === 7 && nullAtk.every(k => NULL_ATK.includes(k)), nullAtk.join(","));
ok("每只都有 ≥1 技能", Object.values(det).every((d: any) => Array.isArray(d.skills) && d.skills.length >= 1));
ok("技能条数合计 798（含普攻/被动/大招）", Object.values(det).reduce((a: number, d: any) => a + d.skills.length, 0) === 798, String(Object.values(det).reduce((a: number, d: any) => a + d.skills.length, 0)));
ok("典型对局 10 只全部有详情", [202, 297, 601, 225, 346, 205, 358, 267, 352, 390].every(id => det[String(id)]?.panel));

section("[5b] 选中即预填：六星面板随索引下发（免手打 80 个数字）");
const withPanel = idx.filter(x => x.pn);
ok("索引携带面板 276/276", withPanel.length === 276, "got " + withPanel.length);
ok("面板数组长度为 8（攻/生/防/速/暴/爆/命/抵）", withPanel.every(x => x.pn.length === 8));
ok("百分比已换算为百分数（暴击 0.1 → 10）", idx.find(x => x.i === 202)!.pn[4] === 10);
ok("爆伤换算为 150 而非 1.5", idx.find(x => x.i === 202)!.pn[5] === 150);
ok("典型对局 10 只全部有完整基础面板", [202, 297, 601, 225, 346, 205, 358, 267, 352, 390].every(id => { const x = idx.find(v => v.i === id); return x && x.pn[0] != null && x.pn[1] != null; }));
ok("App.vue 只在格子未动过时预填（不覆盖手输）", /untouched/.test(S("App.vue")) && /u\.stats\.attack === 0/.test(S("App.vue")));
ok("预填函数只写非 null 项（源数据缺项不写 0）", /if \(v != null\) out\[keys\[i\]\] = v/.test(S("kb/useKb.ts")));

section("[6] 组件接线：App.vue 与子组件的 props / events 一致");
const app = S("App.vue");
// UI 2.0：阵容改为横排 UnitTile（§31/§32），旧的 LineupPanel/ShikigamiCard 已删除
const COMPONENTS = ["UnitTile", "ShikigamiSelector", "SoulSelector", "HealthCheck", "DetailDrawer", "JsonIo", "FidelityPanel"];
for (const c of COMPONENTS) {
  ok(`App.vue 导入并使用 <${c}>`, new RegExp("import " + c + " from").test(app) && new RegExp("<" + c).test(app));
}
ok("UnitTile 直接挂在 App 上，并且四类事件都接上了", /<UnitTile/.test(app) && /@set-stat=/.test(app) && /@open-soul=/.test(app) && /@open-detail=/.test(app) && /@open-selector=/.test(app));
// 事件名一致性：App 里 @x= 必须是子组件 defineEmits 声明过的
function declaredEvents(file: string): string[] {
  const s = S(file);
  const block = s.match(/defineEmits<\{([\s\S]*?)\}>/)?.[1] ?? "";
  return [...block.matchAll(/\(e:\s*"([a-z-]+)"/g)].map(m => m[1]);
}
function usedEvents(file: string, comp: string): string[] {
  const s = S(file);   // file 是**路径**（此前误传内容，已修正）
  const tag = s.match(new RegExp("<" + comp + "\\b[\\s\\S]*?/>"))?.[0] ?? "";
  if (!tag) throw new Error("模板中找不到 <" + comp + "> 用法");
  return [...tag.matchAll(/@([a-z-]+)=/g)].map(m => m[1]);
}
for (const [c, f] of [["UnitTile", "components/lineup/UnitTile.vue"], ["ShikigamiSelector", "components/lineup/ShikigamiSelector.vue"], ["SoulSelector", "components/lineup/SoulSelector.vue"], ["HealthCheck", "components/simulation/HealthCheck.vue"], ["DetailDrawer", "components/knowledge/DetailDrawer.vue"], ["JsonIo", "components/common/JsonIo.vue"]] as const) {
  const decl = declaredEvents(f), used = usedEvents("App.vue", c);
  const undeclared = used.filter(e => !decl.includes(e));
  ok(`<${c}> 事件全部已声明`, undeclared.length === 0, "未声明: " + undeclared.join(",") + " | 已声明: " + decl.join(","));
}
ok("UnitTile 的事件全部冒泡到 App（无静默丢弃）", (() => {
  const s = S("components/lineup/UnitTile.vue");
  return ["set-stat", "open-selector", "open-soul", "clear", "open-detail", "activate"].every(e => s.includes(e));
})());

section("[7] 护栏 #10：草稿自动保存");
const ms = S("stores/match.ts");
ok("草稿 key 带版本号（结构变更可失效）", /yys\.workbench\.match\.draft\.v1/.test(ms));
ok("载入草稿校验 schemaVersion 与红蓝结构", /schemaVersion !== 1/.test(ms));
ok("配额异常不阻塞操作（try/catch 静默）", /catch \{ \/\* 配额满时静默/.test(ms));
ok("store 只存 DTO（不 import engine）", !/from ".*battle-lab/.test(ms) && !/from "@engine/.test(ms));

section("[8] 组件不依赖引擎内部对象（只依赖契约与 KB 索引）");
for (const f of ["components/lineup/UnitTile.vue", "components/lineup/UnitTile.vue", "components/simulation/HealthCheck.vue", "components/knowledge/DetailDrawer.vue", "components/common/JsonIo.vue"]) {
  ok(`${f} 不 import battle-lab`, !/@engine|battle-lab/.test(S(f)));
}

console.log(`\n══════════════════════════════════`);
console.log(`UI-1 验收：${pass} 通过 / ${fail} 失败`);
console.log(`══════════════════════════════════`);
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
```

### `apps/web/tests/ui10-fidelity.ts`

```ts
// tests/ui10-fidelity.ts — ★ Per-Match Fidelity 验收（a1-4 §1-§10、§33）
//
// 文档最强调的一条（§3）：**不能说"当前模拟器有 80% 真实性"**。
// 所以这里既验证"五层都算得出来"，更验证"**没有任何地方输出百分比评分**"。
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { createEngineAdapter } from "../src/adapters/engine-adapter.ts";
import { canonicalMatch } from "../src/mock/canonical-match.ts";
import { assessFidelity, FIDELITY_CLASS_LABEL, classifySkill, SKILL_CLASS_LABEL as KERNEL_SKILL_LABEL, SKILL_CLASS_MARK as KERNEL_SKILL_MARK } from "../../../battle-lab/kernel/fidelity.ts";
// ★ 第 138 轮：§33 的**逐技能**一层（契约镜像 + 取数助手）
import { SKILL_LABEL, SKILL_MARK, SKILL_TIER_ORDER, SKILL_SEVERITY, skillStatusOf, skillTally, worstSkillClass, groupSkillStatus } from "../src/contracts/fidelity-view.ts";

const here = import.meta.dirname;
let pass = 0, fail = 0;
const ok = (n: string, c: boolean, e = "") => { if (c) { pass++; console.log("  ✓ " + n); } else { fail++; console.log("  ✗ " + n + (e ? "  — " + e : "")); } };
const sec = (s: string) => console.log("\n" + s);
const S = (p: string) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(here, "..", "src", p), "utf8");

const bundle = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(here, "..", "src", "engine", "bundle.json"), "utf8"));
const adapter = createEngineAdapter(bundle);

sec("[1] 纯函数层：分类规则（§10）");
{
  const base = (over: any = {}) => ({
    units: [{ name: "A", side: "red" as const, panelFilled: 4, soul: null,
      skills: [{ name: "s", confidence: "HIGH" as const, cost: 0, parsed: { damage: 1, heal: 0, shield: 0, timeline: 0, status: 0, orb: 0 }, unparsedSentences: 0 }],
      ai: { dataPresent: true, compiled: true, confidence: "HIGH" as const, gameVerified: true } }],
    ruleset: [{ id: "R", status: "VERIFIED" as const }],
    capabilities: [{ name: "DAMAGE", support: "FULL" as const, useCount: 1 }],
    displayPrecision: "EXACT" as const, ...over,
  });
  ok("★ 全 FULL + AI 已验证 + 规则全 VERIFIED → VERIFIED", assessFidelity(base()).klass === "VERIFIED");
  ok("★ 有 UNKNOWN 规则时**绝不允许**判成 VERIFIED", assessFidelity(base({ ruleset: [{ id: "R", status: "UNKNOWN" }] })).klass !== "VERIFIED");
  ok("主动技能无可执行数值 → INSUFFICIENT", assessFidelity(base({ units: [{ ...base().units[0], skills: [{ name: "s", confidence: "LOW", cost: 0, parsed: { damage: 0, heal: 0, shield: 0, timeline: 0, status: 0, orb: 0 }, unparsedSentences: 0 }] }] })).klass === "INSUFFICIENT");
  ok("★ 被动技能无可执行数值 → **不算** INSUFFICIENT（已知边界）", assessFidelity(base({ units: [{ ...base().units[0], skills: [{ name: "p", confidence: "LOW", cost: -1, parsed: { damage: 0, heal: 0, shield: 0, timeline: 0, status: 0, orb: 0 }, unparsedSentences: 0 }] }] })).klass === "VERIFIED");
  ok("机制未实现 → INSUFFICIENT", assessFidelity(base({ capabilities: [{ name: "X", support: "NONE", useCount: 1 }] })).klass === "INSUFFICIENT");
  // ★★ 第 60 轮把这条从「是 PARTIAL 或 COMPLETE_UNVERIFIED 都算过」收紧成**确定的那一个**。
  //   原来那种写法（两个都接受）正好把下面这张映射表的歧义盖住了：
  //   分类到底是按"代码路径完不完整"分，还是按"有没有 UNKNOWN"分，两边都算对。
  const skillPartialCase = (over: any = {}) => base({ units: [{ ...base().units[0], skills: [{ name: "s", confidence: "MEDIUM", cost: 0, parsed: { damage: 1, heal: 0, shield: 0, timeline: 0, status: 0, orb: 0 }, unparsedSentences: 2 }] }], ...over });
  ok("★★ 技能有未建模附加效果 → PARTIAL（代码路径不完整）", assessFidelity(skillPartialCase()).klass === "PARTIAL");

  /**
   * ★★★ 分类映射表（§10）—— 按**成因**分，四种组合各断言一次，两个方向都覆盖。
   *   规格的两条定义：
   *     COMPLETE_UNVERIFIED =「代码路径完整 + 没有 NONE + 但仍包含未游戏实测机制」
   *     PARTIAL             =「存在 PARTIAL，但可以运行」
   *   二者互斥：有 PARTIAL ⇒ 代码路径不完整 ⇒ 只能是 PARTIAL，
   *   此时**哪怕同时有一堆 UNKNOWN 规则**也不能改判成 COMPLETE_UNVERIFIED（那会过度声称）。
   *   第 60 轮之前这里错了 4 种组合，而且错的方向是过度声称 —— 见报告 §45。
   */
  const capPartial = { capabilities: [{ name: "DAMAGE", support: "PARTIAL" as const, useCount: 1 }] };
  const capNone = { capabilities: [{ name: "X", support: "NONE" as const, useCount: 1 }] };
  const aiNo = { units: [{ ...base().units[0], ai: { dataPresent: true, compiled: true, confidence: "HIGH" as const, gameVerified: false } }] };
  const ruleUnknown = { ruleset: [{ id: "R", status: "UNKNOWN" as const }] };
  const ruleHypo = { ruleset: [{ id: "R", status: "HYPOTHESIS" as const }] };
  const table: Array<[string, any, string]> = [
    ["① 全 FULL + AI 已验证 + 规则 VERIFIED", base(), "VERIFIED"],
    ["② 有机制 NONE", base(capNone), "INSUFFICIENT"],
    ["③ 有 PARTIAL 机制，规则全 VERIFIED", base(capPartial), "PARTIAL"],
    ["④ 有 PARTIAL 机制 **且** 有 UNKNOWN 规则 ⇒ 仍是 PARTIAL（不得改判）", base({ ...capPartial, ...ruleUnknown }), "PARTIAL"],
    ["⑤ 只有 AI 未验证（无 PARTIAL、无 UNKNOWN）⇒ 代码路径完整", base(aiNo), "COMPLETE_UNVERIFIED"],
    ["⑥ 技能有未建模附加效果", skillPartialCase(), "PARTIAL"],
    ["⑦ 技能有未建模附加效果 **且** 有 UNKNOWN 规则 ⇒ 仍是 PARTIAL", skillPartialCase(ruleUnknown), "PARTIAL"],
    ["⑧ 只有 HYPOTHESIS 规则 ⇒ 代码路径完整", base(ruleHypo), "COMPLETE_UNVERIFIED"],
  ];
  for (const [name, input, want] of table) {
    const got = assessFidelity(input).klass;
    ok("★★ 映射表 " + name, got === want, "期望 " + want + " 实得 " + got);
  }
  // 反向：NONE 优先级最高，压过 PARTIAL 与 UNKNOWN（否则会把不可用的局说成"可以运行"）
  ok("★★★ NONE 压过一切：同时有 NONE / PARTIAL / UNKNOWN ⇒ INSUFFICIENT",
    assessFidelity(base({ ...capNone, ...ruleUnknown, capabilities: [{ name: "X", support: "NONE" as const, useCount: 1 }, { name: "DAMAGE", support: "PARTIAL" as const, useCount: 1 }] })).klass === "INSUFFICIENT");
  // 反向：VERIFIED 是**最严**的一档 —— 任何一个成因缺席都不许出现
  ok("★★★ 四种成因各自缺席时都不许判 VERIFIED",
    assessFidelity(base(capPartial)).klass !== "VERIFIED" &&
    assessFidelity(base(aiNo)).klass !== "VERIFIED" &&
    assessFidelity(base(ruleUnknown)).klass !== "VERIFIED" &&
    assessFidelity(base({ ruleset: [] })).klass !== "VERIFIED");
  const f = assessFidelity(base());
  ok("★ 五层齐备", f.layers.length === 5 && f.layers.map(l => l.layer).join(",") === "INPUT,SKILL,AI,MECHANIC,RULESET");
  ok("★ 输出里**没有**任何百分比真实性评分", !JSON.stringify(f).match(/真实(性)?"?\s*[:：]?\s*\d+(\.\d+)?%/));
  ok("免责声明里明确写了「不给百分比」", f.caveats.some(c => /不给「真实性百分比」/.test(c)));
  // ★★ 御魂三态必须分开：null（输入缺失）/ resolved=false（解析不了）/ true（已识别）。
  //   合并任意两态都会产生"把没有信息当成信息没问题"或反过来的过度声称。
  const soulCase = (soul: any) => assessFidelity(base({ units: [{ ...base().units[0], soul }] }));
  const inputOf = (f: any) => f.layers.find((l: any) => l.layer === "INPUT");
  const missing = inputOf(soulCase(null));
  const unresolved = inputOf(soulCase({ id: 5, resolved: false, support: "UNSUPPORTED" }));
  const okSoul = inputOf(soulCase({ id: 0, resolved: true, support: "FULL" }));
  ok("★★ 态一 输入缺失（soul=null）→ 不判 COMPLETE，且计数里写「御魂输入缺失 1/1」",
    missing.klass !== "COMPLETE" && missing.counts.some((c: any) => /御魂输入缺失/.test(c.label) && c.value === "1/1"), JSON.stringify(missing.counts));
  ok("★★ 态二 解析不了（resolved=false）→ 也不判 COMPLETE，且报的是「无法解析」而不是「缺失」",
    unresolved.klass !== "COMPLETE" && unresolved.gaps.some((g: any) => /无法解析/.test(g.what)), JSON.stringify(unresolved.gaps));
  ok("★★ 态三 已识别（resolved=true）→ COMPLETE（正向对照，防止「永远判 PARTIAL」）",
    okSoul.klass === "COMPLETE", JSON.stringify(okSoul.counts));
  ok("★ 三态互不混淆：缺失态不产生「无法解析」条目，解析失败态不产生「没有御魂信息」条目",
    !missing.gaps.some((g: any) => /无法解析/.test(g.what)) && !unresolved.gaps.some((g: any) => /没有御魂信息/.test(g.what)));
}

sec("[2] 真实对局：典型对局 #001 的 Fidelity 报告");
const v = await adapter.validate(canonicalMatch());
const fid: any = v.fidelity;
ok("validate() 会给出 fidelity（§33：编辑器里就能看到）", !!fid);
ok(`分类 = ${fid.klass}`, ["VERIFIED", "COMPLETE_UNVERIFIED", "PARTIAL", "INSUFFICIENT"].includes(fid.klass));
ok("五层都有计数与结论", fid.layers.every((l: any) => l.counts.length >= 3 && ["COMPLETE", "PARTIAL", "GAPS", "UNKNOWN"].includes(l.klass)));
const input = fid.layers.find((l: any) => l.layer === "INPUT");
  // ★★ 这条断言以前写的是「Input 层 COMPLETE（…10/10 御魂）」—— 那是**假的**：
  //   典型对局的 10 个单位 soulId 全是 null，「没有信息」被算成了「已识别」。
  //   一个断言把过度声称锁了整整若干轮。现在它断言正确的行为。
  ok("★★ 御魂输入缺失必须报成缺失，不得算作已识别（10/10 无信息 → Input 层 PARTIAL）",
    input.klass === "PARTIAL" && input.counts.some((c: any) => /御魂输入缺失/.test(c.label) && /10\/10/.test(c.value)),
    JSON.stringify(input.counts));
  ok("★ 已识别计数如实为 0/10（而不是 10/10）",
    input.counts.some((c: any) => c.label === "御魂已识别" && c.value === "0/10"), JSON.stringify(input.counts));
  ok("★★ 后果写进**顶层** caveat：本局不可能触发御魂效果",
    fid.caveats.some((c: string) => /御魂输入缺失/.test(c)), JSON.stringify(fid.caveats));
  ok("★ 缺口条目说明这不是御魂机制的问题，而是这份输入检验不了它",
    input.gaps.some((g: any) => /没有御魂信息/.test(g.what) && /g30/.test(g.detail)), JSON.stringify(input.gaps));
  // 正向对照：没有它，上面的 PARTIAL 分不清是「输入缺失」还是「永远判 PARTIAL」。
  {
    const withSoul: any = canonicalMatch();
    for (const side of ["red", "blue"] as const) for (const u of withSoul[side].units) u.soulId = 0;
    const v2: any = await adapter.validate(withSoul);
    const in2 = v2.fidelity.layers.find((l: any) => l.layer === "INPUT");
    ok("★★ 正向对照：装上可解析的御魂后 Input 层回到 COMPLETE，且已识别 10/10",
      in2.klass === "COMPLETE" && in2.counts.some((c: any) => c.label === "御魂已识别" && c.value === "10/10"),
      JSON.stringify(in2.counts));
    ok("★ 正向对照里没有「御魂输入缺失」这条 caveat",
      !v2.fidelity.caveats.some((c: string) => /御魂输入缺失/.test(c)));
  }
ok("★ 明确标注显示精度为整数观测（§2）", fid.caveats.some((c: string) => /显示精度/.test(c)));
  ok("★★ 顶层 caveat 点明效果命中/抵抗未参与结算（面板上那两维没有消费者）",
    fid.caveats.some((c: string) => /效果命中 \/ 效果抵抗未参与任何结算/.test(c)), JSON.stringify(fid.caveats));
const skill = fid.layers.find((l: any) => l.layer === "SKILL");
ok("★ Skill 层把被动与主动缺口分开计数", skill.counts.some((c: any) => /被动技能/.test(c.label)) && skill.counts.some((c: any) => /主动技能但无可执行数值/.test(c.label)));
ok("★ 主动技能无缺口（否则本局不该被判为可用）", Number(skill.counts.find((c: any) => /主动技能但无可执行数值/.test(c.label)).value) === 0);
const ai = fid.layers.find((l: any) => l.layer === "AI");
ok("★ AI 层三态分开，且 GAME-VERIFIED 如实为 0（§6）", ai.counts.some((c: any) => /已验证/.test(c.label) && /0/.test(c.value)));
ok("AI 层缺口写明「未经验证」而不是「已实现」", ai.gaps.some((g: any) => /未经游戏内验证/.test(g.detail)));
const mech = fid.layers.find((l: any) => l.layer === "MECHANIC");
ok("★ Mechanic 层是「本局需要的能力」而不是全库覆盖度（§7）", mech.counts.some((c: any) => /本局涉及能力/.test(c.label)) && Number(mech.counts.find((c: any) => /本局涉及能力/.test(c.label)).value) < 20);
const rules = fid.layers.find((l: any) => l.layer === "RULESET");
ok("★ Ruleset 层如实报出 UNKNOWN 数（§8）", Number(rules.counts.find((c: any) => c.label === "UNKNOWN").value) >= 1);
ok("★ 存在 UNKNOWN 时给出明确免责", fid.caveats.some((c: string) => /不能宣称|UNKNOWN/.test(c)));
ok("理由逐条列出（不是一句话打发）", fid.reasons.length >= 3, JSON.stringify(fid.reasons));

sec("[3] 界面接线（§9 真实性报告 / §33 集成到编辑器）");
{
  const panel = S("components/simulation/FidelityPanel.vue");
  ok("FidelityPanel 存在且渲染五层", /layers/.test(panel) && /本局模拟可信度/.test(panel));

  /**
   * ★★★ 第 61 轮：界面文案此前是一份**没人管的私有副本**（键对不对、用词从哪来，都没有约束）。
   *   这一节把它钉到内核那张表上：
   *     ① 键必须一一对应（内核加一档、界面漏掉 ⇒ 红）；
   *     ② 每个词必须在内核文案里原样找得到（界面自造措辞 ⇒ 红）；
   *     ③ PARTIAL 必须保留「可以运行」（§10 的限定语，丢掉它分类就读起来像故障灯）。
   */
  const grab = (name: string): Record<string, string> => {
    const m = panel.match(new RegExp("const " + name + "[^=]*=\\s*\\{([\\s\\S]*?)\\};"));
    const out: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const e of (m?.[1] ?? "").matchAll(/(\w+)\s*:\s*"([^"]*)"/g)) out[e[1]] = e[2];
    return out;
  };
  const uiLabels = grab("KLASS_LABEL");
  const uiTones = grab("KLASS_TONE");
  const want = Object.keys(FIDELITY_CLASS_LABEL).sort().join(",");
  ok("★★★ 界面分类文案的键与内核完全一致（两个方向都查）",
    Object.keys(uiLabels).sort().join(",") === want, "界面 " + Object.keys(uiLabels).join(",") + "｜内核 " + want);
  ok("★★★ 配色表覆盖全部四档（新档不许悄悄退化成 idle 灰）",
    Object.keys(uiTones).sort().join(",") === want, JSON.stringify(uiTones));
  const invented = Object.entries(uiLabels).filter(([k, v]) =>
    v.split(/[·\s]+/).filter(Boolean).some(f => !(FIDELITY_CLASS_LABEL as any)[k]?.includes(f)));
  ok("★★ 界面文案的每个词都来自内核文案（不许自造措辞）", invented.length === 0, JSON.stringify(invented));
  ok("★★★ PARTIAL 保留了「可以运行」这个限定语（§10：存在 PARTIAL，**但可以运行**）",
    /可以运行/.test(uiLabels.PARTIAL ?? ""), uiLabels.PARTIAL);
  ok("★ 界面分类文案里没有百分比", !/[0-9]+\s*%/.test(Object.values(uiLabels).join(" ")), JSON.stringify(uiLabels));
  ok("★ 面板不展示百分比真实性评分（只展示层级状态与计数）", !/真实[性]?\s*[:：]\s*\{\{/.test(panel) && !/真实性\s*\d+%/.test(panel) && /本局模拟可信度/.test(panel));
  ok("面板面向「跑之前」的决策", /跑之前/.test(panel) || /本局模拟可信度/.test(panel));
  const app = S("App.vue");
  ok("★ 阵容编辑器里就挂着 FidelityPanel（§33）", /FidelityPanel/.test(app) && /sim\.validation\?\.fidelity/.test(app));
  const dash = S("components/result/ResultDashboard.vue");
  ok("结果页也带上（结果与可信度一起看）", /FidelityPanel/.test(dash) && /fidelity/.test(dash));
  ok("契约给 ValidationReport 增加了可选 fidelity", /fidelity\?: unknown/.test(S("contracts/worker-api.ts")));
}

sec("[8] ★★★ §33 逐技能机制实现度：载荷 → 契约镜像 → 卡面/抽屉/面板")
{
  const rep: any = await adapter.validate(canonicalMatch());
  const f2 = rep.fidelity;
  const list: any[] = f2?.skills ?? [];
  ok("★★★ 载荷里带上了逐技能一层（30 个技能；第 137 轮之前只有聚合计数）", list.length === 30, String(list.length));
  const tiers = ["FULL", "PARTIAL", "PASSIVE", "NONE"];
  ok("★★★ 每一条都有一个合法的档位，且带得上 side / unit / name（界面按 side+名字取数）",
    list.every(s => tiers.includes(s.klass) && (s.side === "red" || s.side === "blue") && !!s.unit && !!s.name),
    JSON.stringify(list.filter(s => !tiers.includes(s.klass)).slice(0, 3)));
  const skillLayer = f2.layers.find((l: any) => l.layer === "SKILL");
  //   ★ 第一版这里忘了 Number()：value 是字符串 "22"，与数字 22 恒不相等 —— 断言写错（跑出来一对数字明明一样）。
  const cnt = (re: RegExp) => Number(skillLayer.counts.find((c: any) => re.test(c.label))?.value);
  const byClass = (k: string) => list.filter(s => s.klass === k).length;
  ok("★★★ 四档条数 == 面板上的四个计数（适配器不许在组装时丢字段或改口径）",
    byClass("FULL") === cnt(/完整编译/) && byClass("PARTIAL") === cnt(/部分编译/) && byClass("PASSIVE") === cnt(/被动技能/) && byClass("NONE") === cnt(/主动技能但无可执行数值/),
    JSON.stringify({ FULL: byClass("FULL"), PARTIAL: byClass("PARTIAL"), PASSIVE: byClass("PASSIVE"), NONE: byClass("NONE"), counts: [cnt(/完整编译/), cnt(/部分编译/), cnt(/被动技能/), cnt(/主动技能但无可执行数值/)] }));
  ok("★★★ 「四档合计」== 技能总数 == 载荷条数（穷尽性；第 138 轮之前 22+5=27≠30）",
    cnt(/四档合计/) === 30 && cnt(/本局技能总数/) === 30);
  ok("★★★ 仍然没有任何百分比真实性评分", !JSON.stringify(f2).includes("%"));

  // ── 契约镜像不漂移：界面词汇与内核词汇逐字节相等（两向） ──
  ok("★★★ 记号的键与值都与内核逐字节一致", JSON.stringify(SKILL_MARK) === JSON.stringify(KERNEL_SKILL_MARK), JSON.stringify([SKILL_MARK, KERNEL_SKILL_MARK]));
  ok("★★★ 短标签的键与值都与内核逐字节一致", JSON.stringify(SKILL_LABEL) === JSON.stringify(KERNEL_SKILL_LABEL), JSON.stringify([SKILL_LABEL, KERNEL_SKILL_LABEL]));
  const mutated = { ...SKILL_LABEL, FULL: SKILL_LABEL.FULL + "X" };
  ok("★★ 正向对照：上面那条比对不是空的（改一个字就该不相等）", JSON.stringify(mutated) !== JSON.stringify(KERNEL_SKILL_LABEL));
  //   ★ 判据必须**去掉注释**再找：这个文件的头注释里就写着 @engine（说明为什么不能 import 它）。
  //     第一版直接在原文上找 ⇒ 被自己的注释判红 —— 与 g91 第 [5] 节踩的是同一个坑。
  const contractSrc = S("contracts/fidelity-view.ts").split("\n")
    .filter((l: string) => !l.trim().startsWith("//") && !l.trim().startsWith("*") && !l.trim().startsWith("/*")).join("\n");
  ok("★★ 界面层没有 import @engine（vite alias 的约束：页面只读契约，不读引擎源码）",
    !/from\s+"@engine/.test(contractSrc) && !/from\s+"@engine/.test(S("components/lineup/UnitTile.vue")));

  // ── 取数助手：按 side+名字过滤（同名不同边不许串） ──
  const one = skillStatusOf(f2, "red", "三尾狐");
  ok("★★ skillStatusOf 取到该单位的技能（3 条）", one.length === 3 && one.every(s => s.unit === "三尾狐" && s.side === "red"), JSON.stringify(one.map(s => s.name)));
  ok("★★★ 同名不同边不许串：红方的名字拿去蓝方查 → 空", skillStatusOf(f2, "blue", "三尾狐").length === 0);
  ok("★★ 载荷缺失 / 名字对不上 ⇒ 空数组（界面显示「尚未评估」，不是崩）",
    skillStatusOf(null, "red", "谁").length === 0 && skillStatusOf(f2, "red", "不存在").length === 0);
  const g = groupSkillStatus(list);
  ok("★★ groupSkillStatus 按单位分组且**保序**（红 5 只在前，蓝 5 只在后）",
    g.length === 10 && g.slice(0, 5).every(x => x.side === "red") && g.slice(5).every(x => x.side === "blue"),
    JSON.stringify(g.map(x => x.side + ":" + x.unit)));
  ok("★★ 每组都带记数串与「最重档」（卡面小圆点用它上色）",
    g.every(x => /[●◐○✕]/.test(x.tally) && ["FULL", "PARTIAL", "PASSIVE", "NONE"].includes(x.worst!)),
    JSON.stringify(g.map(x => x.unit + "=" + x.tally + "/" + x.worst)));
  ok("★★★ 记数串是可复算的：按档位排、且每个记号后面的数字 == 该档条数",
    g.every(x => x.skills.every(s => x.tally.includes(SKILL_MARK[s.klass]))) && skillTally(list) === SKILL_TIER_ORDER
      .filter(k => list.some(s => s.klass === k)).map(k => SKILL_MARK[k] + list.filter(s => s.klass === k).length).join(" "), skillTally(list));
  ok("★★★ 记数串按**档位表顺序**排（● 在前）—— 与「最重档」用的严重度顺序是两张表（混用会把记数串读成 ◐1 ●2）",
    skillTally([{ klass: "PARTIAL" } as any, { klass: "FULL" } as any]) === "●1 ◐1"
    && worstSkillClass([{ klass: "PARTIAL" } as any, { klass: "FULL" } as any]) === "PARTIAL"
    && JSON.stringify(SKILL_TIER_ORDER) !== JSON.stringify(SKILL_SEVERITY));
  ok("★★★ worstSkillClass 取「最重」而不是「最后一个」：含 PARTIAL 的单位不能报 FULL",
    worstSkillClass([{ klass: "FULL" } as any, { klass: "PARTIAL" } as any]) === "PARTIAL"
    && worstSkillClass([{ klass: "NONE" } as any, { klass: "FULL" } as any]) === "NONE"
    && worstSkillClass([]) === null);
  /**
   * ★★★ 载荷里**不带 parsed**（只带档位与两个计数）—— 这是有意的：分档是内核的事，界面不许自己重判。
   *   所以这里改成「可从载荷自证」的两向判据：档位与它给出的理由必须对得上。
   *   （第一版在这里调用了 classifySkill(payload 条目)，直接 TypeError —— 断言写错，代码没错。）
   */
  ok("★★★ 档位与理由自洽（两向）：FULL ⇒ 未解析 0 且跳过 0；PARTIAL ⇒ 至少有一个 > 0",
    list.every(s => s.klass === "FULL"
      ? (s.unparsed === 0 && s.skipped === 0)
      : s.klass === "PARTIAL"
        ? (s.unparsed > 0 || s.skipped > 0)
        : true),
    JSON.stringify(list.filter(s => s.klass === "FULL" && (s.unparsed > 0 || s.skipped > 0)).slice(0, 3)));
  ok("★★ 载荷条目里没有 parsed（界面不重算分档——分档只有内核一处）",
    list.every(s => !("parsed" in s)));
  //   ★ 又是断言写错：第一版写成 `typeof classifySkill(...) === k` —— typeof 的结果永远是 "string"，
  //     所以这条恒为假（分类器本身没问题，探针逐个档位验过）。
  ok("★★ 而内核侧的判据确实会给出同样的档位（同源：拿同一批输入再分一次）",
    list.every(s => tiers.includes(s.klass)) && tiers.every(k => classifySkill({
      name: "x", confidence: "HIGH", cost: k === "PASSIVE" ? -1 : 1,
      parsed: k === "NONE" || k === "PASSIVE" ? { damage: 0, heal: 0, shield: 0, timeline: 0, status: 0, orb: 0 } : { damage: 1, heal: 0, shield: 0, timeline: 0, status: 0, orb: 0 },
      unparsedSentences: k === "PARTIAL" ? 1 : 0,
    } as any) === k));

  // ── UI 接线（源码级：本仓库没有 DOM 测试环境，见 ui14 的分层处置） ──
  const panel = S("components/simulation/FidelityPanel.vue");
  const tile = S("components/lineup/UnitTile.vue");
  const drawer = S("components/knowledge/DetailDrawer.vue");
  const app = S("App.vue");
  ok("★★★ 面板新增了逐技能一节，且默认收起（30 行会把面板撑长）",
    /逐技能编译状态/.test(panel) && /openSkills/.test(panel) && /groupSkillStatus/.test(panel));
  /**
   * ★★ 第 138 轮改写（按事实）：记号与标签的**渲染**收敛到一个组件 CompiledBadge ——
   *   面板与抽屉都复用它，所以「契约里的记号与标签」只该在那一个组件里被读。
   *   判据随之改成：面板/抽屉都引用它，而它们自己**不再**出现任何档位字面量。
   *   （渲染出来的样子由 tests/ui16-ssr-render.ts 真渲染断言，不再靠正则猜。）
   */
  const badge = S("components/knowledge/CompiledBadge.vue");
  ok("★★★ 逐技能状态的渲染只有一处（CompiledBadge），面板与抽屉都复用它",
    /CompiledBadge/.test(panel) && /CompiledBadge/.test(drawer) && /SKILL_MARK\[/.test(badge) && /SKILL_LABEL\[/.test(badge));
  ok("★★★ 面板与抽屉自己不再写档位字面量（两处各写一遍一定会慢慢写歪）",
    !/FULL: "●"/.test(panel) && !/FULL: "●"/.test(drawer) && !/完整编译/.test(panel) && !/完整编译/.test(drawer));
  ok("★★★ 卡面（UnitTile）按逐技能实现度显示记数串，且带悬停逐条清单",
    /skill-status|skillStatus/.test(tile) && /skillTally/.test(tile) && /worstSkillClass/.test(tile) && /compileTitle/.test(tile));
  ok("★★★ 抽屉里 Raw 与 Compiled **并排**（这个文件的头注释宣称了很久，第 138 轮才真的做到）",
    /Compiled Behavior/.test(drawer) && /skillRows/.test(drawer) && /CompiledBadge/.test(drawer));
  //   ★ 措辞（「没有这个技能的编译记录」）现在住在 CompiledBadge 里，由 ui16 真渲染断言；
  //     这里盯的是**配对逻辑**：按技能名取，取不到就是 null，绝不拿相邻技能顶上。
  ok("★★ 抽屉按技能名配对，取不到就 null（不拿相邻技能顶上）",
    /compiled: compiled\.value\.get\(k\.name\) \?\? null/.test(drawer) && /没有这个技能的编译记录/.test(S("components/knowledge/CompiledBadge.vue")));
  ok("★★★ App 把逐技能状态同时接到卡面与抽屉（按 side + 名字取数）",
    /skillStatusOf/.test(app) && /:skill-status="skillStatusFor\('red'/.test(app) && /:skill-status="skillStatusFor\('blue'/.test(app) && /:skill-status="drawer\.unit/.test(app));
  ok("★★ 抽屉记住了 side/index（同一只式神可以合法地出现在双方，只按名字会串）",
    /side: "red" \| "blue"; index: number/.test(app) || /side, index \}/.test(app), "");
}

console.log(`\n══════════════════════════════════`);
console.log(`Fidelity 验收：${pass} 通过 / ${fail} 失败`);
console.log(`══════════════════════════════════`);
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
```

```ts
// tests/ui11-ui2.ts — ★ UI 2.0 验收（a1-4 §29-§34）
// §29 只重做视觉层与布局，不动分层；§30 和风战术分析终端；§31 横排布局；
// §32 八维默认收起；§33 真实性进编辑器；§34 两条运行模式。
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const here = import.meta.dirname;
let pass = 0, fail = 0;
const ok = (n: string, c: boolean, e = "") => { if (c) { pass++; console.log("  ✓ " + n); } else { fail++; console.log("  ✗ " + n + (e ? "  — " + e : "")); } };
const sec = (s: string) => console.log("\n" + s);
const S = (p: string) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(here, "..", "src", p), "utf8");
const exists = (p: string) => fs.existsSync(path.join(here, "..", "src", p));

sec("[1] §30 视觉方向：和风战术分析终端");
{
  const css = S("styles/base.css");
  ok("★ 背景深色偏暖（墨黑/深褐）", /--bg:\s*#12100e/.test(css) && /--line:\s*#3a3129/.test(css));
  ok("★ 强调色是金色", /--gold:\s*#c9a961/.test(css));
  ok("★ 红蓝是低饱和朱红与靛青（不是纯红纯蓝）", /--red:\s*#b4544a/.test(css) && /--blue:\s*#4a6f96/.test(css));
  ok("★ 卡片有细金边与和纸纹理", /repeating-linear-gradient/.test(css) && /backdrop-filter/.test(css));
  ok("§29 只动视觉层：契约/Store/适配器/Worker 未被 layout 改动", exists("contracts/guessing-match.ts") && exists("stores/match.ts") && exists("adapters/engine-adapter.ts") && exists("workers/protocol.ts"));
}

sec("[2] §31 首页布局：红蓝横排 + VS");
{
  const app = S("App.vue");
  ok("★ 横排阵容（.tiles 横向 flex，不再是左右长列）", /\.tiles\s*\{[^}]*display:\s*flex/.test(app) && /grid-template-columns:\s*1fr auto 1fr/.test(app));
  ok("★ 版式顺序：Header → 阵容 → Validation → Control → Result", app.indexOf("board") < app.indexOf("validation") && app.indexOf("validation") < app.indexOf('class="control"') && app.indexOf('class="control"') < app.indexOf("resultwrap"));
  ok("红蓝两排各自成组，中间是 VS", /\.row\.red|sidehead[\s\S]*🔴 红方/.test(app) && /\.row\.blue|蓝方 🔵/.test(app) && /class="vs"/.test(app));
  ok("两排都用 UnitTile 渲染，且事件齐全", (app.match(/<UnitTile/g) ?? []).length === 2 && /@set-stat=/.test(app) && /@open-selector=/.test(app));
}

sec("[3] §32 八维录入：默认收起，点开才编辑");
{
  const tile = S("components/lineup/UnitTile.vue");
  ok("★ 默认态只显示数字（编辑器被 editing 开关挡住）", /v-if="editing/.test(tile) && /class="nums/.test(tile));
  ok("★ 点击式神才展开编辑器", /editing = !editing/.test(tile));
  ok("八个维度都可编辑", ["attack", "hp", "defense", "speed", "critRate", "critDamage", "effectHit", "effectResist"].every(() => /v-for="k in STAT_KEYS"/.test(tile)));
  ok("百分比维度有 % 提示", /PERCENT_STAT_KEYS/.test(tile));
  ok("★ 旧的「10 张卡全展开」组件已删除（不留死代码）", !exists("components/lineup/LineupPanel.vue") && !exists("components/lineup/ShikigamiCard.vue"));
}

sec("[4] §33 真实性进编辑器 + §34 两条运行模式");
{
  const app = S("App.vue");
  ok("★ 阵容下方直接挂 FidelityPanel（跑之前就能看）", /FidelityPanel/.test(app) && /sim\.validation\?\.fidelity/.test(app));
  ok("★ 两条运行模式：模拟胜率 / 观看一场", /模拟胜率/.test(app) && /观看一场/.test(app) && /mode === 'watch'/.test(app));
  ok("模式决定主按钮行为", /function startMode/.test(app) && /if \(mode\.value === "watch"\) watchOne\(theaterSeed\.value\); else run\(\)/.test(app.replace(/\s+/g, " ")));
  ok("模拟次数 100 / 1k / 10k / 自适应", /\[100, 1000, 10000\]/.test(app) && /自适应/.test(app));
  ok("自适应有明确策略说明（先 1k，区间过宽再加）", /先跑 1k/.test(app) && /runAdaptive/.test(app));
  const tile = S("components/lineup/UnitTile.vue");
  ok("★ 每张卡带数据可用性圆点（技能/AI/面板/御魂）", /面板/.test(tile) && /技能/.test(tile) && /AI/.test(tile) && /御魂/.test(tile) && /class="dot"/.test(tile));
  ok("圆点说的是「数据在不在」而不是真实性评分", /不是"真实性多少"|不是「真实性多少」|数据在不在/.test(tile));
}

sec("[5] 组件与构建");
{
  ok("UnitTile.vue 存在", exists("components/lineup/UnitTile.vue"));
  ok("UnitTile 不 import 引擎（只依赖契约与知识库）", !/@engine|battle-lab/.test(S("components/lineup/UnitTile.vue")));
  const dist = path.join(here, "..", "dist");
  ok("构建产物存在（npm run build 已通过）", fs.existsSync(path.join(dist, "index.html")));
}

console.log(`\n══════════════════════════════════`);
console.log(`UI 2.0 验收：${pass} 通过 / ${fail} 失败`);
console.log(`══════════════════════════════════`);
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
```

### `apps/web/tests/ui12-typecheck.ts`

```ts
// tests/ui12-typecheck.ts — ★★★ 把 vue-tsc 放进套件（第 63 轮）
//
// 由来：第 63 轮的词汇普查发现了几处「类型联合 + 标签表」的配对
// （presenter 的 state 联合 / EntityCard 的 STATE_LABEL、observations 的来源与置信度）。
// 这类漂移的**正确防线是类型系统**：把标签表写成闭合 Record<联合, string>，
// 联合多一个成员而表没跟上 ⇒ 编译错误。
//
// ★ 但那句"类型系统会拦住它"有前提：**有人真的跑类型检查**。
//   apps/web/package.json 里一直有 typecheck: vue-tsc --noEmit，但**测试套件从不跑它** ——
//   于是"TS 会抓到"就成了一句无人执行的承诺，与第 57 轮之前那句"复活没有调用方"同类：
//   写在纸上，没有任何东西在跑。
//   这个文件把那句话变成套件里的一条断言（实测 3.5 秒）。
//
// ★★ 而且它自己也要能自证伪：本文件会**临时写一个故意缺键的探针文件**，
//   确认 vue-tsc 真的会为"闭合 Record 缺键"报错，然后删掉它。
//   不做这一步，这条断言只证明"当前没有类型错误"，不证明"这类错误会被抓住"。
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";

let pass = 0, fail = 0;
const ok = (n: string, c: boolean, e = "") => { if (c) { pass++; console.log("  ✓ " + n); } else { fail++; console.log("  ✗ " + n + (e ? "  — " + e : "")); } };
const sec = (s: string) => console.log("\n" + s);
const WEB = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const TSC = path.join(WEB, "node_modules", "vue-tsc", "bin", "vue-tsc.js");
const PROBE = path.join(WEB, "src", "__typecheck_probe.ts");

const runTsc = () => spawnSync(process.execPath, [TSC, "--noEmit"], { cwd: WEB, encoding: "utf8" });

sec("[1] ★★ 前置：类型检查这一步真的存在（否则下面全是平凡通过）");
ok("★ vue-tsc 可执行文件在 node_modules 里", fs.existsSync(TSC), TSC);
ok("★ package.json 里仍然挂着 typecheck 脚本",
  /"typecheck"\s*:/.test(fs.readFileSync(path.join(WEB, "package.json"), "utf8")));

sec("[2] ★★★ 当前代码库：0 个类型错误");
{
  const r = runTsc();
  const out = String(r.stdout ?? "") + String(r.stderr ?? "");
  ok("★★★ vue-tsc --noEmit 通过（exit 0）", r.status === 0, "exit=" + r.status + "｜" + out.slice(-300));
  ok("★★ 输出里没有 error TS", !/error TS\d+/.test(out), out.slice(-300));
}

sec("[3] ★★★ 自证伪：闭合 Record 缺键，vue-tsc 必须报错");
{
  // 这正是第 61/63 轮的 bug 形状：联合多一个成员，标签表没跟上。
  const bad = [
    "// 临时探针（tests/ui12-typecheck.ts 自动删除）：故意缺一个键",
    'import type { UnitView } from "./theater/presenter.ts";',
    'export const BAD: Record<UnitView["state"], string> = {',
    '  ALIVE: "存活", HP_ZERO: "HP≤0", DEAD: "已阵亡", KILLED: "被击败",',
    "};",
    "",
  ].join("\n");
  fs.writeFileSync(PROBE, bad, "utf8");
  try {
    const r = runTsc();
    const out = String(r.stdout ?? "") + String(r.stderr ?? "");
    ok("★★★ 缺键时报错（exit ≠ 0）", r.status !== 0, "exit=" + r.status);
    ok("★★★ 报的正是缺的那个键 TRANSFORMED", /TRANSFORMED/.test(out), out.slice(-400));
    ok("★★ 报错信息指向探针文件（不是别处）", /__typecheck_probe/.test(out), out.slice(-200));
  } finally {
    fs.unlinkSync(PROBE);
  }
  const r2 = runTsc();
  ok("★★ 删掉探针后回到干净（不给仓库留垃圾）", r2.status === 0, "exit=" + r2.status);
  ok("★ 探针文件确实被删掉了", !fs.existsSync(PROBE));
}

console.log("\n=== 类型检查: " + pass + " passed, " + fail + " failed ===");
if (fail > 0) process.exitCode = 1;
```

### `apps/web/tests/ui13-trace-vocab.ts`

```ts
// tests/ui13-trace-vocab.ts — ★★★ 第 106 轮：**trace 词汇的对齐闸门**（目标 ⑥/⑦/①）
//
// 由来：机制一直在加（放置单位 / 偷取 / 免疫 / 吸血 / 免死 / 反击 / 锁条 / 抵抗…），
// 而 Theater 的 Presenter 只认得其中一部分 —— 新机制在剧场里**只剩一个没有解释的空条目**。
// 「什么都没发生」与「发生了但没渲染」在画面上是一样的，所以这条必须**机械守**：
//   引擎（+ 适配器）发出的每一种 trace 类型，要么 Presenter 有 case，要么在下面的允许清单里**写明理由**。
//
// ★ 判据两侧都做：允许清单不许有僵尸条目（已经标注了、或已经没人发了）；
//   而且抽取器本身要能被证伪（合成一段代码，它必须抽得出来）。
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
// ★ 第 137 轮：观测档位映射（原来它只是适配器里的两个字符串字面量，没有任何断言）
import { traceLevelFor } from "../src/adapters/engine-adapter.ts";
import { createEngineAdapter } from "../src/adapters/engine-adapter.ts";
import { canonicalMatch } from "../src/mock/canonical-match.ts";

let pass = 0, fail = 0;
const ok = (n: string, c: boolean, e = "") => { if (c) { pass++; console.log("  ✓ " + n); } else { fail++; console.log("  ✗ " + n + (e ? "  — " + e : "")); } };
const sec = (s: string) => console.log("\n" + s);
const here = import.meta.dirname;
const ROOT = path.resolve(here, "..", "..", "..");

/** 从一段源码里抽出所有 trace 产出点的类型名（导出出来是为了让自检能喂合成输入） */
export function extractTraceTypes(src: string): string[] {
  const RE = /trace\??\.push\??\.?\(\{?\s*t:\s*"([A-Za-z_]+)"/g;
  return [...src.matchAll(RE)].map(m => m[1]);
}
/** 从 Presenter 里抽出所有 case 标签 */
export function extractLabels(src: string): string[] {
  return [...src.matchAll(/case\s+"([A-Za-z_]+)"/g)].map(m => m[1]);
}

/**
 * ★ 允许清单：**不渲染到 Banner 但必须写明理由**的类型。
 * 判据不是「渲染不渲染」，而是「看不见的时候有没有解释」。
 */
export const UNLABELED_OK: Record<string, string> = {
  TURN_TYPE: "回合类型（NORMAL/NEW_TURN/…）是**结构性**信息：Theater 由视图模型的 turnType 字段呈现，不占 Banner 条目",
  ORB_INIT: "开局鬼火初始化：初始值已经显示在鬼火条上，再报一条是重复",
  AI_TARGET: "AI 选目标：属于 **G5 Inspector** 的职责（aiDecision 直接读这些帧），Banner 只呈现技能",
};

const walk = (d: string, skip: string[], acc: string[] = []) => {
  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    if (e.name === "node_modules" || e.name.startsWith(".") || skip.includes(e.name)) continue;
    const p = path.join(d, e.name);
    if (e.isDirectory()) walk(p, skip, acc); else if (p.endsWith(".ts")) acc.push(p);
  }
  return acc;
};
const presenterPath = path.join(here, "..", "src", "theater", "presenter.ts");
const presenterSrc = fs.readFileSync(presenterPath, "utf8");
const labeled = new Set(extractLabels(presenterSrc));
const emitted = new Set<string>();
const where = new Map<string, string>();
for (const f of [...walk(path.join(ROOT, "battle-lab"), ["tests", "tools", "fixtures"]),
                   ...walk(path.join(ROOT, "apps", "web", "src"), ["tests"])]) {
  for (const t of extractTraceTypes(fs.readFileSync(f, "utf8"))) {
    emitted.add(t);
    if (!where.has(t)) where.set(t, path.relative(ROOT, f).replace(/\\/g, "/"));
  }
}

sec("[1] 抽取器自检（合成输入两侧都验）")
{
  ok("★ 单行 trace.push 抽得到", extractTraceTypes('this.trace.push({ t: "DAMAGE", id: 1 });').join() === "DAMAGE");
  ok("★★ 可选调用 + 跨行也抽得到（第 98–105 轮的写法正是这种）",
    extractTraceTypes('battle?.trace?.push?.({\n    t: "SUMMON_CREATE", owner: 1,\n  });').join() === "SUMMON_CREATE");
  ok("★★ 反向：普通对象里的 t: D 不算 trace（否则判定会被枚举值污染）",
    extractTraceTypes('const v = { t: "CANDIDATE", family: "x" };').length === 0);
  ok("★ Presenter case 抽得到", extractLabels('case "DAMAGE":\ncase "HEAL":').join() === "DAMAGE,HEAL");
}

sec("[2] 前置：两边都真的抽到东西")
{
  ok("★★ 引擎发出的 trace 类型 ≥ 50 种（抽不到就是抽取器坏了，不是「没人发」）", emitted.size >= 50, String(emitted.size));
  ok("★★ Presenter 的 case ≥ 50 个（第 106 轮补齐后）", labeled.size >= 50, String(labeled.size));
}

sec("[3] ★★★ 每一种 trace 要么有 Banner，要么在允许清单里写明理由")
{
  const missing = [...emitted].filter(t => !labeled.has(t) && !(t in UNLABELED_OK)).sort();
  ok("★★★ 没有「发得出、又不渲染、又没理由」的类型", missing.length === 0,
    JSON.stringify(missing.map(t => t + " @" + where.get(t))));
  if (missing.length) for (const t of missing) console.log("      · " + t + "  ← " + where.get(t));
```
