```ts
sec("[3] ★★★ 小框里的文字来自**游戏数据本身**（不是我们重写的）");
{
  const txt = (name: string) => String((TABLES.buffs.concat(TABLES.tips)).find((x: any) => x.name === name)?.desc ?? "");
  ok("★★★ 盛放 =「【增益、印记】攻击、防御与速度提升50%」", txt("盛放").includes("攻击、防御与速度提升50%"), txt("盛放"));
  ok("★★★ 新蕊 =「【通用、印记】携带者行动开始时为其治疗灼华桃花妖生命上限10%的生命…触发3次后移除并使携带者获得盛放，持续3回合」",
    txt("新蕊").includes("携带者行动开始时") && txt("新蕊").includes("触发3次后移除") && txt("新蕊").includes("持续3回合"), txt("新蕊"));
  ok("★★★ 唯一效果 =「若是有多个同名式神在场，只有其中一个式神的此技能会生效」", txt("唯一效果").includes("多个同名式神在场"), txt("唯一效果"));
  ok("★★★ 先机 =「在战斗开始时进行动作」", txt("先机").includes("在战斗开始时进行动作"), txt("先机"));
}

sec("[4] ★★★ 接线：技能详情真的用它渲染（而且正文不再直出原文）");
{
  const d = S("components/knowledge/DetailDrawer.vue");
  ok("★★★ 引了解析器与两张表", /from "\.\.\/\.\.\/kb\/resolve-refs\.ts"/.test(d) && /from "\.\.\/\.\.\/engine\/bundle\.json"/.test(d));
  ok("★★★ 正文走 parts 渲染（kw 带 title=游戏原文）", /v-for="\(p, i\) in v\.desc"/.test(d) && /class="kw" :title="p\.text"/.test(d));
  ok("★★★ 升级句也解析了（Lv.5 的「先机：…」不再是 [tips_7]）", /v-for="\(p, i\) in u\.parts"/.test(d));
  ok("★★★ 右边小框渲染出来了（印记在前、术语在后：collectSkillRefs 已按此排序）", /class="refbox"/.test(d) && /v-for="ref in v\.box"/.test(d));
  ok("★★★ 反向：正文**不再**直出带占位符的原文", !/\{\{ r\.raw\.description \}\}/.test(d) && !/\{\{ u\.effect \}\}/.test(d));
}

sec("[5] ★★★ 反向：认不出的占位符**不许静默吞掉**（原样留在正文里，并记进 unknown）");
{
  const r = resolveRefs("造成伤害[tips_9999]并施加[buff_not_exist]。", TABLES);
  ok("★★★ 认不出的原样显示（正文里看得见）", r.text.includes("[tips_9999]") && r.text.includes("[buff_not_exist]"), r.text);
  ok("★★★ 而且记进了 unknown（谁都能复核）", r.unknown.length === 2, JSON.stringify(r.unknown));
  ok("★★ 单字名不参与「按名命中」（否则「火」这种名字会把满篇正文都命中）",
    collectSkillRefs(["这里提到火这个字"], { tips: [], buffs: [{ id: "x", name: "火", desc: "" }] }).buffs.length === 0);
}

console.log("\nui22-skill-refbox: " + pass + " passed, " + fail + " failed");
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
```

### `apps/web/tests/ui5-knowledge.ts`

```ts
// tests/ui5-knowledge.ts — UI-5（知识库页面 + 机制实验台）验收测试
// 运行：node tests/ui5-knowledge.ts   （工作目录 apps/web）
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { dirname, join } from "node:path";
import { groupRedZones, STATUS_ORDER, TONE_VAR } from "../src/components/mechanics/redZoneGroups.ts";

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const src = join(here, "..", "src");
const root = join(here, "..", "..", "..");
let pass = 0, fail = 0;
const ok = (name: string, cond: boolean, extra = "") => {
  if (cond) { pass++; console.log("  ✓ " + name); }
  else { fail++; console.log("  ✗ " + name + (extra ? "  — " + extra : "")); }
};
const section = (s: string) => console.log("\n" + s);

const J = (p: string) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(src, p), "utf8"));
const S = (p: string) => readFileSync(join(src, p), "utf8");
const RJ = (p: string) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, p), "utf8"));
const CODE = "components/knowledge/CodexView.vue";
const MECH = "components/mechanics/MechanicsView.vue";

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
section("[0] 生成物存在性");
const IDX_PATH = "kb/experiments.json";
if (!existsSync(join(src, IDX_PATH))) {
  console.log("  ✗ 缺少 " + IDX_PATH + " —— 先运行：node tools/build-experiments-index.mjs");
  console.log("\nUI-5 验收：0 通过 / 1 失败");
  process.exit(1);
}
ok(IDX_PATH + " 存在", true);
ok("生成脚本 tools/build-experiments-index.mjs 存在", existsSync(join(here, "..", "tools", "build-experiments-index.mjs")));
for (const f of [CODE, MECH]) ok(f + " 存在", existsSync(join(src, f)));

const idx = J(IDX_PATH) as any;
const srcAll = RJ("battle-lab/experiments/results/_all.json") as any[];
const verifiedSrc = RJ("battle-lab/experiments/verified-rules.json") as any;
const covSrc = RJ("battle-lab/capability/coverage.json") as any;

section("[1] 实验索引：44 条、四组齐全（A10 / B10 / C12 / D12）");
ok("experiments 恰好 44 条", idx.experiments.length === 44, "got " + idx.experiments.length);
const byGroup = (g: string) => idx.experiments.filter((e: any) => e.group === g).length;
ok("A 组 10 条", byGroup("A") === 10, "got " + byGroup("A"));
ok("B 组 10 条", byGroup("B") === 10, "got " + byGroup("B"));
ok("C 组 12 条", byGroup("C") === 12, "got " + byGroup("C"));
ok("D 组 12 条", byGroup("D") === 12, "got " + byGroup("D"));
const ids = idx.experiments.map((e: any) => e.id);
ok("id 唯一且 44 个", new Set(ids).size === 44);
ok("id 集合与源文件 _all.json 完全一致（无编造、无遗漏）",
  JSON.stringify(ids) === JSON.stringify(srcAll.map((e: any) => e.experiment)), ids.slice(0, 3).join(","));
ok("groups 汇总与实算一致（total / discriminating）", idx.groups.every((g: any) =>
  g.total === idx.experiments.filter((e: any) => e.group === g.group).length &&
  g.discriminating === idx.experiments.filter((e: any) => e.group === g.group && e.discriminating).length));

section("[2] 索引条目字段完整，且逐条来自源文件（不得手写捏造）");
const NF = ["id", "group", "title", "hypothesis", "method", "result", "verdict", "redZone"];
ok("每条都有 " + NF.join("/") + " 且非空", idx.experiments.every((e: any) =>
  NF.every(k => typeof e[k] === "string" && e[k].trim().length > 0)));
ok("discriminating 都是 boolean", idx.experiments.every((e: any) => typeof e.discriminating === "boolean"));
ok("verdictLevel ∈ {UNKNOWN, HYPOTHESIS, VERIFIED}", idx.experiments.every((e: any) =>
  ["UNKNOWN", "HYPOTHESIS", "VERIFIED"].includes(e.verdictLevel)));
ok("每条至少 1 个候选假设且 id/note 非空", idx.experiments.every((e: any) =>
  Array.isArray(e.hypotheses) && e.hypotheses.length >= 1 && e.hypotheses.every((h: any) => h.id && h.note)));
const srcOf = (id: string) => srcAll.find((x: any) => x.experiment === id);
ok("title = 源文件 question", idx.experiments.every((e: any) => e.title === srcOf(e.id).question));
ok("method = 源文件 inGameObservation", idx.experiments.every((e: any) => e.method === srcOf(e.id).inGameObservation));
ok("discriminating = 源文件", idx.experiments.every((e: any) => e.discriminating === srcOf(e.id).discriminating));
ok("hypothesis 覆盖源文件全部候选假设的 id 与 note", idx.experiments.every((e: any) => {
  const s = srcOf(e.id);
  return e.hypotheses.length === s.hypotheses.length &&
    s.hypotheses.every((h: any) => e.hypothesis.includes(h.id) && e.hypothesis.includes(h.note));
}));
const compact = (v: any) => JSON.stringify(v === undefined ? null : v).replace(/\s+/g, " ");
ok("result 逐条复用源文件各假设的 observable（未改写数值）", idx.experiments.every((e: any) =>
  srcOf(e.id).hypotheses.every((h: any) => e.result.includes(compact(h.observable)))));
ok("红区名带组号（来自定义文件段注释）", idx.experiments.every((e: any) => e.redZone.startsWith(e.group + " ")));
ok("status/confidence/chosenHypothesis 原样透传源文件", idx.experiments.every((e: any) => {
  const s = srcOf(e.id);
  return e.status === s.status && e.confidence === s.confidence && e.chosenHypothesis === s.chosenHypothesis;
}));
ok("confidence 是源文件里的字符串（不是自造的数字）", idx.experiments.every((e: any) =>
  e.confidence === null || typeof e.confidence === "string"));
ok("结论文案里不含任何置信度数字", idx.experiments.every((e: any) => !/[0-9]/.test(e.verdict)));
ok("源文件全部 NOT_RUN 时，索引里 0 条 VERIFIED（不越权宣称已验证）",
  srcAll.some((s: any) => s.confidence !== "NOT_RUN") || idx.experiments.every((e: any) => e.verdictLevel !== "VERIFIED"));

section("[3] 红区状态：UNKNOWN / HYPOTHESIS / VERIFIED 三态，且与 verified-rules.json 一致");
ok("redZones 条目 > 0", idx.redZones.length > 0, "got " + idx.redZones.length);
ok("status ∈ {UNKNOWN, HYPOTHESIS, VERIFIED}", idx.redZones.every((z: any) =>
  ["UNKNOWN", "HYPOTHESIS", "VERIFIED"].includes(z.status)));
ok("每条红区都有 zone / id / question 字段", idx.redZones.every((z: any) =>
  typeof z.zone === "string" && z.zone && typeof z.id === "string" && z.id && typeof z.question === "string"));
ok("redZoneStatusCounts 与实算一致", ["UNKNOWN", "HYPOTHESIS", "VERIFIED"].every(s =>
  idx.redZoneStatusCounts[s] === idx.redZones.filter((z: any) => z.status === s).length));
ok("VERIFIED 计数 = verified-rules.json 的 rules 条数（当前 " + (verifiedSrc.rules?.length ?? 0) + "）",
  idx.redZoneStatusCounts.VERIFIED === (verifiedSrc.rules?.length ?? 0));
ok("verifiedRules.count 与源文件一致", idx.verifiedRules.count === (verifiedSrc.rules?.length ?? 0));
const zoneText = idx.redZones.map((z: any) => z.zone).join("|");
for (const z of ["开局结算顺序", "同时触发优先级", "死亡", "竞猜残局", "鬼火"]) {
  ok("红区覆盖「" + z + "」", zoneText.includes(z));
}
ok("UNKNOWN 与 HYPOTHESIS 都有条目（两态可区分）",
  idx.redZoneStatusCounts.UNKNOWN > 0 && idx.redZoneStatusCounts.HYPOTHESIS > 0);

section("[4] 机制覆盖度：FULL / PARTIAL / NONE 计数与明细");
ok("coverage 条目 > 0", idx.coverage.length > 0, "got " + idx.coverage.length);
ok("status ∈ {FULL, PARTIAL, NONE}", idx.coverage.every((c: any) => ["FULL", "PARTIAL", "NONE"].includes(c.status)));
ok("每条有 capability 且在源文件 rows 里存在", idx.coverage.every((c: any) => c.capability && covSrc.rows.some((r: any) => r.key === c.capability)));
ok("note 字段存在（缺说明时为空串，不编造）", idx.coverage.every((c: any) => typeof c.note === "string"));
ok("coverageSummary 计数与实算一致", ["FULL", "PARTIAL", "NONE"].every((s: string) =>
  idx.coverageSummary[s.toLowerCase()] === idx.coverage.filter((c: any) => c.status === s).length));
ok("coverageSummary 与源文件 cov 一致", idx.coverageSummary.total === covSrc.cov.total &&
  idx.coverageSummary.full === covSrc.cov.full && idx.coverageSummary.partial === covSrc.cov.partial &&
  idx.coverageSummary.none === covSrc.cov.none);
ok("anomalies 是自检产出（数组，记录数据缺口）", Array.isArray(idx.anomalies) && idx.anomalies.length > 0);

section("[5] 知识库一致性：术语 156 条 / 御魂 70 条（与既有索引相同）");
const glossary = J("kb/glossary.json") as any[];
ok("术语表 156 条", glossary.length === 156, "got " + glossary.length);
ok("每条含 id/name/text 且正文非空", glossary.every(g => Number.isInteger(g.id) && g.name && g.text));
const souls = J("kb/soul-index.json") as any[];
ok("御魂索引 70 条", souls.length === 70, "got " + souls.length);
ok("每条含 2 件套 / 4 件套 / Engine Support", souls.every(s => "suit" in s && "desc" in s && ["FULL", "PARTIAL", "UNSUPPORTED"].includes(s.engine.support)));
const shiki = J("kb/shikigami-index.json") as any[];
ok("式神索引 276 条且每条带完整度字段 has", shiki.length === 276 && shiki.every(s => s.has && typeof s.has.skills === "number" && typeof s.has.ai === "boolean"));

section("[6] 组件接线：只依赖契约与知识库索引，不碰引擎");
for (const f of [CODE, MECH]) {
  const s = S(f);
  ok(f + " import 了 experiments.json 或 useKb", /experiments\.json|useKb/.test(s));
  ok(f + " 不 import 引擎（无 battle-lab / @engine 字样）", !/battle-lab|@engine/.test(s));
  // 只看 script + template（样式里的 100% 之类不算文案），确保不出现数据之外的百分比
  const view = s.split("<style")[0];
  ok(f + " 不含任何置信度数字（不给出数据外的比例）", !/[0-9]{1,3}(\.[0-9]+)?%/.test(view));
}
const code = S(CODE), mech = S(MECH);
ok("CodexView 复用 DetailDrawer（未重写抽屉）", /import DetailDrawer from "\.\/DetailDrawer\.vue"/.test(code) && /<DetailDrawer/.test(code));
ok("CodexView 传入 GuessingUnitInput 形状（unitId + shikigamiId + stats）",
  /unitId: "codex-"/.test(code) && /shikigamiId: x\.i/.test(code) && /stats: \{/.test(code));
ok("CodexView stats 取 pn 面板且缺项填 0", /x\.pn \?\? \[\]/.test(code) && /pn\[i\] \?\? 0/.test(code));
ok("CodexView 三个页签：式神图鉴 / 御魂图鉴 / 术语表",
  /式神图鉴/.test(code) && /御魂图鉴/.test(code) && /术语表/.test(code));
ok("CodexView 顶部说明数据来源与版本（GameData 2026-10-01）", /GameData 2026-10-01/.test(code) && /sourcebar/.test(code));
ok("CodexView 搜索复用 searchShikigami / searchSouls", /searchShikigami/.test(code) && /searchSouls/.test(code));
ok("CodexView 稀有度筛选来自 RARITIES", /RARITIES/.test(code));
ok("CodexView 御魂三色圆点 + 中文标签", /var\(--ok\)/.test(code) && /var\(--warn\)/.test(code) && /var\(--danger\)/.test(code) && /SOUL_SUPPORT_LABEL/.test(code));
ok("CodexView 明写「静态属性不参与计算」", /静态属性不参与计算/.test(code));
ok("CodexView 显示完整度徽标（技能 / AI / 面板）", /has\.skills/.test(code) && /has\.ai/.test(code) && /has\.panel/.test(code));
ok("CodexView 计数取自知识库长度（不写死条数）", /SHIKIGAMI\.length/.test(code) && /SOULS\.length/.test(code) && /GLOSSARY\.length/.test(code));

ok("MechanicsView 懒加载 experiments.json", /await import\("\.\.\/\.\.\/kb\/experiments\.json"\)/.test(mech));
  // ★★★ 第 62 轮：这一条原来是**形状断言**（"源码里有 STATUS_ORDER / zonesByStatus / v-UNKNOWN 这几个名字"）——
  //   它看起来在守"分组"，实际上守不了任何东西：原来那段代码遍历写死的清单去 filter，
  //   数据里多一个状态就静默丢条目，而这条断言照样是绿的。
  //   现在改成对**抽出来的纯函数**做行为断言（见下），形状断言只保留"接线"那一层。
  ok("MechanicsView 的分组走纯函数（接线，不是形状）",
    /groupRedZones\(redZones\.value\)/.test(mech) && !/STATUS_ORDER\.map/.test(mech),
    "仍在源码里自己遍历写死的清单");
  ok("MechanicsView 圆点颜色取自语气映射（不再是嵌套三元的绿色兜底）",
    /TONE_VAR\[g\.tone\]/.test(mech), "颜色仍在用三元兜底");

  // ★★★ 行为断言 ①：今天的数据里，每一条红区都必须落在某个组里（总数守恒）
  {
    const groups = groupRedZones(idx.redZones as any);
    const rendered = groups.reduce((n, g) => n + g.rules.length, 0);
    ok("★★★ 每条红区都被渲染（分组总数 == 数据条数）", rendered === idx.redZones.length,
      rendered + " vs " + idx.redZones.length);
    const covered = new Set(groups.map(g => g.status));
    const present = new Set(idx.redZones.map((z: any) => z.status));
    ok("★★★ 数据里出现的每个状态都有对应的组", [...present].every(s => covered.has(s as string)),
      JSON.stringify([[...present], [...covered]]));
  }
  // ★★★ 行为断言 ②：喂一个**清单里没有的状态** —— 这正是原来会静默丢条目的场景
  {
    const fake = [
      ...(idx.redZones as any[]),
      { zone: "Z 假想", id: "FAKE_1", status: "DISPUTED", question: "?", evidence: "?", source: "test" },
      { zone: "Z 假想", id: "FAKE_2", status: "DISPUTED", question: "?", evidence: "?", source: "test" },
    ];
    ok("★★ 前置：DISPUTED 确实不在显示清单里", !STATUS_ORDER.includes("DISPUTED"));
    const groups = groupRedZones(fake as any);
    ok("★★★ 清单外的状态照样成组（不会静默丢条目）", groups.some(g => g.status === "DISPUTED"),
      JSON.stringify(groups.map(g => g.status)));
    ok("★★★ 总数守恒：清单外的条目也全在", groups.reduce((n, g) => n + g.rules.length, 0) === fake.length);
    const g = groups.find(x => x.status === "DISPUTED")!;
    ok("★★★ 清单外的状态用中性色（**不许**被画成 ok 绿）", g.tone === "idle" && TONE_VAR[g.tone] !== TONE_VAR.ok,
      g.tone);
    ok("★★ 清单外的状态文案写明界面未适配，且提示要补什么",
      /界面未适配/.test(g.label) && /STATUS_LABEL/.test(g.help), g.label + "｜" + g.help);
    ok("★ 已知状态的顺序不受影响（清单只管顺序）",
      groups.slice(0, STATUS_ORDER.filter(s => new Set(fake.map(z => z.status)).has(s)).length).map(x => x.status)
        .join(",") === STATUS_ORDER.filter(s => new Set(fake.map(z => z.status)).has(s)).join(","));
  }
ok("MechanicsView 明写「UNKNOWN 机制不会被猜测，只会被显式标注」", /UNKNOWN 机制不会被猜测，只会被显式标注/.test(mech));
ok("MechanicsView 覆盖度面板含 FULL/PARTIAL/NONE 计数与明细", /COV_ORDER/.test(mech) && /covCounts/.test(mech) && /covList/.test(mech));
ok("MechanicsView 实验表可按组筛选（A/B/C/D）", /GROUP_ORDER/.test(mech) && /groupFilter/.test(mech));
ok("MechanicsView 实验表可按判别力筛选", /discFilter/.test(mech) && /discriminating/.test(mech));
ok("MechanicsView 每行展开显示 假设/方法/结果/结论", /假设/.test(mech) && /方法/.test(mech) && /结果/.test(mech) && /结论/.test(mech) && /expanded/.test(mech));
ok("MechanicsView 假设对比工具（同红区、结论相反；否则显示「无相反结论的实验对」）",
  /contrastPairs/.test(mech) && /无相反结论的实验对/.test(mech));
ok("MechanicsView 计数取自索引长度（不写死 44）", /exps\.length/.test(mech) && !/=== 44/.test(mech));
ok("MechanicsView 明确区分已验证与仍是假设", /仍是<b>假设<\/b>|仍是假设/.test(mech) && /已验证/.test(mech));

console.log("\n══════════════════════════════════");
console.log("UI-5 验收：" + pass + " 通过 / " + fail + " 失败");
console.log("══════════════════════════════════");
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
```

### `apps/web/tests/ui6-hud-lab.ts`

```ts
// tests/ui6-hud-lab.ts — UI-6（Developer HUD + 观测登记）验收测试
// 运行：node tests/ui6-hud-lab.ts   （工作目录 apps/web）
// 风格与 ui1 / ui5 一致：无框架、手写 ok()/section()、Node 直接跑 .ts。
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { dirname, join } from "node:path";

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const src = join(here, "..", "src");
const root = join(here, "..", "..", "..");
let pass = 0, fail = 0;
const ok = (name: string, cond: boolean, extra = "") => {
  if (cond) { pass++; console.log("  ✓ " + name); }
  else { fail++; console.log("  ✗ " + name + (extra ? "  — " + extra : "")); }
};
const section = (s: string) => console.log("\n" + s);

const J = (p: string) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(src, p), "utf8"));
const S = (p: string) => readFileSync(join(src, p), "utf8");
const RJ = (p: string) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, p), "utf8"));

const FACTS_FILE = "dev/facts.ts";
const OBS_FILE = "lab/observations.ts";
const HUD_FILE = "components/dev/DeveloperHud.vue";
const MECH_FILE = "components/mechanics/MechanicsView.vue";

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
section("[0] 文件存在性");
for (const f of [FACTS_FILE, OBS_FILE, HUD_FILE, MECH_FILE]) ok(f + " 存在", existsSync(join(src, f)));

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
section("[1] facts.ts：事实清单的结构与必列条目的实测数值");
const factsMod = await import("../src/dev/facts.ts");
const FACTS: any[] = factsMod.FACTS;
ok("FACTS 是数组且至少 8 条", Array.isArray(FACTS) && FACTS.length >= 8, "got " + (FACTS?.length ?? "n/a"));
ok("每条 title / detail / evidence 非空（id 亦然）", FACTS.every(f =>
  ["id", "title", "detail", "evidence"].every(k => typeof f[k] === "string" && f[k].trim().length > 0)));
ok("severity 都是 INFO / WARN / BLOCK", FACTS.every(f => ["INFO", "WARN", "BLOCK"].includes(f.severity)));
ok("id 唯一且形如 F01 / F14", new Set(FACTS.map(f => f.id)).size === FACTS.length && FACTS.every(f => /^F[0-9]{2}$/.test(f.id)));
ok("三种 severity 都出现过（不是全 INFO）", new Set(FACTS.map(f => f.severity)).size === 3, FACTS.map(f => f.severity).join(","));
const sevCount = factsMod.countBySeverity();
ok("countBySeverity 与实算一致", ["INFO", "WARN", "BLOCK"].every(s => sevCount[s] === FACTS.filter(f => f.severity === s).length));
ok("每条 evidence 都能指到测试 / 数据 / 报告（不是空话）",
  FACTS.every(f => /tests\/|\.json|\.ts|\.md|报告/.test(f.evidence)));

const blob = FACTS.map(f => [f.id, f.title, f.detail, f.evidence].join(" ")).join("\n");
const REQUIRED: Array<[string, RegExp]> = [
  ["① 技能解析顺序无关性 + 全库 818 条锁死", /parse-determinism/],
  ["① 座敷童子「魂之火」被静默丢弃的 lastIndex bug", /lastIndex/],
  ["② AI 规则 141\/421 cond 为空 + 思金神 601", /141[^0-9][\s\S]{0,400}421|421[\s\S]{0,400}141/],
  ["② 0 鬼火被反复施放", /思金神[\s\S]{0,200}0 鬼火|0 鬼火[\s\S]{0,200}思金神/],
  ["③ 引擎没有暴击事件（EV 无 CRI）+ 针女 / 地藏像", /CRI[\s\S]{0,400}针女[\s\S]{0,400}地藏像|针女[\s\S]{0,400}地藏像/],
  ["④ 7 只缺失攻 / 生 / 防（433 … 593）", /433[\s\S]{0,120}593|593[\s\S]{0,120}433/],
  ["⑤ 44 条实验全 NOT_RUN、chosenHypothesis 全 null、VERIFIED = 0", /NOT_RUN[\s\S]{0,300}chosenHypothesis|null[\s\S]{0,120}VERIFIED = 0|VERIFIED = 0/],
  ["⑥ 顺序 seed 相关性未检出（卡方 49.7 / 散列 47.3 / df=39）", /49\.7[\s\S]{0,200}47\.3|47\.3[\s\S]{0,200}49\.7/],
  ["⑥ 明确写成否定结果", /未检出|否定结果/],
  ["⑦ 引擎未记录鬼火消耗，avgOrbSpent 恒为 0", /avgOrbSpent[\s\S]{0,80}0|恒为 0|恒填 0/],
  ["⑧ 性能基线 1.6 ms / 642 局每秒", /1\.6\s?ms[\s\S]{0,120}642|642[\s\S]{0,200}1\.6/],
  ["⑨ 御魂静态属性不参与计算（面板即截图终值）", /静态属性不参与计算|静态属性不参与/],
];
for (const [label, re] of REQUIRED) ok("必列事实：" + label, re.test(blob), "正则 " + String(re));
ok("facts.ts 里出现「44」条实验与「df = 39」", /44/.test(blob) && /df\s?=\s?39/.test(blob));

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
section("[2] facts.ts 的数值与源数据一致（防止清单被改飘）");
const aiRaw = RJ("data/kb/ai.json") as any[];
let castRules = 0, emptyCond = 0;
for (const u of aiRaw) for (const c of (u.cast ?? [])) { castRules++; if (!String(c.cond ?? "").trim()) emptyCond++; }
ok("源数据 ai.json：272 只 / 421 条 cast 规则 / 141 条空 cond",
  aiRaw.length === 272 && castRules === 421 && emptyCond === 141, aiRaw.length + "/" + castRules + "/" + emptyCond);
const full = RJ("data/kb/shikigami.full.json") as any[];
const onmyoji = full.filter(x => x.rarity === "阴阳师" || x.rarity === "英杰");
ok("源数据式神 282 只（含 6 阴阳师 / 英杰）/ 技能 818 条",
  full.length === 282 && full.reduce((a, x) => a + (x.skills?.length ?? 0), 0) === 818 && onmyoji.length === 6,
  full.length + "/" + full.reduce((a, x) => a + (x.skills?.length ?? 0), 0) + "/" + onmyoji.length);
const buffs = RJ("data/kb/buffs.json") as any;
ok("源数据 buff 338 条", Object.keys(buffs).length === 338, String(Object.keys(buffs).length));
const shiki = J("kb/shikigami-index.json") as any[];
ok("网页索引 276 只 / 拼音 276 / has.ai 272 / 技能合计 798",
  shiki.length === 276 && shiki.filter(x => x.p).length === 276 && shiki.filter(x => x.has?.ai).length === 272 &&
  shiki.reduce((a, x) => a + (x.has?.skills ?? 0), 0) === 798);
const souls = J("kb/soul-index.json") as any[];
const soulDist = souls.reduce((a: any, s: any) => (a[s.engine.support] = (a[s.engine.support] ?? 0) + 1, a), {});
// 同上：火灵在第 25 轮升为 FULL（ORB_START），索引重新生成后分布变为 6/32/32。
ok("御魂 70 条，支持度 FULL 6 / PARTIAL 32 / UNSUPPORTED 32（火灵已升为 FULL）",
  souls.length === 70 && soulDist.FULL === 6 && soulDist.PARTIAL === 32 && soulDist.UNSUPPORTED === 32, JSON.stringify(soulDist));
ok("术语 156 条", (J("kb/glossary.json") as any[]).length === 156);
const expIdx = J("kb/experiments.json") as any;
ok("实验 44 条，confidence 全 NOT_RUN / chosenHypothesis 全 null / VERIFIED 0",
  expIdx.experiments.length === 44 &&
  expIdx.experiments.every((e: any) => e.confidence === "NOT_RUN" && e.chosenHypothesis === null) &&
  expIdx.redZoneStatusCounts.VERIFIED === 0);
ok("parse-determinism.ts 存在且锁死 818 条技能", (() => {
  const p = join(root, "battle-lab", "tests", "parse-determinism.ts");
  return existsSync(p) && /818/.test(readFileSync(p, "utf8"));
})());
const adapter = S("adapters/engine-adapter.ts");
ok("适配器：avgOrbSpent 恒 0 且写进 unsupportedCapabilities",
  /avgOrbSpentRed: 0, avgOrbSpentBlue: 0/.test(adapter) &&
  /avgOrbSpent\*（引擎未记录鬼火消耗计数器）/.test(adapter));

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
section("[3] observations.ts：没有 localStorage 时绝不抛");
const obs = await import("../src/lab/observations.ts");
const API = ["loadAll", "listFor", "add", "remove", "exportJson", "stats"];
ok("导出 loadAll / listFor / add / remove / exportJson / stats", API.every(k => typeof obs[k] === "function"),
  API.filter(k => typeof obs[k] !== "function").join(","));
ok("存储 key = yys.lab.observations.v1", obs.OBSERVATIONS_KEY === "yys.lab.observations.v1", String(obs.OBSERVATIONS_KEY));
ok("来源 4 选 1 / 置信度 3 选 1 的常量齐全",
  JSON.stringify([...obs.OBSERVATION_SOURCES]) === JSON.stringify(["IN_GAME", "COMMUNITY", "DATAMINE", "THEORY"]) &&
  JSON.stringify([...obs.OBSERVATION_CONFIDENCES]) === JSON.stringify(["LOW", "MEDIUM", "HIGH"]));
ok("政策说明写明「不会自动改成 VERIFIED」", /不会自动把任何规则改成 VERIFIED/.test(obs.OBSERVATION_POLICY_NOTE));

// 先确保真的没有 localStorage（Node 默认没有；有就显式置空，保证这段测的是容错分支）
try { Object.defineProperty(globalThis, "localStorage", { value: undefined, configurable: true, writable: true }); } catch { /* ignore */ }
ok("当前环境确实没有 localStorage（前置条件）", typeof (globalThis as any).localStorage === "undefined");
ok("没有 localStorage：loadAll() 返回 [] 且不抛", (() => {
  try { const r = obs.loadAll(); return Array.isArray(r) && r.length === 0; } catch { return false; }
})());
ok("没有 localStorage：listFor() / stats() / exportJson() 也不抛", (() => {
  try {
    const a = obs.listFor("A01"), s = obs.stats(), e = obs.exportJson();
    return Array.isArray(a) && a.length === 0 && s.total === 0 && JSON.parse(e).length === 0;
  } catch { return false; }
})());
ok("没有 localStorage：remove() 返回 false 而不是抛", obs.remove("nope") === false);
ok("没有 localStorage：isPersistent() 为 false（不假装已持久化）", obs.isPersistent() === false);

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
section("[4] observations.ts：内存版 localStorage 打桩后完整工作");
const store = new Map<string, string>();
const stub = {
  getItem: (k: string) => (store.has(k) ? store.get(k)! : null),
  setItem: (k: string, v: string) => { store.set(k, String(v)); },
  removeItem: (k: string) => { store.delete(k); },
  clear: () => store.clear(),
  key: (i: number) => [...store.keys()][i] ?? null,
  get length() { return store.size; },
};
Object.defineProperty(globalThis, "localStorage", { value: stub, configurable: true, writable: true });

const recA = obs.add({
  experimentId: "A01", source: "IN_GAME", claim: "先机先于御魂结算",
  evidenceText: "录屏 00:12", confidence: "MEDIUM", author: "tester",
  observedAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
});
const recB = obs.add({
  experimentId: "B02", source: "THEORY", claim: "同优先级按注册顺序",
  evidenceText: "", confidence: "LOW", author: "", observedAt: "2026-02-01T00:00:00.000Z",
});
ok("add 返回完整记录（id / observedAt 自动补全）",
  !!recA.id && /^obs-/.test(recA.id) && !!recA.observedAt && recA.claim === "先机先于御魂结算");
ok("两条 id 不同", recA.id !== recB.id, recA.id + " / " + recB.id);
ok("确实写进了 localStorage（不是只留在内存）",
  JSON.parse(store.get(obs.OBSERVATIONS_KEY) ?? "[]").length === 2);
ok("loadAll() 返回 2 条且按时间倒序（新登记的在前）",
  obs.loadAll().length === 2 && obs.loadAll()[0].experimentId === "B02" && obs.loadAll()[1].experimentId === "A01");
ok("listFor('A01') 只返回该实验的观测", obs.listFor("A01").length === 1 && obs.listFor("A01")[0].id === recA.id);
ok("listFor('ZZ') 返回空数组", obs.listFor("ZZ").length === 0);
const st = obs.stats();
ok("stats：总数 2 / 按来源 IN_GAME 1 + THEORY 1 / 按实验各 1",
  st.total === 2 && st.bySource.IN_GAME === 1 && st.bySource.THEORY === 1 &&
  st.bySource.COMMUNITY === 0 && st.bySource.DATAMINE === 0 && st.byExperiment.A01 === 1 && st.byExperiment.B02 === 1,
  JSON.stringify(st));
const exported = obs.exportJson();
const reparsed = JSON.parse(exported) as any[];
ok("exportJson() 能被 JSON.parse 回来且条数一致", reparsed.length === obs.loadAll().length && reparsed.length === 2);
ok("导出的每条都带全部 8 个字段", reparsed.every(r =>
  ["id", "experimentId", "observedAt", "source", "claim", "evidenceText", "confidence", "author"].every(k => k in r)));
ok("remove 存在的 id 返回 true，删完只剩 1 条", obs.remove(recA.id) === true && obs.loadAll().length === 1);
ok("remove 同时落盘（localStorage 里也只剩 1 条）",
  JSON.parse(store.get(obs.OBSERVATIONS_KEY) ?? "[]").length === 1);

const ids = new Set<string>();
for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
  ids.add(obs.add({ experimentId: "X99", source: "DATAMINE", claim: "第 " + i + " 条", evidenceText: "", confidence: "HIGH", author: "" }).id);
}
ok("add 生成的 id 唯一（200 条全不同，且都带 obs- 前缀）", ids.size === 200 && [...ids].every(x => /^obs-/.test(x)));

// 容错：损坏 / 结构不对 / 混入垃圾
store.set(obs.OBSERVATIONS_KEY, "{oops");
ok("JSON 损坏时 loadAll() 返回 [] 而不是抛", (() => {
  try { const r = obs.loadAll(); return Array.isArray(r) && r.length === 0; } catch { return false; }
})());
store.set(obs.OBSERVATIONS_KEY, '"not an array"');
ok("JSON 合法但不是数组时也返回 []", obs.loadAll().length === 0);
store.set(obs.OBSERVATIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify([{ experimentId: "C01", claim: "半残条目" }, 42, "junk", null]));
const salvaged = obs.loadAll();
ok("混入垃圾时保留对象条目、丢掉非对象（不抛、不改写原值）",
  salvaged.length === 1 && salvaged[0].experimentId === "C01" && salvaged[0].claim === "半残条目" &&
  typeof salvaged[0].id === "string" && salvaged[0].id.length > 0);

// add 的输入校验（明确报错，而不是静默存一条废记录）
let throws = 0;
try { obs.add({ experimentId: "", source: "THEORY", claim: "x", evidenceText: "", confidence: "LOW", author: "" }); } catch { throws++; }
try { obs.add({ experimentId: "A01", source: "THEORY", claim: "   ", evidenceText: "", confidence: "LOW", author: "" }); } catch { throws++; }
ok("add 对空 experimentId / 空 claim 明确抛错（不存废记录）", throws === 2, "throws=" + throws);

// 写入失败（配额满 / 隐私模式）→ 不抛，本会话内仍可读
store.set(obs.OBSERVATIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify([]));
stub.setItem = () => { throw new Error("QuotaExceededError"); };
let quotaThrew = false;
let quotaRec: any = null;
try { quotaRec = obs.add({ experimentId: "D04", source: "IN_GAME", claim: "配额满时仍要能登记", evidenceText: "", confidence: "HIGH", author: "" }); }
catch { quotaThrew = true; }
ok("setItem 抛异常（配额满）时 add 不抛，并返回记录", !quotaThrew && !!quotaRec && /^obs-/.test(quotaRec.id));
ok("配额满后本会话内仍能读到刚登记的观测（退化为内存，不假装已持久化）",
  obs.loadAll().some(r => r.id === quotaRec.id) && obs.isPersistent() === false);

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
section("[5] observations.ts：只存观测，绝不动实验状态");
const obsSrc = S(OBS_FILE);
ok("只碰 localStorage，不 import / 不读实验索引与 verified-rules（注释里点名不算）",
  /localStorage/.test(obsSrc) &&
  !/from\s+["'][^"']*experiments/.test(obsSrc) && !/verified-rules["']/.test(obsSrc) &&
  !/readFileSync|fetch\(|XMLHttpRequest|require\(/.test(obsSrc));
ok("模块内没有任何 VERIFIED 赋值（只出现在说明文案里）",
  !/=\s*["']VERIFIED["']/.test(obsSrc));

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
section("[6] DeveloperHud.vue：只读 HUD，不碰引擎");
ok(HUD_FILE + " 存在", existsSync(join(src, HUD_FILE)));
const hud = S(HUD_FILE);
ok("props: { open: boolean }", /defineProps<\{\s*open:\s*boolean\s*\}>/.test(hud));
ok("emit: close", /defineEmits<\{\s*\(e:\s*"close"\)/.test(hud));
ok("不 import 引擎模块（全文无引擎路径字样）", !/battle-lab|@engine/.test(hud));
ok("引用 dev/facts.ts 的 FACTS", /dev\/facts\.ts/.test(hud) && /FACTS/.test(hud));
const hudImports = [...hud.matchAll(/import[^;]*?from\s+"([^"]+)"/g)].map(m => m[1])
  .concat([...hud.matchAll(/import\("([^"]+)"\)/g)].map(m => m[1]));
ok("import 清单里没有 621KB 数据包（不把 bundle.json 拉进主 chunk）",
  hudImports.length > 0 && !hudImports.some(p => /bundle\.json/.test(p)), hudImports.join(" · "));
ok("版本：引擎 / 游戏数据 / 契约 schemaVersion 都来自常量或结果 DTO",
  /ENGINE_VERSION/.test(hud) && /GAME_DATA_VERSION/.test(hud) && /GUESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION/.test(hud));
ok("数据规模从索引动态算（SHIKIGAMI / SOULS / GLOSSARY 的 length）",
  /SHIKIGAMI\.length/.test(hud) && /SOULS\.length/.test(hud) && /GLOSSARY\.length/.test(hud));
ok("算不出来的写「—」而不是编数字", /"—"/.test(hud));
ok("本次运行读 useSimulationStore", /useSimulationStore/.test(hud) && /sim\.result/.test(hud) && /sim\.validation/.test(hud));
ok("校验报告展示 blockers 与 warnings 数量", /blockers\.length/.test(hud) && /warnings\.length/.test(hud));
ok("策略快照覆盖 5 个红区（开局结算 / 同时触发 / 死亡结算 / 竞猜残局 / 鬼火）",
  /开局结算/.test(hud) && /同时触发/.test(hud) && /死亡结算/.test(hud) && /竞猜残局/.test(hud) && /鬼火/.test(hud) &&
  /strategySnapshot/.test(hud));
ok("UNKNOWN 用警示色（.st.unknown → var(--danger)）", /\.pol \.st\.unknown/.test(hud) && /var\(--danger\)/.test(hud));
ok("覆盖度：units / skills / ai / souls + unsupportedCapabilities 逐条",
  /unitsJoined/.test(hud) && /skillsAvailable/.test(hud) && /aiHigh/.test(hud) && /soulsResolved/.test(hud) &&
  /unsupportedCapabilities/.test(hud));
ok("覆盖度标注了口径：本局 10 只，不是全库", /本次对局 10 只|本局/.test(hud));
ok("实测事实清单渲染 facts.ts 并按 severity 着色",
  /v-for="f in FACTS"/.test(hud) && /SEV_CLASS\[f\.severity\]/.test(hud) &&
  /sev-block/.test(hud) && /sev-warn/.test(hud) && /sev-info/.test(hud));
ok("文件地图含 contracts / engine / adapters / workers / stores / components / tests / tools",
  ["contracts/", "engine/", "adapters/", "workers/", "stores/", "components/", "tests/", "tools/"].every(d => hud.includes(d)));
ok("底部抽屉：fixed 定位 + 约 60vh + 可滚动",
  /position: fixed/.test(hud) && /height: 60vh/.test(hud) && /overflow-y: auto/.test(hud));
ok("折叠由 props.open 控制（关着时不占指针事件）", /\.hudwrap\.open/.test(hud) && /pointer-events: none/.test(hud));
ok("懒加载 experiments.json（不在主 chunk 里塞 78KB 索引）", /await import\("\.\.\/\.\.\/kb\/experiments\.json"\)/.test(hud));

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
section("[7] MechanicsView.vue：新增「观测登记」区块（其余部分保持原样）");
const mech = S(MECH_FILE);
ok("含「观测登记」区块", /观测登记/.test(mech));
ok("import 了 lab/observations.ts", /from "\.\.\/\.\.\/lab\/observations\.ts"/.test(mech));
ok("写明「不会自动…VERIFIED」的意思（文件内字面 + 渲染仓库常量）",
  /不会自动把任何规则改成 VERIFIED/.test(mech) && /OBSERVATION_POLICY_NOTE/.test(mech));
ok("表单：实验下拉（来自 experiments.json 的 exps）+ 来源 4 选 1 + 置信度 3 选 1",
  /v-model="obsForm\.experimentId"/.test(mech) && /v-for="e in exps"/.test(mech) &&
  /OBSERVATION_SOURCES/.test(mech) && /OBSERVATION_CONFIDENCES/.test(mech));
ok("表单：结论文本 / 证据文本 / 登记人", /obsForm\.claim/.test(mech) && /obsForm\.evidenceText/.test(mech) && /obsForm\.author/.test(mech));
ok("保存前校验（空实验 / 空结论给出明确错误）", /请先选择一个实验/.test(mech) && /结论文本不能为空/.test(mech));
ok("列表按时间倒序 + 可删除", /obsShown/.test(mech) && /deleteObservation/.test(mech) && /按观测时间倒序/.test(mech));
ok("统计：总数 / 按来源", /obsStat\.total/.test(mech) && /obsStat\.bySource/.test(mech));
ok("支持按实验筛选", /obsFilter/.test(mech) && /按实验筛选/.test(mech));
ok("导出按钮：复制到剪贴板，失败退化为只读 textarea",
  /navigator\.clipboard\.writeText/.test(mech) && /obsExportText/.test(mech) && /readonly/.test(mech));
ok("保存成功后仍强调状态没有被改动", /实验状态没有被改动/.test(mech));
const mechTpl = mech.slice(mech.indexOf("<template>"), mech.indexOf("<style"));
ok("区块排在实验表之后（模板里「观测登记」晚于实验表标题）",
  mechTpl.indexOf("<!-- ───────── 实验表 ───────── -->") >= 0 &&
  mechTpl.indexOf("观测登记") > mechTpl.indexOf("<!-- ───────── 实验表 ───────── -->"),
  "实验表@" + mechTpl.indexOf("<!-- ───────── 实验表 ───────── -->") + " 观测登记@" + mechTpl.indexOf("观测登记"));
ok("原 UI-5 结构仍在（红区状态 / 机制覆盖度 / 假设对比 / 数据异常）",
  /红区状态/.test(mech) && /机制覆盖度/.test(mech) && /假设对比/.test(mech) && /数据异常与缺口/.test(mech));
ok("不 import 引擎（全文无引擎路径字样）", !/battle-lab|@engine/.test(mech));

console.log("\n══════════════════════════════════");
console.log("UI-6 验收：" + pass + " 通过 / " + fail + " 失败");
console.log("══════════════════════════════════");
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
```

### `apps/web/tests/ui7-guardrails.ts`

```ts
// tests/ui7-guardrails.ts — ★ 十条 UX 护栏的逐条证据（M1.8 目标 ④）
// 每条护栏都必须有一条**可执行的断言**，而不是文档里的一句"已实现"。
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { dirname, join } from "node:path";

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const S = (p: string) => readFileSync(join(here, "..", "src", p), "utf8");
let pass = 0, fail = 0;
const ok = (n: string, c: boolean, e = "") => { if (c) { pass++; console.log("  ✓ " + n); } else { fail++; console.log("  ✗ " + n + (e ? "  — " + e : "")); } };
const sec = (s: string) => console.log("\n" + s);

// 内存版 localStorage（Node 里没有），必须在创建 store 之前装好 —— 护栏 #10 要用
const mem = new Map<string, string>();
(globalThis as any).localStorage = {
  getItem: (k: string) => (mem.has(k) ? mem.get(k)! : null),
  setItem: (k: string, v: string) => void mem.set(k, String(v)),
  removeItem: (k: string) => void mem.delete(k),
};

const bundle = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(here, "..", "src", "engine", "bundle.json"), "utf8"));
const { createEngineAdapter } = await import("../src/adapters/engine-adapter.ts");
const { canonicalMatch } = await import("../src/mock/canonical-match.ts");
const { swapSides } = await import("../src/contracts/guessing-match.ts");
const { createPinia, setActivePinia } = await import("pinia");
const { useMatchStore } = await import("../src/stores/match.ts");
const adapter = createEngineAdapter(bundle);

console.log("══ M1.8 十条 UX 护栏逐条验收 ══");

sec("护栏 #1 · 红蓝互换必须对称（换边不该凭空改变强弱）");
const N = 1000;   // 敏感性扫描的门槛是 800 局，低于它 modelUncertainty 会是 null
const a = await adapter.simulate(canonicalMatch(), { simulations: N, seed: 777, progressEvery: N }, () => {}, () => false);
const swapped = swapSides(canonicalMatch());
const b = await adapter.simulate(swapped, { simulations: N, seed: 777, progressEvery: N }, () => {}, () => false);
const pa = a.redWinRate!, pb = b.redWinRate!;
console.log(`      原局红方胜率 ${(pa * 100).toFixed(1)}%  ·  换边后(原蓝方为红) ${(pb * 100).toFixed(1)}%`);
// ★★★ 第 84 轮：容差从 **8 个百分点收紧到 4**（第 83 轮 §12 记下的那条待办）。
//   收紧前先**量了分布**，不是估：20 组（N ∈ {200,500,1000,2000} × 5 个 seed）实测
//     均值 0.948pp｜标准差 0.708pp｜**最大 3.00pp**（出现在 N=200）
//   本测试用 N=1000，同一 N 下的实测范围是 0.40 ~ 1.40pp。
//   ⇒ 4pp = 全局最大值的 1.33 倍、N=1000 最大值的 2.9 倍 —— 既不会闪，又把灵敏度翻了一倍
//     （旧的 8pp 是全局最大值的 2.67 倍，等于只挡得住 ≥8pp 的偏置）。
//   ★ 为什么容差不是 0：这条量的是**聚合**性质，任何合法的机制改动都会让它移动几个十分之一 pp；
//     容差要吸收的是"合法改动的正常抖动"，不是"运行噪声"（同 seed 同 N 的结果是确定的）。
ok("★ 换边后红方胜率 ≈ 原局蓝方胜率（差 < 4 个百分点）", Math.abs(pb - (1 - pa)) < 0.04, `差 ${((pb - (1 - pa)) * 100).toFixed(2)} 个百分点`);
ok("换边后仍无异常局", b.errors === 0);
ok("互换是 DTO 级一等操作（swapSides 不共享引用）", (() => { const m = canonicalMatch(); const s = swapSides(m); s.red.units[0].name = "X"; return s.blue.units[0].name !== "X" && m.red.units[0].name !== "X"; })());

sec("护栏 #2 · UNKNOWN 永不隐藏");
const app = S("App.vue");
ok("顶栏常驻 UNKNOWN 徽标", /UNKNOWN/.test(app) && /badge/.test(app));
ok("★ 未接引擎时显示「UNKNOWN 未评估」而不是 0", /UNKNOWN 未评估/.test(app));
ok("校验报告里的 UNKNOWN 计数被真实使用", /strategySnapshot\.unknownCount/.test(app));
ok("结果页把 5 个红区的 id 与 status 全列出来", (() => {
  const s = S("components/result/ResultDashboard.vue");
  return ["startup", "triggerPriority", "death", "endgame", "resource"].every(k => new RegExp("s\\." + k).test(s))
    && ["开局结算顺序", "同时触发优先级", "死亡 / 转化", "竞猜残局", "鬼火初始化与回火"].every(x => s.includes(x));
})());
ok("★ 红色的 UNKNOWN 必须能点开看到「为什么」", (() => {
  const s = S("components/result/ResultDashboard.vue");
  return /zoneNotes/.test(s) && /为什么这个状态/.test(s) && /不编造原因/.test(s);
})());
ok("★ 「未评估」不得画成红色（红只留给真问题）", /mark\.idle/.test(S("components/simulation/HealthCheck.vue")) && /尚未校验/.test(S("components/simulation/HealthCheck.vue")));

sec("护栏 #3 · 竞猜禁止六件御魂编辑器");
const gm = await import("../src/contracts/guessing-match.ts");
ok("★ GuessingUnitInput 结构里没有 soulPieces", !("soulPieces" in gm.emptyUnit()));
ok("STAT_KEYS 恰好 8 维（六件御魂才会有的那些维度不存在）", gm.STAT_KEYS.length === 8);
ok("UI 组件源码里搜不到 soulPieces 编辑入口", !/soulPieces/.test(S("components/lineup/UnitTile.vue")));

sec("护栏 #4 · 御魂静态属性禁止重复应用");
const { createKit, assembleMatch } = await import("../src/engine/assemble.ts");
const kit = createKit(bundle);
const noSoul = assembleMatch(kit, canonicalMatch(), { seed: 1 });
const withSoulMatch = canonicalMatch(); withSoulMatch.red.units[0].soulId = 3;   // 网切
const withSoul = assembleMatch(kit, withSoulMatch, { seed: 1 });
ok("★ 换御魂后八维面板逐项不变", JSON.stringify(noSoul.red.map((x: any) => x.resolution.exact)) === JSON.stringify(withSoul.red.map((x: any) => x.resolution.exact)));
ok("★ soulStaticApplied 恒为 false", withSoul.red.every((x: any) => x.resolution.soulStaticApplied === false));
ok("但 4 件套战斗效果确实被加载", withSoul.red[0].resolution.soulBattleEffects.length === 1);
ok("UI 明写「静态面板不重复计算」", /静态面板不重复计算|静态属性不参与/.test(S("components/lineup/SoulSelector.vue") + S("components/knowledge/CodexView.vue")));

sec("护栏 #5 · Unsupported 必须在开跑前显示");
const vm = canonicalMatch(); vm.red.units[0].soulId = 0;   // 雪幽魂（PARTIAL）
const vres = await adapter.validate(vm);
ok("★ 校验阶段就报出御魂实现度不足", vres.warnings.some(w => w.kind === "SOUL_EFFECT_UNSUPPORTED"), JSON.stringify(vres.warnings.map(w => w.kind)));
ok("御魂索引带三态 support 供 UI 着色", (() => { const idx = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(here, "..", "src", "kb", "soul-index.json"), "utf8")); return idx.every((x: any) => ["FULL", "PARTIAL", "UNSUPPORTED"].includes(x.engine.support)); })());
ok("HealthCheck 在跑之前就渲染覆盖度", /soulEffectsPartial/.test(S("components/simulation/HealthCheck.vue")));

sec("护栏 #6 · 版本号随结果一起保存");
ok("结果带引擎版本与游戏数据版本", !!a.engineVersion && !!a.gameDataVersion, a.engineVersion + " / " + a.gameDataVersion);
ok("结果带创建时间与 matchId（可追溯）", !!a.createdAt && a.matchId === "canonical-001");
ok("UI 把版本显示在结果头部", /engineVersion/.test(S("components/result/ResultDashboard.vue")));

sec("护栏 #7 · Seed 可复制、可 replay");
const seed = a.seeds![0].seed;
const rp = await adapter.replay(canonicalMatch(), seed);
ok("★ 同 seed 回放的胜负与回合数与模拟记录一致", rp.winner === a.seeds![0].winner && rp.turns === a.seeds![0].turns, `${rp.winner}/${rp.turns} vs ${a.seeds![0].winner}/${a.seeds![0].turns}`);
ok("回放器提供「复制 seed」", /复制 seed/.test(S("components/result/ReplayViewer.vue")));
ok("结果页可从代表 seed 直接进入回放/观看", (() => {
  const dash = S("components/result/ResultDashboard.vue");
  const app = S("App.vue");
  // G4 起主入口是「观看战斗」（Theater），事件级回放仍保留；两者都必须从样本对局一键可达
  return /emit\('watch'/.test(dash) && /BattleTheater/.test(app) && /ReplayViewer/.test(app);
})());

sec("护栏 #8 · 错误局永不进分母");
ok("★ decisive 严格等于红胜 + 蓝胜", a.decisive === a.redWins + a.blueWins);
ok("★ 全部局数可以配平（红+蓝+平+超时=总局数）", a.redWins + a.blueWins + a.draws + a.timeouts === a.simulations);
ok("胜率分母用的是 decisive 而不是 simulations", Math.abs(a.redWinRate! - a.redWins / a.decisive) < 1e-12);
ok("有异常局时会给出 BLOCK/WARN 级警告", /errors/.test(S("../src/adapters/engine-adapter.ts")) && /DATA_MISSING/.test(S("../src/adapters/engine-adapter.ts")));

sec("护栏 #9 · sampling uncertainty 与 model uncertainty 分开");
ok("★ 抽样误差：Wilson 区间独立成字段", a.confidenceInterval!.method === "WILSON" && a.confidenceInterval!.level === 0.95);
ok("★ 模型不确定性：策略敏感性独立成字段", !!a.modelUncertainty && a.modelUncertainty.perStrategy.length >= 4);
ok("两者在界面上是两个分开的区块", /抽样误差（加大模拟次数可以缩小）/.test(S("components/result/ResultDashboard.vue")) && /模型不确定性（加大模拟次数也不会缩小）/.test(S("components/result/ResultDashboard.vue")));
ok("★ 敏感时给出 BLOCK 级警告（不许淡化）", a.modelUncertainty!.verdict !== "SENSITIVE" || a.warnings.some(w => w.severity === "BLOCK"));

sec("护栏 #10 · 输入草稿自动保存");
setActivePinia(createPinia());
const store = useMatchStore();
store.replace(canonicalMatch());
ok("★ 改动后写入 localStorage", mem.has("yys.workbench.match.draft.v1"));
ok("★ 草稿内容是可恢复的合法 DTO", (() => { const d = JSON.parse(mem.get("yys.workbench.match.draft.v1")!); return d.schemaVersion === 1 && d.red.units.length === 5; })());
ok("savedAt 被记录", typeof store.savedAt === "string");
ok("配额异常不阻塞（store 内 try/catch 静默）", /catch \{ \/\* 配额满时静默/.test(S("stores/match.ts")));

console.log(`\n══════════════════════════════════`);
console.log(`护栏验收：${pass} 通过 / ${fail} 失败`);
console.log(`══════════════════════════════════`);
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
```

### `apps/web/tests/ui8-theater.ts`

```ts
// tests/ui8-theater.ts — ★ G4 验收：Battle Theater（a1-4.md §12-§19、§34、§59）
//
// 文档对 Theater MVP 的要求（§59）：10 张卡 / HP / 行动条 / 鬼火 / Buff / 当前技能 /
// 伤害数字 / 死亡 / 播放暂停 / 速度 / 拖动。**不要**立绘动画、技能特效、粒子。
//
// 本测试盯住三件事：
//   ① Presenter（纯函数层）对**每一帧**都能算出正确的视图模型，且满足战斗不变量
//   ② 播放完全不需要引擎（§36）—— 打开录像、拖到任意帧、读事件链，装配次数纹丝不动
//   ③ 界面接线正确（组件存在、不 import 引擎战斗模块、App 里有入口）
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { Presenter } from "../src/theater/presenter.ts";
import { createEngineAdapter } from "../src/adapters/engine-adapter.ts";
import { canonicalMatch } from "../src/mock/canonical-match.ts";
import { assembleCount } from "../src/engine/assemble.ts";

const here = import.meta.dirname;
const ROOT = path.resolve(here, "..", "..", "..");
let pass = 0, fail = 0;
const ok = (n: string, c: boolean, e = "") => { if (c) { pass++; console.log("  ✓ " + n); } else { fail++; console.log("  ✗ " + n + (e ? "  — " + e : "")); } };
const sec = (s: string) => console.log("\n" + s);
const S = (p: string) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(here, "..", "src", p), "utf8");

const bundle = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(here, "..", "src", "engine", "bundle.json"), "utf8"));
const fixture = path.join(ROOT, "battle-lab", "fixtures", "replay-demo-123456.json");

sec("[1] 用真实录像喂 Presenter：每一帧都要算得出来");
const p = Presenter.fromSerialized(fs.readFileSync(fixture, "utf8"));
ok(`录像帧数 ${p.frameCount}（>${50}）`, p.frameCount > 50);
ok("终局帧号解析正确", p.lastFrame >= p.frameCount - 1, `lastFrame=${p.lastFrame} frameCount=${p.frameCount}`);
let bad = 0;
const views = [];
for (let i = 0; i < p.frameCount; i++) {
  const v = p.view(i);
  if (!v || v.units.length !== 10) { bad++; continue; }
  views.push(v);
}
ok("★ 每一帧都能产出视图模型（10 个单位齐全）", bad === 0, `失败 ${bad} 帧`);
ok("红蓝各 5 个", views[0].units.filter(u => u.side === "red").length === 5 && views[0].units.filter(u => u.side === "blue").length === 5);

sec("[2] 战场不变量（Presenter 算出来的东西必须自洽）");
{
  const hpBad = views.filter(v => v.units.some(u => u.hpPct < 0 || u.hpPct > 100)).length;
  ok("HP 百分比恒在 [0,100]", hpBad === 0, String(hpBad));
  const deadBad = views.filter(v => v.units.some(u => !u.alive && u.hp > 0)).length;
  ok("★ 死亡与血量自洽（alive=false ⇒ hp<=0）", deadBad === 0, String(deadBad));
  const tlBad = views.filter(v => v.units.some(u => u.timeline < 0 || u.timeline > 1)).length;
  ok("行动条位置恒在 [0,1]", tlBad === 0, String(tlBad));
  const orbBad = views.filter(v => v.teams.some(t => t.orb < 0 || t.orb > 8)).length;
  ok("鬼火恒在 [0,8]", orbBad === 0, String(orbBad));
  ok("存活数与卡面一致", views.every(v => v.teams[0].aliveCount === v.units.filter(u => u.side === "red" && u.alive).length && v.teams[1].aliveCount === v.units.filter(u => u.side === "blue" && u.alive).length));
}

sec("[3] HP 只会在治疗/回复帧上升（防止 Presenter 把数字算反）");
{
  const frames = p.player.frames;
  let violations: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 1; i < views.length; i++) {
    for (const u of views[i].units) {
      const prev = views[i - 1].units.find(x => x.unitId === u.unitId)!;
      if (u.hp > prev.hp + 1e-6) {
        const f = frames[i];
        const healed = f && ["HEAL", "RECOVER"].includes(f.t) && String((f as any).dst) === String(u.id);
        const revived = f && f.t === "REVIVE" && String((f as any).id) === String(u.id);
        if (!healed && !revived) violations.push(`frame ${i} ${u.name} ${prev.hp.toFixed(0)}→${u.hp.toFixed(0)} (${f?.t})`);
      }
    }
  }
  ok("★ 血量上升只出现在治疗/复活帧", violations.length === 0, violations.slice(0, 3).join(" | "));
}

sec("[4] 鬼火收支在视图层也必须对得上");
{
  const pays = p.player.frames.filter(f => f.t === "ORB_PAY") as any[];
  ok(`存在扣火帧（${pays.length} 条）`, pays.length > 0);
  ok("★ 每条扣火：before − amount === after", pays.every(f => Math.abs(f.before - f.amount - f.after) < 1e-9), JSON.stringify(pays.slice(0, 2)));
  const gains = p.player.frames.filter(f => f.t === "ORB_GAIN") as any[];
  ok("回火帧 after ≥ before", gains.every(f => f.after >= f.before));
}

sec("[5] 行动条：拉条/推条必须在相邻帧上看得见（§14）");
{
  const shifts = p.player.frames.map((f, i) => ({ f, i })).filter(x => (x.f as any).t === "TIMELINE_SHIFT") as Array<{ f: any; i: number }>;
  ok(`存在拉条/推条帧（${shifts.length} 条）`, shifts.length > 0);
  let moved = 0;
  for (const { f, i } of shifts) {
    if (i === 0 || i + 1 >= views.length) continue;
    const before = views[i - 1]?.units.find(u => u.id === f.id)?.timeline ?? 0;
    const after = views[i + 1]?.units.find(u => u.id === f.id)?.timeline ?? 0;
    if (Math.abs(after - before) > 1e-9) moved++;
  }
  ok("★ 每一条拉条/推条都能在前后帧的位置差上体现出来", moved === shifts.length, `${moved}/${shifts.length}`);
  ok("位置变化方向与拉/推一致", shifts.every(({ f }) => (f.posAfter - f.posBefore) * (f.direction === "pull" ? 1 : -1) >= -1e-6));
}

sec("[6] Action Banner（§16）：技能释放不能只是一行日志");
{
  const casts = p.player.frames.map((f, i) => ({ f, i })).filter(x => (x.f as any).t === "SKILL_CAST") as Array<{ f: any; i: number }>;
  ok(`存在技能帧（${casts.length} 条）`, casts.length > 10);
  const okBanner = casts.every(({ f, i }) => {
    const b = views[i].banner;
    return b && b.kind === "SKILL" && b.skill === f.skill && !!b.actor;
  });
  ok("★ 每条 SKILL_CAST 都产出带技能名与行动者的 Banner", okBanner);
  const dmgFrames = p.player.frames.map((f, i) => ({ f, i })).filter(x => (x.f as any).t === "DAMAGE") as Array<{ f: any; i: number }>;
  ok("伤害帧产生飘字（数字可见）", dmgFrames.every(({ i }) => views[i].floats.length >= 1));
  ok("暴击飘字被标成 crit", dmgFrames.filter(x => x.f.crit).every(({ i }) => views[i].floats.some(fl => fl.tone === "crit")));
}

sec("[7] 死亡状态机可视化（§18）");
{
  const deaths = p.player.frames.map((f, i) => ({ f, i })).filter(x => (x.f as any).t === "DEATH") as Array<{ f: any; i: number }>;
  ok(`存在阵亡帧（${deaths.length} 条）`, deaths.length > 0);
  const first = deaths[0];
  const unit = views[first.i].units.find(u => u.id === first.f.id);
  ok("★ 阵亡帧上该单位已经是非存活状态", !!unit && !unit.alive && unit.hp <= 0);
  ok("阵亡帧产出 DEATH 类 Banner", views[first.i].banner?.kind === "DEATH");
  const last = views[views.length - 1];
  ok("★ 终局时败方全员阵亡（与胜负一致）", p.result.winner === 0
    ? last.units.filter(u => u.side === "blue").every(u => !u.alive)
    : last.units.filter(u => u.side === "red").every(u => !u.alive));
}

sec("[8] ★ 播放不需要引擎（§36）");
{
  const before = assembleCount;
  const p2 = Presenter.fromSerialized(fs.readFileSync(fixture, "utf8"));
  for (const f of [0, 1, 17, 99, p2.frameCount - 1]) p2.view(f);
  p2.ancestry(p2.frameCount - 2);
  ok("★ 拖遍全片 + 读事件链，装配次数一次都没变", assembleCount === before, `${before} → ${assembleCount}`);
  // 事件链必须真的能读出来：取一个确实带 ev 的帧，链上至少要有它自己
  const withEv = p2.player.frames.map((f: any, i: number) => ({ f, i })).filter(x => x.f.ev !== null && x.f.ev !== undefined).pop();
  ok("事件链可读（Inspector 的数据来源）", !!withEv && p2.ancestry(withEv.i).length >= 1, withEv ? `frame ${withEv.i} → ${p2.ancestry(withEv.i).length} 节点` : "没有带 ev 的帧（这本身就不正常）");
}

sec("[9] 接口接线：适配器真的会产出 Replay 文件");
{
  const adapter = createEngineAdapter(bundle);
  const r: any = await adapter.replay(canonicalMatch(), 123456);
  ok("REPLAY 返回里带 replay 载荷", !!r.replay, Object.keys(r).join(","));
  ok("载荷含帧流/Checkpoint/Delta/终局指纹", Array.isArray(r.replay.frames) && Array.isArray(r.replay.checkpoints) && Array.isArray(r.replay.deltas) && !!r.replay.finalHash);
  const pv = Presenter.fromSerialized(r.replay);
  ok("★ 适配器产出的录像能被 Presenter 直接播放", pv.frameCount === r.replay.frames.length && pv.view(pv.frameCount - 1)?.units.length === 10);
  ok("终局视图与回放结果一致", (() => { const last = pv.view(pv.frameCount - 1); return last && last.frame >= 0 && last.units.length === 10; })());
}

sec("[10] 分层与接线（Presenter 不许 import 引擎战斗模块）");
{
  const pres = S("theater/presenter.ts");
  // 只看 **import 语句**：注释里解释"这里不 import assemble"不该被当成违规（第一次就是被自己的注释绊倒的）
  const imports = pres.split("\n").filter(l => /^\s*import\b/.test(l)).join("\n");
  ok("★ Presenter 的 import 里没有 assemble/battle/compile（只借纯播放器读文件）", !/assemble|kernel\/battle|kernel\/team|compile\//.test(imports), imports.replace(/\n/g, " | ").slice(0, 160));
  ok("Presenter 是纯函数层（无 Vue 依赖）", !/from "vue"/.test(pres));
  ok("BattleTheater.vue 存在且引用 Presenter", /Presenter/.test(S("components/theater/BattleTheater.vue")));
  ok("EntityCard.vue 存在且使用 UnitView", /UnitView/.test(S("components/theater/EntityCard.vue")));
  // ★★ 八维里有两维**没有任何消费者**：效果命中 / 效果抵抗 ——
  //   引擎里不存在「先判定命中、再结算」这一级（EV.HIT / EV.BEFORE_HIT / EV.RESIST 从未派发）。
  //   不加区分地平铺显示，看的人会以为它在参与结算。
  const ec = S("components/theater/EntityCard.vue");
  ok("★★ EntityCard 把效果命中/抵抗标为「未结算」",
    /unused/.test(ec) && /未结算/.test(ec), (ec.match(/命 \{\{[^\n]*/) ?? [""])[0].slice(0, 80));
  ok("★ 标记不是删掉它：数值仍然显示（它确实是输入的一部分）",
    /unit\.stats\.efh/.test(ec) && /unit\.stats\.efr/.test(ec));
  ok("★ 提示文案写明了原因，不是「暂不支持」这种含糊说法", /先判定命中、再结算/.test(ec));
  const app = S("App.vue");
  ok("App 里有『观看战斗』入口", /观看战斗|watchOne/.test(app) && /BattleTheater/.test(app));
  ok("结果页的样本对局能直接跳进 Theater", /emit\('watch'/.test(S("components/result/ResultDashboard.vue")));
  ok("契约给 REPLAY_PREPARED 增加了可选 replay 载荷", /replay\?: unknown/.test(S("contracts/worker-api.ts")));
}


sec("[11] ★★★ 偷取帧在 Theater 里必须看得见（第 99 轮自己声明的缺口，第 100 轮补上）")
{
  /**
   * 第 99 轮把属性偷取做进引擎时，Presenter 加了 5 个 case，但**没有任何断言盯它**
   * （当时的理由写在报告 §84.10：既有录像夹具里不含偷取帧）。
   * 这一段的做法不是再造一个夹具文件，而是**当场生成一段含偷取的录像** ——
   * 与 g60 §5 同一手法（把典型对局红方第一位换成一目连），好处是夹具不会过期。
   */
  const KB = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, "data", "kb", "shikigami.full.json"), "utf8"));
  const YML = KB.find((s: any) => s.name === "一目连");
  ok("★ 前置：知识库里有「一目连」（否则这一段是空跑）", !!YML);
  const m = canonicalMatch();
  m.red.units[0] = { ...m.red.units[0], shikigamiId: YML.id, name: "一目连" };
  const adapter = createEngineAdapter(bundle);
  const rr: any = await adapter.replay(m, 20261002);
  const pv = Presenter.fromSerialized(rr.replay);
  const stealFrames = rr.replay.frames.map((f: any, i: number) => ({ f, i })).filter((x: any) => x.f.t === "STAT_STEAL");
  ok("★★★ 录像里真的有偷取帧（没有的话下面全是空断言）", stealFrames.length > 0, "STAT_STEAL × " + stealFrames.length);
  const v0 = pv.view(stealFrames[0].i);
  const lines = (v0?.banner?.lines ?? []).map(l => l.label + "=" + l.value).join("｜");
  ok("★★★ Banner 上写出「偷取 + 属性 + 数值」（不是只有一行「发生了某件事」）",
    v0?.banner?.kind === "STATUS" && /偷取/.test(lines) && /攻击/.test(lines), lines);
  ok("★★★ 而且写明**会归还**、持续几回合（借据的期限是机制的一部分）",
    /归还/.test(lines) && /持续/.test(lines), lines);
  ok("★★★ 飘字在**双方**身上各出现一次（目标 −、偷取者 +）—— 只画一边等于没解释属性为什么变了",
    (v0?.floats ?? []).length === 2 && (v0?.floats ?? []).some(f => f.tone === "dmg" && f.text.startsWith("−")) &&
    (v0?.floats ?? []).some(f => f.tone === "heal" && f.text.startsWith("+")),
    JSON.stringify(v0?.floats));
  ok("★★ 全片每一帧仍然算得出来（新 case 没有把别的帧弄坏）",
    Array.from({ length: pv.frameCount }, (_, i) => i).every(i => !!pv.view(i)));
  const cons = stealFrames.filter((x: any) => x.f.conservation);
  ok("★★ 口径是守恒的（默认 TRANSFER）⇒ 不该出现「不守恒」告警行", cons.length === 0, String(cons.length));
}
console.log(`\n══════════════════════════════════`);
console.log(`UI-8 Theater 验收：${pass} 通过 / ${fail} 失败`);
console.log(`══════════════════════════════════`);
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
```

### `apps/web/tests/ui9-inspectors.ts`

```ts
// tests/ui9-inspectors.ts — ★ G5 验收（界面侧）：四个检查器 + 接线
// 对应 a1-4.md §20 RNG / §25 AI / §26 Damage / §27 状态检查器 / §28 事件链
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { Presenter } from "../src/theater/presenter.ts";

const here = import.meta.dirname;
const ROOT = path.resolve(here, "..", "..", "..");
let pass = 0, fail = 0;
const ok = (n: string, c: boolean, e = "") => { if (c) { pass++; console.log("  ✓ " + n); } else { fail++; console.log("  ✗ " + n + (e ? "  — " + e : "")); } };
const sec = (s: string) => console.log("\n" + s);
const S = (p: string) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(here, "..", "src", p), "utf8");

const p = Presenter.fromSerialized(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, "battle-lab", "fixtures", "replay-demo-123456.json"), "utf8"));
const frames = p.player.frames as any[];

sec("[1] Damage Inspector（§26）：每个伤害数字都能拆开看");
{
  const dmgIdx = frames.map((f, i) => ({ f, i })).filter(x => x.f.t === "DAMAGE");
  ok(`录像里有 ${dmgIdx.length} 条伤害`, dmgIdx.length > 5);
  const bd = p.damageBreakdown(dmgIdx[dmgIdx.length - 1].i);
  ok("★ 能取到伤害拆解", !!bd && !!bd.data);
  ok("拆解含六个环节", ["base", "rate", "crit", "modifiers", "defense", "float", "steps"].every(k => k in bd!.data));
  ok("★ 拆解的算式能复算出最终伤害", (() => {
    const b = bd!.data;
    const expect = b.type === "FIXED" ? b.base * b.rate : b.base * b.rate * b.crit.cridMultiplier * b.defense.multiplier * b.modifiers.product * b.float.value;
    return Math.abs(expect - b.final) < 1e-6;
  })());
  ok("人话版算式不少于 5 步", bd!.data.steps.length >= 5);
  ok("★ 从任意帧往前找，都能找到最近一次伤害拆解", [10, 60, p.frameCount - 1].every(f => !!p.damageBreakdown(f) || f < dmgIdx[0].i));
}

sec("[2] AI Inspector（§25）：为什么放这个技能");
{
  const d = p.aiDecision(p.frameCount - 1);
  ok("★ 能取到 AI 决策", !!d && !!d.skill);
  ok("决策带逐条规则的判定", Array.isArray(d!.skill.rules) && d!.skill.rules.length >= 1);
  ok("决策有明确原因与选中技能", typeof d!.skill.reason === "string" && !!d!.skill.chosen);
  ok("★ 能取到目标选择（打谁、依据是什么）", !!d!.target, JSON.stringify(d!.target ?? null).slice(0, 80));
  ok("规则表每行都有 鬼火是否够 / 条件是否成立", d!.skill.rules.every((r: any) => "affordable" in r && "cond" in r));
}

sec("[3] 状态检查器（§27）：看生命周期而不是快照");
{
  const tl = p.statusTimeline();
  ok(`录像里共 ${tl.length} 条状态记录`, tl.length > 0);
  ok("每条都带 单位/状态/起始帧", tl.every(s => s.unitId && s.status && typeof s.from === "number"));
  ok("★ 已结束的状态有终止帧，且终止晚于起始", tl.filter(s => s.to !== null).every(s => (s.to as number) > s.from));
  // 打开/关闭必须自洽：to===null 的不能标记为已过期，已过期的一定有终止帧
  ok("状态开关自洽（未结束的不算过期，已结束的必有终止帧）", tl.every(s => (s.to === null) === !s.expired));
  ok("按单位过滤可用", p.statusTimeline(tl[0].unitId).every(s => s.unitId === tl[0].unitId));
  ok("有时长与来源字段（数据缺失时如实为空）", tl.every(s => "duration" in s && "source" in s && typeof s.known === "boolean"));
}

sec("[4] RNG Inspector（§20）");
{
  const s = p.rngSummary();
  ok(`全场抽数 ${s.total} 次`, s.total > 0);
  ok("★ 带用途标签的抽数被统计出来", s.labeled > 0 && s.labeled + s.unlabeled === s.total);
  ok("★ 未标注的如实计入 unlabeled（不假装全知道）", s.unlabeled >= 0 && (s.byTag["unlabeled"] ?? 0) === s.unlabeled);
  ok("暴击/浮动标签可见", Object.keys(s.byTag).some(t => t.startsWith("crit:")) && Object.keys(s.byTag).some(t => t.startsWith("float:")));
  const dmgIdx = frames.findIndex(f => f.t === "DAMAGE");
  const r = p.rngAt(dmgIdx);
  ok("★ 伤害帧能列出它用掉的随机数（含序号与用途）", !!r && r.draws.length >= 1 && r.draws.every(d => typeof d.n === "number" && typeof d.tag === "string"));
  // 不消耗随机数的帧必须返回 null（而不是编一个出来）
  const noRoll = frames.findIndex(f => f.t === "SKILL_CAST");
  ok("不消耗随机数的帧如实返回 null", noRoll >= 0 ? p.rngAt(noRoll) === null : true, noRoll >= 0 ? `frame ${noRoll} (${frames[noRoll].t})` : "无此帧");
}

sec("[5] 界面接线：五个检查器页签都在");
{
  const th = S("components/theater/BattleTheater.vue");
  ok("★ 伤害拆解 / AI 决策 / 状态生命周期 / 随机数 / 事件链 五个页签", ["伤害拆解", "AI 决策", "状态生命周期", "随机数", "事件链"].every(t => th.includes(t)));
  ok("页签引用了 Presenter 的四个检查器方法", ["damageBreakdown", "aiDecision", "statusTimeline", "rngAt", "rngSummary"].every(m => th.includes(m)));
  ok("状态检查器把「源数据缺失」显式标出来", /源数据缺失/.test(th));
  ok("随机数页签说明未标注项不假装知道用途", /不假装每一个随机都知道用途/.test(th));
}

console.log(`\n══════════════════════════════════`);
console.log(`UI-9 Inspectors 验收：${pass} 通过 / ${fail} 失败`);
console.log(`══════════════════════════════════`);
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
```

### `apps/web/tools/build-engine-bundle.mjs`

```js
// tools/build-engine-bundle.mjs — 把知识库切片打包成浏览器可直接 import 的引擎数据
// 为什么需要：引擎的装载器 compile/load.ts 依赖 node:fs，浏览器无法使用。
// 装配逻辑本身已抽到 compile/character.ts（纯函数），这里只负责把**数据**搬过去。
import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";

const ROOT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "..");
const KB = path.join(ROOT, "data", "kb");
const OUT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..", "src", "engine");
const read = async f => JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(path.join(KB, f), "utf8"));

const shikiRaw = await read("shikigami.full.json");
const ai = await read("ai.json");
const buffs = await read("buffs.json");
const souls = await read("soul.json");
// ★ 第 71 轮：占位符表。[tips_2] 就是「基础概率」—— 被动文案里 721 处占位符全靠它才读得懂，
//   不带上它，"40% [tips_2]" 会被分类器看成"无条件"（第 69 轮踩过）。
// ★★★ 第 101 轮：**desc 也要带上**。原来这里写着"desc 用不到"，那是当时的实情；
//   第 101 轮起编译期要从 tips 的 desc 认出"姿态/化身"类放置单位（生命基准、免疫项、回归时机都写在 desc 里），
//   而网页侧装配用的是**这个 bundle**。只带 id/name 的后果是**静默的**：
//   引擎仓库里的测试（自己读 data/kb/tips.json）解析出 2 条形态，网页侧却是 0 条 ——
//   正是"两条装配路径装了两套不同的运行时"那一类（第 87 轮的教训）。实测抓法见 tests/g62 与 TIPS_NO_DESC 这个跳过原因。
const tipsRaw = await read("tips.json");
const tips = tipsRaw.map(t => ({ id: String(t.id), name: String(t.name ?? ""), desc: String(t.desc ?? "") }));
let onmyojiAI = { profiles: [] };
try { onmyojiAI = await read("onmyoji-ai.json"); } catch { /* 可选 */ }

// 只保留装配真正读取的字段（技能/面板/品阶），丢掉 wiki 原文与标签，体积可减半以上
const shiki = shikiRaw.map(s => ({
  id: Number(s.id), name: s.name, rarity: s.rarity ?? null, year: s.year ?? null,
  grade: s.grade ?? null, gradeRaw: s.gradeRaw ?? null,
  panel: s.panel ?? null,
  skills: (s.skills ?? []).map(k => ({
    id: k.id, name: k.name, cost: k.cost, brief: k.brief ?? "",
    description: k.description ?? "", upgrades: k.upgrades ?? [], tags: k.tags ?? [],
  })),
}));

// 能力覆盖矩阵：Fidelity 的 Mechanic 层要用它把"本局需要的能力"映射到支持度。
// 放进 bundle 而不是让适配器直接 import JSON —— Node 对裸 JSON import 要求 import attributes，
// 而 Vite 不需要；放这里两边都能跑，数据来源也只有一处。
let capabilities = { rows: [], cov: null };
try { capabilities = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(path.join(ROOT, "battle-lab", "capability", "coverage.json"), "utf8")); } catch { /* 缺了就如实为空 */ }

const bundle = {
  schemaVersion: 1,
  capabilities,
  engineVersion: "battle-lab 0.1-experimental",
  gameDataVersion: "2026-10-01",
  builtAt: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  shiki, ai, buffs, souls, onmyojiAI, tips,
};
await fs.mkdir(OUT, { recursive: true });
const file = path.join(OUT, "bundle.json");
await fs.writeFile(file, JSON.stringify(bundle), "utf8");
const kb = (await fs.stat(file)).size / 1024;
console.log("=== 引擎数据包已生成 ===");
console.log(`  文件          src/engine/bundle.json`);
console.log(`  体积          ${kb.toFixed(1)} KB`);
console.log(`  式神          ${shiki.length} 只（技能 ${shiki.reduce((a, s) => a + s.skills.length, 0)} 条）`);
console.log(`  AI 档案       ${ai.length} 条`);
console.log(`  buff 定义     ${buffs.length} 条`);
console.log(`  御魂          ${souls.length} 条`);
console.log(`  阴阳师 AI     ${(onmyojiAI.profiles ?? []).length} 条`);
console.log(`  能力矩阵      ${(capabilities.rows ?? []).length} 条（Fidelity 的 Mechanic 层数据源）`);
console.log(`  原始 KB 对照  ${((await Promise.all(["shikigami.full.json","ai.json","buffs.json","soul.json"].map(async f => (await fs.stat(path.join(KB, f))).size)))).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/1024|0} KB`);
```

### `apps/web/tools/build-experiments-index.mjs`

```js
// tools/build-experiments-index.mjs — 生成前端「机制实验台」索引
// 运行：node tools/build-experiments-index.mjs   （工作目录 apps/web）
//
// 原则：只做数据整形，不复制任何引擎逻辑。
//   · 44 个实验条目  ← battle-lab/experiments/results/_all.json（唯一数值来源）
//   · 红区规则元数据  ← 直接 import 引擎的 Policy 常量（id/status/question/evidence）
//   · 已验证规则      ← battle-lab/experiments/verified-rules.json
//   · 机制覆盖度      ← battle-lab/capability/coverage.json
// 任何字段都不得手写：源文件缺什么就留空，并记入 anomalies。
import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";

import { StartupPolicies, DeathPolicies, GuessingRulesets } from "../../../battle-lab/kernel/policies.ts";
import { ResourcePolicies } from "../../../battle-lab/kernel/resource.ts";
import { PriorityPolicies } from "../../../battle-lab/kernel/events.ts";
import { TieBreaks } from "../../../battle-lab/kernel/timeline.ts";

const ROOT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "..");
const OUT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..", "src", "kb", "experiments.json");

const SRC = {
  all: "battle-lab/experiments/results/_all.json",
  definitions: "battle-lab/experiments/definitions.ts",
  groupsBcd: "battle-lab/experiments/groups-bcd.ts",
  verified: "battle-lab/experiments/verified-rules.json",
  coverage: "battle-lab/capability/coverage.json",
};
const readJSON = async p => JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(path.join(ROOT, p), "utf8"));
const readText = p => fs.readFile(path.join(ROOT, p), "utf8");

const anomalies = [];
const note = (kind, msg) => anomalies.push({ kind, msg });

// ───────────────────────── 1. 实验条目 ─────────────────────────
const raw = await readJSON(SRC.all);

// 组 → 红区名：从两个实验定义文件的段注释里取，不手写
function parseGroupZones(ts) {
  const out = {};
  for (const line of ts.split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const m = line.match(/^\s*\/\/\s*-{4,}\s*([ABCD])\s+(.+?)\s*-{4,}\s*$/);
    if (m) out[m[1]] = m[2];
  }
  return out;
}
const defsTs = await readText(SRC.definitions);
const bcdTs = await readText(SRC.groupsBcd);
const GROUP_ZONE = Object.assign({}, parseGroupZones(defsTs), parseGroupZones(bcdTs));
for (const g of ["A", "B", "C", "D"]) {
  if (!GROUP_ZONE[g]) {
    note("GROUP_ZONE_MISSING", "实验定义文件缺少 " + g + " 组的段注释，redZone 退化为组号");
  }
}
const zoneOf = g => (GROUP_ZONE[g] ? g + " " + GROUP_ZONE[g] : g);

// TS 定义文件里的实验 id（用于与 _all.json 交叉校验，防漏跑/多跑）
const idsInTs = ts => [...ts.matchAll(/"([ABCD]\d{2})"/g)].map(m => m[1]);
const tsIds = new Set([...idsInTs(defsTs), ...idsInTs(bcdTs)]);
for (const e of raw) if (!tsIds.has(e.experiment)) note("EXPERIMENT_NOT_IN_TS", e.experiment + " 出现在 _all.json，但定义文件里找不到");
for (const id of tsIds) if (!raw.some(e => e.experiment === id)) note("EXPERIMENT_NOT_RUN", id + " 在定义文件里，但 _all.json 里没有结果");

// verdict：只反映数据里的「是否已实测 / 是否已选定假设」，绝不编造置信度数值
function verdictOf(e, verified) {
  if (verified.has(e.experiment)) return { level: "VERIFIED", text: "已验证 · 游戏内实测确认" };
  if (e.chosenHypothesis) return { level: "HYPOTHESIS", text: "已选定假设 " + e.chosenHypothesis + " · 尚未游戏内实测" };
  if (e.status === "AWAITING_IN_GAME_OBSERVATION") return { level: "UNKNOWN", text: "未验证 · 待游戏内观测" };
  if (e.status === "NOT_RUN") return { level: "UNKNOWN", text: "未验证 · 未运行" };
  return { level: "UNKNOWN", text: "未验证 · 状态 " + e.status };
}

const verifiedJson = await readJSON(SRC.verified);
const verifiedIds = new Set((verifiedJson.rules || []).map(r => r.id || r.experiment || ""));
if (verifiedIds.size === 0) {
  note("NO_VERIFIED_RULE", "verified-rules.json 的 rules 为空：当前没有任何机制被标为 VERIFIED（" + (verifiedJson._note || "") + "）");
}

// 观测值压缩成一行，供表格「结果」列展示（不改数值，只去掉空白）
const compact = v => JSON.stringify(v === undefined ? null : v).replace(/\s+/g, " ");

// 判别性自检：同一实验内各假设的观测（去掉 note 字段后）完全相同 → 实际不可判别
const NON_OBS_KEYS = ["note", "hypothesis", "specNote", "source"];
function observableFingerprint(obs) {
  if (obs === null || typeof obs !== "object" || Array.isArray(obs)) return JSON.stringify(obs);
  const o = {};
  for (const k of Object.keys(obs).sort()) if (NON_OBS_KEYS.indexOf(k) < 0) o[k] = obs[k];
  return JSON.stringify(o);
}
function observableIsEmpty(obs) {
  if (obs === null || obs === undefined) return true;
  if (Array.isArray(obs)) return obs.length === 0;
  if (typeof obs !== "object") return false;
  const keys = Object.keys(obs).filter(k => NON_OBS_KEYS.indexOf(k) < 0);
  if (keys.length === 0) return true;
  return keys.every(k => {
    const v = obs[k];
    return v === null || (Array.isArray(v) && v.length === 0) || (typeof v === "object" && v !== null && Object.keys(v).length === 0);
  });
}

const experiments = raw.map(e => {
  const hyps = e.hypotheses || [];
  const fps = new Set(hyps.map(h => observableFingerprint(h.observable)));
  if (e.discriminating && hyps.length > 1 && fps.size === 1) {
    note("NON_DISCRIMINATING_OBSERVABLE", e.experiment + " 标称可判别，但各假设的观测（除 note 外）完全相同");
  }
  if (hyps.length > 1 && hyps.every(h => observableIsEmpty(h.observable))) {
    note("EMPTY_OBSERVABLE", e.experiment + " 各假设的观测为空，实验台未产出可判别的现象");
  }
  const v = verdictOf(e, verifiedIds);
  return {
    id: e.experiment,
    group: e.group,
    title: e.question || "",
    hypothesis: hyps.map(h => h.id + "：" + h.note).join("；"),
    method: e.inGameObservation || "",
    result: hyps.map(h => h.id + " → " + compact(h.observable)).join("；"),
    verdict: v.text,
    verdictLevel: v.level,
    discriminating: !!e.discriminating,
    redZone: zoneOf(e.group),
    // 以下为可追溯字段（UI 展开行 / 交叉校验用）
    kind: e.kind === undefined ? null : e.kind,
    status: e.status === undefined ? null : e.status,
    confidence: e.confidence === undefined ? null : e.confidence,
    chosenHypothesis: e.chosenHypothesis === undefined ? null : e.chosenHypothesis,
    distinctObservables: e.distinctObservables === undefined ? null : e.distinctObservables,
    hypotheses: hyps.map(h => ({ id: h.id, note: h.note, observable: h.observable === undefined ? null : h.observable })),
    recordedAt: e.recordedAt || null,
  };
});

const GROUP_ORDER = ["A", "B", "C", "D"];
const groupCounts = GROUP_ORDER.map(g => {
  const list = experiments.filter(e => e.group === g);
  return { group: g, zone: zoneOf(g), total: list.length, discriminating: list.filter(e => e.discriminating).length };
});
for (const g of GROUP_ORDER) if (groupCounts.filter(x => x.group === g)[0].total === 0) note("GROUP_EMPTY", g + " 组没有任何实验");

// 同组内多个实验共用同一套候选假设 → 说明判别力在组内是重复的
const bySig = new Map();
for (const e of raw) {
  const sig = e.group + "|" + (e.hypotheses || []).map(h => h.id).sort().join(",");
  if (!bySig.has(sig)) bySig.set(sig, []);
  bySig.get(sig).push(e.experiment);
}
for (const entry of bySig) {
  const sig = entry[0], list = entry[1];
  if (list.length > 2) note("DUPLICATE_HYPOTHESIS_SET", sig.split("|")[0] + " 组 " + list.length + " 个实验共用同一套候选假设（" + list.join(",") + "）");
}

// ───────────────────────── 2. 红区规则 ─────────────────────────
const POLICY_STATUS = ["UNKNOWN", "HYPOTHESIS", "VERIFIED"];
const redZones = [];
function pushPolicy(zone, source, p, questionKey) {
  let status = p.status;
  let rawStatus = null;
  if (POLICY_STATUS.indexOf(status) < 0) {   // 源文件出现了 PolicyStatus 之外的状态值
    rawStatus = status;
    status = "UNKNOWN";
    note("POLICY_STATUS_OUT_OF_ENUM", source + " 的 " + p.id + " status=" + rawStatus + " 不在 {UNKNOWN,HYPOTHESIS,VERIFIED} 内，已按 UNKNOWN 归一");
  }
  const q = (questionKey ? p[questionKey] : undefined) || p.question || "";
  if (!q) note("POLICY_QUESTION_MISSING", source + " 的 " + p.id + " 没有 question 字段，索引中留空");
  const item = { zone: zone, id: p.id, status: status, question: q, evidence: p.evidence || "", source: source };
  if (rawStatus) item.rawStatus = rawStatus;
  redZones.push(item);
}
const ZONE_A = zoneOf("A"), ZONE_B = zoneOf("B"), ZONE_C = zoneOf("C");
const ZONE_D_GUESS = zoneOf("D") + " · 竞猜残局";
const ZONE_D_ORB = zoneOf("D") + " · 鬼火初始化与回火";
for (const p of Object.values(StartupPolicies)) pushPolicy(ZONE_A, "battle-lab/kernel/policies.ts", p);
for (const p of Object.values(PriorityPolicies)) pushPolicy(ZONE_B, "battle-lab/kernel/events.ts", p);
for (const p of Object.values(TieBreaks)) pushPolicy(ZONE_B, "battle-lab/kernel/timeline.ts", p, "note");
for (const p of Object.values(DeathPolicies)) pushPolicy(ZONE_C, "battle-lab/kernel/policies.ts", p);
for (const p of Object.values(GuessingRulesets)) pushPolicy(ZONE_D_GUESS, "battle-lab/kernel/policies.ts", p);
for (const p of Object.values(ResourcePolicies)) pushPolicy(ZONE_D_ORB, "battle-lab/kernel/resource.ts", p);

const redZoneStatusCounts = POLICY_STATUS.reduce((a, s) => (a[s] = redZones.filter(z => z.status === s).length, a), {});
if (redZoneStatusCounts.VERIFIED === 0 && verifiedIds.size === 0) {
  note("NO_VERIFIED_RED_ZONE", "红区规则 0 条 VERIFIED —— 与 verified-rules.json 为空一致（不是解析错误）");
}

// ───────────────────────── 3. 机制覆盖度 ─────────────────────────
const covJson = await readJSON(SRC.coverage);
const cov = covJson.cov || {};
const coverage = (covJson.rows || []).map(r => ({
  capability: r.key,
  status: r.support,
  note: r.note || "",
  tag: r.tag || "",
  evidence: r.evidence || "",
  freq: r.freq === undefined ? null : r.freq,
}));
const covStatus = ["FULL", "PARTIAL", "NONE"];
for (const r of coverage) if (covStatus.indexOf(r.status) < 0) note("COVERAGE_STATUS_OUT_OF_ENUM", r.capability + " support=" + r.status);
const counted = covStatus.reduce((a, s) => (a[s] = coverage.filter(c => c.status === s).length, a), {});
if (coverage.length !== cov.total) note("COVERAGE_TOTAL_MISMATCH", "rows " + coverage.length + " != cov.total " + cov.total);
for (const pair of [["FULL", "full"], ["PARTIAL", "partial"], ["NONE", "none"]]) {
  if (counted[pair[0]] !== cov[pair[1]]) note("COVERAGE_COUNT_MISMATCH", pair[0] + " rows=" + counted[pair[0]] + " != cov." + pair[1] + "=" + cov[pair[1]]);
}
const emptyNote = coverage.filter(c => !c.note);
if (emptyNote.length) note("COVERAGE_NOTE_EMPTY", emptyNote.length + " 条覆盖度没有 note（" + emptyNote.map(c => c.capability).join(",") + "）");
const noneNoNote = emptyNote.filter(c => c.status === "NONE");
if (noneNoNote.length) note("COVERAGE_NONE_NO_REASON", noneNoNote.length + " 条 NONE 没有写未实现原因（" + noneNoNote.map(c => c.capability).join(",") + "）");
const missingInRows = (cov.unsupported || []).filter(k => !coverage.some(c => c.capability === k));
if (missingInRows.length) note("COVERAGE_UNSUPPORTED_MISSING", "cov.unsupported 里的 " + missingInRows.join(",") + " 在 rows 里找不到");

// ───────────────────────── 4. 汇总与写出 ─────────────────────────
const recordedAt = raw.map(e => e.recordedAt).filter(Boolean).sort().pop() || null;
const gameVersions = [...new Set(raw.map(e => e.gameVersion).filter(Boolean))];
if (gameVersions.length > 1) note("GAME_VERSION_MIXED", "实验的 gameVersion 不一致：" + gameVersions.join(" / "));

const out = {
  meta: {
    dataVersion: "GameData 2026-10-01",
    gameVersion: gameVersions[0] || null,
    dataRecordedAt: recordedAt,
    generatedBy: "apps/web/tools/build-experiments-index.mjs",
    sources: Object.values(SRC),
    policySources: [
      "battle-lab/kernel/policies.ts", "battle-lab/kernel/resource.ts",
      "battle-lab/kernel/events.ts", "battle-lab/kernel/timeline.ts",
    ],
  },
  groups: groupCounts,
  experiments: experiments,
  redZones: redZones,
  redZoneStatusCounts: redZoneStatusCounts,
  verifiedRules: {
    count: verifiedIds.size,
    ids: [...verifiedIds],
    note: verifiedJson._note || "",
  },
  coverage: coverage,
  coverageSummary: {
    total: cov.total === undefined ? coverage.length : cov.total,
    full: cov.full === undefined ? counted.FULL : cov.full,
    partial: cov.partial === undefined ? counted.PARTIAL : cov.partial,
    none: cov.none === undefined ? counted.NONE : cov.none,
    kernelCoverage: cov.kernelCoverage === undefined ? null : cov.kernelCoverage,
    hardCoverage: cov.hardCoverage === undefined ? null : cov.hardCoverage,
    unsupported: cov.unsupported || [],
  },
  anomalies: anomalies,
};

await fs.writeFile(OUT, JSON.stringify(out), "utf8");

// ───────────────────────── 5. 打印统计 ─────────────────────────
const kb = (await fs.stat(OUT)).size / 1024;
const dist = g => groupCounts.filter(x => x.group === g)[0].total;
const verdictDist = experiments.reduce((a, e) => (a[e.verdictLevel] = (a[e.verdictLevel] || 0) + 1, a), {});
const byZone = new Map();
for (const e of experiments) { if (!byZone.has(e.redZone)) byZone.set(e.redZone, []); byZone.get(e.redZone).push(e); }
let contrastPairs = 0;
for (const list of byZone.values()) for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < list.length; j++) {
  if (list[i].verdictLevel !== list[j].verdictLevel) contrastPairs++;
}
console.log("=== 机制实验索引已生成 ===");
console.log("  输出 " + path.relative(ROOT, OUT).replace(/\\/g, "/") + "  (" + kb.toFixed(1) + " KB)");
console.log("  experiments  " + experiments.length + " 条（A " + dist("A") + " / B " + dist("B") + " / C " + dist("C") + " / D " + dist("D") + "）"
  + "｜可判别 " + experiments.filter(e => e.discriminating).length + " / 不可判别 " + experiments.filter(e => !e.discriminating).length);
console.log("  verdictLevel  " + JSON.stringify(verdictDist));
console.log("  redZones     " + redZones.length + " 条（" + POLICY_STATUS.map(s => s + " " + redZoneStatusCounts[s]).join(" / ") + "）"
  + "｜VERIFIED 规则 " + verifiedIds.size + " 条");
console.log("  coverage     " + coverage.length + " 条（" + covStatus.map(s => s + " " + counted[s]).join(" / ") + "）"
  + "｜kernelCoverage " + cov.kernelCoverage + "% / hardCoverage " + cov.hardCoverage + "%");
console.log("  假设对比     " + contrastPairs + " 对（同一红区内 verdictLevel 不同）");
console.log("  异常/缺口    " + anomalies.length + " 条");
for (const a of anomalies) console.log("    · [" + a.kind + "] " + a.msg);
```

### `apps/web/tools/build-kb-index.mjs`

```js
// tools/build-kb-index.mjs — 生成前端的紧凑知识库索引
// 索引分两层：selector 索引（首屏必备，~13KB） + detail 索引（懒加载）
import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { pinyin } from "pinyin-pro";
// ★ 从引擎导入效果解释器 —— 不复制逻辑，避免与引擎漂移
import { compileSoulEffect } from "../../../battle-lab/soul/effects.ts";
import { interpretEffect } from "../../../battle-lab/soul/effect-system.ts";

const ROOT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "..");
const KB = path.join(ROOT, "data", "kb");
const OUT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..", "src", "kb");
await fs.mkdir(OUT, { recursive: true });

const ONMYOJI = new Set([10, 11, 12, 13, 15, 16]);
const shiki = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(path.join(KB, "shikigami.full.json"), "utf8"))
  .filter(s => !ONMYOJI.has(Number(s.id)));
const souls = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(path.join(KB, "soul.json"), "utf8"));
const glossary = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(path.join(KB, "glossary.json"), "utf8"));

const RARITY_ORDER = ["SP", "SSR", "SR", "R", "N", "UR", "联动", "呱", "素材"];

function py(name) {
  try { return pinyin(name, { toneType: "none", type: "array" }).join(""); } catch { return ""; }
}
function initials(name) {
  try { return pinyin(name, { pattern: "first", toneType: "none", type: "array" }).join(""); } catch { return ""; }
}

// ---------- selector 索引 ----------
const index = shiki.map(s => {
  const nick = Array.isArray(s.nickname) ? s.nickname.join(" ") : (s.nickname || "");
  return {
    i: Number(s.id),
    n: s.name,
    r: s.rarity || "?",
    y: Number(s.year) || null,
    k: nick || undefined,          // 别名
    p: py(s.name) || undefined,     // 全拼
    s: initials(s.name) || undefined, // 首字母
    t: (s.tags ?? []).slice(0, 4),
    // ★ 六星觉醒面板，供「选中式神即预填八维」用（避免逐格手打 80 个数字）
    // 顺序 = [攻, 生, 防, 速, 暴击%, 爆伤%, 效果命中%, 效果抵抗%]；源数据缺失处为 null
    pn: s.panel ? [s.panel.atk, s.panel.hp, s.panel.def, s.panel.spd,
                   s.panel.cri == null ? null : Math.round(s.panel.cri * 1000) / 10,
                   s.panel.crid == null ? null : Math.round(s.panel.crid * 1000) / 10,
                   s.panel.efh == null ? null : Math.round(s.panel.efh * 1000) / 10,
                   s.panel.efr == null ? null : Math.round(s.panel.efr * 1000) / 10] : null,
    /**
     * ★★★ 第 162 轮：**「有面板」的判据收紧**。
     *   原来判的是 `!!s.panel` —— 而实测有 4 只（生剥鬼 / 慧明灯 / 盗人神 / 湍津姬）的 panel 对象
     *   只有速度/暴击/抵抗，**攻/生/防全是 null**（来源 Data:Attribute.json）。
     *   于是图鉴圆点写着「六星面板存在」，而面板其实只有半张 —— 属于「有字段 ≠ 有数据」。
     *   现在判据改成：**攻/生/防至少一项非 null** 才算有面板。
     *   ★ 注意：这不影响预填（panelPrefill 本来就跳过 null，见 apps/web/src/kb/useKb.ts），
     *     只影响**界面上那句声明**是否说得过满。
     */
    has: { panel: !!(s.panel && (s.panel.atk != null || s.panel.hp != null || s.panel.def != null)), skills: (s.skills ?? []).length, ai: !!s.ai },
  };
}).sort((a, b) => {
  const ra = RARITY_ORDER.indexOf(a.r), rb = RARITY_ORDER.indexOf(b.r);
  return (ra < 0 ? 99 : ra) - (rb < 0 ? 99 : rb) || (b.y ?? 0) - (a.y ?? 0) || a.i - b.i;
});
await fs.writeFile(path.join(OUT, "shikigami-index.json"), JSON.stringify(index), "utf8");

// ---------- detail 索引（懒加载）----------
const detail = {};
for (const s of shiki) {
  detail[String(s.id)] = {
    panel: s.panel ?? null,
    tags: s.tags ?? [],
    extraTags: s.extraTags ?? null,
    skills: (s.skills ?? []).map(k => ({
      id: k.id, name: k.name, cost: k.cost, brief: k.brief,
      description: k.description, upgrades: k.upgrades ?? [], tags: k.tags ?? [],
    })),
    ai: s.ai ?? null,
  };
}
await fs.writeFile(path.join(OUT, "shikigami-detail.json"), JSON.stringify(detail), "utf8");

// ---------- 御魂索引 ----------
const soulIndex = souls.map(s => {
  const ir = compileSoulEffect(s);
  const interp = interpretEffect(ir);
  const supported = interp.kind !== "UNSUPPORTED" && !!ir.trigger;
  return {
    i: Number(s.id), n: s.name, tag: s.Tag || "", suit: s.suit || "", desc: s.desc || "",
    type: Number(s.type) || 1,
    // 2 件套的机器可读形式（UI 展示 + 后续校验共用）
    suitBonus: parseSuit(s.suit),
    // ★ 引擎实现状态：UI 必须在开跑前告知用户（UX 护栏 #5）
    engine: {
      trigger: ir.trigger,
      effectKind: interp.kind,
      magnitudes: ir.magnitudes,
      support: supported ? "FULL" : (ir.trigger ? "PARTIAL" : "UNSUPPORTED"),
      note: interp.note,
    },
  };
}).sort((a, b) => a.i - b.i);
await fs.writeFile(path.join(OUT, "soul-index.json"), JSON.stringify(soulIndex), "utf8");

// ---------- 术语表（Knowledge 页用）----------
await fs.writeFile(path.join(OUT, "glossary.json"), JSON.stringify(
  glossary.map(g => ({ id: g.id, name: g.name, text: g.text }))), "utf8");

function parseSuit(t) {
  if (!t) return null;
  const P = [["攻击加成", "attack"], ["生命加成", "hp"], ["防御加成", "defense"], ["暴击伤害", "critDamage"],
             ["暴击", "critRate"], ["效果命中", "effectHit"], ["效果抵抗", "effectResist"]];
  for (const [label, key] of P) {
    const m = t.match(new RegExp(label + "\\s*(\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)%"));
    if (m) return { key, rate: parseFloat(m[1]) / 100 };
  }
  const sp = t.match(/速度\\s*\\+?\\s*(\\d+)/);
  if (sp) return { key: "speed", flat: parseFloat(sp[1]) };
  return null;
}

const bytes = async f => (await fs.stat(path.join(OUT, f))).size;
console.log("=== KB 索引已生成 ===");
for (const f of ["shikigami-index.json", "shikigami-detail.json", "soul-index.json", "glossary.json"]) {
  console.log(`  ${f.padEnd(26)} ${(await bytes(f) / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`);
}
console.log(`\n式神 ${index.length} 只｜御魂 ${soulIndex.length} 条｜术语 ${glossary.length} 条`);
const withPy = index.filter(x => x.p).length;
console.log(`拼音覆盖 ${withPy}/${index.length}`);
console.log("稀有度分布:", JSON.stringify(index.reduce((a, x) => (a[x.r] = (a[x.r] || 0) + 1, a), {})));
console.log("样例:", JSON.stringify(index.find(x => x.n === "夜溟彼岸花")));
```

### `apps/web/tools/ssr-harness/build.ts`

```ts
// tools/ssr-harness/build.ts — ★★★ 第 139 轮：SSR 验收的**共用构建器**（ui16 / ui17 都走它）
//
// 为什么抽出来：构建产物与加载方式有几条硬约束（都是实测踩出来的），写在两处必然走样：
//   ① 产物必须落在能解析到依赖的地方 —— 系统临时目录里解析不到 vue（ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND），
//      只有 node_modules 下面才行；
//   ② 又不能落在源码目录里 —— 本进程 import 过之后 Windows 上就删不掉了
//      （fs.rmSync 报成功但目录仍在，子进程轮询也删不掉，只有本进程退出才行）；
//   ③ 加载要用 file:// URL（Windows 上直接 import 绝对路径会 ERR_UNSUPPORTED_ESM_URL_SCHEME）。
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

export const WEB = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..");
export const HARNESS_DIR = import.meta.dirname;
export const SSR_OUT = path.join(WEB, "node_modules", ".dsh-ssr-harness");
export const VITE_BIN = path.join(WEB, "node_modules", "vite", "bin", "vite.js");
export const ENTRY_REL = "tools/ssr-harness/entry.ts";

/** 构建 + 加载。失败时返回 ok:false 与原始输出（测试自己决定怎么报）。 */
```
